(1580) GIKATILLA, JOSEPH

**The Book of the Gates of Justice by Rabbi Joseph Gikatilla**
<note>original Hebrew: *Sefer Sha'arei Tzedek le-Rabbi Yosef Gikatilla*</note>

-><note>This describes a bookplate from the *Bibliotheca Philosophica Hermetica* (The Ritman Library), an institution dedicated to the study of Western spirituality.</note>
A circular bookplate is centered on the page. Around the border is the text **"Hermetic Philosophy"** <note>original Latin: *Philosophia Hermetica*</note>. Inside the circle is a shield; the upper portion depicts a **"pelican in her piety"** feeding her young from her breast, with rays of light emanating from a divine sun above. The lower portion of the shield contains four rosettes arranged in a cross pattern.<-

Gikatilla [Hebrew: **Gates of Justice**]
<note>original transcription: *Scha-are Zedek*</note>
Riva di Trento, Jacob Marcaria, 1561
<note>Riva di Trento was a notable center for Hebrew printing in the mid-16th century, where the physician Jacob Marcaria operated a press under the protection of the local Cardinal.</note>

<summary>This page serves as a library cataloging entry and provenance record for a 1561 edition of Joseph Gikatilla's "Sha'are Tzedek," a foundational text of the Kabbalah.</summary>
<keywords>Joseph Gikatilla, Sha'are Tzedek, Gates of Justice, Riva di Trento, Jacob Marcaria, Hermetic Philosophy, Kabbalah, bookplate, 16th-century printing</keywords>

<insert>
Joseph ben Abraham Gikatilla. **Gates of Justice** <note>original Hebrew: *Sha-are Zedek*</note> (in Hebrew).
(A Kabbalistic work on the ten <term>Sefiroth|The ten divine attributes or emanations through which the Infinite interacts with the universe</term> and the names of God).
Riva di Trento, Jacob Marcaria, 1561. *Three large ornamental*
*woodcut Hebrew characters on the title page. The first word of the text*
*proper is surrounded by a fine, large woodcut foliage border (105 x*
*80 mm). Printed in double columns.* 52 leaves. Quarto. 18th-century
half calf binding (slightly rubbed, corners worn).

*Steinschneider CB* <note>Moritz Steinschneider's "Catalogus Librorum Hebraeorum," a seminal bibliography of Hebrew books</note> 5923.17. *Fürst I*, 174 (listed under Chiquitilla).
*Zedner* 330. *Roest* 564. *Amram, "Makers of Hebrew Books in Italy"*
305. Rare first edition of this fundamental introduction to
the philosophy of the <term>Kabbalah|The ancient Jewish tradition of mystical interpretation of the Bible</term> by Joseph ben Abraham Gikatilla (Giquatilla), 1248–1305, a pupil of Abraham Abulafia and Moses
of Leon during the years when the <term>Zohar|The primary text of Jewish mysticism</term> originated, with which he
was no doubt familiar. He was a leading Spanish Kabbalist at
the end of the 13th century, who wrote several influential works
on the symbolism of the Sefiroth. The present work was printed
by the famous physician Jacob Marcaria, who established a
notable Hebrew press in Riva di Trento in 1558. "In an active
period of five years this press produced upwards of 30 books
of value and importance." (Amram). On the back of the title page is
a short preface by the learned printer. Not in the Short Title Catalogue (STC) of
Italian books. — Old notes on title; slightly waterstained.
</insert>

<summary>This page provides a detailed bibliographical description of the 1561 first edition of Joseph Gikatilla's "Sha'are Tzedek," a major Kabbalistic text. It highlights the work's historical importance and its production by the famous physician-printer Jacob Marcaria in Riva di Trento.</summary>

<margin><unclear>Vi</unclear></margin>

<margin><unclear>2829</unclear></margin>

<insert>
Isaac ben Abraham Chiyyut: **The Two Eyes of Isaac** (in Hebrew).
Dogmatic work on the 613 <term>mitzvot|The religious commandments or duties in Jewish law</term> and the names of God.
Livorno, Jacob ben Abraham Meldola, 1587. First rare edition.
The text contains several references to the <term>Kabbalah|The ancient Jewish tradition of mystical interpretation of the Bible</term> and to the *Sefer ha-Yetzirah* <note>The "Book of Creation," one of the oldest and most cryptic texts of Jewish mysticism</note>.
Folio. 20 leaves. Small woodcut on the title page. (Compare Steinschneider, *Catalog of Hebrew Books* 5334, 1).
Cloth spine, slightly rubbed, corners worn.
</insert>

---

Steinschneider, *Catalog of Hebrew Books* 5334, 1. First edition (listed under Chajut). Zedner, page 191.
Roest, page 254. Amsterdam Catalog page 248. Cowley, page 256. (There is also a second edition of this work printed in Livorno, 1785).

---

Chajut (also spelled Chajjat), 1538–1608, a pupil of Nissim Ashkenazi and Moses
Isserles <note>Moses Isserles, known as the Rema, was a preeminent Polish rabbi and authority on Jewish law</note>, was of French origin according to some, but according
to others, he was a leading Spanish <term>Kabbalist|A practitioner or student of Jewish mysticism</term> at
the end of the 16th century who wrote several commentaries
on the symbolism of the <term>Sefiroth|The ten divine attributes or emanations through which the Infinite interacts with the universe</term>. The present work was edited
by the famous physician Jacob Meldola, the head of the
well-known Jewish family in Italy in 1588. "He was active
beginning with the fine press, producing upwards of 30 books
of value and rarity" (Amram); on the <term>verso|The back side of a leaf of paper</term> of the last leaf is a
short preface by the learned printer. Not in Zedner or the British Museum catalog.
Old Hebrew notes on the title page, slightly waterstained.

<summary>This page catalogs a rare 1587 first edition of "The Two Eyes of Isaac" by Isaac ben Abraham Chiyyut, a work exploring the 613 commandments and mystical divine names. It provides bibliographical references and biographical context for the author and the printer, Jacob Meldola.</summary>

<keywords>Isaac ben Abraham Chiyyut, Shenei Einei Yitzchak, mitzvot, Kabbalah, Sefer Yetzirah, Livorno printing, Jacob Meldola, Sefiroth, Moses Isserles</keywords>

<image-desc>A circular logo for the Library of Hermetic Philosophy <note>original: Bibliotheca Philosophia Hermetica</note>. The central image depicts a pelican in its piety, pecking its breast to feed its young with its own blood—a traditional symbol of sacrifice and Christ. <insert>
**Joseph ben Abraham Gikatilla. Gates of Justice (*Sha’are Zedek*). Riva di Trento, Jacob Macaria, 1561**
One of Gikatilla’s disquisitions on the ten <term>Sefiroth|The ten creative powers or attributes in Kabbalah through which the Infinite interacts with the universe</term> under the title *The Gates of Justice*. Gikatilla was the first to equate the *En Sof* <note>The Infinite; God as He exists prior to any self-manifestation</note> with the first Sefira, *keter* <note>The "Crown," representing the Divine Will or the most hidden emanation</note>. The book was printed by the physician Jacob Macaria, who in 1558 established a renowned Hebrew press in Riva di Trento.
</insert>

---

Gikatilla. [Hebrew: *The Gates of Justice* (Sha’are Zedek)].
Riva di Trento, J. Macaria, 1561.

### Jacob ben Abraham Gikatilla: The Book of the Name (*Sefer ha-Shem*) <note>original Hebrew: Sefer ha-Shem. Although the slip names him "Jacob," this author is historically known as Joseph ben Abraham Gikatilla.</note>

Kabbalistic work on the <term>Tetragrammaton|The sacred four-letter name of God (YHWH) in Hebrew tradition</term> and the names of God.
Riva di Trento, Jacob Marcaria, 1561. First three leaves (original).
Title page in Hebrew characters. The first word of the text has a woodcut border. [36] leaves, small <term>quarto|A book size where each sheet of paper is folded twice to create four leaves</term>. Quarter leather binding on boards. [36] leaves. Last leaf blank.
Well-preserved (though slightly rubbed, with worn corners).

---

Steinschneider, *Catalog of Hebrew Books* <note>original Latin: Catalogus Librorum Hebraeorum</note> 5522, 11. First edition, I, 11 (under Gikatilla).
Zedner 330; Roest 254. Amram, *The Makers of Hebrew Books in Italy*, page 305. The first edition of this fundamental work was printed in—

---

(Gikatilla), 1248–1305. A pupil of Abraham Abulafia, from whom he learned the origin of the <term>Kabbalah|The Jewish mystical tradition</term> with which he was no doubt familiar. He was a leading Spanish Kabbalist at the end of the 13th century, who wrote several influential books on the symbolism of the <term>Sefiroth|The ten creative attributes or emanations through which the Divine interacts with the universe</term>. The present work was produced by the famous physician Jacob Marcaria, who established the press at his house in Riva di Trento in 1558. "He was acting <unclear>as director</unclear>." During the five years this press produced upwards of 30 books of value and importance (according to Amram). On the <term>verso|The back side of a page</term> of leaf 1, there is a short preface by the learned printer. Not held in the British Museum or the Bodleian Library.
Italian paper, old notes on the title page; slightly water-stained.

<summary>This page contains a detailed bibliographic description of a 1561 edition of "Sefer ha-Shem" (The Book of the Name) by the Spanish Kabbalist Joseph Gikatilla. It highlights the historical significance of the work and its printer, the physician Jacob Marcaria of Riva di Trento.</summary>
<keywords>Joseph Gikatilla, Sefer ha-Shem, Kabbalah, Tetragrammaton, Riva di Trento, Jacob Marcaria, Sefiroth, Hebrew printing, bibliography</keywords>

<insert>1580</insert> GIKATILLA, JOSEPH

**The Gates of Justice, with an explanation of the ten Sefirot** <note>original Hebrew: Sha'are Tzedek be-vi'ur eser sefirot</note>

<image-desc>A circular bookplate or sticker is affixed to the center of the page. The border features the text "HERMETIC PHILOSOPHY" <note>original Latin: PHILOSOPHIA HERMETICA</note> in golden serif capital letters. Inside the circle, a white pelican is depicted "in its piety," wounding its breast to feed three chicks in a nest with its own blood. This is a traditional symbol of self-sacrifice and devotion. The nest rests upon a blue square base or altar, which is decorated with a thin white cross separating four golden floral rosettes. Above the pelican, a golden sun-like arc emits rays of light or drops of blood into the scene.</image-desc>

Gikatilla. [Hebrew: *The Gates of Justice* (Sha'are Tzedek)]

Riva di Trento, J. Marcaria, 1561

<note>This entry describes an edition of Joseph Gikatilla's foundational Kabbalistic text, *Sha'are Tzedek*. Gikatilla was a major Spanish Kabbalist whose work helped organize the complex system of the <term>Sefirot|The ten creative attributes or divine emanations through which the Infinite interacts with the world</term>. This specific copy was printed in 1561 in Riva di Trento, Italy, by Jacob Marcaria, a physician who ran a prolific Hebrew printing press under the patronage of the local Cardinal.</note>

<summary>This page is a bibliographic frontispiece for a 1561 edition of Joseph Gikatilla's "The Gates of Justice," featuring a bookplate from the Library of Hermetic Philosophy.</summary>
<keywords>Joseph Gikatilla, Sha'are Tzedek, Gates of Justice, Kabbalah, Sefirot, Riva di Trento, Jacob Marcaria, Hermetic Philosophy, bibliography</keywords>

--- Page 16 ---
<meta>This page transitions from the title page to the author's personal introduction. Rabbi Joseph Gikatilla addresses a student or colleague ("friend of my soul") who has asked for guidance in understanding the Sefirot. The text moves from the philosophical necessity of proper instruction to the cosmological origins of Israel's unique spiritual status.</meta>

# You Asked
<note>original: שאלת (She'alt). The large woodcut heading translates to "You asked," referring to the inquiry posed to the author that prompted the writing of this book.</note>

...from me, beloved of my soul, to illuminate one of the inner understandings—to know in which way the light of the Lord <note>original: ידוד. A common scribal substitution for the Tetragrammaton.</note> proceeds regarding the matter of the **Ten Sefirot**. 
<term>Sefirot|The ten attributes or emanations in Kabbalah through which the Infinite (Ein Sof) reveals itself and interacts with the universe.</term>
And behold, I have granted your request to fulfill your desires, and may God look upon you with favor. The beginning of the path for a person to enter into the unity of the Creator, blessed be He, is the knowledge of the "Gate." For do you not know that if a person wishes to see the face of the King and toils for many days, but after all that toil he has not merited to know the location of the gate, what benefit is there in all the labor he exerted and the entire journey he traveled? If he does not know the gate through which to enter to see the face of the King, his effort is in vain. In this same way, most of those who exert themselves to enter the **Pardes** 
<term>Pardes|Literally "orchard," referring here to the realm of mystical and esoteric study.</term>
do not know and do not understand, walking in darkness; there is no benefit in their labor. Therefore, I have seen fit to illuminate a path before you, in the way...

<column-break/>

...in which you shall walk and the gate through which you shall enter. Then you shall be desired and accepted before the Lord, blessed be He, and you shall be among those who merit "to behold the graciousness of the Lord and to visit His temple" (Psalm 27:4). "This is the gate of the Lord; the righteous shall enter through it" (Psalm 118:20). Know that the Lord, blessed be He, chose Israel when He divided all the languages during the **Generation of Division**, 
<note>original: דור הפלגה (Dor Ha-Pallagah). This refers to the biblical account of the Tower of Babel when humanity was split into different nations and languages.</note>
each man to his own language, according to their families and their nations. He gave to every single nation a "Minister" and a ruler over them, and He also gave to every nation a portion of the land. In this manner, the nations and the lands were individualized, each to a known Minister. Concerning this it is said, "From these the maritime nations spread out into their territories by their clans within their nations, each with its own language" (Genesis 10:5). This is the secret of the matter of the Division: all **seventy nations** were left delivered into the hands of **seventy Ministers** above, and those seventy Ministers surround...

<sig>A B G D V H [Be Strong]</sig>
<note>These characters likely represent gathering signatures for the printer, followed by a traditional Hebrew closing "Chazak" (Be Strong).</note>

<summary>Rabbi Joseph Gikatilla begins his introduction by explaining that mystical study requires "knowing the gate" to be effective. He establishes the cosmological framework of the seventy nations being ruled by angelic ministers, setting the stage for Israel's direct relationship with God.</summary>

# And You, the Reader

It shall be a merit for us that we bring to light the mysteries of wisdom:
**The Gates of Justice**; <note>original: *Sha'arei Tzedek*. This title refers to the specific mystical sphere (Sefirah) of Justice/Righteousness, often associated with the entry point into the divine realm.</note> for light has already shone for us through the book **The Gates of Light** <note>original: *Sha'arei Orah*. This was Joseph Gikatilla's primary work on the Sefirot, printed shortly before this volume.</note> printed already here; may it be reckoned to us as righteousness. In righteousness, behold His face; for you shall understand and know the secret of **Kabbalah**. As it is said: "One who comes to purify himself, they open the way for him," <note>A common Talmudic maxim (Shabbat 104a) suggesting that divine assistance follows human initiative.</note> and the gates have not been locked. It is a "ladder set on the earth, with its top reaching the heavens" and the **Work of the Chariot** <term>Ma'aseh Merkavah|The "Work of the Chariot," referring to the earliest form of Jewish mysticism centered on the vision of the divine throne-chariot in the Book of Ezekiel.</term>. "Lift up your heads, O gates!" and "Justice, justice shall you pursue." "Turn it over and turn it over, for everything is in it" <note>A quote from the Ethics of the Fathers (Pirkei Avot 5:22) regarding the infinite depth of sacred study.</note>. "Enter His gates with thanksgiving"; then my tongue shall sing: "Open for me the gates of righteousness, I will enter them and thank the Lord."

->Declared by the physician to these<-

->Jacob Marcaria<-
<note>Jacob Marcaria (d. 1562) was a physician and the operator of the famous Hebrew press in Riva di Trento. He was instrumental in disseminating Kabbalistic texts during the 16th century.</note>

# You Have Asked

You, my soul’s friend, have asked me to illuminate one of the inner perceptions <note>original: *hassagot*; this refers to intellectual or spiritual attainments or insights into the divine realm.</note> so as to know which way the light goes concerning the matter of the **Ten Sefirot** <term>Ten Sefirot|The ten creative powers or attributes through which the Infinite (Ein Sof) reveals itself and governs the universe.</term>. And behold, I have granted your request to fulfill your desires, and may God find favor in you. The start of the path upon which a person enters into the unity of the Creator, blessed be He, is the knowledge of the "Gate." For do you not know that if a person wishes to see the face of the King and toils for many days, yet after all that toil he does not merit to know the location of the gate, what benefit is there in all the toil he spent and the entire way he walked? Since he does not know the gate through which to enter to see the face of the King. In this manner, most people trouble themselves to enter the **Orchard** <note>original: *Pardes*. A famous acronym representing four levels of Torah interpretation, often used here to refer specifically to the "orchard" of mystical/esoteric study.</note> but they neither know nor understand; they walk in darkness, and there is no profit in their labor. Therefore, I have seen fit to illuminate a path before you regarding the way

<column-break/>

in which you shall walk and the gate through which you shall enter. Then you will be desired and accepted before the Lord, <note>The Hebrew text uses a modified form of the Tetragrammaton (Y-H-W-H) to avoid writing the holy name in a way that could be erased.</note> blessed be He, and you will be among those who merit to behold the pleasantness of the Lord and to visit His palace. "This is the gate of the Lord; the righteous shall enter it." **Know that the Lord, blessed be He, chose Israel** when He separated all the languages during the **Generation of the Division** <note>original: *Dor Ha-Palga*. The generation of the Tower of Babel (Genesis 11), when humanity was split into different nations and tongues.</note>, each man to his own language, according to their families and their nations. He gave to every single nation a Ministering Angel <note>original: *Sar*. A celestial prince or planetary power assigned to govern a specific nation.</note> and a ruler over them, and He also gave to every nation a portion of the earth. In this way, the nations and the lands were each set apart for a specific Minister. Concerning this, it is said: "From these the coastlands of the nations were separated in their lands, each according to its language, according to their families, in their nations." This is the secret of the matter of the Division: all **seventy nations** remained delivered into the hands of seventy ministers above, and those seventy ministers surround

<sig>1 2 6 6 outside</sig> <note>These are printer’s signatures used to keep the pages in order during binding.</note>

<summary>The author begins his response to a student's inquiry about the Ten Sefirot by using the metaphor of a "Gate" to the King's palace, arguing that mystical study requires knowing the proper entry point. He introduces the concept of the "Generation of the Division" to explain how the world's seventy nations are governed by intermediary celestial ministers, setting the stage for Israel's unique spiritual position.</summary>

<meta>This page serves as a printer's preface or post-script by Jacob Marcaria, the physician-printer of the Riva di Trento press. It transitions from the author's introductory words to a promotional and celebratory note about the publication of the book "Sha'are Tzedek" (Gates of Justice), linking it to the previous publication of Gikatilla's "Sha'are Orah" (Gates of Light).</meta>

# And You, the Reader

And it shall be a merit for us that we bring to light the hidden mysteries of wisdom: **Gates of Justice** <note>original: *Sha'are Tzedek*; the title of the present work by Joseph Gikatilla.</note>, after light has shone for us through the book **Gates of Light** <note>original: *Sha'are Orah*; Gikatilla's most famous work, which the printer Jacob Marcaria had published shortly before this one.</note> which was already printed here. May this be counted as righteousness for us, and through this righteousness, may you behold His face. For you shall understand and know the secret of **Kabbalah** <term>Kabbalah|The tradition of Jewish mystical interpretation of the Bible, focused on the nature of the divine.</term>, as it is said: "One who comes to purify himself is helped from above" <note>A common Talmudic idiom suggesting that a person's sincere spiritual efforts are met with divine assistance.</note>, and the gates have not been locked. This is a ladder set upon the earth, with its top reaching the heavens, and the **Work of the Chariot** <term>Work of the Chariot|Hebrew: *Ma'aseh Merkavah*. An ancient and central discipline of Jewish mysticism based on the celestial visions of the prophet Ezekiel.</term>. Lift up your heads, O gates! "Justice, justice shall you pursue" <note>Deuteronomy 16:20; a pun on the book's title, *Gates of Justice*.</note>. Turn it and turn it, for everything is within it <note>A reference to the Mishnah (Pirkei Avot 5:22), emphasizing that all wisdom is contained within sacred study.</note>. Enter His gates with thanksgiving; then my tongue will sing: "Open for me the gates of justice, I will enter them and thank God."

->The words of the physician, though the work is not his own,<-

->**Jacob Marcaria**<-

<summary>This page features a short preface by the printer Jacob Marcaria, celebrating the publication of "Sha'are Tzedek" (Gates of Justice) as a companion to his previous printing of Gikatilla's "Sha'are Orah." He uses various biblical and Talmudic metaphors to describe the mystical value of the text as a ladder to the divine.</summary>
<keywords>Jacob Marcaria, Sha'are Tzedek, Sha'are Orah, Gates of Justice, Kabbalah, Ma'aseh Merkavah, printer's preface, Riva di Trento</keywords>

--- Page 20 ---
<meta>This page transitions from the printer's introductory remarks to the core mystical text of Joseph Gikatilla. The author begins by exploring the symbolic "Gate" mentioned in the book's title, moving into a deep Kabbalistic discussion about the Patriarchs—Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—and their roles in establishing the divine "Chariot."</meta>

Woe to the creatures for the insult to this verse! And concerning this it is said, "The righteous is gathered in before the evil comes" <note>Isaiah 57:1</note>. This is what the Torah says: "This is the law: when a man dies in a tent..." <note>Numbers 19:14. In mystical tradition, this suggests that the Torah's secrets are revealed to those who "slay" their worldly desires within the "tent" of study.</note>

**Let us return** to the intention of the verse: "This is the gate of the Lord; the righteous shall enter through it" <note>Psalm 118:20</note>. This means we must seek the gate through which one enters into the presence of the Lord, blessed be He, since four partitions surround His palace.

**We say** that since Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob came, they established all the **Chariots** <term>Chariots|Hebrew: *Merkavot*. The mystical "vehicles" or structures through which the Divine Presence manifests in the world.</term>, both upper and lower, the outer ones and the inner ones. It is found that Abraham and Isaac bound the outer chariot on the outside through Ishmael and Esau <note>In Kabbalah, Ishmael and Esau represent the "husks" or external forces that branched off from the holiness of Abraham and Isaac.</note>. The inner chariot was prepared through Jacob. When Jacob came, he was perfect, without a blemish or flaw, refined sevenfold through the line of Seth, Enoch, Noah, Shem; with Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob being the seventh in that lineage of merit. Of them it is said: "Silver refined in a furnace of earth, purified sevenfold" <note>Psalm 12:6</note>. Thus his "bed" was complete <note>A traditional idiom meaning that all of Jacob's children were righteous, unlike Abraham (who had Ishmael) or Isaac (who had Esau).</note>. Therefore, the Name chose him, for he is the **Middle Line** <term>Middle Line|Hebrew: *Kav ha-Emtza'i*. The central path of the Sefirot (divine emanations) that balances the right side (Mercy) and the left side (Judgment).</term>, and he unified the pure with the pure. Then he found the Lord and had a portion in prophecy, as it is written: "For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself, Israel for His treasure" <note>Psalm 135:4</note>. This is the portion that fell to him in all the world, and this is the secret of the verse: "For the Lord's portion is His people; Jacob is the lot of His inheritance" <note>Deuteronomy 32:9</note>. It is also the secret of "And He brought him outside" <note>Genesis 15:5</note>, and "He found him in a desert land... the Lord alone did lead him" <note>Deuteronomy 32:10-12</note>. Since He chose Jacob, who is the "middle" in the **Middle Line**, Jacob became a complete throne for the Lord, blessed be He. For Abraham and Isaac were only called "Thrones of the Patriarchs," as symbolized by the phrase "The God of Abraham, the God of Isaac, and the God of Jacob." Concerning this it is said: "You have affirmed the Lord... and the Lord has affirmed you" <note>Deuteronomy 26:17-18</note>. The secret of the twelve tribes corresponds to the twelve Names that are formed from the unique Name <note>The Tetragrammaton, YHWH</note>, blessed be He. The verse proclaims: "All these are the twelve tribes of Israel... and this is what their father spoke to them, and he blessed them." And then

<column-break/>

Jacob became a complete throne for the **Chariot**—that is, the "throne of the body" and not the "throne of the earth." For the **Shekhinah** <term>Shekhinah|The Divine Presence that dwells within the physical world.</term> did not dwell below in a permanent state of rest until the "fourth leg" arrived <note>This refers to the idea that the Divine Throne has four legs; the three Patriarchs are the first three, and the fourth is often identified as King David or the Shekhinah itself.</note>. Previously, it was only temporary, even in the days of Moses our Teacher, peace be upon him. Concerning this, it says of the Jubilee: "They shall come up to the mountain" <note>Exodus 19:13</note>.

**After** you have understood this, know that just as the Lord chose Jacob from among the other nations, so He chose Zion from among all lands to dwell in, and Jerusalem from among all locations. Just as a specific guardian angel fell to every nation and land during the "Generation of the Dispersion" <note>The Tower of Babel</note>, so did the portion of the Lord, blessed be He, rise: Jacob from the nations, the Land of Israel from the lands, and Jerusalem from the locations. Of this it is said: "For Your name is called upon Your city and upon Your people" <note>Daniel 9:19</note>. Just as Jacob is in the middle of the Patriarchs, so Jerusalem is in the middle of the inhabited world. Of this it is said: "This is Jerusalem; I have set her in the midst of the nations and the countries that are round about her" <note>Ezekiel 5:5</note>. Thus the secret of "the land and the outskirts" is explained.

Concerning this it is said, "You have affirmed the Lord... and the Lord has affirmed you." You may now learn the secret of "And He brought him outside" <note>Genesis 15:5</note>: Abraham had no visible root or place of attachment except in the "externality" and the "husks" <note>Original: *Kelippot*, the shells of impurity.</note> which are called the **Foreskin** <term>Foreskin|Hebrew: *Orlah*. In Kabbalah, this represents the spiritual blockage or "husk" that conceals holiness.</term>. Therefore, the beginning of the Covenant of **Circumcision** <term>Circumcision|Hebrew: *Milah*. Mystically, the act of removing the "husk" to reveal the light.</term> was given to Abraham. When Abraham was uprooted from the place where he was planted, the Holy One, blessed be He, took him and replanted him in the "inner place." The sign for this is: "And I took your father Abraham from the other side of the river..." <note>Joshua 24:3</note>. Guard this great principle, for it is an inner secret from the chambers of the Torah.

**After** you have understood this, know that the Name of the Lord, the great Name, blessed be He, is called upon Jacob. Since the root of Jacob is the inheritance of the Great Lord, the Lord informed Jacob which "gate" he should enter when he needs to enter His inner chambers—something not granted to any other nation or tongue. Concerning this it is said: "He declares His words to Jacob, His statutes and His judgments to Israel. He has not dealt so with any nation..." <note>Psalm 147:19-20</note>. And from where do we know that the Lord informed Jacob how to come to the Gate to enter His palaces? It is written: "And he encountered the place, and stayed there..." <note>Genesis 28:11; refers to Jacob's dream of the ladder.</note>

<summary>This page explains Jacob's unique status as the "Middle Line" of the divine emanations and the complete "throne" for God's presence. It draws parallels between the election of the people of Israel and the sanctity of Jerusalem as the spiritual center of the world.</summary>
<keywords>Jacob, Middle Line, Chariot, Merkavah, Jerusalem, Shekhinah, Circumcision, Husks, Patriarchs, Zion, Divine Throne</keywords>

outside the **Throne of Glory**, and they are called the secret of the
**Shells** <term>Shells|Hebrew: *Kelipot*. In Kabbalah, these are the spiritual "husks" or layers of impurity that conceal the divine light.</term>. Within them, the secret of the **Foreskin** <term>Foreskin|Hebrew: *Orlah*. Used here as a mystical term for spiritual blockage or uncircumcised impurity that surrounds the holy.</term> takes hold. 
And when **Abraham our father**, peace be upon him, arrived
in that category, he provided a place of attachment
for the "Crowns" that were on the Right Side through **Ishmael**, 
comparable to the thread that joins pieces 
of fabric together. Concerning this, it is said: *And as for Ishmael, I have heard you* <note>Genesis 17:20</note>. 
And this is the secret of: *His hand shall be against every man, and every man's hand against him* <note>Genesis 16:12</note>, and *he shall dwell in the presence of all his brethren*. 
And when **Isaac** arrived in that category 
in which he stood, he provided a place of attachment for the Crowns that were 
on the Left Side through **Esau**, 
comparable to 
the thread we mentioned above. Concerning this, it is said: *The pride of 
your heart has deceived you, you who dwell in the clefts of the rock, whose 
habitation is high* <note>Obadiah 1:3</note> and so on. 
Thus we learn that **Ishmael** 
and **Esau** are the secret of the attachment of the **Seventy Crowns** <note>These represent the seventy spiritual princes or guardian angels of the nations of the world, surrounding the core of holiness.</note> surrounding 
the **Chariot**. And all of them are called **Outer Shells** 
and **Foreskin**. 
In Scripture, they are called "Outsides," as it is said: 
*While as yet He had not made the earth, nor the outsides* <note>Proverbs 8:26. The author interprets "outsides" (fields/streets) as the external spiritual realms.</note>. This is the secret 
of *And He brought him outside* <note>Genesis 15:5</note>, and *The wife of 
the deceased shall not marry outside* <note>Deuteronomy 25:5</note>. 
And when **Jacob our father**, peace 
be upon him, arrived, he stood in the middle. 
Abraham on the Right Side, 
and Isaac on the Left Side, 
and Jacob in the middle. 
The secret is: *And Jacob was a simple man, dwelling in tents* <note>Genesis 25:27</note>. 
Thus, Abraham and Isaac prepared the entire Chariot, 
both outer and inner. They prepared the inner, 
which is attached to Jacob in **Beauty** <term>Beauty|Hebrew: *Tiferet*. The central Sefirah (divine attribute) that balances the kindness of Abraham and the severity of Isaac.</term> on the Central Line; 
and they prepared the outer, which are the attachment of the Seventy 
Crowns to the hand of the **LORD** <note>original: "יד וד" (Y-D V-D). A scribal substitution for the four-letter Name of God (Y-H-V-H) to avoid writing it directly.</note>, blessed be He, through Ishmael and Esau. 
It follows that when Jacob arrived, he was pure, without any dross. 
Therefore, he is in the middle. This is the secret of what he said: 
"Lest, Heaven forbid, there be any flaw in my bed" <note>A Midrashic reference to Jacob's concern that, unlike Abraham and Isaac, one of his children might be spiritually unworthy. The answer was that all were righteous.</note>—
unlike Abraham from whom came Ishmael, and Isaac 
from whom came Esau. They all opened their mouths and said: 
*Hear, O Israel...* <note>The Shema: Deuteronomy 6:4</note>. 
Thus Jacob and his sons 
take hold of the **Central Line**, which is called the **Inner Point** 
and the **Inner Palace**. This Inner Palace, called the 
Central Line, is what is called the Name of the **LORD**, blessed be He, 
and it is in the middle. And Seventy Crowns surround 
it. This is what is said in the *Book of Creation*: "The Holy 

<column-break/>

Palace is set exactly in the middle" <note>Sefer Yetzirah 4:3</note>. And this is the secret of **Earth** 
and **Outsides** that we mentioned above. And this is the place 
called the Central Line. We must know 
at which place and through which gate one enters it: 
**We say:** Know that since the Seventy Nations were divided among the Seventy 
Crowns—each nation and its land under a known Prince—
the Name, blessed be He, did not find a portion among them all because He 
is pure and they are all impure. As it is written: *You are of purer eyes 
than to behold evil* <note>Habakkuk 1:13</note>. Concerning this, it is said: *And it came to pass, as they journeyed from the east* <note>Genesis 11:2</note>—they certainly journeyed and separated themselves from the **Ancient One** 
of the world. They had no portion among all 
the nations and all the lands because they were all 
impure, until Jacob came, who is balanced 
between Abraham and Isaac. He is the portion of the **LORD**, 
blessed be He. For the **LORD**, blessed be He, dwells in the 
Inner Palace, and the Princes surround His throne, just like the 
**Fruit and the Shells**. And the great secret that includes 
everything: *It is better to take refuge in the LORD than to trust in man. 
It is better to take refuge in the LORD than to trust in princes. All 
nations surrounded me...* and so on. *In the name of the LORD I will cut them off. 
They surrounded me, yes, they surrounded me; in the name of the LORD I will cut them off. 
They surrounded me like bees; they are quenched as the fire of thorns; in the name of 
the LORD I will cut them off* <note>Psalm 118:8-12</note>. He said "I will cut them off" three times, 
and "surrounded me" four times. 
This is the secret of the **Four Partitions** that Ezekiel saw 
and that Elijah saw; and the fourth was "fine" <note>The "still small voice."</note>. And here 
I am hinting at the "Casing" called the **Outer Throne**, 
which is called **Adam** <note>Humanity/Man</note>, while the **Inner Throne** is called **Adonai** <note>My Lord</note>. 
And the secret of these and those: *When you come into the land... 
then you shall count the fruit thereof as forbidden [foreskin]...* and so on. 
<note>margin: The secret of "foreskin" mentioned three times.</note> *Three years it shall be... and in the fourth year... all 
the fruit thereof shall be [holy]... and in the fifth year you shall eat the 
fruit thereof...* <note>Leviticus 19:23-25. The author links the agricultural laws of forbidden fruit to the spiritual stages of transcending the "Shells" to reach holiness.</note> When you merit to understand what we have written, 
you will grasp the secret of **Earth and Outsides**, and you will grasp: *It is better to take refuge 
in the LORD than to trust in princes*. And the secret is hinted at in 
the likeness of the Throne: *the likeness as the appearance of a man [Adam]* <note>Ezekiel 1:26</note>, and the secret: 
*When the plague of leprosy is in a man [Adam], then he shall be brought unto the priest* <note>Leviticus 13:9</note>.

->I<-

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic structure of the universe, where the Patriarchs Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob represent the balance of divine forces. Jacob, as the "Central Line," dwells in the "Inner Palace" of holiness, while the 70 Nations and their "Princes" form the protective yet impure "Outer Shells" (*Kelipot*) that surround it.</summary>

--- Page 22 ---
<meta>This page continues the Kabbalistic discussion regarding the Patriarchs' relationship to the Divine realm. While the previous page discussed how Ishmael and Esau represent the "outer shells" or "foreskin" attached to Abraham and Isaac, this page explains why Jacob was the first to truly inherit the "Gate" of the Divine Presence.</meta>

"And I will make of you a great nation..." and it concludes, "and you shall be a blessing." <note>Genesis 12:2</note>
What is the meaning of "and you shall be a blessing"? It means: Through you, I serve
the **Shekhinah** <term>Shekhinah|The Divine Presence, the feminine aspect of God that dwells among humanity.</term>, which is the "Pool" <note>The Hebrew words for "blessing" (*Berakhah*) and "pool" (*Berikhah*) share the same root letters.</note>. She is the **Tenth Sefirah** <term>Sefirah|One of the ten divine attributes or emanations in Kabbalah.</term>, which receives an overflow of light from the nine
**shining mirrors** <term>Mirrors|Hebrew: *aspaklariaot*, derived from the Latin *specularia*. These are the lenses or mirrors through which prophets perceive divine truth.</term>. And even though 
He handed over the "Pool" to **Abraham**, He did not hand over the "Gate" to him. 
Similarly for **Isaac**, because he was associated with the refuse 
of **Ishmael** and **Esau** <note>Meaning that because they produced "impure" descendants, they could not fully possess the holy Gate.</note>. However, **Jacob**, who had 
no refuse in him, was given the "Gate," as we have explained. 
And regarding what the Blessed Lord said to **Abraham**, "And in you all 
the families of the earth shall be blessed," <note>Genesis 12:3</note> it is all explained by the fact 
that the seventy nations are attached to **Abraham** and **Isaac** 
by the principle of "grafting" and "interweaving" <note>The Hebrew word *v'nivrekhu* ("shall be blessed") is here linked to the agricultural term for grafting a vine.</note>. Were it not for **Abraham** 
and **Isaac**, the seventy nations would have no attachment to the **Divine Chariot**. 
And this is the secret of the seventy bulls offered during the Festival <note>Reference to the seventy bulls sacrificed during the holiday of Sukkot, traditionally thought to represent the seventy nations of the world.</note>.

### And after
you have understood this, know that the Gate 
called **Righteousness** <note>Hebrew: *Tzedek*, a name for the tenth Sefirah.</note>, even though 
**Abraham** is the root of that Sefirah, he did not inherit the 
Gate until **Jacob** arrived. And concerning this Gate, 
it is said regarding **Abraham**: "Who raised up righteousness from the east, 
calling it to his feet?" <note>Isaiah 41:2</note> And this is the secret of the "Gates of Righteousness."

### And know
that from the time **Jacob** understood the Gate 
and took the key, no 
creature in the world may enter toward the Blessed Lord 
except through **Jacob** and his sons—meaning, until they convert 
and are circumcised and enter under the wings of the **Shekhinah**. 
And through that gate they enter the "Gates of Righteousness," and because of this, 
true converts are called "Converts of Righteousness" <note>Hebrew: *Gerei Tzedek*. The author suggests converts are named after this specific divine Gate.</note>. 
That is to say, the converts enter the Palace of the Blessed Lord 
through the Gate of Righteousness. Observe this principle: because 
**Abraham** is the root of the Sefirah of **Righteousness**, 
all converts are called by his name, "Converts of Righteousness." And the secret 
of "In you they shall be blessed" is that they are literally grafted in.

### And after
you have understood this, we must illuminate 
a path before you regarding the secret of this attribute 
called "The Gate for the Lord" <note>Psalm 118:20</note>. We say that no 
man is worthy to enter through this Gate as a permanent resident, 
except for a temporary entrance to come in and go out. 
But one may not remain inside as a permanent dweller—not even

<column-break/>

**Abraham**, **Isaac**, and **Jacob**, and all the prophets were not privileged 
to remain inside permanently, but rather entered and exited. 
No one remained as a permanent dweller before Him through 
this Gate except for **Moses our Teacher**, peace be upon him, alone. 
And regarding this, it is said: "Go, say to them: Return to 
your tents. But as for you, stand here with Me." <note>Deuteronomy 5:27-28</note> And this is 
what the Sages meant when they said: "All the prophets looked through 
a dim mirror, but **Moses** looked 
through a clear mirror." <note>A famous Talmudic distinction (Yebamot 49b) between the clarity of Moses' prophecy versus other prophets.</note> The reason is because 
he entered inside this Gate. He entered 
with permission and established his dwelling there. And the secret is: "And 
**Moses** was not able to come into the Tent of Meeting because the 
cloud settled on it, and the glory of the Lord filled the Tabernacle." <note>Exodus 40:35</note>
Then the Blessed Lord called to him and he entered with permission. And regarding 
this, it is said: "And He called to **Moses**, and the Lord spoke to him 
from the Tent of Meeting," <note>Leviticus 1:1</note> and it is written, "But as for you, stand here with Me." 
And once he established his dwelling there, he never ceased. And regarding this 
it is said: "Let him kiss me with the kisses of his mouth." <note>Song of Songs 1:2</note> Because 
**Moses** entered inside the Gate and established his 
dwelling there, he was always seeing through the 
clear mirror. But the rest of the prophets, whose 
dwellings were not permanent inside the Gate, even 
at the time they were looking, they were looking from outside the Gatehouse. They 
saw from behind the tenth partition, which is 
 the "dim mirror." And this is the secret of: 
"Hear now My words: If there be a prophet among you..." and so on. 
"Not so My servant **Moses**; in all My house he is trusted." <note>Numbers 12:6-8</note>
"In all My house" literally means he entered and established his dwelling 
inside the Gate. Thus he was found trusted with all 
the King's property, which are the chambers called the King's 
house; "In all My house he is trusted."

### And after
you have understood this, know that this 
attribute called the **Gates of 
Righteousness**, which **Jacob** inherited—he handed over all 
its keys into the hands of his sons. And this Gate 
has thirteen keys: twelve 
for the twelve chambers, and the inner key 
which completes the thirteen—he handed that one 
to **Levi**. And this is the secret of the **Priestly Blessing**, for from there 
the blessings flow.

<summary>This page discusses the mystical "Gate of Righteousness," explaining that while Abraham and Isaac were roots of the divine structure, only Jacob fully inherited the Gate. It further distinguishes Moses as the only prophet to dwell permanently within this divine realm, seeing through a "clear mirror."</summary>

<meta>Continuity: This page follows the discussion of how Abraham and Isaac, despite their greatness, were linked to "outer shells" (Ishmael and Esau), while Jacob represents the pure "Gate." And he stayed there and dreamed, and behold, a ladder was set up on the earth <note>Genesis 28:12</note>. Jacob knew the majesty of the Tetragrammaton <note>The four-letter name of God, written here as "Y' D' V' D'" to avoid writing the holy name directly.</note>, may He be blessed, but he did not know through which gate he should enter to reach Him. Therefore, the Blessed Name showed him the way of entry. From where do we know this? As it is written: "And he dreamed, and behold, a ladder was set up on the earth... and behold, the Lord stood above it" <note>Genesis 28:12-13</note>. He said: "Behold, see the gate through which one enters the ladder," as it is written of it, "and behold, the Lord stood above it." And what is written after this? "And Jacob awoke from his sleep and said, 'Surely the Lord is in this place... and this is the gate of heaven'" <note>Genesis 28:16-17</note>. Thus, Jacob merited to perceive the gate through which one enters the courts of the Lord, may He be blessed, as it is written: "This is the gate for the Lord; the righteous shall enter through it" <note>Psalm 118:20</note>. And since he perceived this gate, he merited to enter the twelve diagonal boundaries <term>Diagonal boundaries|A concept from the *Sefer Yetzirah* (Book of Creation) representing the structural limits of the universe.</term>, which are the secret of the upper and lower worlds. Everything is held together by the secret of the thirteen attributes through which the Torah is interpreted, and the secret of the twelve tribes (which are thirteen) <note>Counting the two sons of Joseph, Ephraim and Manasseh, or including Levi.</note>, and the secret of the twelve months (which are thirteen in the secret of the leap year) <note>referring to the intercalary month in the Hebrew calendar</note>.

## And Know
When Jacob came and perceived the place of the gate, he ascended to the top of the ladder—something that Abraham and Isaac did not merit or perceive. For Abraham and Isaac only reached the place of the "shoulders," and this is the secret of "underneath are the arms of the world" <note>Deuteronomy 33:27</note>. They did not ascend further. Were it not for this, the descendants of Jacob would have no survival among the nations, between Ishmael and Esau. Those "shoulders" are called the "heights of the earth." The secret of the reward is "the God of Abraham, the God of Isaac," but above them is "and the God of Jacob" <note>Exodus 3:6</note>—the **Middle Line** <term>Middle Line|The central column of the Sefirot, representing balance and harmony, associated with Jacob.</term> which ascends until it takes hold of the **Crown** <term>Crown|*Keter*, the highest of the ten Sefirot.</term>. "This your stature is like a palm tree," but above it, "and your breasts like clusters" <note>Song of Songs 7:8</note>. And the secret of "He made him ride on the high places of the earth" <note>Deuteronomy 32:13</note>, and "you shall tread upon their high places" <note>Deuteronomy 33:29</note>. "The eternal God is a dwelling place, and underneath are the arms of the world; and He drove out the enemy from before you, and said, 'Destroy!'" <note>Deuteronomy 33:27</note>. "And Israel shall dwell in safety alone, the eye of Jacob" <note>Deuteronomy 33:28</note>—and not the eye of Abraham or Isaac. For Abraham and Isaac are the "arms" and they are "underneath," because Ishmael, Esau, and the seventy crowns <note>The seventy guardian angels/nations of the world.</note> take hold of them. But

<column-break/>

Jacob, who merited to perceive the gate and ascend by way of the ladder, took hold of the **Middle Line** unto the height of heights, unto the **Infinite** <term>Infinite|*Ein Sof*, the Boundless Divine reality before emanation.</term> by way of the "Neck." Therefore, concerning Abraham and Isaac, it is written: "Lift up now your eyes... westward and northward and southward and eastward" <note>Genesis 13:14</note>. And so too with Isaac. But with Jacob, it says: "And you shall spread out to the west and to the east and to the north and to the south" <note>Genesis 28:14</note>, which is a "heritage without boundaries." Because no ministering prince of any of the nations ascends to that place. Regarding this, it is said: "For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself" <note>Psalm 135:4</note>, certainly, "Israel for His own treasure." And it is written: "Then you shall delight yourself in the Lord" <note>Isaiah 58:14</note>—certainly—"and I will cause you to ride upon the high places of the earth, and feed you with the heritage of Jacob your father." The heritage of Jacob, and not the heritage of Abraham and Isaac.

### And after
we have informed you of these principles, know that the secret of this gate which Jacob merited and perceived is the secret of the **Tenth Sefirah**, which is called:

# Shekhinah
And the sign is: "And Israel shall dwell in safety alone, the eye of Jacob." And when Jacob perceived this gate, which is the secret of the **Shekhinah** called **Righteousness** (*Tzedek*), the scripture cries out and says: **"Open for me the gates of righteousness; I will enter into them, I will praise the Lord"** <note>Psalm 118:19</note>. And it is added: "For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself." And to what place shall I enter? To the place called the **Neck**—"I will enter into them, I will praise the Lord." For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself, and this is the secret of: **"As for me, I shall behold Your face in righteousness"** <note>Psalm 17:15</note>. That is to say: Since I have merited to find the gate, I shall then enter to see the face of the King.

### And after
you have known this, know that this gate through which one enters to perceive and see the face of the Blessed King is the secret of the **Unification of our Creator**, may He be blessed. It is the secret of the **Blessing** <note>Hebrew: *Berakhah*</note> that the Blessed Name handed over to Abraham. For all types of flow and emanation that are drawn from the Upper Eden, which is called the **Crown** (*Keter*), by way of **Beauty** (*Tiferet*) which is called the **River**, all enter into the Tenth **Pool** <note>Hebrew: *Berikhah*, which shares the same root as *Berakhah* (blessing).</note>, which is the secret of **Kingship** (*Malchut*). Regarding this, it is written: "The wells of Abraham and Isaac." And the Blessed Lord handed over to Abraham this "Pool" so that all the nations would be blessed by it, and this is what He said—

"And I will make you a great nation" etc., "and you shall be a blessing" <note>Genesis 12:2</note>. What is the meaning of "and you shall be a blessing"? It is as if to say: "In you, I shall draw down the Divine Presence <term>Shekhinah|The indwelling Presence of God, associated with the tenth and final Sefirah, Malchut.</term>, which is the pool <note>The Hebrew word for blessing, *berakhah*, is phonetically linked here to *berekhah*, meaning a pool or reservoir.</note>." And she is the tenth Sefirah, which receives abundance from the nine shining mirrors <term>Mirrors|Hebrew: *aspaklaria*, a term used in Kabbalah and the Talmud to describe the "lenses" through which prophets perceive divine light.</term>. Even though He handed the "pool" over to Abraham, He did not hand over the "Gate" to him. Likewise for Isaac, because he contained the dross of Ishmael and Esau. But Jacob, in whom there was no dross, was given the Gate, as we have explained. If it were not for Abraham and Isaac, the seventy nations would have no hold in the Chariot <term>Chariot|Hebrew: *Merkavah*, the divine structure of the spiritual realms.</term>.

->And this is the secret of the seventy bulls sacrificed on the Festival:<- <note>Referring to the seventy bulls offered during the holiday of Sukkot, which Jewish tradition teaches were sacrificed on behalf of the seventy nations of the world.</note>

<meta>Continuity: This page builds on the distinction between the spiritual levels of the Patriarchs. Jacob was worthy to attain the Gate and to ascend upon the way of the Ladder. He took hold of the Middle Line <term>Middle Line|Hebrew: *Kav Ha-Emtza'i*, the central pillar of the Sefirotic tree representing balance and truth, associated with Jacob.</term> until the height of the most high, even unto the Infinite <term>Infinite|Hebrew: *Ein Sof*, the unknowable, boundless aspect of God that precedes all emanation.</term> by way of the "Neck." For this reason, regarding Abraham and Isaac it is written, "Raise your eyes... westward, northward, southward, and eastward" <note>Genesis 13:14 and 28:14</note>—and likewise for Isaac. But regarding Jacob, it says, "And you shall spread abroad to the west, and to the east, and to the north, and to the south" <note>Genesis 28:14</note>. This is an "inheritance without borders" <note>Hebrew: *Nachalah bli metzarim*. This is a wordplay; *metzarim* means "borders" or "constraints," but it is spelled the same as *Mitzrayim* (Egypt).</note>. Since no minister among all the ministers of the nations <note>The "Seventy Ministers" are the celestial guardians of the non-Jewish nations.</note> ascends to that place, it is said of this: "For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself, Israel for His treasure" <note>Psalm 135:4</note>. And it is written: "Then you shall delight yourself in the Lord" <note>Isaiah 58:14</note>—"in the Lord" specifically—"and I will cause you to ride on the high places of the earth, and feed you with the heritage of Jacob your father." It says the heritage of Jacob, and not the heritage of Abraham or Isaac.

### And after
we have informed you of these principles, know that the secret of this Gate which Jacob merited and attained is the secret of the Tenth Sphere <term>Sphere|Hebrew: *Sefirah*, one of the ten divine attributes or emanations.</term> which is called

# Shekhinah
And the sign for this is: "And Israel shall dwell in safety alone, the fountain of Jacob" <note>Deuteronomy 33:28</note>. When Jacob attained this Gate, which is the secret of the Divine Presence <term>Shekhinah|The indwelling Presence of God, the feminine aspect of the Divine, and the tenth Sefirah (Malchut).</term> called **Righteousness** (*Tzedek*), then the Scripture proclaims and says: "Open for me the gates of righteousness; I will enter into them, I will praise the Lord" <note>Psalm 118:19</note>, and so on. For "the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself." And to what place shall I enter? To the place called the "Neck." "I will enter into them, I will praise the Lord," for Jacob chose the Lord for himself. This is the secret of the verse: "As for me, I will behold Your face in righteousness" <note>Psalm 17:15</note>. That is to say: since I have been worthy to find the Gate, I am prepared to see the face of the King.

### And after
you have known this, know that this Gate, through which one enters to attain and see the face of the Blessed King, is the secret of the Unity of our Blessed Creator. It is the secret of the Blessing that the Blessed Name handed over to Abraham. For all types of flow and emanation that are drawn from the Supreme Eden, which is called the Crown <term>Crown|Hebrew: *Keter*, the highest Sefirah.</term>, by way of the Beauty <term>Beauty|Hebrew: *Tiferet*, the central Sefirah, often called the "River" because it channels divine flow.</term> called the "River"—all of them enter into the "Tenth Pool," which is the secret of the Kingdom <term>Kingdom|Hebrew: *Malchut*, the final Sefirah.</term>. Regarding this, it is written as it was with the patriarchs Abraham and Isaac. The Blessed Lord handed this "Blessing" over to Abraham so that all the nations would be blessed through him. And this is what is meant by the verse: "And I will make you..."

"...and he tarried there... and he dreamed, and behold, a ladder set up on the earth" <note>Genesis 28:11-12</note>. He knew the Blessed Kingdom, but he did not know through which gate to enter it. The Blessed Name showed him the way of entry. From where do we know this? As it is written: "And he dreamed, and behold, a ladder set up on the earth," etc., "and behold, the Lord stood over it." He said: "Behold, see the Gate through which he shall enter the Ladder," about which it is written: "And behold, the Lord stood over it." And what is written after it? "And Jacob awaked out of his sleep, and he said: Surely the Lord is in this place... and this is the gate of heaven." Behold, Jacob was worthy to attain the Gate through which one enters the courtyards of the Blessed Lord, as it is written: "This is the gate of the Lord; the righteous shall enter into it" <note>Psalm 118:20</note>. And since he attained this Gate, he was worthy to enter the Twelve Diagonal Boundaries <note>A concept from the *Sefer Yetzirah* (Book of Formation) describing the edges of the universe.</note>, which are the secret of the upper and lower worlds. Everything is held together by the secret of the Thirteen Attributes by which the Torah is expounded, and the secret of the Twelve Tribes which are [actually] thirteen, and the secret of the twelve months which are [sometimes] thirteen in the secret of the Leap Year.

## And know
that when Jacob came and attained the place of the Gate, he ascended to the top of the Ladder—something that Abraham and Isaac were not worthy of and did not attain. For Abraham and Isaac only reached the place of the "Shoulders," and this is the secret of "underneath are the arms of the world" <note>Deuteronomy 33:27</note>. They did not ascend further. Were it not for this, Jacob’s seed would have no survival among the nations—among Ishmael and Esau. Those shoulders are called the "high places of the earth." The great secret is "the God of Abraham, the God of Isaac," but above them is "and the God of Jacob." He is the Middle Line that ascends until it takes hold of the Crown. "This your stature is like to a palm tree" <note>Song of Songs 7:7</note>, but above, "and your breasts to clusters of grapes." "The eternal God is a dwelling place, and underneath are the arms of the world; and He thrust out the enemy from before you, and said: Destroy! And Israel shall dwell in safety alone, the fountain of Jacob." <meta>Continuity: This page continues the discussion of the patriarchs' roles in the divine structure. Following the previous page's focus on Jacob's unique inheritance and the "Middle Line," this page shifts to how the Patriarchs—particularly Abraham—helped establish the Tenth Sefirah (Malchut) as a dwelling place for the Divine Presence on earth, connecting it to the concept of one hundred blessings and the Oral Torah.</meta>

And the tent is not spread in its proper order... therefore the Divine Presence <term>Divine Presence|Hebrew: *Shekhinah*, the indwelling presence of God in the world, often associated with the feminine aspect of divinity and the Tenth Sefirah.</term> does not rest upon something flawed. This is the secret of the verse: "A tent that shall not be taken down; not one of its stakes shall ever be removed, nor shall any of its cords be broken" <note>Isaiah 33:20</note>. We find that everyone who recites one hundred blessings <note>Hebrew: *Me'ah Berakhot*; a traditional Jewish practice to recite at least 100 blessings daily.</note> is as if they have spread the tent and fixed it upon one hundred sockets <term>Sockets|Hebrew: *Adanim*, the silver bases that held the planks of the Tabernacle.</term>. Then they are joined through the hundred blessings, because the hundred blessings correspond to the hundred sockets made from a hundred talents [of silver]. And all of them are bored through with a hole; thus, there are one hundred windows of light open toward the hundred blessings. Whoever knows this knows the secret of the "Gift" <term>Gift|Hebrew: *Trumah*, the offering given to the priests, literally meaning "uplifting."</term> and the secret of "Two from a Hundred" <note>original: *terumah mi-me'ah*. A reference to the priestly portion; the author suggests the spiritual essence of the offering depends on the full count of 100.</note>. For if there are not a hundred, there is no "Two" [Trumah] in its true sense.

**And after** we have explained this, know that this Gate is called everywhere in the Torah **Lord** <note>Hebrew: *Adonai*, spelled Aleph-Dalet-Nun-Yod.</note> and it is the palace of the Tetragrammaton <note>The four-letter name of God, Y-H-V-H.</note>, may He be blessed. Therefore, He is always called by this name because the Name [Y-H-V-H] always dwells in this house; understand this well. And because the tenth Sefirah <term>Sefirah|One of the ten divine attributes or emanations in Kabbalistic thought.</term> corresponds to the Earth, its secret is "The man, the lord of the land" <note>Genesis 42:30</note>. Because the first man (Adam) caused the Presence to be removed from him below until Noah came and restored it for a short time and no more, it was then said: "Noah, Noah, a righteous man, perfect in his generations... Noah walked with God" <note>Genesis 6:9</note>. This is the secret of why he was called Noah, from the expression "The spirit of Elijah rested <note>Hebrew: *Nacha*</note> upon Elisha" <note>2 Kings 2:15</note>. "And he called his name Noah, saying: 'This one shall comfort us'" <note>Genesis 5:29</note>. "And Noah found favor in the eyes of the Lord." "And Noah built an altar... and the Lord smelled the soothing aroma <note>Hebrew: *rei'ach ha-nichoch*</note>." "And the Lord said in His heart: 'I will not again [curse the ground]...'" Even though all this was done, the land was not truly blessed until Abraham came and established for them the sign of the places of blessings. "And he built there an altar to the Lord." "And he called upon the name of the Lord" <note>Genesis 12:8</note>. And Abraham our father, peace be upon him, prepared himself and made his "Right Side" <note>Associated with the attribute of *Chesed* or Loving-kindness.</note> a throne for the Chariot <term>Chariot|Hebrew: *Merkabah*, the divine throne-chariot representing how God's presence is "carried" into the world by the righteous.</term>, and this attribute rested upon him. Therefore it is said: "And you shall be a blessing" <note>Genesis 12:2</note>. And when he entered the land... he established the places for the Divine Presence and began to call upon the name of the Lord by the name of the Land... for it is His portion. And regarding this, the Sages of blessed memory said: "And cause Your face to shine upon Your sanctuary that is desolate, for the sake of the Lord" <note>Daniel 9:17</note>. For the sake of whom?

<column-break/>

For the sake of the one whom he called Lord. Abraham our father, peace be upon him, established this. And when you understand these matters, many "Gates of Light" <note>A likely reference to the title of the work, *Sha'arei Orah*.</note> will open before you, and you will know and gain insight from the source of the matter. You will see it in every place you find in the Torah where the name Y-H-V-H is written but pronounced as **Adonai**, for this is the secret of the Tenth Sefirah, which is a "mirror that does not shine" <term>Mirror that does not shine|Hebrew: *Aspaklaria she-einah me'irah*, a term for the Sefirah of Malchut, which has no light of its own but reflects the light of the higher realms.</term>. It requires illumination from that which is above it. Therefore, you will find that Abraham prepared a place for it, like one who takes hold of a thing. However, its permanent dwelling was not established on the ground until the "Fourth Leg" <term>Fourth Leg|The fourth support of the Divine Throne, often associated with King David or the completion of the earthly Temple.</term>. At that time, it was said: "This is my resting place forever; here I will dwell, for I have desired it" <note>Psalm 132:14</note>. This is the secret of "For Your name is called upon Your city and upon Your people" <note>Daniel 9:19</note>, and understand this very well.

**And after** we have explained this matter to you, know that this attribute is called in the Song of Songs **Bride**. The secret is "As a bridegroom decks himself with a garland, and as a bride adorns herself with her jewels" <note>Isaiah 61:10</note>. "Come with me from Lebanon, my bride" <note>Song of Songs 4:8</note>. And "He is like a bridegroom coming out of his chamber" <note>Psalm 19:6</note>.

**Know** and understand that this attribute is called everywhere **Oral Torah** <note>Hebrew: *Torah she-be'al peh*</note>, because she is the mate of the Written Torah, which is the "Script" and the "Taste." This is called among those who know "The Secret of the Moon." The reason for these two matters is the same: just as the moon has no light of its own, only from the sun, so the Oral Torah has no foundation except from the Written Torah. Therefore, the Oral Torah was not given at Sinai in writing—to avoid establishing a fixed place for it—except as it rests upon the Written Torah, because of the "Secret of the Kiss." This is the secret meaning of "Let him kiss me with the kisses of his mouth" <note>Song of Songs 1:2</note>. It was written down later only as a decree, lest the Torah be forgotten from Israel due to the persecutions <note>Hebrew: *shmadot*</note>.

**And here** we hint at the beginning and the end... that in every place, "Speaking" <note>Hebrew: *Dibur*</note> is the secret of the Written Torah, and "Saying" <note>Hebrew: *Amirah*</note> is the secret of the Oral Torah. This is what is said throughout the entire Torah: "And the Lord *spoke* to Moses, *saying*..." One is nested in the other, and this one requires the "Gates of Justice."

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic significance of the Tenth Sefirah (Malchut), identifying it with the name Adonai, the Oral Torah, and the "Bride." It connects the practice of reciting 100 blessings to the 100 silver sockets of the Tabernacle, framing Abraham as the one who finally established a dwelling for the Divine Presence on earth.</summary>

The blessings spread * and they go forth into twelve chambers of twelve keys that belong to each and every tribe * and this is the secret of the verse: "All these are the twelve tribes of Israel; this is what their father said to them as he blessed them, blessing each with the blessing appropriate to him" <note>Genesis 49:28</note>. And when Moses our Teacher, peace be upon him, came and blessed Israel, he made known that the key which includes all the keys is handed over to the tribe of Levi * as he said, "And of Levi he said: Your Thummim and your Urim be for your holy one" <note>Deuteronomy 33:8</note>. The secret of the *Urim and Thummim* <term>Urim and Thummim|Ancient oracular devices worn by the High Priest on his breastplate, used to determine the divine will.</term> is the secret of all the gates, and here I am hinting at the secret of "Light" [Hebrew: *Orah*, relating to *Urim*] and "Jacob was a simple man" [Hebrew: *Tam*, relating to *Thummim*] <note>The author is using a play on words to link the Urim and Thummim to the spiritual light of the Patriarch Jacob.</note>. Therefore it says in the Torah, "Speak to Aaron and his sons, saying: Thus shall you bless the children of Israel... and they shall put My name upon the children of Israel, and I will bless them" <note>Numbers 6:23-27</note>. And therefore the Scripture says, "Therefore Levi shall have no portion or inheritance with his brothers; the Lord is his inheritance" <note>Deuteronomy 10:9</note>. And guard this great principle well, for the Blessed Name is certainly his inheritance.

### And after
I have made this known to you, know that the Tent of Meeting <term>Tent of Meeting|Hebrew: *Ohel Moed*, the portable sanctuary or Tabernacle used by the Israelites in the wilderness.</term>—which is the secret of the *Shekhinah* <term>Shekhinah|The indwelling Divine Presence on earth, often described as the feminine aspect of God.</term>, the root of Jacob and his portion and inheritance—he bequeathed it to his children as an inheritance * and regarding this it is said, "And I will set My tabernacle among you... and I will be your God" <note>Leviticus 26:11-12</note>. And it is written, "Who dwells with them in the midst of their impurity" <note>Leviticus 16:16</note>—literally in their impurity. Happy is the people for whom it is so; happy is the people whose God is the Lord <note>Psalm 144:15</note>.

### And behold
this attribute has one hundred corners, and for every single corner there is a receptacle. From every single corner, springs of blessing flow to the world. From there, Isaac our father, peace be upon him, drew the one hundred kinds of blessings through which he became wealthy * and regarding this it is said, "And Isaac sowed in that land and found in that year a hundredfold" <note>Genesis 26:12; in Hebrew, "hundredfold" is *Me'ah She'arim*, which literally translates to "one hundred gates."</note>—literally "one hundred gates" * Therefore, "the Lord blessed him" * and therefore, "the man became great" and so on. Therefore the Philistines and kings envied him because of the abundance of his blessings. And these one hundred gates that Isaac found in that which he was blessed are the one hundred gates and springs surrounding the "Gates of Righteousness" <term>Gates of Righteousness|Hebrew: *Sha'arei Tzedek*, a name for the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingship), the spiritual gateway through which divine energy enters the physical world.</term> from which blessing, abundance, and emanation go forth to all

<column-break/>

created beings through Israel * and therefore you will find in the Tent of Meeting one hundred sockets for one hundred talents, a talent for each socket <note>Exodus 38:27 describes the construction of the Tabernacle using 100 silver sockets.</note>. And there is no socket among them all that did not have a receptacle for a column. The secret of the columns is known within the hooks <note>Hebrew: *Vavim*, also the name of the letter *Vav*, which is shaped like a vertical column.</note> and the sign of the *Yod* of the columns. And the secret is the "Middle Line" <term>Middle Line|The central axis of the Sefirotic tree that balances the right (Mercy) and left (Judgment) sides.</term> and the Foundation of Jacob. And because it is the path through which every single person of Israel is blessed with one hundred kinds of blessings and kinds of abundance and emanation, every person of Israel must bless one hundred blessings every day. That is to say, every single blessing has a spring and a source from which it is poured and emanated. And if, Heaven forbid, a person is missing even one of the hundred blessings each day, he blemishes the entire Torah. Thus he is found to be ruining all the "pipes" <term>Pipes|Hebrew: *Tzinorot*, the spiritual channels through which divine energy flows from the upper worlds to the lower worlds.</term>. And your sign is: Nefesh, Ruach, and Neshamah <term>Nefesh, Ruach, Neshamah|The three primary levels of the soul: the vital soul (Nefesh), the spirit or emotional self (Ruach), and the higher divine breath (Neshamah).</term> are "upright" [*Yashar*] for the Levite-Priest. The secret is: "To the children of Levi I have given every tithe in Israel for an inheritance in return for their service" <note>Numbers 18:21</note> and it is written, "Speak to the Levites and say to them: When you take the tithe... then you shall set aside from it a gift for the Lord, a tithe of the tithe" <note>Numbers 18:26</note>. We find that Israel gives to the Levite ten out of every hundred, and the Levite gives to the Priest one out of every ten. If there is no "hundred" for Israel, there is no "ten" for the Levite. If there is no "ten" for the Levite, there is no "one" for the Priest. Then the bundle is separated, and there is no longer a vessel to pour out His blessing. In this way, if the *Nefesh* [Soul] does not bless one hundred blessings every day, ten will not reach the *Ruach* [Spirit]. And when the *Ruach* does not receive ten, the "one" will not reach the *Neshamah* [Higher Soul]. And behold, we are obligated every day to bind the *Nefesh* to the *Ruach*, and the *Ruach* to the *Neshamah*, and the *Neshamah* cleaves to the Lord, may He be blessed. And the secret is: "And the soul of my lord shall be bound in the bundle of life with the Lord your God" <note>1 Samuel 25:29</note>. And this is the secret of "Bless the Lord, O my soul" <note>Psalm 103:1</note> and it is written, "Let every soul [breath] praise the Lord" <note>Psalm 150:6</note>. And in this way we say that any person who does not complete one hundred blessings every day blemishes his form and "cuts the plantings" <term>Cuts the Plantings|Hebrew: *Katzetz ba-Neti'ot*, a Kabbalistic idiom for causing a spiritual division or committing a form of heresy by separating aspects of the Divine.</term> of his soul. Woe to him for his deficiency, for he shall not enter the Gates of Righteousness, because the sockets for the columns are not arranged, and there is

<summary>This page explains the mystical significance of the Jewish practice of reciting one hundred blessings daily, linking them to the one hundred silver sockets of the Tabernacle and the spiritual inheritance of the Patriarchs and the tribe of Levi. It describes how these blessings maintain the flow of divine abundance through the three levels of the soul—Nefesh, Ruach, and Neshamah.</summary>
