<meta>Continuity: This page continues the discussion of how divine blessings are distributed. It moves from the specific role of the tribe of Levi as the keepers of the "keys" of blessing to a broader explanation of how human actions (mitzvot and transgressions) affect the flow of divine energy through the cosmic channels.</meta>

This too is the nature of the Attribute <note>The author is referring to *Malchut*, the final Sephirah in the Kabbalistic tree, which represents the manifest Presence of God</note>, for through neglect and the cancellation of the commandments, the channels are broken and the flows of influence are cut off. For transgressions break the **channels**; as it says, "Go down, for your people have corrupted themselves" <note>Exodus 32:7</note>, and "He is a companion to a man who destroys" <note>Proverbs 18:9</note>, and "the earth was corrupted" <note>Genesis 6:11</note>. By the cancellation of the commandments, the flows are cut off, as it says: "Why is the land perished and burnt up like a wilderness... because they have forsaken My law" <note>Jeremiah 9:11-12</note>. It also says: "My soul thirsts for the living God... my flesh longs for You" <note>Psalm 42:3; 63:2</note>. For this reason, it says in the Torah: "If you walk in My statutes... then I will give you your rains in their season, and the land shall yield her produce" <note>Leviticus 26:3-4</note>. This means that by performing the commandments, you repair the channels, and the blessings are drawn down from the **Infinite** <term>Ein Sof|The Infinite; the highest, unknowable aspect of God from which all creation emanates.</term>. Thus, the upper and lower worlds are blessed, as it is said: "He who blesses himself in the earth shall bless himself by the God of truth" <note>Isaiah 65:16</note>.

But if, Heaven forbid, they transgress the Torah, what is written? "If you shall despise My statutes..." and "I will also do this to you... I will make your heavens as iron and your earth as brass" <note>Leviticus 26:15-19</note>. For this reason, this Attribute is sometimes called **Shaddai** <note>original: שדי - The Almighty</note>; that is to say, when the tablets and the channels were broken, the blessings and flows were cut off. This matter is hinted at in the verse, "And God called the dry land Earth" <note>Genesis 1:10</note>, and it is written: "And God saw the children of Israel, and God knew" <note>Exodus 2:25</note>. Seeing is the secret of **Providence** <note>original: פקידה - Pekidah, meaning divine visitation or remembrance</note>. Sometimes this Attribute is called:

### Sea

This is because all the channels and flows run toward it, rushing from the depth of the **Infinite**. This is the secret of the verse: "All the rivers run into the sea, yet the sea is not full" <note>Ecclesiastes 1:7</note>. This is because it is a **speculum that does not shine** <term>Aspaklaria she-einah me'irah|A mystical term for the Sephirah of Malchut, which has no light of its own but reflects the light of the higher Sephirot.</term>, and it requires illumination from the side of **Foundation** <term>Yesod|The ninth Sephirah, which acts as the funnel or connector between the higher realms and the physical world.</term>, as we hinted regarding the verse: "Blessed be the Lord... who builds up Jerusalem" <note>Psalm 147:2</note>. This is the secret of "And God called the dry land Earth, and the gathering together of the waters He called Seas" <note>Genesis 1:10</note>. This is also the secret of the filling of the moon and the increase of its phases: it increases with the moon's wax and decreases with the moon's wane. For when the **Assembly of Israel** <note>original: כנסת ישראל - Knesset Yisrael, a personification of the Shekhinah</note> is influenced

<column-break/>

and filled and saturated, then through its blessing all created beings, both upper and lower, are blessed. And this Attribute is called in the Torah:

### New

The sign for this is: "This is the burnt offering of every month [*Chodesh*] in its month" <note>Numbers 28:14</note>; this is "this" burnt offering, certainly. The reason for the "he-goats of the New Moons" is that it says regarding them "a sin offering to the Lord" <note>Numbers 28:15</note>. This is the secret of the **diminishing of the moon** and its fullness, and the secret of the **moon's grievance** against the sun. The reason is that "two kings cannot use one crown" <note>A famous Talmudic midrash (Chullin 60b) where the moon complains that it and the sun are the same size, leading God to tell the moon to diminish itself.</note>. And I am hinting to you: if they both used one crown, there would be no offspring in this world. This is the reason for the command: "Go and diminish yourself."

One who knows this reason will understand why the luminaries were hung on the fourth day. At first, there were "two great luminaries," but in the end, there was "the great luminary to rule the day and the small luminary" and so on <note>Genesis 1:14-16</note>. Therefore, the activity of the sun and the moon among the lower beings is by the word of God. This is the meaning of: "And God set them in the firmament of the heaven to give light upon the earth, and to rule over the day and over the night" <note>Genesis 1:17-18</note>. And the secret of: "for the precious fruits brought forth by the sun, and for the precious things put forth by the moon" <note>Deuteronomy 33:14</note>. For this reason, the Sabbath belongs to the sun and the Festivals to the moon.

The great secret is: "O give thanks to the Lord, for He is good... O give thanks to the God of gods... O give thanks to the Lord of lords" <note>Psalm 136:1-3</note>. This is the secret of "from one New Moon to another, and from one Sabbath to another, all flesh shall come" <note>Isaiah 66:23</note>. One who knows this reason knows the secret of **sanctifying the New Moon** by the authority of the High Court and the Lesser Court, and the sign of Perez and Zerah <note>The twin sons of Judah; Perez is often associated with the moon's cycles.</note>. The core principle is that "the light of the moon shall be as the light of the sun, and the light of the sun shall be sevenfold" <note>Isaiah 30:26</note>. The secret of **sevenfold** refers to the forty-nine gates <note>The Gates of Understanding</note>, and the forty-nine days leading to the Giving of the Torah, and the forty-nine years of the **Jubilee** <term>Yovel|The fiftieth year following seven cycles of Sabbatical years, representing total spiritual liberation.</term>. Then "you shall grant a redemption for the land," "it shall have redemption," and "in the Jubilee it shall go out" <note>Leviticus 25</note>. He who understands the great reasons we have hinted at here will understand the secret between the upper whiteness and the lower whiteness—"fair as the moon, clear as the sun" <note>Song of Songs 6:10</note>. And the secret of "He appointed the moon for seasons" <note>Psalm 104:19</note>, and the secret of "the round goblet" <note>Song of Songs 7:3</note> which is the **Gates of Righteousness**.

It focuses on the moon as a symbol of the Sephirah Malchut, explaining its cycles and its relationship to the sun as the source of time, festivals, and the eventual messianic restoration of light.</summary>

<meta>Continuity: This page continues the exposition of the various names and states of the final Sephirah, Malchut (the Divine Presence). The previous page ended by describing how human neglect of the commandments causes the divine channels to dry up. This page elaborates on how this Sephirah is named according to its relationship with the higher divine forces and the conduct of the people of Israel.</meta>

...his son, and these are not separated one from the other. One who reads the Scripture without Mishnah <note>The first major written collection of the Jewish oral traditions</note> and Talmud <note>The central text of Rabbinic Judaism</note> is like one who has no God. "A whisperer separates a close friend" <note>Proverbs 16:28; here interpreted as separating the divine aspects</note>. "And God spoke to Moses and said to him: I am the Lord" <note>Exodus 6:2</note>. And this is what is called in every place:

### Crown
The sign in the Torah is: "And you shall be a crown of glory in the hand of the Lord" <note>Isaiah 62:3; original: *Atarah*</note>, "with the crown with which his mother crowned him on the day of his wedding" <note>Song of Songs 3:11</note>. One who understands the commandment "you shall not take the mother with the young" <note>Deuteronomy 22:6</note> will understand the secret of the **Crown** with which his mother <note>In Kabbalah, the "Mother" often refers to the Sephirah of *Binah* (Understanding)</note> crowned him. This is certainly the secret of the Union on the day of his wedding. And sometimes it is called:

### Justice
The secret is "Lower Justice" and "Upper Justice." "Justice, justice shall you pursue, that you may live and inherit the land" <note>Deuteronomy 16:20; original: *Tzedek*</note>. Because it is the "Measure of the Bottom" <note>The final Sephirah</note> and she justifies the Omnipresent, it is written: "Justice lodged in it, but now murderers" <note>Isaiah 1:21</note>—either this or that. "As for me, I will behold Your face in justice" <note>Psalm 17:15</note>, for the Lower Justice is called "His back," and certainly there is an Upper Justice called "Face." Moses did not reach the attainment of "His Face" because it is the secret of attaining the Upper Faces. This attribute is also called:

### Jerusalem
For Zion and Jerusalem are the secrets of *Yesod* <term>Foundation|The ninth Sephirah, representing the generative or connecting force</term> and *Malchut* <term>Kingdom|The tenth Sephirah, representing the manifest world and the Divine Presence</term>. The City of David is Zion, and "there is no sitting in the Temple Courtyard except for the kings of the House of David alone." The verse proclaims and says: "Blessed be the Lord out of Zion, who dwells in Jerusalem" <note>Psalm 135:21</note>, for Jerusalem is the Gate of Heaven. And this attribute is called:

### Eagle
For when she is united with Israel, and they are righteous and upright lovers of the Most High, then this attribute—which is their portion—is renewed like the renewal of the moon. Concerning this it is written: "Your youth is renewed like the eagle's" <note>Psalm 103:5; original: *Nesher*</note>. But if, Heaven forbid, Israel transgresses the Torah, violates the statutes, and breaks the everlasting covenant, then this attribute is "plucked" and "shaken." Regarding this, the feathers fall, and it is called an **Eagle** indeed. In every place, this attribute is called **Bathsheba** <note>The wife of King David</note>, for she is the mate of David. The reason is that from the Sephirah of *Binah* <note>Understanding</note>, she draws and suckles by way of the six middle Sephirot. The sign of this is: "For in six days the Lord made

<column-break/>

the heavens and the earth..." etc. "and on the seventh day He rested and was refreshed" <note>Exodus 31:17</note>. And they said in the Midrash *Bereshit Rabbah*: "Because the Sabbath has no mate among the days, God said to her: The Congregation of Israel shall be your mate." This is the secret of David and Bathsheba, through which David merited the kingdom. They said: "Bathsheba was destined for David since the six days of Creation, but he consumed her as 'unripe fruit' <note>Meaning he took her before the proper time</note>." And behold, this attribute is called:

### Land
A "Land of Desire." The reason is that when the Union is complete and true, she is found like the Sabbath in the state of "Remember" and "Observe" <note>The two aspects of the Sabbath commandment</note>, in the secret of "You shall fear every man his father and his mother, and keep My sabbaths" <note>Leviticus 19:3</note>. Thus it says: "And God finished on the seventh day" <note>Genesis 2:2</note>, and the Translation of Jonathan <note>An ancient Aramaic translation/paraphrase of the Bible</note> renders it: "And He desired it as the most desirable of days." "And the tree was desirable to make one wise" <note>Genesis 3:6</note>, giving good insight and grace. And sometimes it is called:

### The Land
"The Good Land"—that is to say, when the attribute is called "Good." When the Indweller <note>The Divine Light</note> is within her, she is called by His name, "Good." Thus it says: "And God saw the light, that it was good; and God divided..." <note>Genesis 1:4</note>. When the light is within her, which is good, she is also called "Good." Also: "The Lord shall give that which is good; and our land shall yield her increase" <note>Psalm 85:13</note>. Likewise, to Saul: "And has given it to a neighbor of yours, who is better than you" <note>1 Samuel 15:28</note>. She is then called **Light**. But when the light that is "Good" is not within her, she is called **Darkness** and is called **Night**, as in: "She weeps sore in the night" <note>Lamentations 1:2</note>. And sometimes this attribute is called:

### Land
A "Broad Land." The reason is when she suckles in full abundance from the "Broad Places of the River" <note>A Kabbalistic term for the flow from *Binah*</note>, which is the eighth attribute above her, she then continues to expand. The sign is: "And there was an enlarging, and a winding about still upwards" <note>Ezekiel 41:7</note>. This is the secret of the saying: "The people stood crowded together, yet they prostrated themselves with wide spaces" <note>Pirkei Avot 5:5; describing a miracle in the Temple</note>. "There shall yet old men and old women dwell in the broad places of Jerusalem" <note>Zechariah 8:4</note>. "Enlarge the place of your tent" <note>Isaiah 54:2</note>. Understand this great principle: this Sephirah is called in many places by the name of the attribute from which she is suckling at that moment. Understand this very well, for you will have great need of it in many places. And sometimes she is called:

### Land
"Dry Land"—that is to say, when Israel causes her "hands" to be empty of the commandments and the attributes of Good depart from her, then she is found

<summary>This page explores the various names given to the Sephirah of Malchut (the Divine Presence) based on its spiritual state, ranging from "Crown" and "Justice" to "Jerusalem" and "The Good Land." It emphasizes that its name and character change depending on whether it is receiving divine light or is "dry" due to human sin.</summary>

<meta>Continuity: This page continues the exploration of the divine attributes (Sephirot) and their relationship to the people of Israel and the Temple. Having previously discussed the "Crown" and "Justice," the author now explores how the unity of Israel on earth mirrors and sustains the divine unity above, eventually transitioning into the mystery of the divine name "Elohim."</meta>

Furthermore, "And My servant David shall be king over them, and they shall all have one shepherd" <note>Ezekiel 37:24</note>. Understand this well. The reason is that since the Scripture <note>the Torah</note> was written by the Lord for the assembly of Israel, He placed the kings of Judah as guardians over it in the Holy of Holies. When the People of Israel accepted the Torah and the Tablets and placed them in the Ark, the nine **Sephirot** <term>Sephirot: The ten divine emanations or channels through which God interacts with the universe</term> became bound to the tenth through the secret of the Tablets placed within the Ark that Israel constructed.

Therefore, through the People of Israel, all the Sephirot were unified. For if they had not accepted the Torah from below, there would be no union of the Sephirot from above. This is because the place of the **Shekhinah** <term>Shekhinah: The Divine Presence, localized here as the tenth Sephirah, Malchut</term> was in the "lower land." This is what the Sages meant when they said: "The primary dwelling of the Shekhinah was among the lower beings" <note>A classic Midrashic teaching from Bereshit Rabbah 19:7</note>. If the "Central Line" <note>The vertical axis of the Sephirot, typically associated with Tiferet (Beauty/Harmony)</note> does not extend downward to reach the place of the Shekhinah, then there is no longer any union at all. For when the Shekhinah is not in her proper place below, there is no true union.

Behold, when the world was created, the line of **Tiferet** <note>Beauty/Harmony, the central Sephirah</note> was above, drawing abundance downward to the place of the Shekhinah. Then the heavens and the earth were held together. This is the secret of the verse: "And the heavens and the earth were finished [*Vaykhulu*], and all their host" <note>Genesis 2:1</note>. The word *Vaykhulu* is related to the language of "Groom and Bride" [*Chatan ve-Kallah*]. The Aramaic translation [Targum] renders it as "and they were perfected," meaning that the lower realms became a vehicle for the higher realms. They enter one into the other and are joined together; then the Blessed Lord is One in the world in a state of perfection.

But when Adam the First came and damaged the "channels" <note>The spiritual conduits of divine energy</note>, the Shekhinah departed and the "bundle" was separated. Thus, the world was found in ruins until Israel came and received the Written Torah—which represents the seven Sephirot of the Central Line—and drew it downward. They prepared the Ark as a model for the Shekhinah. Thus, through the People of Israel, "The Lord is one and His name is one." This is the secret of "The Lord is One," for it is written: "Who is like Your people Israel, one nation in the earth?" <note>2 Samuel 7:23</note>. This is the secret of the plea: "What will You do for Your great Name?" <note>Joshua 7:9</note>. As it were, through the People of Israel, the Lord becomes One. 

The secret lies in the two Tablets placed in the Ark. He who understands this secret will understand how great the power of receiving the Torah was. The secret is: "From heaven You caused judgment to be heard; the earth feared and was still" <note>Psalm 76:9</note>. It is also written: "The heaven is My throne, and the earth is My footstool" <note>Isaiah 66:1</note>, and "I will honor the place of My feet" <note>Isaiah 60:13</note>. He who understands these 

<column-break/>

great principles we have hinted at will understand the meaning of "Your name is called upon Your city and upon Your people" <note>Daniel 9:19</note>, and the secret of "You shall call Me 'My Husband' [*Ishi*] and no longer call Me 'My Master' [*Ba'ali*]" <note>Hosea 2:18</note>. The secret continues: "And I will betroth you to Me in righteousness..." <note>Hosea 2:21</note>.

Also, consider the secret of "For the Lord your God is in your midst" <note>Deuteronomy 7:21</note>, and the secret of "I will not enter into your midst"—meaning "into your very midst" <note>Hosea 11:9</note>—and the secret of "The Holy One in your midst; I will not enter the city." This refers to the saying of the Sages: "I [God] will not enter the Jerusalem above until I enter the Jerusalem below" <note>Talmud, Taanit 5a</note>. 

He who understands this will understand the secret of the "Hiding of the Ark" <note>The tradition that King Josiah hid the Ark to protect it from invaders</note> and the secret of "I will lay your sanctuaries waste" <note>Leviticus 26:31</note>. Regarding this, the Sages of blessed memory said: "Even though they are desolate, they remain in their holiness." My explanation is that the Ark and the Covenant are the "Ark of all the Earth," and the Tablets are hidden there. This is the secret of "The Ark of the Covenant, the Lord of all the earth" <note>Joshua 3:11; a play on the phonetic similarity between *Aron* (Ark) and *Adon* (Lord)</note>. All of this is hinted at and clarified for those to whom the Blessed Lord has granted knowledge. 

This attribute is sometimes called:

## **Elohim**
<note>God / Divine Judgment</note>

I shall now hint at the eighth attribute. It is written as the Tetragrammaton <note>original: YHWH</note> but read as **Elohim**. This is the secret of the "Might of the Lord" [*Gevurat YHWH*]. It is this that draws the Judgments downward. From it emanates the attribute of **Gevurah** <term>Gevurah: Strength, Severity, or Judgment; the fifth Sephirah</term>, which is everywhere called **Elohim**. 

He who understands the secret of *Elohim* will understand the secret of "In the beginning *Elohim* created" <note>Genesis 1:1</note>. Which "Beginning" is meant? In this context, it refers to "The Lord of Hosts" [*Elohim Tzevaot*]. From Gevurah, the attribute of Judgment is emanated to shine forth, as in the phrase "God of Hosts."

When all the upper Sephirot are perfected through the side of **Chesed** <term>Chesed: Kindness or Mercy; the fourth Sephirah</term> and they pour into the tenth, it is called the Lord [YHWH]. But when the attributes of Judgment [*Din*] and Might [*Gevurah*] are perfected and pour into it, it is also called *Elohim*. This is the meaning of the phrase "For My Name is within him" <note>Exodus 23:21</note>.

These matters are even deeper. From them, you may enter the secret of why the Torah says "In the beginning God [*Elohim*] created." For the word "In the beginning" [*Bereshit*] includes all types of beginnings, each according to its subject. Similarly, the word *Elohim* in the verse of "In the beginning" includes every instance of the name *Elohim* mentioned in the Torah, each according to its subject. 

When the Torah says "In the beginning *Elohim* created," it hints at the expansion of **Binah** <term>Binah: Understanding; the third Sephirah</term> from **Chokhmah** <term>Chokhmah: Wisdom; the second Sephirah</term>. For it is from there that the name *Elohim* begins in the world of Judgment. Behold, Binah is the "beginning" for the seven [lower] Sephirot, and it hints at the creation of the world.

<summary>This page explains how the physical Ark and the people of Israel serve as the earthly foundation for divine unity, specifically linking the Sephirot to the Tabernacle. It then transitions into a linguistic and mystical analysis of the name Elohim, associating it with the attributes of Judgment (Gevurah) and Understanding (Binah).</summary>

<meta>Continuity: This page continues the exploration of the divine attributes (Sephirot) and their relationship to the people of Israel and the Temple. Having previously discussed the "Crown" and "Justice," the author now explores how the unity of Israel on earth mirrors and sustains the divine unity above, eventually transitioning into the mystery of the divine name "Elohim."</meta>

...by means of the "thread of hope" <note>original: "Tikvat Chut Ha-Shani" – a wordplay on Rahab’s scarlet cord in Joshua 2:18 and the Hebrew word for hope, *Tikvah*</note>. And the Scripture proclaims and says: "There is hope for your future, says the Lord, and children shall return to their borders" <note>Jeremiah 31:16</note>. This is based on this "Hope" which is bound to the "End" <note>Acharyt</note> and bound to the People of Israel. Therefore, "children shall return to their borders," etc. For "Hope" <note>Tikvah</note> is related to the word for a "Line" <note>Qav, a measuring line</note>. This is also hinted at through the inner foundation of Ezekiel’s building <note>the prophetic vision of the Third Temple</note>, where "a line of thirty cubits" is mentioned, and the word for "Line" is written as *Kaveh* <note>original: קוה</note> but read as *Qav*. It is also written in the prophecies of the latter days: "a line shall be stretched out over Jerusalem" <note>Zechariah 1:16</note>. One who understands these great principles will understand the secret of "Hope" and the "Line" in every instance. Through "Hope," we return to the Central Line <note>the vertical axis of the Sephirot connecting the highest to the lowest</note>, for "a line shall be stretched out over Jerusalem."

# The Ark of the Covenant

This divine attribute is sometimes called the "Ark." One who knows this secret knows the mystery of the Tablets that were placed in the Ark. This is the secret of the verse: "Behold, the Ark of the Covenant, the Lord of all the earth" <note>Joshua 3:11</note>. This attribute is called *Adonai* <term>Adonai: A Hebrew name for God, here identifying the Shekhinah/Divine Presence as the 'threshold' of the divine</term>, while the Foundation <note>the Sephirah of Yesod</note> is surely called "the Lord of all the earth." When the Tablets are in the Ark, then the **Shekhinah** <term>Shekhinah: The Divine Presence, localized here as the tenth Sephirah, Malchut (Kingship)</term> dwells within Israel as an eternal heritage. Wherever they go, they are the heritage of the Omnipresent, and they can never be separated from Him. There is no "bill of divorce" there, and this is the secret of the verse: "Where is the bill of your mother’s divorce, whom I have sent away?" <note>Isaiah 50:1</note>. 

And if you should say: "But is it not written afterward, 'And I saw that for all the causes whereby backsliding Israel committed adultery, I had put her away and given her a bill of divorce'?" <note>Jeremiah 3:8</note>. God forbid! That refers to the Kingdom of Jeroboam <note>the Northern Kingdom of Israel</note>, which has no return; so too with the Kingdom of Samaria. But Judah, which is the secret of the Assembly of Israel, where the Shekhinah dwells in Jerusalem and Judah through David—regarding them, the verse says: "and her treacherous sister Judah saw it not, and she also went and played the harlot." <note>Jeremiah 3:8</note>. This is the secret of "Where is the bill of your mother's divorce?" <note>implying that for the line of Judah, the marriage remains valid despite sins</note>. Safeguard this very great principle. Through Judah, Samaria shall return, but it shall not return as a separate kingdom, for the Name <note>God</note> gave it a bill of divorce regarding its kingship. This is the secret of the verse: "And I will make them one nation in the land, upon the mountains of Israel, and one king shall be king to them all; and they shall be no more two nations, neither shall they be divided into two kingdoms any more" <note>Ezekiel 37:22</note>. 
<meta>catchword: More</meta>

<column-break/>

...the Kindness <note>Chesed</note> that nourishes and protects, and she is sometimes called:

# The End

The reason is because she <note>the Shekhinah</note> is the "End" <note>original: Acharyt</note> of the **Sephirot** <term>Sephirot: The ten divine emanations or attributes of God</term>. The sign for this is the verse: "And you shall see my back" <note>Exodus 33:23; the Hebrew word for 'back' and 'afterward' share a root</note>. One who understands even a little of this will understand: "And it shall come to pass in the end of days, that the mountain of the Lord's house shall be established at the top of the mountains" <note>Isaiah 2:2</note>. Understand this with great insight: at the end it is written, "For out of Zion shall go forth the Torah" <note>Isaiah 2:3</note>. This is the essence of the redemption and its beginning for Israel. 

Because the Shekhinah is always with Israel, both in the Land and outside the Land, it is written: "Who dwells with them in the midst of their impurity" <note>Leviticus 16:16</note>. This is what Rabbi Shimon bar Yochai said: "How beloved is Israel, for in every place they were exiled, the Shekhinah went with them. They were exiled to Egypt—the Shekhinah was with them; they were exiled to Babylon—the Shekhinah was with them" <note>Talmud, Megillah 29a</note>. 

One who understands the secret of the exile in Egypt and the Foundation <note>Yesod</note>—where it says, "And I will pass through the land of Egypt" <note>Exodus 12:12</note> and it does not say "And I will descend"—understands that "A king is held captive in the tresses" <note>Song of Songs 7:6</note>. But at Mount Sinai, it says to Moses: "And the Lord descended... and He descended in a cloud and stood with him there and called" <note>Exodus 34:5</note>, and afterward, "And the Lord passed by before him and called" <note>Exodus 34:6</note>. 

Since the Shekhinah is with Israel, if they are righteous, she is like "a joyful mother of children," and it is written: "Your father shall be glad, and she who bore you shall rejoice" <note>Proverbs 23:25</note>. But if, God forbid, Israel sins, he is "a grief to his father" and "a foolish son is the sorrow of his mother" <note>Proverbs 10:1</note>. It is also written: "A wise son makes a glad father, but a foolish son..." Our Sages have already hinted at this with a parable of a child who soiled his clothes. The King said, "Let his nursemaid <note>original: 'Sanikto' – likely a Greek-derived term for an advocate or nurse</note> come and wipe away the filth." The secret is: "And for your transgressions was your mother sent away" <note>Isaiah 50:1</note>. Because the Assembly of Israel <note>the Shekhinah</note> bears the filth that Israel creates, what is written in the midst of it? "In your distress, when all these things are come upon you, in the end of days..." <note>Deuteronomy 4:30</note>. Therefore, "you shall return to the Lord your God."

# And Behold

I am opening for you the "Gates of Light" <note>likely a reference to the classic Kabbalistic text 'Sha'are Orah'</note>. Know that because this attribute is called "The End" <note>Acharyt</note>, we are destined to return to Zion by way of repentance. And behold, I am hinting that the attribute called "The End" is attached to the Central Line upon...
<meta>catchword: upon</meta>

It uses the metaphor of a mother caring for a child to describe the Shekhinah's role in absorbing Israel's sins and accompanying them into exile.</summary>

<meta>Continuity: This page continues the exploration of divine names and their manifestations. After discussing the "Ark of the Covenant" and the "Line of Hope," the author now transitions into the mystery of the divine name *Shaddai* (Almighty), its connection to the Temple, and the nature of prophecy compared across the generations of the Patriarchs and Moses.</meta>

"...and my breasts are like towers" <note>Song of Songs 8:10</note>. Like a bannered army, she was in my eyes "as one who finds peace." Let the wise consider this verse, for it teaches that when the defiled ones <note>The enemies of Israel</note> gashed the Sanctuary and the Temple, they introduced forces of impurity into it. Then, through the divine attribute called **Shaddai** <note>original: "Shaddai" - typically translated as Almighty, but here linked to "Shedim" (demons) and "Shud" (destruction)</note>, they brought into that holy place the forces of impurity called "demons" <note>Shedim</note>, as it is written: "the destruction that wastes at noonday" <note>Psalm 91:6</note>, and "the tents of robbers are at peace" <note>Job 12:6</note>. Woe to that hour! For then, the Sanctuary was defiled. "The kings of the earth did not believe, nor any of the inhabitants of the world, that the adversary and the enemy should enter the gates of Jerusalem" <note>Lamentations 4:12</note>. For it was said: "no more shall the uncircumcised and the unclean enter into you" <note>Isaiah 52:1</note>, and "I will cause the idols and the spirit of impurity to pass out of the land" <note>Zechariah 13:2</note>.

Observe that the spirit of impurity and the demons are called "spirits," as is mentioned in the Talmudic tractate *Chagigah* and several other places. The secret of this is that the "day of the Lord is near; it shall come as a destruction from the Almighty" <note>original: "Ke-shod mi-Shaddai" – a wordplay from Isaiah 13:6, linking Shaddai to Shod (destruction)</note>. The conclusion of the matter is that when the flow of nourishment from the "Hand of Shaddai" is cut off, then it is "destroyed" <note>shdedah</note>. 

Therefore, this attribute is called the **Speculum that does not shine** <note>term: *Aspaklaria she-eina me'ira* — a Kabbalistic concept referring to the Shekhinah (the Divine Presence), which receives light from higher levels rather than generating its own</note>. It does not shine from its own essence, but only from the abundance that comes to it through the divine "channels." When it is filled, it is called **Shaddai**, signifying that there is "enough" <note>Dai</note> for the whole world. But when the channels are cut off, then "destruction" <note>Shod</note> is found there. Thus, it is called a "looking glass" that does not shine of itself, except through the letter *Yod* <note>The final letter of Shaddai</note>. 

Behold, all the prophets did not actually enter within it; they only reached the gatehouse. It served as a "Veil" <note>Parokhet</note> at the gates where the prophets reached, and from behind which they saw what they saw. They would hear the voice of the Lord of Hosts in a prophetic vision. Because of this, matters were not entirely clear to them, because a partition stood before them, preventing them from seeing the "Hosts" who "assembled at the door of the Tent of Meeting" <note>Exodus 38:8</note>. Regarding this, it is written: "Hear now my words: if there be a prophet among you, I the Lord will make myself known to him in a vision, and will speak to him in a dream" <note>Numbers 12:6</note>. But Moses, our teacher, entered within and perceived through the **Polished Speculum** <note>term: *Aspaklaria hametzuchtzachet* — the clear, direct prophetic vision unique to Moses</note>. He entered beyond the "speculum that does not shine." What is written concerning Abraham? "The Lord appeared to him by the terebinths of Mamre, as he sat in the tent door" <note>Genesis 18:1</note>. But of Moses it is written: "The Lord called to Moses and spoke to him *from* the Tent of Meeting" <note>Leviticus 1:1</note>. Therefore, the prophecy of Moses is distinct from all other prophets; for all other prophets saw from the door of the tent, but Moses saw from within 

<column-break/>

the tent. This is what is meant by: "Hear now my words: if there be a prophet among you... not so my servant Moses; he is faithful in all my house" <note>Numbers 12:7</note>. You already know that the "House of Beauty" <note>Tiferet</note> is *Shaddai*, and it is the "Tent of Meeting." Therefore, the Scripture said to Moses: "I appeared to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob as God Almighty <note>El Shaddai</note>, but by my name YHWH I was not known to them" <note>Exodus 6:3</note>. This means: they saw my name YHWH only through the lens of *El Shaddai* and through the "speculum that does not shine." Therefore, I was not known to them with ultimate clarity, for they did not perceive my true essence except from behind the "Veil" and the secret of the "pillars and the sockets." 

One who understands this secret of what we have explained regarding the attribute of *Shaddai* will understand the mystery of the verse: "Can you find out the deep things of God? Can you find out the limit of the Almighty?" <note>Job 11:7</note>. Also: "And I appeared to Abraham... and my name YHWH etc." Open your eyes and you will see wonders; if you are worthy, you shall see. And this "Heifer" <note>referring to the Red Heifer or the Shekhinah</note> is also called:

### The Bed

The meaning of this is: "Behold the bed of Solomon; sixty brave men are around it, of the valiant of Israel" <note>Song of Songs 3:7</note>. This is because it is surrounded by all kinds of accusers and destroyers in the world. Various types of destruction and retribution surround it, and the sign of this is "as a rose among the thorns" <note>Song of Songs 2:2</note>. This is Jerusalem in the midst of the nations. It is placed in the center of the earth and its outskirts. And it leans slightly toward the center—that is to say, from the Tree of Life in the midst of the garden. Thus, its upper part is caught among the thorns and torn. But in a time when Israel is worthy and whole, and they perform the "peace offerings" properly, then the verse is fulfilled: "And I will give peace in the land" <note>Leviticus 26:6</note>. Then the "Bed of Solomon" was truly complete.

### And Know

That this "Bed" is the inheritance of Jacob our father (peace be upon him) entirely and above all, because he was a "perfect firstborn," as it is said: "Israel is my son, my firstborn." Therefore, Jacob inherited this Bed. Abraham did not inherit it, for he had the flaw of Ishmael; nor did Isaac inherit it, for he had the flaw of Esau. The thorns and briars surrounding the Bed represent these, along with the other external forces and the princes of the nations. But Jacob, who is "perfect" <note>Tam</note> and stands in the center <note>The Central Pillar of the Sephirot</note>, inherited this Bed, as it is said: "Israel is my son, my firstborn." Therefore, this Bed returned to him as it was at the beginning of the world's creation. Because of this, Israel "bows at the head of the bed" <note>Genesis 47:31</note>. What is the reason? It is written: "And his tabernacle was in Salem" <note>original: "vayihi be-Shalem suko" — Psalm 76:3, wordplay on Salem/Shalem meaning 'perfect' or 'peace'</note>...

<summary>This page discusses the divine name Shaddai, explaining how it acts as a "looking glass" for prophecy and how its meaning shifts from "sufficiency" to "destruction" based on Israel's actions. It also contrasts the direct prophecy of Moses with that of the Patriarchs and describes the "Bed of Solomon" as a symbol of the protected Divine Presence inherited by the perfect Jacob.</summary>

<meta>Continuity: This page continues the discussion of the divine names and the Sefirah (divine attribute) of Malchut (Kingship/Sovereignty), specifically how this attribute interacts with the lower worlds and changes its "name" based on the actions of the people of Israel.</meta>

...the world of the angels and all the rest of the world, and the Sovereignty <note>original: "Malchut" – the final of the ten Sefirot, representing the Divine Presence in the physical world</note> which is called **Beginning** <note>original: "Reshit"</note> and is called **God** <note>original: "Elohim"</note>. When you understand this, you will merit to understand all that our sages of blessed memory taught regarding the word **"In the beginning"** <note>Genesis 1:1</note>: that "Beginning" refers to nothing other than Wisdom <note>Chokhmah</note>, or the Torah, or Israel, and all other things called "Beginning."

You will also understand what was said: that "for the sake of Moses the world was created," as it is written: "And he saw a **beginning** for himself, for there the portion of the lawgiver was hidden" <note>Deuteronomy 33:21</note>. Everything is stored away in the treasuries of the Torah, and everything is clear to those who have eyes to see. For through the revealed things, those with eyes may enter into the hidden mysteries. The sign for this is: "Apples of gold in settings of silver" <note>Proverbs 25:11; a classic metaphor for a hidden inner meaning inside a beautiful outer shell</note>.

And now, observe: when the attribute of Kingship is called **God** <note>Elohim</note>, it is at the time when Kingship is filled from the side of Strength <note>Gevurah - the attribute of Judgment</note>. The conclusion of the matter is that in every place, she is called according to the thing that she is currently filled with. The sign for this is the **"Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil."** You must guard this great principle for every place in the Torah: that this attribute is named after the thing she receives from, as it says: "for My name is within him" <note>Exodus 23:21</note>.

Therefore, when Israel is pure and righteous, they fill that **Speculum** <note>original: "Aspaklaria" – a visionary mirror or lens</note> that is above her, which is comprised of the 613 commandments. But when Israel turns from the straight path and sins, defiling the world, then she becomes filled with those "external things" <note>Chitzonim – the forces of the "Other Side" or impurity</note> which are the forms of transgressions. Then she is called "a sword full of blood" <note>Isaiah 34:6</note>. It is written: "it was full of justice; righteousness lodged in it, but now murderers" <note>Isaiah 1:21</note>. The final word is that she is never empty: she is either "full of justice" or "murderers."

This is what the sages of blessed memory meant when they said: "From the day the Temple was destroyed, even though the four death penalties were abolished, their judgment was not abolished." Here lies the reason: either "Justice" or "Murderers." Keep this principle well. From it, you can grasp the great power of one who fulfills the commandments and purifies the world, meritously influencing the masses and guiding them in a straight path—or conversely, the power of those who commit transgressions and defile the world, sinning and causing others to sin. Consider how far the power of their evil reaches, causing a "blemish" in the Assembly of Israel <note>the Shekhinah</note>. This is the meaning of: "A foolish son is a grief to his father and bitterness to her who bore him" <note>Proverbs 17:25</note>, while "A wise son makes a glad father" <note>Proverbs 15:20</note>. And: "through your transgressions your mother was sent away" <note>Isaiah 50:1</note>. And remember:

<column-break/>

"My son, if your heart is wise, my heart will be glad, even mine" <note>Proverbs 23:15</note>. The secret of this is: "A seat for the King’s mother" <note>1 Kings 2:19</note>. The root of this principle is what is written: "A mother of children is happy" <note>Psalm 113:9</note>, and "Let your father and your mother be glad, and let her who bore you rejoice" <note>Proverbs 23:25</note.

Now that we have explained this principle and these great matters to you, know that this attribute is also called **Shaddai** <note>Almighty</note>. The reason is that from her, the abundance <note>Shefa</note> comes to all existing things in the world. She is the sustainer of all creatures; from her, every creature receives strength, existence, standing, life, and livelihood. This is because she is the **Gate** to all the Sefirot, and there is no way for any side in the world to receive anything from the rest of the Sefirot except through her. 

It is like the "river that goes out of Eden to water the garden" <note>Genesis 2:10</note>. Up until the "Garden," that is the place of abundance, emanation, and "nursing." From there and below is the "World of Separation." Thus, from Kingship and below, all creatures are separated according to their kinds. They each receive abundance and strength according to their species. From the attribute of Kingship, everyone receives joy, existence, and life—"each man according to his eating" <note>Exodus 16:21</note> and according to his kind. Therefore, this attribute is called **Shaddai**, because there is in her **enough** <note>original: "Dai" - a play on Shaddai (She-dai: He who said 'enough')</note> for the livelihood, life, existence, and standing of all beings in the world, and she lacks nothing. 

All things nurse from her. This is what was said by Chanina in the name of the One who said "Enough" to the world. That is to say: in this attribute, there is enough, and rectification, and standing, and existence for all beings, each according to their circumstances. This is the secret by which the prophets prophesy through "rectification." 

When Israel does the will of the Place <note>God</note>, then all the "channels" from above come to her in full measure, and the blessings flow into Israel first. From there onward, the blessings are drawn out to the rest of all creatures and beings in the world, each according to its kind. The hint here is the matter of the **breasts** <note>original: "Shadayim" - another wordplay on Shaddai</note> from which an infant nurses. 

But when they later sin and provoke anger, then the channels are cut off from above, and no blessing is found among the creatures. This is the secret of: "We have a little sister, and she has no breasts; what shall we do for our sister in the day when she shall be spoken for?" <note>Song of Songs 8:8</note>. "If she be a wall, we will build upon her a turret of silver" <note>Song of Songs 8:9</note>—from the upper white light. "But if she be a door"—meaning she is impoverished <note>original: "Dalah" - a pun on 'Delet' (door)</note>—"we will enclose her with boards of cedar," and not just any cedar. "I am a wall, and my breasts are like towers" <note>Song of Songs 8:10</note>—I am strong in the commandments.

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic dynamics of the Sefirah of Malchut, showing how it acts as a mirror reflecting human conduct and a gateway (Shaddai) that provides "enough" nourishment for all of creation.</summary>
<keywords>Malchut, Shaddai, Shefa (Abundance), Aspaklaria (Mirror/Speculum), 613 Mitzvot, Divine Names, Nursing, Channels (Tzinorot), Sefirot</keywords>

--- Page 34 ---
<meta>Continuity: This page continues the exploration of the divine attribute of Sovereignty (Malchut). The previous page explained how this attribute takes on different names, such as "God" (Elohim) when acting through judgment, and how it is the "Beginning" of the revealed world. This page transitions into the historical and mystical significance of the Exodus from Egypt and the crossing of the Red Sea as demonstrations of this divine power over the celestial "princes" of the nations.</meta>

Egypt is a neighbor to the Land <note>Israel</note>, just as a foreign child might be born in the land of Egypt. Therefore, the Blessed Creator gave signs and wonders against that great Prince <note>original: "Sar" — the celestial patron or guardian angel of a nation</note>, the Prince of Egypt. This was done so that people would not say: "There is a great Prince in the heavens whom the Blessed Name cannot overcome." For this reason, it says: **"Smite the capitals, that the thresholds may shake"** <note>Amos 9:1</note>.

Because of this, the Torah says: **"That you may remember the day you came out of the land of Egypt all the days of your life"** <note>Deuteronomy 16:3</note>. This is so you may be confident that there is no prince or ruler among all the nations whom the Holy One, Blessed be He, cannot overcome. It is written: **"Against all the gods of Egypt I will execute judgments"** <note>Exodus 12:12</note>. Therefore, the Blessed Name Himself executed judgments against all the gods of Egypt, for there is no prince or ruler who can stand against the Name alone. This is why the verse says: **"And I will pass through the land of Egypt... I, and not an angel... and against all the gods of Egypt I will execute judgments... I, and not a messenger"** <note>A traditional Haggadah commentary on Exodus 12:12</note>.

Consequently, He struck the firstborn, and struck the constellation of Aries <note>the Ram</note> which is the head of the twelve zodiac signs, and struck in the month of Nisan <note>roughly April</note> which is the head of the twelve months. It is written: **"Observe the month of Abib"** <note>Exodus 13:4; Deuteronomy 16:1</note>, which means "The Father <note>Ab</note> of the Ten <note>represented by the Hebrew letter Yod</note>." 

And behold, Egypt—which is the "constriction" <note>original: "Meitzar," a pun on Mitzrayim (Egypt)</note> adjacent to the Land of Israel—pursued Israel until they reached the Red Sea <note>original: "Yam Suf"</note>. When they arrived at that place, they were enveloped by that divine attribute called **Yam Suf**. This is the secret of the Sea called **Suf** <note>End</note>, because it represents the end of the levels within the **Ten Sefirot** <note>the ten creative powers of God</note>. The secret is found in: **"And I will set your border from the Red Sea [Yam Suf] even unto the sea of the Philistines"** <note>Exodus 23:31</note>. Open your eyes and see! 

Therefore, this attribute is called a "Whirlwind" <note>original: "Sufah," sharing a root with Suf/End</note>. It is written: **"The Lord has His way in the whirlwind [Sufah] and in the storm"** <note>Nahum 1:3</note>. A great secret is hinted at here: the "End" <note>Suf</note> of the Letter **He** <note>the final letter of God's name, representing the Sefirah of Kingship</note> is the end of the ten Sefirot. The sign for this is that "Whirlwind" <note>Sufah</note> is adjacent to "Storm" <note>Sa'arah</note>, the place that separates the boundaries. The sign is: **"And it shall be very tempestuous [Nis'arah] round about Him"** <note>Psalm 50:3</note>; and **"Then the Lord answered Job out of the whirlwind [Sa'arah]"** <note>Job 38:1</note>; **"Who crushes me with a tempest [Sa'arah]"** <note>Job 9:17</note>; and **"He shall go with whirlwinds of the south"** <note>Zechariah 9:14</note>. 

Therefore, "His way is in the whirlwind and the storm." This is what our Sages of blessed memory meant when they said: **"The Holy One, Blessed be He, is as meticulous with the righteous as a hair's breadth"** <note>The Hebrew word for "hair" (Se'arah) is spelled the same as "storm" (Sa'arah)</note>. This is because the "Secret of the Hair" surrounds the righteous. The sign for this is: **"And his hand took hold of Esau's heel"** <note>Genesis 25:26</note>—and Esau is "hairy" <note>Se'ir</note>. This is the place where the lower worlds separate. **"And they shall no more sacrifice their sacrifices unto the goat-demons [Se'irim]"** <note>Leviticus 17:7</note>. For when the attribute of the "Whirlwind" <note>Sufah</note> passes, it inspects every place where it finds a "hairy crown" <note>original: "Kodkod Se'ar," meaning a sinful head</note>, the hair of one who walks in his guilt.

<column-break/>

And when this attribute finds the wicked, it consumes them from the world. The secret is: **"When the whirlwind [Sufah] passes, the wicked is no more, but the righteous is an everlasting foundation"** <note>Proverbs 10:25</note>. For behold, **"The Lord will come with fire... and His chariots like a whirlwind [Sufah], to render His anger with fury, and His rebuke with flames of fire"** <note>Isaiah 66:15</note>. 

Therefore, it was found during the Exodus from Egypt that the Egyptians pursued Israel until the Red Sea, and at the Red Sea a miracle was performed for them. For that is the place of miracles; there, the Egyptians were punished and the miracle was performed for Israel. **"Your right hand, O Lord, is glorious in power; Your right hand, O Lord, shatters the enemy"** <note>Exodus 15:6</note>. Your right hand sinks them and saves you. 

The reason it is called **Sufah** <note>Whirlwind</note> is because it has a "front" and a "back" in one single attribute: inside is Mercy, but outside is Retribution. This is also how it will be in Edom <note>a historical coding for Rome/the West</note> at the time of the Redemption; it will come to consume the "Hair" <note>Se'ir</note> surrounding it, for that is Edom, which is called **Seir** <note>meaning "Hairy"</note>. 

From that point onward, He will "shave the hairy crown above," and nothing will remain of their strength. **"The Lord shall punish the host of the high heaven on high, and the kings of the earth upon the earth"** <note>Isaiah 24:21</note>. **"Who is this that comes from Edom?"** <note>Isaiah 63:1</note>. And then it is written: **"Enlarge the place of your tent"** <note>Isaiah 54:2</note>. **"The Lord shall shave with a razor that is hired"** <note>Isaiah 7:20</note>. And it is written: **"But God shall wound the head of His enemies, and the hairy crown [Kodkod Se'ar] of such a one as walks on in his guilt"** <note>Psalm 68:21</note>. 

When no "hair" remains in **Seir** <note>Edom</note>, then **"Saviors shall come up on Mount Zion to judge the mount of Esau; and the kingdom shall be the Lord’s"** <note>Obadiah 1:21</note>. Then **"The Lord shall be king over all the earth"** <note>Zechariah 14:9</note>. This is the meaning of the war of the Lord against Amalek. **"Who is this that comes from Edom?"** Just as there was a war with Egypt, which is a neighbor to the Land of Israel, so too will there be a war with Seir, which is a neighbor to the Land of Israel. It is written: **"Your south quarter shall be from the wilderness of Zin along by the side of Edom"** <note>Numbers 34:3</note>. This is the meaning of: **"His hand took hold of Esau’s heel."** 

Therefore, **"As in the days of your coming out of the land of Egypt, I will show him marvelous things"** <note>Micah 7:15</note>. Then He will shave the hairy crown of Seir, and "his mischief shall return upon his own head, and his violence shall come down upon his own crown." For thus it is said regarding the final Redemption: **"For behold, the Lord will come with fire, and His chariots like a whirlwind [Sufah], to render His anger with fury, and His rebuke with flames of fire"** <note>Isaiah 66:15</note>. And nearby it says: **"For by fire and by His sword will the Lord plead with all flesh"** <note>Isaiah 66:16</note>. And this is: **"For My sword shall be bathed in heaven"** <note>Isaiah 34:5</note>. Behold the **Gates of Righteousness** <note>original: "Sha'arei Tzedek"</note>.

<summary>This page explains the mystical connection between the Exodus, the Red Sea (Yam Suf), and the future redemption. It interprets "Suf" (End) and "Sufah" (Whirlwind) as manifestations of divine judgment that protect Israel while consuming their enemies, specifically focusing on the destruction of the celestial "Princes" of Egypt and Edom.</summary>
<keywords>Exodus, Red Sea, Sefirot, Whirlwind, Judgment, Edom, Esau, Redemption, Divine Names, Prince of Egypt</keywords>

<meta>Continuity: This page continues the discussion of the divine attribute of Sovereignty (Malchut). Having previously discussed the Exodus and the power of the Divine Name over the celestial "Princes" of the nations, the text now focuses on the patriarch Jacob as the embodiment of spiritual "completion" and the mystical meaning of "gathering" at the time of death.</meta>

...and it is written: **"Jacob was a perfect man, dwelling in tents"** <note>Genesis 25:27</note>. We translate this <note>into Aramaic</note> as "a complete man." Therefore, "Behold the bed of Solomon" <note>Song of Songs 3:7—a mystical reference to the divine presence or the spiritual lineage</note>. If you should say that concerning Abraham it is written: **"Walk before me and be perfect"** <note>Genesis 17:1</note>, these two concepts are related. For Abraham, it is written "walk before me," referring to perfection achieved through movement <note>active progression</note>, not through "sitting." For Abraham did not sit upon the Throne of the Eternal Name <note>original: "Yod-Yod," a scribal shorthand for the Tetragrammaton, YHWH</note>.

But regarding Jacob, it is written: **"a perfect man, dwelling in tents"**; his perfection was through "dwelling," for Jacob sat upon the Throne of the Eternal Name. The sign for this is: **"Israel, in whom I will be glorified"** <note>Isaiah 49:3</note>—with the beauty of a man sitting in a house. Therefore, it is written of him that he was a perfect man dwelling in "tents" <note>plural</note>. This refers to the Torah, as it says: **"This is the Torah: when a man dies in a tent"** <note>Numbers 19:14</note>.

Consequently, it is said that Abraham’s "bed" <note>his spiritual legacy or progeny</note> was not "complete," nor was Isaac's (may his memory be a blessing). But Jacob said: "Heaven forbid there should be any defect in my bed," such as Abraham, from whom Ishmael emerged, or Isaac, from whom Esau emerged. They all answered him and said: **"Hear O Israel, the Eternal our God, the Eternal is One."** Thus his "bed" was found to be complete. Therefore, at the time of his passing, he clung to it completely, and this is the meaning of: **"He gathered his feet into the bed, and expired, and was gathered to his people"** <note>Genesis 49:33</note>. 

In every instance regarding the righteous, "gathering" refers to the place of the "bed," which is the place of purity. For this reason, He said to Moses: **"Be gathered to your people."** And it is written of the sons of Jacob: **"Gather yourselves together, that I may tell you..."** and **"All Israel were gathered to the city as one man, comrades"**—this refers to the purity of all Israel during the Pilgrimage festivals. Therefore, wherever you find "gathering" in the Torah regarding the righteous, it refers to their being gathered to the "bed," which is the place of purity. For outside of this "bed," everything is a place of impurity on all sides. 

Thus, it is written: **"If there be among you any man that is not clean by reason of a chance by night, then shall he go abroad out of the camp, he shall not come within the camp"** <note>Deuteronomy 23:11</note>. And it is written: **"That they put out of the camp every leper, and every one that has an issue"** <note>Numbers 5:2</note>. And regarding Miriam, it is written: **"Let her be shut out from the camp seven days, and after that let her be gathered in"** <note>Numbers 12:14</note>. Open your eyes and see wonders! Regarding Naaman, it is written: **"And I would gather him from his leprosy"** <note>2 Kings 5:11</note>. The great secret is that **"the righteous is gathered from the evil"** <note>Isaiah 57:1</note>, and **"Gather my saints together unto me,"** and **"Men of kindness are gathered."** Those who understand this secret will know:

<column-break/>

the secret of **"And he gathered his feet into the bed."** Therefore, every person must be careful at the time of their death that none of their "limbs" <note>spiritual components</note> remain outside of this "bed." For concerning anything that remains outside of this bed, it is said: **"it shall not be gathered, nor shall it be buried; it shall be for the beasts of the field"** and **"I have given you for food to the fowls of the heaven."** This is because surrounding this "bed" are all kinds of destroyers and agents of retribution. Any of a person's "limbs" remaining outside this "bed" dies in great pain.

This is the secret of what we said: **"Behold the bed of Solomon; sixty valiant men are round about it, of the valiant of Israel. They all hold swords, being expert in war: every man has his sword upon his thigh because of fear in the night"** <note>Song of Songs 3:7-8</note>. This means: because of the fear of Gehenna <note>Hell</note>, which is compared to "night." Therefore, it is written of Jacob: **"And he gathered his feet into the bed."** Every person must prepare themselves for this "gathering," for without it, there would be great suffering. Contemplate these great principles that I have opened for you in this place, and from them, you will see many wondrous secrets. As we have said: there is nothing between the Garden of Eden and Gehenna but a hair's breadth. The sign for this is: **"And round about Him it is very tempestuous"** <note>Psalm 50:3; the Hebrew word 'nish'arah' (tempestuous) is wordplayed with 'se'arah' (hair)</note>. Therefore, a person must ensure they do not remain outside, but are "gathered to their people." This small section prepares all types of "gathering" mentioned regarding the righteous.

->**And this attribute** is called the **Red Sea**<-
->and therefore it was split<-

to receive His children and save them from the hand of His enemies. Have I not told you that surrounding the "bed" are thorns, briers, and thistles? And behold, the land of Egypt is the "second" land to it <note>to the Land of Israel</note>. Therefore, it is called *Mitzrayim* <note>Egypt</note>, which is related to the language of *metzarim* <note>boundaries or straits</note> bordering the inheritance. For the *mitzar* <note>border/strait</note> creates a division between territories. 

Behold, the scripture proclaims: **"And Hebron was built seven years before Zoan in Egypt"** <note>Numbers 13:22</note>. This means the "joining of hands" <note>wordplay on Hebron/Chibur</note> was built for seven years—it is the "Daughter of Seven" <note>Bat Sheva/Sovereignty</note>—and Zoan in Egypt is adjacent to it. This is why they said that the land of Egypt is second to the Land of Israel. For the "Prince of Egypt" is second in the celestial Chariot, as it says: **"And he made him to ride in the second chariot which he had"** <note>Genesis 41:43</note>. Therefore, the Blessed Name found it necessary to perform signs and wonders in the land of Egypt, because its Prince is superior to all other Princes, being second...

<summary>This page explores the mystical "perfection" of Jacob, explaining that his spiritual lineage (his "bed") was pure, unlike Abraham and Isaac. It details the concept of "gathering" at death as a transition to a state of purity, protected from the "destroyers" of Gehenna, and begins to link this to the geography and celestial status of Egypt.</summary>

<meta>Continuity: This page follows a discussion on the perfection of the patriarch Jacob and his association with the "Throne of Glory." The text now shifts to the mystical significance of the "Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil" and the divine attribute known as "Sovereignty" (Malchut), often personified as the "Bride."</meta>

The Name for the gates of Jerusalem <note>A common Kabbalistic metaphor for the entry points into the divine realm</note> is certainly from the Lord; it refers to those partitions that cling from the outside to the Chariot <note>original: *Merkavah*, the divine throne-chariot described in the vision of Ezekiel</note>, which You commanded "shall not enter into your congregation." For this reason, this attribute is called the **"Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil."** 

Observe this principle closely, because both "Good" and "Evil" cling to it. As it is written: **"See, I have set before you life and good, death and evil"** <note>Deuteronomy 30:15</note>, "blessing and curse." And everything depends on Israel; whichever thing they desired, they would draw down from this attribute, whether good or evil. Everything is through their hands. It is written: **"Let your father and mother be glad"** <note>Proverbs 23:25</note>, and it is written: **"A foolish son is the grief of his mother"** <note>Proverbs 10:1</note>. And in the portion "Where is the bill of your mother’s divorcement," it says: **"for your transgressions your mother was sent away"** <note>Isaiah 50:1</note>. 

Therefore, you will find in the covenant of circumcision that "Good" and "Evil" are intertwined, and the "foreskin" <note>original: *Orlah*, also meaning a spiritual barrier or "husk"</note> surrounds the "Good." Once the foreskin is removed, the "Good" appears and is revealed—this is the secret of the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil.** Observe this principle very carefully. 

Behold, the first man <note>Adam</note> was commanded to use the Kingdom <note>original: *Malchut*, the tenth divine emanation</note> through the "Good" alone, but he used it through the "Evil." He "brought an idol into the Temple" <note>a metaphor for introducing impurity into a holy space</note>; he defiled the sanctuary of the Lord. The Divine Presence <note>original: *Shekhinah*</note> departed, the "bundle" was separated, and man was cursed. The sign for this is: **"Your sins have withheld good from you"** <note>Jeremiah 5:25</note>. They withheld the "Good" indeed, and thus "Evil" was found clinging to the earth, and a curse was sent upon the ground. 

For this reason, even thought was defiled when the Priest entered on the Day of Atonement <note>Yom Kippur</note> to sanctify the holy beams. Therefore: **"He shall not come at all times into the Holy place"** <note>Leviticus 16:2</note>, for there are "times" for good and "times" for evil. But it is written: **"With *this* [Zot] shall Aaron come into the Holy place"** <note>Leviticus 16:3</note>. "*This*" [Zot] is Jerusalem; "*this*" is the Torah; **"and *this* is the blessing"**; **"*this* is the heritage of the servants of the Lord, and their righteousness."** 

For this reason, the Elders were punished when **"they beheld God and did eat and drink"** <note>Exodus 24:11—suggesting they viewed the Divine in an inappropriate or sensory way</note>. And it is written of the sons of Aaron: **"and they offered strange fire before the Lord"** <note>Leviticus 10:1</note>, to teach you and to guard you from "strange fire." The end of the matter is: as long as the "foreskin" <note>husk</note> is attached to the "Good," the "Good" is hidden and concealed. 

Therefore: **"When you shall come into the land and plant all manner of trees for food, then you shall count the fruit thereof as uncircumcised [forbidden]; three years it shall be as uncircumcised to you"** <note>Leviticus 19:23</note>. One shall not eat its fruit, for these are the "three husks" <note>original: *kelipot*, the layers of spiritual impurity</note> of the foreskin, and they are called a "sickness of evil." The fourth year is intermediate. But in the fifth year—which is the secret of the Kingdom <note>Malchut</note>, the secret of the "final He" <note>the last letter of the Name YHWH</note>—everything is pure and it is permitted 

<column-break/>

to eat its fruit, **"to yield unto you the increase thereof"** <note>Leviticus 19:25</note>, for then everything is "Good" and there is no "Evil" in it. Thus have been explained to you some of the wonders concerning the matter of the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil.** From this, you may enter into many chambers. And this attribute is called in the *Song of Songs*:

## Bride
<note>original: *Kallah*</note>
The secret of the **"Bride"** is when "Evil" is distanced from her and all the "husks" <note>kelipot</note> are removed. When she is complete in her sovereignty, there is no defect or "foreign" relation in her. For she comes from the attribute that is the Foundation of all <note>the sefirah of *Yesod*</note>. He "visits the earth and waters it" <note>Psalm 65:9</note>, and types of abundance, emanation, and blessings come to her from the head of the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*, the highest emanation</note>. 

She is called "Bride" <note>Kallah</note> from the language of "All" <note>Kol</note>, because she contains all abundance, emanation, blessing, and life. This is **"a garden locked, my sister, my bride"** <note>Song of Songs 4:12</note>, meaning that the forces of impurity shall not enter her. Then she is sanctified and adorned with all kinds of jewelry that come to her—the nine mirrors <note>original: *aspaklaria*, referring to the nine preceding emanations reflecting into her</note>. **"As a bridegroom decks himself with a priestly crown, and as a bride adorns herself with her jewels"** <note>Isaiah 61:10</note>. When the Groom truly "decks himself with a priestly crown," then she, like a bride, "adorns herself with her jewels." 

Therefore: **"I will greatly rejoice in the Lord."** **"Thou shalt no more be termed Forsaken, neither shall thy land any more be termed Desolate"** <note>Isaiah 62:4</note>. Contemplate the secret of: **"I am come into my garden, my sister, my bride."** Into "my garden" literally. And therefore: **"as the bridegroom rejoiceth over the bride, so shall thy God rejoice over thee."** 

But if, Heaven forbid, she is defiled by sins, she is then banished from her place, and **"the sword of the Lord is filled with blood"** <note>Isaiah 34:6</note>. May the Blessed Name save us from this punishment! 

And behold, I am hinting: when the "husk" <note>kelipah</note> goes outside, and all is united under the wedding canopy <note>Chuppah</note>, and **"no stranger is with them in the house,"** then all types of "foreskins" and "husks" are on the outside. It is written: **"spread therefore thy skirt over thine handmaid; for thou art a near kinsman [redeemer]"** <note>Ruth 3:9</note>. The sign is: **"it shall have a redemption"** and **"in the jubilee he shall go out."** 

The Jubilee is the fiftieth year <note>original: *Yovel*</note>, when all kinds of abundance and extensions are separated and come to the "Foundation of the World" <note>Yesod</note>, which is called **K-L** <note>the Hebrew letters Kaf (20) and Lamed (30), equaling 50</note>. And when she is united with the King, W-H <note>the letters Vav and He of the Divine Name</note>, which is the secret of the "final He," then she is called **Bride** <note>Kallah</note>. "Hand to hand" <note>original: *Yad Yad*, referring to the power of the union</note>, "the Lord is one and His name is one." This is a beautiful explanation; know it well. And this attribute is called:

## This
<note>original: *Zot*</note>
**"And *this* [Zot] is the blessing, wherewith Moses the man of God blessed..."** <note>Deuteronomy 33:1</note>

<summary>This page explores the dual nature of the divine attribute of Malchut (Sovereignty), represented by the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil, which can manifest as blessing or curse depending on Israel's actions. It identifies Malchut as the "Bride" (Kallah) and explains the mystical necessity of removing spiritual "husks" (kelipot) to achieve divine union.</summary>
<keywords>Tree of Knowledge, Good and Evil, Malchut, Bride (Kallah), Shekhinah, Husks (Kelipot), Foreskin (Orlah), Jubilee, Zot (This), Divine Union</keywords>

--- Page 37 ---
<meta>Continuity: This page continues the mystical exploration of the "Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil," specifically how it relates to the physical and spiritual borders of Israel and the surrounding nations like Moab, Edom, and the Amorites. It transitions from the general nature of the "Tree" to its manifestation in biblical history and geography.</meta>

between Moab and the Amorites. And it is written that Israel inherited the land of the Amorites: **"And Israel dwelt in the land of the Amorites"** <note>Numbers 21:31</note>. Therefore, the "Wadis of Arnon" <note>The Arnon River served as a significant boundary in the Bible</note> refers to the war fought by the Kingdom at the Red Sea, which is called by this name. The "crossings of Arnon" are called Arnon because they are situated near the Amorites; and a war was fought on behalf of Israel in every place fitting for its name—the Red Sea against the Egyptians, and Arnon against the Amorites. 

From here, you may enter the secret of the **"lily among the thorns"** <note>Song of Songs 2:2; a common Kabbalistic metaphor for the Divine Presence (the lily) surrounded by harsh external forces (the thorns)</note>. This is also the secret of Jerusalem being set "in the midst of the nations" <note>Ezekiel 5:5</note>, with lands surrounding her. It is further the secret of the **"sixty warriors"** <note>Song of Songs 3:7</note> who surround her, all of them "clutching swords." In this manner, you will understand many hidden things from the Torah. 

This divine attribute is called:
->**THE TREE**<-
of Knowledge. That is to say, the Tree serves as a "throne" for Knowledge <note>original: *Da'at*, representing the central balancing force of the divine emanations</note>. It draws life, abundance, and authority from Knowledge—which is Beauty <note>original: *Tiferet*, the central male principle</note>—just as the light of the moon draws its light from the sun. 

If the "channels" of flow from Knowledge to this Tree were to be cut off, then the "thorns," the types of impurity, and the "foreskin" <note>original: *Orlah*, referring to the spiritual husks or barriers to holiness</note> that surround it would take hold. These rebellious and thorny forces would cling to it, and then "an image stands in the Temple and filth is cast within." This is the meaning of: **"When the adversary and enemy enter the gates of Jerusalem"** <note>Lamentations 4:12</note>, and "She saw nations enter her sanctuary, whom You commanded should not enter Your congregation." 

Therefore, it is called the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil.** While it draws from Knowledge, it leans toward Justice/Righteousness, which is called "Good." As they say: **"Tell the righteous it shall be well [good]"** <note>Isaiah 3:10</note>. At that time, the moon is full, the Temple is in its purity, and the Tree of Knowledge is "Good." **"She does him good and not evil all the days of her life"** <note>Proverbs 31:12</note>. This means that as long as the divine emanations <note>original: *Sefirot*</note> draw from the "World of Life" through the connection that binds them to Sovereignty <note>original: *Malchut*</note>, she does only good and not evil. 

But if the channels of Knowledge are cut off from reaching Sovereignty, then the attribute of "Good" departs and "Evil" enters. This is the secret of the verse: **"Also, a soul without knowledge is not good"** <note>Proverbs 19:2</note>. Because one is linked to the other, when the impure forces, the "husks," and the "filth" hold sway, then the Righteous One is gathered away—for **"the righteous is taken away from the evil to come"** <note>Isaiah 57:1</note>. For evil descends from 

<meta>catchword: the Name</meta>

<column-break/>

the Lord upon Edom <note>Biblical Edom is often used as a cipher for Rome or the forces of harsh judgment</note>. **"The sword of the Lord is filled with blood... Who is this that comes from Edom, with dyed garments... Why is Your apparel red?"** <note>Isaiah 34:6, 63:1-2</note>. 

Behold, wondrous secrets have been explained to you regarding the borders of the Land of Israel; all of them are destined to be purged. Then: **"The Lord your God will enlarge your border"** and **"Enlarge the place of your tent."** At that time, **"The Lord shall be King over all the earth."** 

From this vantage point, you can understand the secret of the **two goats** on the Day of Atonement: **"The goat shall bear upon it all their iniquities to a barren land"** <note>Leviticus 16:22</note> and he shall send the goat into the wilderness. Your "southern side" shall be from the Wilderness of Zin near Edom. **"I loved Jacob, but Esau I hated; I made his mountains a waste and his inheritance for the jackals of the desert"** <note>Malachi 1:2-3</note>. 

Here is the secret of what our Sages said: "The Holy One, blessed be He, is as exacting with the righteous as a **hair's breadth**" <note>A famous Rabbinic teaching on divine justice</note>. This is because the region of "Seir" <note>The territory of Esau/Edom; the name *Seir* sounds like the Hebrew word for "hair," *se'ar*</note> shares a border with the attribute called Justice (*Tzedek*). Therefore, Jacob and Esau were born as one <note>as twins</note>. And thus it is written: **"You are passing through the border of your brothers, the children of Esau, who dwell in Seir... be very careful; do not contend with them, for I have given Mount Seir to Esau as an inheritance"** <note>Deuteronomy 2:4-5</note>. 

The reason is that the Holy One, blessed be He, left them there to discipline Israel through them, and to be exacting with them "as a hair's breadth," as it says: **"and round about Him it shall be very tempestuous"** <note>Psalm 50:3; the Hebrew word for "tempestuous," *nis'arah*, shares a root with "hair," *se'ar*</note>. These are the nations the Lord left to test Israel. 

But in the end: **"Saviors shall go up on Mount Zion to judge the mount of Esau, and the kingdom shall be the Lord's"** <note>Obadiah 1:21</note>. **"Though you soar as high as the eagle, and though you set your nest among the stars, from there I will bring you down, says the Lord"** <note>Obadiah 1:4</note>. 

We have now explained to you great principles regarding the Red Sea and the "storm" <note>original: *suphah*, a pun on *Yam Suph*, the Red Sea</note>. Guard them, for they are hidden in many places in the Torah, and the sign for this is: **"Therefore it is said in the Book of the Wars of the Lord: 'Eth and Heb in Suphah, and the wadis of Arnon'"** <note>Numbers 21:14</note>. 

The explanation of this verse is that at the Red Sea were the "Wars of the Lord," but in the "Wadis of Arnon" is the secret of **partnership**. This relates to the *Arnona* <term>Arnona: A land or produce tax paid to a government; the text uses this as a pun on the river Arnon</term> dough-offering and the *Arnona* livestock-tax. One who knows this secret knows the secret of the "crossings of Arnon" and the great miracles performed there. Arnon is the secret of the "neighboring place" between Moab and Israel, as it is written: **"For Arnon is the border of Moab."**

<summary>This page provides a Kabbalistic interpretation of Israel's borders, linking the Arnon River and the Red Sea to the "Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil." It explains how the Divine Presence (Malchut) is surrounded by external forces like Edom and Moab, which serve as "thorns" to test and discipline Israel until the final redemption.</summary>
<keywords>Tree of Knowledge, Arnon, Edom, Esau, Seir, Malchut, Da'at, Tiferet, Red Sea, borders of Israel, divine justice, Arnona tax, Moab</keywords>
