<meta>Continuity: This page continues the mystical exploration of the "Tree of Knowledge," moving from its role as a "throne" for the divine central balance to its specific identity as the "Book of Life" and its connection to the holiday of Shemini Atzeret. It further examines how different biblical figures and festivals represent the unification of divine energies.</meta>

...and so forth. And the secret is: **"And let us make for ourselves a name"** <note>Genesis 11:4, referring to the Tower of Babel</note>, and for this reason he called its name Babel, and this is the "wisdom of the children of the East." 
And Balaam the wicked was proficient in this wisdom from Aram, as it is written: **"Balak king of Moab led me from Aram, from the mountains of the East"** <note>Numbers 23:7</note>. Know that there is a distinction between the "mountains of the East" <note>Hebrew: *Qedem*</note> and "from the head of the mountains of the East," a difference as great as the advantage of light over darkness; you will grasp more of this by the decree of the Blessed Name. 

This divine attribute is called:
->**THE BOOK**<- 
of Life. This is the attribute that receives life from **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*, the third of the ten divine emanations or Sephirot, often associated with the divine Mother and the source of life's flow</note>, which is the eighth attribute counting toward it. **Understanding** is itself called "Life," and when this attribute is filled from that life, it is called a "Book." It is also called the "Tree of Life," meaning the "Earth" that receives the abundance of life from **Understanding** by way of the **Living God** <note>original: *El Chai*</note>, which is the attribute of **Foundation** <note>original: *Yesod*, the channel of divine procreation and connection</note>. 

Concerning this, it is said: **"Let the earth bring forth a living soul according to its kind"** <note>Genesis 1:24</note>. Our Sages of blessed memory said this refers to the soul of the Messiah. When human beings purify themselves and fulfill the commandments that extend from **Understanding**—which is the essence of life—they then merit to fill this attribute with life and cleave to it. Regarding this, it is said: **"But you who cleave to the Lord your God are all alive today"** <note>Deuteronomy 4:4</note>. 

And this is the secret of: **"Behold, I have set before you life and good"** <note>Deuteronomy 30:15</note>, meaning that life comes to this attribute through the **Righteous One** who is called **"Good"** <note>A common Kabbalistic association: the Sephirah *Yesod* is the "Righteous One" (*Tzadik*) who is also "Good" (*Tov*)</note>. Therefore, it is called the "Book of Life," meaning it is reached through the righteous who "recount" <note>original: *soferim*, a wordplay on "Scribes" and "those who count/recount"</note> the Torah, and they draw down "good" to it from **Understanding**. 

The reason for this is the same as: **"Everyone who is written for life in Jerusalem"** <note>Isaiah 4:3</note>. Behold, the righteous connect the Tree of Life to the Tree of Knowledge and unify them, and then they are written in the Book of Life. Guard this principle very well. Also: **"She deals him good and not evil all the days of her life"** <note>Proverbs 31:12</note>, meaning while life is being flowed to her through the "Good" <note>the Sephirah *Yesod*</note>. This is also the secret of **"Benaiah son of Jehoiada, a living man, great in deeds, from Kabzeel"** <note>2 Samuel 23:20; a verse often interpreted mystically to describe the flow of divine energy</note>; a wonderful and great matter is hinted at in this verse. 

This is the "Book of Life" that is opened on Rosh Hashanah, in which the perfectly righteous are literally written and sealed, as it is said: **"Everyone who is written for life in Jerusalem."** 

This attribute is also called:
->**THE EIGHTH**<-
Day of Assembly <note>original: *Shemini Atzeret*</note>. I am now opening great gates before you; open your eyes and incline your ear, and you shall see intellectual concepts perceived through the senses. 
<meta>catchword: In the Torah as in Passover</meta>

<column-break/>

**"Six days you shall eat unleavened bread, and on the seventh day is an assembly to the Lord your God"** <note>Deuteronomy 16:8</note>. They said that the seventh day of Passover is an "Assembly" <note>original: *Atzeret*</note>, but it belongs "to the Lord" and not "to Israel." Yet regarding the festival of Sukkot, it says: **"on the eighth day an assembly it shall be to you"** <note>Numbers 29:35</note>. It says that on the day of *Shemini Atzeret*, it is literally "ours." 

Now, contemplate: the seventh day is an "Assembly," and it is on His day, for it represents the **Righteous One** <note>the Sephirah *Yesod*</note>, the foundation of the world, and it is the seventh <note>counting from the higher Sephirot downward</note>. It is among the nine upper **illuminating mirrors** <note>original: *ispaklaryot*, a term for the divine Sephirot that transmit light</note>. 

However, regarding the festival of Sukkot, it says that the "Eighth" is an "Assembly" for us. This is **Kingship** <note>original: *Malchut*, the tenth Sephirah</note>, which is the **Divine Presence** <note>original: *Shekhinah*</note> that dwells within Israel. She is called "Bat-Sheba" <note>literally "Daughter of Seven"</note>, but she is literally the eighth. Thus, the "Assembly" of Passover is His, while the "Assembly" of Sukkot is ours. 

Know that His "Assembly" is the seventh and ours is the eighth. Before the Torah was given, they were unified above; but after the giving of the Torah, the "Eighth" day of the festival, the "Assembly," became ours. Guard this principle very well. 

You will find that the term "Assembly" <note>original: *Atzeret*</note> is a term of **Kingship** <note>original: *Malchut*</note>, as it is said: **"This one shall rule over my people"** <note>1 Samuel 9:17; the Hebrew word for "rule" here, *ya'atzor*, shares a root with *atzeret*</note>. Know that the "Assembly" of Passover and the "Assembly" of Sukkot are both unified through the **Sabbath**, which consists of "Remembering" and "Observing." "Remember" <note>Zakhor</note> refers to the day, and "Observe" <note>Shamor</note> refers to the night. For this reason, the Sabbath has no partner, and the "Assembly of Israel" is its partner. 

Remember the holiday of **Shavuot** <note>the Feast of Weeks</note>, where they are unified as they were at the giving of the Torah—the Upper Assembly with the Lower Assembly, meaning **Foundation** (*Yesod*) with **Kingship** (*Malchut*). 

Open your eyes and see the secret of the **cantillation marks** <note>original: *ta'amim*, the musical notations for chanting the Torah</note> written in the Torah. Any word that has two marks upon it through the verse signifies the secret of the union of two things as one. It is written: **"And on the day of the firstfruits, when you offer a new meal-offering to the Lord, on your Weeks"** <note>Numbers 28:26</note>. Here everything is explicit: His and ours are unified as one. 

As it is said: **"on the day of his wedding, and on the day of his heart’s gladness"** <note>Song of Songs 3:11</note>. "The day of his heart's gladness" refers to the building of the Temple; "the day of his wedding" refers to the giving of the Torah. In this same way, it is said: **"And they said: 'Indeed, we will return with you to your people'"** <note>Ruth 1:10</note>; one is the primary and the second is secondary. Similarly: **"And Rachel and Leah answered and said to him"** <note>Genesis 31:14</note>. Remember this whenever you find a word divided by two cantillation marks in this manner. Understand this, for on the festival of Shavuot, there was given
<meta>catchword: the Torah</meta>

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic significance of the "Book of Life" and the "Assembly" (Atzeret) festivals, identifying them with the divine attributes of Understanding, Foundation, and Kingship. It details how the festivals of Passover, Sukkot, and Shavuot facilitate the mystical union between the Divine and the Assembly of Israel.</summary>
<keywords>Book of Life, Shemini Atzeret, Binah (Understanding), Yesod (Foundation), Malchut (Kingship), Shekhinah, Shavuot, Cantillation Marks, Sefirot, Wisdom of the East</keywords>

<meta>Continuity: This page transitions from the general description of the "Tree of Life" as a container of divine flow to the specific mystical distinction between Upper Wisdom and Lower Wisdom (represented by the attribute of Kingdom). It focuses on the limitations of human and even Solomonic comprehension compared to the hidden source of divine emanation.</meta>

With our ears we have heard a report; they said: understand its way, and He knows its place. God surely understood its way, and He knows its place. Concerning this it is said: **"How very deep are Your thoughts"** <note>Psalm 92:6</note>, and it is written: **"Deep, deep; who can find it?"** <note>Ecclesiastes 7:24</note>. Regarding that attribute, no creature can truly stand, and concerning it is said: The **Torah** is the "fading flower" of the Wisdom above <note>original: *Novelet Chokhmah*; a Talmudic concept suggesting that the earthly Torah is a derivative or "fallen fruit" of a much higher, inaccessible divine Wisdom</note>. 

These are the **channels** <note>original: *Tzinorot*; the mystical conduits through which divine energy flows</note> and the extensions that pour forth from it in hiddenness by way of the channels until reaching the **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malchut*, the tenth and final Sephirah, representing the manifest world</note>, or she is filled from it in the mystery of: **"For the earth shall be full of the knowledge of the Lord, as the waters cover the sea"** <note>Isaiah 11:9</note>. Then, that same abundance that comes to her from the **Higher Wisdom** <note>original: *Chokhmah Elyona*</note>, filling her, causes her to also be called by its name: **Wisdom**. It calls her the **Last Wisdom** <note>original: *Chokhmah Acharonah*</note>. 

This is the "Kindness" <note>original: *Chesed*</note> of which it is said: **"And in the heart of all who are wise-hearted I have placed wisdom"** <note>Exodus 31:6</note>. This is the wisdom that was given to Solomon. But regarding the **First Wisdom**, there is no person in the world who can stand upon it; concerning it, Solomon says: **"All this I have tested with wisdom; I said, 'I will be wise,' but it was far from me"** <note>Ecclesiastes 7:23</note>. Rather, thus says Solomon: even though the **Last Wisdom** was given to me as a gift, I endeavored to enter further, perhaps to attain the **Higher Wisdom**. But I saw all the paths and all the channels were blocked before me. There is no entering the Higher Wisdom, for it is not handed over to any creature. Concerning it, it is said: **"God understands its way and He knows its place."** 

Thus you find it taught that Solomon only attained the **Last Wisdom**. This is the secret of: **"And Solomon sat on the throne of the Lord as king"** <note>1 Chronicles 29:23</note>. And regarding this it is written: **"And Solomon’s wisdom excelled the wisdom of all the children of the East and all the wisdom of Egypt"** <note>1 Kings 5:10</note>. You already know what we have told you: that Egypt is second to the Land of Israel. As for the "children of the East," their wisdom was great because they received it during the **Generation of the Dispersion** <note>original: *Dor Ha-Palagah*; the generation of the Tower of Babel</note>, regarding the matter of: **"And it came to pass as they journeyed from the East..."** <note>Genesis 11:2</note>. They said: **"Let us make for ourselves a name."** See how far their wisdom reached—for in that same place the vessels of the Holy Temple were exiled. One who understands this will understand the "image" made by Nebuchadnezzar, which he commanded all nations to bow down before. Concerning this it is said: **"And I will punish Bel in Babylon..."** <note>Jeremiah 51:44</note>.

<column-break/>

**"This is what their father spoke to them and blessed them"** <note>Genesis 49:28</note>. It is a sign to leave a blessing within your house. I will now hint: **"This shall be called Woman, for from Man she was taken"** <note>Genesis 2:23</note>. 
<margin>#</margin>
**"THIS"** <note>original: *Zot*</note>. This is a great secret from the secrets of the faith. This foundation is called **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malchut*</note>, and it is called **"This"** <note>original: *Zot*, the feminine form of "this" in Hebrew</note> because all the treasures of the King are in her hand, from *Aleph* to *Tav* <note>The first and last letters of the Hebrew alphabet, signifying totality</note>. 

**"This is the heritage of the servants of the Lord, and their righteousness is from Me, says the Lord"** <note>Isaiah 54:17</note>. **"Fear the Lord your God."** **"This is the burnt offering of every month..."** <note>Numbers 28:14</note>. And it was that one who merits it, merits the **Kingdom**. **"And this for Judah, and he said..."** <note>Deuteronomy 33:7</note>. David proclaimed: **"This was for me, because I kept Your precepts"** <note>Psalm 119:56</note>. Therefore, the righteous cling to this attribute; thus it is called: **"This is the Torah which Moses set"** <note>Deuteronomy 4:44</note>. 

Every High Priest who must enter the innermost sanctum <note>the Holy of Holies</note> on the Day of Atonement does not enter except by means of this attribute. The secret is in the verse: **"With this <note>original: *Be-Zot*</note> shall Aaron come into the holy place"** <note>Leviticus 16:3</note>. If she <note>the Shekhinah/Kingdom</note> does not enter with him, he will achieve nothing of his needs and will not see the face of the Blessed King, except through this attribute. This is what is meant by: **"But let him who glories glory in this..."** <note>Jeremiah 9:23</note>. 

The great secret: **"Whither has your beloved gone, O fairest among women? Whither has your beloved turned, that we may seek him with you?"** <note>Song of Songs 6:1</note>. This means: if we wish to seek the face of the Blessed Name, you cannot attain Him except "with you" <note>through the attribute of Kingdom/Shekhinah</note>. **"And it came to pass that everyone who sought the Lord went out to the Tent of Meeting."** **"And I will meet with you there."** **"And let them make Me a sanctuary, that I may dwell among them."** **"Who is this <note>original: *Zot*</note> that looks forth like the dawn?"** <note>Song of Songs 6:10</note>. **"For this <note>original: *Zot*</note> let everyone who is godly pray to You in a time when You may be found"** <note>Psalm 32:6</note>—literally "at the time of finding" <note>original: *Le-et matza*; a play on words suggesting finding the Presence</note>. 

Behold, the master Kabbalists have called this attribute everywhere the **Last Wisdom**. The reason is that the Thought clinging to the **Highest Crown** <note>original: *Keter Elyon*, the first Sephirah</note> is the secret of the letter *Yod* of the Name of God <note>the Tetragrammaton, Y-H-V-H</note>, and it is called **Wisdom**. Within that Wisdom, no creature in the world can know its place so as to attain it. Regarding this, it is said: **"But where <note>original: *Me-ayin*, which can also mean 'from nothing'</note> shall wisdom be found?"** <note>Job 28:12</note>. It is found literally from **Nothing** <note>original: *Ayin*, referring to the state of divine nothingness beyond comprehension</note>, from the place called "Nothing." 

That Wisdom is called the "Essence of Thought," and it is the beginning of **Emanation** <note>original: *Atzilut*, the highest of the four worlds in Kabbalah</note>. Concerning it, he said: **"Deep, deep; who can find it?"** Therefore, the Void said, "It is not in me," and the Sea said, "It is not with me." Destruction and Death said...

<summary>This page explores the Kabbalistic distinction between "Higher Wisdom," which is hidden and inaccessible to human thought, and "Last Wisdom" (the Sephirah of Kingdom), which serves as the accessible interface for prophecy and prayer. It identifies King Solomon as having achieved the latter while being barred from the former, and links the word "This" (Zot) to the indwelling Presence of God.</summary>

<keywords>Wisdom, Higher Wisdom, Last Wisdom, Malchut, Kingdom, Solomon, Zot, Shekhinah, Channels, Emanation, Atzilut, High Priest, Tower of Babel</keywords>

--- Page 40 ---
<meta>Continuity: Following the previous page’s discussion on the "Lower Wisdom" (the Sephirah of Kingdom) and how it receives divine flow from the "Higher Wisdom," this page describes how the three Patriarchs—Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—functioned as cosmic architects. They are depicted here as restoring the divine channels that were damaged by Adam’s original sin, specifically by balancing the "Right" (Kindness) and "Left" (Judgment) sides of the divine structure.</meta>

And the **Dread** <note>original: *Pachad*; a mystical name for the attribute of Judgment or *Gevurah* associated with Isaac</note> existed before Jacob and Esau were born; understand this well.
Isaac represents **sixty warriors** on the north side, for **"Isaac was sixty years old when she bore them"** <note>Genesis 25:26</note>. And when Isaac came and established himself among the warriors, it was then written: **"They all handle the sword, expert in war; every man has his sword upon his thigh because of dread in the night"** <note>Song of Songs 3:8</note>—this refers literally to the **Dread of Isaac** <note>original: *Pachad Yitzchak*; Genesis 31:42. In Kabbalah, Isaac embodies the "Left Side" of divine power, which is restrictive and fearsome</note>. 

Regarding this Dread of Isaac:
**Abraham** extended himself to start the **channels** <note>the conduits of divine energy</note> from the **Right** <note>the side of *Chesed* or Lovingkindness</note>.
**Isaac** fenced off all the breaches on the **Left** <note>the side of *Gevurah* or Might</note> because of the fear of impurity.
Then **Jacob** came and built structures within the wall that Isaac had made.
Thus, Abraham and Isaac act as guardians on the sides, while Jacob stands in the **middle** <note>representing *Tiferet*, the attribute of Balance or Harmony</note>. 

At that point, Jacob established a place for the **Shekhinah** <note>the Divine Presence</note>, as it is said: **"And Jacob went out from Beersheba..."** and **"He encountered the place... and behold, a ladder was set on the ground... and behold, the Lord stood over him and said: 'I am the Lord, God of Abraham your father and the God of Isaac'..."** And Jacob awoke from his sleep and said: **"How awesome is this place! This is none other than the house of God"**—this refers to the actual Godhead. 

Then Jacob rose early in the morning and took the stone he had placed under his head, and he called the name of that place **Bethel** <note>literally "House of God"</note>. He said: **"And this stone which I have set as a monument shall be God’s house; and of all that You give me, I will surely give a tenth to You."** 

<unclear>And we find</unclear> that just as Solomon later wrote, Jacob established a place for the Shekhinah and fixed a resting place for her. The text suggests that he intended to draw down all **ten Sephirot** <note>the ten divine emanations/attributes</note> and to arrange the channels in their proper order in perfect unity. 

What his grandfather Abraham had begun—moving "east of Bethel"—Jacob continued. He sought to arrange all ten Sephirot and create an order to repair all the **corruptions** <note>spiritual damage caused by human sin</note> until the **Kingdom** <note>the tenth Sephirah, *Malchut*</note>, which is "Bethel," is unified with the **Supreme Crown** <note>the highest Sephirah, *Keter*</note>, which is holiness itself. When he said, **"I will surely give a tenth,"** he meant he would elevate the "tenth" <note>the Sephirah of Kingdom</note> back to the ten Sephirot of the hidden mysteries. 

Behold, Jacob repaired the Shekhinah and arranged her all the way up to the Crown in a unity of ten ordered Sephirot—something our fathers Abraham and Isaac, peace be upon them, could not fully do. For Abraham and Isaac ascended only to the "Crowns" <note>intermediate high levels</note> and no further; but Jacob ascended to the very **Crown** through the mystery of the **Middle Line**.

<column-break/>

This mystery extends all the way down to the source of the Shekhinah, who dwells among the lower beings. As it is said: **"The heaven is My throne and the earth is My footstool"** <note>Isaiah 66:1</note>. Thus the Name became One and the world became One, with all things following the order of the true celestial system.

This remained so until **Adam the First Man** arrived and corrupted all the channels, and through his actions, "the bundle was separated" <note>a phrase meaning the unity of the divine attributes was broken</note>. This lasted until **Abraham**, our father, peace be upon him, arrived and saw the corruption of the celestial orders. He understood and knew how the Shekhinah had departed because of the sin Adam committed. 

Abraham began to reorder the channels from the **Right side**. He fixed the order on the Right side of the Creator—may He be blessed—to emanate and flow into the Shekhinah from the **Supreme Crown** down to the **Kingdom**. This is the secret of the verse: **"And he moved from there to the mountain east of Bethel."** "East" <note>original: *Mi-Kedem*, also meaning "from the ancient"</note> is the mystery of the Supreme Crown, reaching down to "Bethel," which is the mystery of the Kingdom, the "House of God" <note>original: *Beito shel El*</note>. 

All of Abraham’s journeys were intended to repair the Shekhinah from the Right side, because Adam had corrupted things from the Left. Therefore, it is written: **"And Abraham journeyed, going on still toward the South"** <note>the South represents the Right/Kindness</note>. Since it is written that **"from the North evil shall break forth"** <note>Jeremiah 1:14</note>, Abraham began to fix the corrupted channels to bring the abundance from the Right side, as the verse says: **"His left hand is under my head, and his right hand embraces me"** <note>Song of Songs 2:6</note>. 

When the Blessed Name saw the deeds of our father Abraham, He said to him: **"Sit at My right hand,"** and He made Abraham’s "Right" a throne for the Supernal Chariot. Thus, the Kingdom receives its flow through Abraham via the repair of the Right side. 

As for the verse **"he moved from there to the mountain east of Bethel,"** then **Isaac** came and fenced off the breaches in the **North**. He guarded the northern wind, which is the place of **Dread**, so that the forces of impurity would have no place to enter—for Adam the First Man had breached those defenses. This is the meaning of the **"Dread of Isaac"**—it is like the fear one has of an enemy or an oppressor. 

Finally, **Jacob**, our father, stood up and fenced all the breaches on the North side against the forces of impurity. This is the matter of **"Solomon's bed: sixty warriors surround it, of the mighty men of Israel."** As you already know, Isaac is the mystery of **Might** <note>original: *Gevurah*</note>.

<summary>This page explains how the three Patriarchs acted as cosmic repairmen to fix the divine channels damaged by Adam's sin. Abraham established the side of Kindness (Right), Isaac fortified the side of Judgment (Left) against impurity, and Jacob unified them through the Middle Line, connecting the highest "Crown" to the "Kingdom."</summary>
<keywords>Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Shekhinah, Sephirot, Kabbalah, Supreme Crown, Kingdom, Gevurah, Chesed, Bethel, Jacob's Ladder</keywords>

--- Page 41 ---
<meta>Continuity: Following the previous page’s discussion on the three Patriarchs—Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—as cosmic architects who balanced the divine attributes of Mercy and Judgment, the text now transitions into how the attribute of Kingdom (Malchut) relates to prayer and the holiday cycle.</meta>

### Foundational Principle
<meta>catchword: Continued</meta>

For it is the secret of true Unity, just as the guardian of its true essence is **subordinate** <note>original: *tafel*; meaning secondary or adjunct. This is a wordplay on the Hebrew word for prayer, *tefillah*</note> to the attribute of **Beauty** <note>original: *Tiferet*; the central divine attribute associated with the Sun and the Written Torah</note>, similar to the relationship between the Written Torah and the Oral Torah, or like the light of the Moon compared to the Sun. Therefore, prayer is called "service," "tilling the earth," and "satisfaction with bread," for it unites the attribute of **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malchut*</note> with the **Foundation** <note>original: *Yesod*; the attribute of connection and procreation</note>. 

Through prayer, every person testifies about themselves that they have a Master who provides for and sustains them, and that they have no existence except through that Master. This is the secret behind the verse regarding prayer: **"Can that which is tasteless [subordinate] be eaten without salt?"** <note>Job 6:6. The author links the word *tafel* (tasteless/subordinate) to *tefillah* (prayer)</note>. Anyone who does not pray is like one who denies the Root <note>the fundamental existence of God</note>, acting as if they have no Master and no need to ask anything of His divinity. Of such a person it is said: **"And you have cast Me behind your back"** <note>1 Kings 14:9</note>. 

This is also the secret of **"Accepting the Yoke of the Kingdom of Heaven,"** meaning that one makes themselves subordinate <note>*tafel*</note> while the Blessed Name is the Essential Root. Regarding this, one who wishes to accept the Yoke of the Kingdom of Heaven should wash their hands, don Tefillin, recite the *Shema*, and pray; this constitutes the complete Yoke of the Kingdom of Heaven. Thus we learn how great the power of prayer is, and how severe is the punishment for one who neglects it. 

Through the prayer a person performs below, they stir the "Supernal Chariots" <note>the divine structures of the upper worlds</note> and open several "conduits" from the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*; the highest emanation</note> which then flow into **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malchut*</note>, and the one who prays is blessed. This is the meaning of: **"He who blesses himself in the earth shall bless himself by the God of Truth [Amen]"** <note>Isaiah 65:16</note>. This means that first, the attribute of Kingdom is blessed, and when it is blessed, it pours the power of that blessing upon the one who prays. This is the secret of the verse: **"I will bring them to My holy mountain and make them joyful in My house of prayer"** <note>Isaiah 56:7</note>. Deep secrets are hidden in this place regarding the sages' question: **"From where do we know that the Holy One, Blessed be He, prays?"** <note>a famous Talmudic passage in Berakhot 7a exploring God’s "inner life"</note>. All this is hinted at for those who merit to understand it.

After we have understood these wondrous matters, know that this divine attribute is called **Beit-El** <note>House of God</note>, because it is the home of the Almighty. I tell you this: know that when the world was created and all the supernal and lower systems were perfected, and all systems were coupled on the Seventh Day—from the Giver to the Receiver—then the entire world became one for the one Master, with Beauty above.

<column-break/>

Then came the "Thanksgiving," and **Beauty** <note>*Tiferet*</note> and **Kingdom** <note>*Malchut*</note> were united on "the day of his wedding"—which refers to the time before the giving of the Torah, at Passover—and on **Shemini Atzeret** <note>The Eighth Day of Assembly</note> for "the Lord your God." During the holiday of Sukkot, which occurs after the giving of the Torah, it is written: **"The eighth day shall be an assembly for you"** <note>Numbers 29:35</note>. Behold, His and ours are all one. **"And let them make Me a sanctuary, that I may dwell among them"** <note>Exodus 25:8</note>. 

The Sages already offered a parable: This is like a king who had an only daughter and married her off; the king then returned to dwell with her. Likewise, "Make Me a sanctuary and I will dwell among them." Since the Blessed Name gave them the holiday of Shemini Atzeret—which corresponds to the "Daughter of Seven" <note>Bathsheba, a symbolic name for the seventh/final Sephirah, *Malchut*</note>—He comes to dwell with us. Thus, the seventh day of Passover is also ours when it is united with Kingdom, which is the attribute given to us. Guard this great foundational principle well. You will find that across the appointed seasons and festivals, this principle is vast. 

However, we must awaken a great matter regarding the saying that **Shemini Atzeret** is a festival in its own right. In truth:
*   **Passover** is the festival of Abraham <note>representing *Chesed* / Kindness</note>.
*   **Shavuot** is the festival of Isaac <note>representing *Gevurah* / Strength</note>.
*   **Sukkot** is the festival of Jacob <note>representing *Tiferet* / Beauty</note>.
*   **Shemini Atzeret** is a festival in its own right, for it is the festival of the "Fourth" <note>The fourth leg of the Divine Throne</note> and belongs to **David** <note>representing *Malchut* / Kingdom</note>.

This is the secret of "David's deputy." Guard this great foundational principle. This attribute is also called:

# Prayer

And she <note>the attribute of Kingdom/Prayer</note> is permitted before the Lord constantly, in order to remove all types of thorns and thistles, to clear away the stones, and to prepare the path for Israel. This is the secret of: **"O God, do not keep silent; hold not Your peace, and be not still"** <note>Psalm 83:1</note>, for **"behold, Your enemies are in an uproar."** This is the secret of: **"In return for my love they are my adversaries, but I am prayer"** <note>Psalm 109:4</note>. Woe to the creatures for the affront to the Torah! 

I will hint to you why it is called "Prayer" <note>*Tefillah*</note>: it implies that she has no power, light, existence, or joy of her own, but only through the flow of **Beauty** <note>*Tiferet*</note>. Your sign for this is: **"Can that which is tasteless [tafel] be eaten without salt?"** It is as if she is not a thing in her own right, but is subordinate <note>*tafel*</note> to the **Foundation** <note>*Yesod*</note>, which is the **Salt** that seals the covenant. Therefore, the Kingdom of the House of David is a "Covenant of Salt." Just as salt provides flavor to all foods, so too does the Foundation provide the "flavor" to all the powers of Kingdom, all its springs, its virtues, and its entire essence. This is the secret of the necessity and excellence of prayer.

<note>Original vocabulary: Prayer, Kingdom, Beauty, Foundation, House of God, The Eighth Day of Assembly, Secret, Accepting the Yoke of the Kingdom of Heaven</note>

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic significance of prayer as the attribute of Kingdom (Malchut), which is inherently "tasteless" or subordinate until it is united with the "Salt" of the Foundation (Yesod). It also maps the Jewish holidays to the Patriarchs and King David, identifying Shemini Atzeret as the specific festival of the Davidic Kingdom.</summary>
<keywords>Prayer (Tefillah), Malchut (Kingdom), Tiferet (Beauty), Yesod (Foundation), Shemini Atzeret, Covenant of Salt, Beit El, Yoke of Heaven, Divine Union</keywords>

<meta>Continuity: Following the previous discussion on the nature of prayer as a means of connecting the lower realm to the divine "Root," the text now explores the symbolic architecture of the Tabernacle and Temple—specifically the Altars—as physical manifestations of the divine attributes of Mercy and Judgment.</meta>

# 11

And this is the secret of the life of the **World to Come** <note>Hebrew: *Olam Ha-Ba*; the spiritual realm of reward and divine proximity</note>. Know that the **Altar of Burnt Offering** <note>Hebrew: *Mizbeach Ha-Olah*; the outer altar in the Tabernacle courtyard</note> represents the "Courtyard," and through it, the "spirit of the pride of Israel" is elevated. The Blessed Name commanded and said: **"An altar of earth you shall make for Me, and you shall sacrifice upon it your burnt offerings and your peace offerings"** <note>Exodus 20:21</note>. It says "your peace offerings" <note>Hebrew: *shelamecha*</note> literally, because this attribute brings **peace and completion** <note>Hebrew: *mashlemet*</note> between Israel and their Father in Heaven.

Furthermore, it is said: **"You shall build the altar of the Lord your God of whole stones, and you shall offer burnt offerings upon it"** <note>Deuteronomy 27:6</note>. This contains a great secret: that you shall not build them as **hewn stones** <note>Hebrew: *gazit*; stones cut and shaped by iron tools</note>. The reason is that this divine attribute <note>the Altar/Mercy</note> desires the well-being of Israel and does not scrutinize them regarding the multitude of their sins and transgressions. Therefore, it says: "Whole stones you shall build." Even though the stone is not shaped in a precise form—perhaps it is wide on one side, narrow on another, thick here, and thin there—so too is the offering that Israel brings accepted upon the Altar of Burnt Offering. This means that this attribute is not overly exacting with them when they come to bring an offering; even if a person is wicked or lawless, everything is accepted with favor. 

However, **hewn stones** <note>*gazit*</note> are made with great precision and known measurements. If they have uneven spots, they are struck with a hammer until they are level. Regarding such stones, it is said they are not fit for the Altar of Burnt Offering. This is why it says: "An altar of earth you shall make for Me... you shall not build them of hewn stones." The reason for the term *gazit* <note>hewn/cut</note> is that *gazit* refers to all the **accusing forces** <note>Hebrew: *kotrigim*</note> that prosecute Israel and scrutinize them for every iniquity and every sin. Therefore, they are not fit for the Altar of Atonement, for they seek to find Israel guilty. This is what the verse means: **"He has hedged my ways with hewn blocks"** <note>Lamentations 3:9</note>, similar to the idea of: **"But your iniquities have separated..."** <note>Isaiah 59:2; implying that rigid judgment creates a barrier</note>.

But even though the Altar was not hewn, the construction of the **Temple** <note>the "House"</note> itself was made of hewn stones. This is because this attribute is called the "House of God," which is the **Lower Court of Justice** <note>Hebrew: *Beit Din shel Mata*; the divine court that manages earthly consequences</note>. Therefore: **"The king commanded, and they quarried great stones... hewn stones"** <note>1 Kings 5:31</note>. The reason is that these stones represent the attribute of **Harsh Judgment** <note>Hebrew: *Midat Ha-Din Ha-Kashah*</note>. When the "channels" of flow do not come to it from the World of Mercy, it becomes a force of destruction and death. The core of its instruction is the secret of: **"I myself will punish 

<column-break/>

you sevenfold for your sins"** <note>Leviticus 26:24</note>. This is the secret called the **"sword that executes vengeance for the covenant"** <note>Leviticus 26:25</note>. 

For this reason, its foundation is "hewn" <note>*gazit*</note>. Similarly, the place where the **Great Sanhedrin** <note>the supreme legislative and judicial council of Israel</note> sat to provide spiritual sustenance was in the **Chamber of Hewn Stones** <note>Hebrew: *Lishkat Ha-Gazit*</note>, for in judgment, they do not show [undue] mercy. The law is compared to the nature of hewn stone: everything must be investigated and scrutinized according to the letter of the law. Observe these principles carefully: for in the "Place of Judgment," the reality is the opposite of the Altar of Burnt Offering. In Judgment, there is no mercy, whereas the Altar is entirely about atonement and compassion.

Know that the attribute of the **Altar of Incense** <note>the inner altar</note> is the secret of **Understanding** <note>Hebrew: *Binah*; the higher divine mother/intellect</note>, which is the secret of the "Knot of the Chariot" <note>the point where the divine emanations are bound together</note>, as we will yet explain with God's help. This attribute is called **Beersheba** <note>literally "Well of Seven"</note>, meaning the pool that is filled by the **seven emanations** <note>the seven lower *Sefirot*</note> that are above it. This is the secret of **Bathsheba** <note>literally "Daughter of Seven"</note>, as was explained previously. 

It is also called the **Garden of Eden**. I will now explain: "And the Lord planted a garden in Eden." From where was it planted? **"From before"** <note>Hebrew: *mi-kedem*; Genesis 2:8. *Kedem* also refers to the highest emanation, the Crown</note>. This means that all the emanations that proceed from the **Crown** <note>*Keter*</note>, which is called "Before," are all planted in the **Kingdom** <note>*Malchut*</note>. Behold, the attribute of **Beauty** <note>*Tiferet*</note>, which is the **Tree of Life**, is planted within the "Garden" <note>*Malchut*</note>, as it is written that it is planted "within," for it is the secret of the Kingdom. The sign for this is: **"the work of My hands, that I may be glorified"** <note>Isaiah 60:21. The Hebrew for "that I may be glorified" is *le-hitpa'er*, a wordplay on the attribute *Tiferet*</note>. 

This is a great secret regarding the secret of **Unity** <note>Hebrew: *Yichud*</note>. In this place, there is a deep matter called the secret of **"cutting the shoots"** <note>Hebrew: *ketzitzah be-netiot*; a Talmudic metaphor for heresy or causing separation within the divine unity</note>. Anyone who separates a "shoot" <note>an emanation</note> from the place where it is connected is called one who "cuts the shoots." The reason is that the Kingdom <note>*Malchut*</note> is the secret of this place where all the emanations are planted to produce "branches and fruit." 

I will give a hint: "And the **Tree of Life** was in the midst of the garden." Truly, the Life is literally inside the Garden. This is the "shoot of My planting, that I may be glorified" <note>Isaiah 60:21</note>, and this is the secret of connection and Unity. However, regarding the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**, it does not say that it is "in the garden." Understand this well, for the Tree of Knowledge and the Garden are different matters. But the Tree of Life is planted *inside* the Garden. The sign for this is "the hooks of the pillars" <note>Exodus 27:10. The Hebrew letter *Vav* (hook) represents the connecting force of Tiferet</note>. 

One who "cuts" <note>commits heresy</note> treats [an emanation] as if it were a single, isolated shoot. Concerning such a person, it is said: **"He also is a brother to him that is a great destroyer"** <note>Proverbs 18:9</note>. This refers to the "brother" of **Jeroboam ben Nebat** <note>the king who led the Northern Kingdom into idolatry</note>, who corrupted Israel before their Father in Heaven. **"Go, get you down; for your people... have dealt corruptly"** <note>Exodus 32:7</note>. For "all flesh had corrupted its way upon the earth" <note>Genesis 6:12</note>. Thus: **"And God saw the earth, and behold, it was corrupted."** Similarly: **"And it came to pass, when he went in unto his brother's wife, that he spilled it on the ground"** <note>Genesis 38:9; a reference to wasting seed, seen here as a corruption of the divine flow</note>. 

**Gates of Righteousness.**

<sig>D 1 4 I</sig>
<meta>catchword: And the secret</meta>

<summary>This page explains the symbolic differences between the outer Altar (Mercy) and the Temple building/Sanhedrin (Judgment), using the contrast between "whole stones" and "hewn stones." It then delves into the Kabbalistic structure of the Garden of Eden, identifying the Tree of Life as the attribute of Beauty (Tiferet) and warning against the heresy of "cutting the shoots"—separating the divine emanations from their unified source.</summary>

<meta>Continuity: Following the previous discussion on the nature of prayer and the symbolism of the Outer Altar as a place of mercy for Israel's offerings, the text now transitions into the mystical significance of Jacob’s ladder and how the Patriarchs correspond to the divine structure of the Sephirot (Divine Attributes).</meta>

The ladder from below to above ascends from **Kingdom** <note>Hebrew: *Malchut*; the tenth and lowest Sephira, representing the physical world and the Divine Presence</note> until the **Crown** <note>Hebrew: *Keter*; the highest Sephira, representing the Divine Will</note>. It is found that Abraham and Isaac are called the "Arms of the World" <note>representing the attributes of Kindness and Strength</note>, but Jacob, who is the **Middle Pillar** <note>Hebrew: *Kav Ha-Emtza’i*; the balancing force in the center of the Sephirot</note>, ascended and took hold of the "Head." Thus all the "Arms" are found to be connected to him through Abraham and Isaac, who are the two arms. Jacob is found to ascend until the **Crown**. This is the secret of the verse: **"Then you shall delight in the Lord; and I will cause you to ride upon the high places of the earth, and feed you with the heritage of Jacob your father"** <note>Isaiah 58:14</note>. This "heritage" refers to the life of the **World to Come** <note>Hebrew: *Olam Ha-Ba*</note> which flows from **Understanding** <note>Hebrew: *Binah*; the third Sephira, often associated with the source of souls</note>. It is the "heritage of Jacob" specifically, and not the heritage of Abraham or Isaac. This is the secret of the three highest Sephirot: **Crown, Wisdom, and Understanding**. This is the meaning of **"and feed you with the heritage of Jacob your father,"** for this is the life of the World to Come from **Understanding** and above. Leah <note>Jacob's wife, often representing the hidden world of Binah in Kabbalah</note> has no path in this place. This is the secret of the cantillation marks *Zarka*, *Maqqef*, and *Segol*. The secret is: **"For the Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself"** <note>Psalm 135:4</note>. "Israel" is the secret of the *Segula* <note>a "precious treasure" or "remedy"; a play on the vowel sign *Segol*</note>, because Israel is His treasure.

Open your eyes and see wonders! This is the secret of: **"The Lord alone did lead him, and there was no strange god with him"** <note>Deuteronomy 32:12</note>. No other power was present, for the "upper ministers" of the nations—called "strange gods"—have no ascent to the place of Jacob, which is from the "Five Arms" and above. This is why the verse follows with: **"He made him ride on the high places of the earth"** <note>Deuteronomy 32:13</note>. Understand these wonders. Jacob is found to unify all the Sephirot from bottom to top and to draw influence from top to bottom. From bottom to top, it says: **"And behold, a ladder set up on the earth, and the top of it reached to heaven; and behold, the angels of God ascending and descending on it. And behold, the Lord stood above it, and said: I am the Lord..."** <note>Genesis 28:12-13</note>. This describes the flow from top to bottom. **"And Jacob rose up early in the morning, and took the stone that he had put under his head, and set it up for a pillar, and poured oil upon the top of it"** <note>Genesis 28:18</note>. This is the secret of: **"Like the precious oil upon the head, running down upon the beard, even Aaron’s beard; that went down to the skirts of his garments; as the dew of Hermon, and as the dew that descended upon the mountains of Zion; for there the Lord commanded the blessing, even life for evermore"** <note>Psalm 133:2-3</note>. You already know the secret of "descending upon the mountains of Zion." Jacob said another thing, for he drew down influence like the pouring of oil. The "oil and the goodness" in this place is achieved through the "Arms" from the East until the **House of God** <note>Hebrew: *Beit El*</note>. This is why he said: **"of all that You shall give me I will surely give the tenth unto You"** <note>Genesis 28:22</note>. As for why Jacob called this place "House of God" (*Beit El*) in this portion at times, and at other times "House of the Almighty" (*Beit Elohim*), it is according to the order of the two **Rectifications** <note>Hebrew: *Tikkunim*; spiritual repairs</note> that Abraham and Isaac made for the **Kingdom**. When he mentions "House of God" (*Beit El*), it is the secret of the rectification...

<column-break/>

...that Abraham made for the **Divine Presence** <note>Hebrew: *Shekhinah*</note> from the side of the Right, which is called **God** <note>Hebrew: *El*; the name associated with Kindness</note>. Therefore, he called it by his name, "House of God" (*Beit El*), for **"A King sits on a throne of mercy"** and **"He called there on the name of the Lord, the Everlasting God"** <note>Genesis 21:33</note>. When he called it "House of the Almighty" (*Beit Elohim*), he named it after the rectification made by Isaac, which is the attribute of **Justice** <note>Hebrew: *Elohim*; the name associated with Judgment</note> in the secret of **Strength** (*Gevurah*) and **Fear** (*Pachad*). Watch these two names that Jacob called this place—*Beit El* and *Beit Elohim*—carefully. Do not forget them, for you have great need of this knowledge. I have already written to you above concerning the "Attribute" that is called by the name of the thing that fills it, according to the secret of: **"For My name is in him"** <note>Exodus 23:21</note>. This refers to the Name of the Lord <note>the Tetragrammaton, YHWH</note>; this is now clear. This divine attribute, when it is joined through the "channels" and the "mixtures" of **Understanding** (*Binah*), both are called *Dar* and *Socheret* <note>Terms from Esther 1:6, literally "marble" and "mother-of-pearl," but here interpreted through wordplay</note>. **Kingdom** (*Malchut*) is called *Dar*, because it is a "dwelling house" (*beit dirah*) for the Name of the Lord. She <note>the Shekhinah</note> dwells among us, as it is written: **"Who dwells with them in the midst of their uncleanness"** <note>Leviticus 16:16</note> and **"I will set My tabernacle among you"** <note>Leviticus 26:11</note>. Understand this very well. And the attribute of **Understanding** (*Binah*) unifies the **Crown** (*Keter*) which encompasses all the Sephirot and all the celestial arrangements; she is called *Socheret* from the language of "surrounding" <note>Hebrew: *sochor sochor*; to go around or encompass</note>. This is why Abraham our father (may he rest in peace) purchased the **Cave of Machpelah**—to ascend from there upwards from the "Arms" to the place of the **Crown**. This is the secret of the verse: **"four hundred shekels of silver, current money with the merchant"** <note>Hebrew: *over la-socher*; Genesis 23:16</note>. Behold, this is the place to enter from there into the World to Come, for it is the gateway that stands before the "Arms of the World," and from there one enters the life of the World to Come. This is the secret of "passing to the merchant" (*over la-socher*) and the secret of *Dar* and *Socheret*. Open your eyes and see how high the status of the Field of Machpelah is, for it is the gatehouse to the **Upper Eden** to enter the place of **Understanding**. Keep this and know it for yourself. The secret of these "Two Stones" corresponds to the secret of: **"And you shall take two onyx stones, and engrave on them the names of the children of Israel"** <note>Exodus 28:9</note>. One who is worthy to enter this **Kingdom** is worthy of the life of the World to Come. One who is worthy of *Dar* <note>the Dwelling</note> is worthy of *Socheret* <note>the Encompassing</note>. This is what is meant by the saying: **"All Israel have a portion in the World to Come."** And this is: **"Justice, justice shall you pursue, that you may live..."** <note>Deuteronomy 16:20</note>. This is the secret of the "Two Stones" and the secret of *Dar* and *Socheret*. And this divine attribute is called:

# The Altar
# of Burnt Offering * And I am hinting at the
->Way of the Altar of Burnt Offering<-
By this, Israel is worthy to ascend to the Upper Realm, and it is the secret of the ascent to the gateway of the **Holy of Holies**.

<summary>This page explains Jacob's spiritual ascent to the highest divine levels (Keter and Binah), identifying him as the Middle Pillar who unifies the attributes of Abraham and Isaac. It further describes the Cave of Machpelah and the Altar of Burnt Offering as mystical gateways through which Israel accesses the World to Come.</summary>

<meta>Continuity: Following the previous discussion on the nature of prayer and the symbolism of the Outer Altar as a place of mercy, the text now transitions into the mystical significance of the "Sword of Vengeance" and the distinction between "whole stones" and "hewn stones" in the structure of the Altar and the Divine Court.</meta>

"...seven times for your sins" <note>original: "שבע על חטאתיכם"; a reference to Leviticus 26:24</note>. And this is the mystery called **"the sword that shall execute the vengeance of the covenant"** <note>Leviticus 26:25</note>. For this reason, its foundation is hewn stone <note>Hebrew: *Gazit*; stones cut with metal tools</note>. Likewise, the place where the Great Sanhedrin <note>the supreme religious assembly in ancient Israel</note> used to sit was in the **Chamber of Hewn Stone**, because they do not show [lenient] mercy in strict judgment. The likeness of judgment is like the likeness of hewn stone: everything they could investigate and scrutinize according to the law [was done there]. 

Keep these principles well, for in the "Place of Judgment," the opposite of the **Altar of Burnt Offering** <note>Hebrew: *Mizbe'ach Ha-Olah*</note> is found. This is because in strict judgment, there is no mercy, whereas the Altar is entirely for atonement and mercy.

Know that the measure of the **Altar of Incense** is the secret of **Understanding** <note>Hebrew: *Binah*; the third Sephira, associated with the source of the soul and the higher world</note>, which is the secret of the "knot" of the entire Divine Chariot. We will explain this further with God’s help. This attribute is called **Beersheba** <note>Literally "Well of the Seven"</note>; that is to say, the pool that was filled from the Seven <term>Sephirot</term> <note>the divine emanations</note> above it, following the mystery of **Bathsheba** <note>Literally "Daughter of Seven"</note>.

This has already been explained above. It is also called the **Garden of Eden**. Behold, I am explaining that "the Lord God planted a garden in Eden" <note>Genesis 2:8</note>. From where did He plant it? From **the East** <note>Hebrew: *Keder*; also meaning "Ancient" or "Primordial"</note>, meaning all the Sephirot that emanate from the **Crown** <note>Hebrew: *Keter*</note>, which is called the Ancient One. All of them are "planted" within the **Kingdom** <note>Hebrew: *Malchut*; the tenth Sephira, the "Garden"</note>. And behold, **Beauty** <note>Hebrew: *Tiferet*; the central Sephira</note>, which is the **Tree of Life**, is in the place where it is planted within her—this is the secret of the Kingdom. The sign of this is: "the work of My hands, that I may be glorified" <note>Isaiah 60:21</note>. 

This is a great secret of the mystery of Unity. In this place, there is a deep matter, which is the mystery of **"cutting the shoots"** <note>Hebrew: *Ketzitzah ba-Neti'ot*; a Kabbalistic term for heresy, specifically the act of separating a Sephira (usually Malchut) from the rest of the divine unity</note>. For anyone who separates a "shoot" from its place of connection is called one who "cuts the shoots." The reason is that the Kingdom is the mystery of the "Place" in which all the Sephirot are planted to grow a branch and produce fruit. 

I am hinting here: "and the Tree of Life in the midst of the garden" <note>Genesis 2:9</note>. Truly, Life is literally inside the Garden. This is "the branch of My planting, that I may be glorified," and this is the mystery of the Connection and the Unity. But regarding the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**, it does not say that it is "the Garden." Understand this well, for the Tree of Knowledge and the Garden are different matters, but the Tree of Life is planted *within* the Garden, etc. "The hooks of the pillars" <note>Exodus 27:10</note>—one who "cuts" [separates] is like one who cuts all the shoots. Concerning such a person it is said, "He also who is slack in his work is brother to him who is a great destroyer" <note>Proverbs 18:9</note>. He is a "brother" to Jeroboam son of Nebat, who corrupted Israel before their Father in Heaven... <unclear>for he corrupted</unclear> all flesh, his way upon the earth... "And God saw the earth, and behold, it was corrupt" <note>Genesis 6:12</note>. And likewise: "And it came to pass, when he went in unto his brother's wife, that he spilled it on the ground" <note>Genesis 38:9</note>.

<column-break/>

This is the secret of the life of the **World to Come**. Know that the **Altar of Burnt Offering** is the "Courtyard," and through it, Israel receives atonement. The Blessed Name commanded: **"An altar of earth you shall make for Me, and shall sacrifice on it your burnt offerings and your peace offerings"** <note>Exodus 20:21</note>. Your "peace offerings" <note>Hebrew: *Shelamecha*</note> literally, because it "completes" <note>Hebrew: *Mashlemet*</note> the peace between Israel and their Father in Heaven. 

Furthermore, it is said: **"You shall build the altar of the Lord your God of whole stones; and you shall offer burnt offerings on it"** <note>Deuteronomy 27:6</note>. This is a great secret: that you should not build them of **hewn stone** <note>stones cut by iron tools</note>. The reason is that this divine attribute desires the well-being of Israel and does not scrutinize them strictly for the multitude of their sins and transgressions. Therefore, He said, "of whole stones you shall build." Even if the stone is not shaped in a precise form—but is wide on one side, narrow on another, coarse on one side, and thin on another—so too are the people of Israel: whatever they bring is acceptable to the Altar of Burnt Offering. This means that this attribute does not scrutinize them when they come to bring a sacrifice, even if one be a wicked person or a lawbreaker; overall, everything is received with favor.

But **hewn stones** are made with great precision and known measurements. If there are parts that are not equal, one strikes them with a hammer until they are even. Thus, it says that hewn stones are not fit for the Altar of Burnt Offering. Therefore, He said: "An altar of earth you shall make for Me; you shall not build it of hewn stones." 

The reason for "hewn stone" (*Gazit*) is that "Gazit" is the name for all the accusing forces that prosecute Israel and scrutinize them for every iniquity and every sin. Therefore, they are not fit for the Altar of Atonement, because they prosecute Israel greatly. This is what the scripture means: **"He has walled up my ways with hewn stone"** <note>Lamentations 3:9</note>, similar to the matter of "But your iniquities have separated [between you and your God]" <note>Isaiah 59:2</note>.

However, it says that the building of the **Temple** was with hewn stones, because this attribute is called the **House of God**, which is the **Lower Court of Justice**. Therefore, "the king commanded, and they brought great stones, costly stones, hewn stones" <note>1 Kings 5:31</note>. The reason is that they sometimes represent the attribute of **Strict Judgment**, and when the "channels" of mercy do not reach it from the World of Mercy, it destroys and kills. The essence of its instruction is the mystery of: "And I will chastise you..."

<summary>This page explores the mystical distinction between the Altar of Burnt Offering (associated with mercy and "whole stones") and the Chamber of Hewn Stone (associated with strict judgment and "hewn stones"). It explains how the Sephirot, particularly the Tree of Life and the Kingdom, are unified in the "Garden," while warning against the heresy of "cutting the shoots" or separating divine attributes.</summary>
<keywords>Altar of Burnt Offering, Altar of Incense, Hewn Stone (Gazit), Whole Stones, Kingdom (Malchut), Understanding (Binah), Garden of Eden, Tree of Life, Cutting the Shoots, Strict Judgment, Atonement</keywords>

<meta>Continuity: Following the previous discussion on the nature of the Altar and the Chamber of Hewn Stone, the text now explores the mystical significance of the names given to holy places by the Patriarchs and their connection to the Sephirot (Divine Emanations).</meta>

...that Abraham made for the **Shekhinah** <note>The Divine Presence</note> from the side of the Right <note>The Sephirah of Chesed, or Lovingkindness</note>, which is called by the Name [Y-H-W-H]. For its beauty, he called it by his name, **Bet-El** <note>House of God; original: "בית אל"</note>, for "The King sits upon a throne of mercy" <note>A liturgical phrase describing God's compassionate judgment</note>. Thus, "he called there on the name of the Lord, the Everlasting God" <note>Genesis 21:33</note>. 

And when he called it **Bet-El**, he was calling it so because of the **rectification** <note>Hebrew: *Tikkun*; a spiritual restoration or alignment</note> he performed for Isaac, which is the attribute of **Fear** <note>The Sephirah of Gevurah, or Strength/Severity</note> within the mystery of Might and Fear. This is the reason for the two names Jacob called this place: **Bet-El** and **Bet Elohim** <note>House of the Almighty</note>. Do not forget this, for it is of great necessity to you.

I have already written to you above regarding the **Attribute** <term>Middah</term> <note>A specific quality or Sephirah through which God acts</note> that is named after the thing that fills it, according to the secret of "for My name is in him" <note>Exodus 23:21</note>; this is now clarified. When this attribute is joined with the channels and the ascents of **Understanding** <note>Hebrew: *Binah*</note>, both are called **Dar** and **Socheret** <note>original: "ד' ר וסוחר'"; terms for precious stones/marble from Esther 1:6, used here to represent different levels of Divine light</note>. **Kingdom** <note>the Sephirah of *Malchut*</note> is called **Dar**, for it is a "dwelling house" <note>Hebrew: *Beit Dirah*; a play on the word *Dar*</note> for the Divine Name [Y-H-W-H], and it dwells among us in the sense of "He who dwells with them in the midst of their impurity" <note>Leviticus 16:16</note> and "I will set My tabernacle among you." Understand this well.

The letter *Chet* represents **Understanding**. It unites the **Crown** <note>the highest Sephirah, *Keter*</note> and encompasses all the **Sephirot** and all the arrangements. It is called **Charut** <note>Engraved</note>, from the language of "merchandise" <note>Hebrew: *Socher*</note>, and this is why Abraham—our father, peace be upon him—took the **Cave of Machpelah** to ascend from there to the heights: from the "Arms" <note>Chesed and Gevurah</note> to the place of the **Crown**. This is the secret of the "four hundred shekels of silver, current money with the merchant" <note>Genesis 23:16</note>. 

Behold, this place is for entering into the **World to Come**, for it is the gateway that stands as the "Arms of the World," and from there one enters the life of the World to Come. This is the secret of "current with the merchant" and the secret of *Dar* and *Socheret*. Open your eyes and see how great is the level of the **Field of Machpelah**, for it is the gatehouse to the Upper Eden to enter the place of **Understanding**. Guard this and know it for yourself. 

The secret of these **Two Stones** is the secret of "And you shall take two onyx stones and engrave on them the names of the children of Israel" <note>Exodus 28:9</note>. He who merits to enter this **Kingdom** merits the life of the **World to Come**; he who merits *Dar* merits *Socheret*. This is the meaning of "All Israel has a portion in the World to Come" and "Justice, justice shall you pursue, that you may live" <note>Deuteronomy 16:20</note>. This is the secret of the two stones and the secret of *Dar* and *Socheret*. And this attribute is called:

->**The Altar of Burnt Offering. And here I hint at**<-
->**the way of the Altar of Burnt Offering**<-

Through it, Israel merits to ascend to the Upper Chamber, which is the mystery of the ascent to the gate of the Holy of Holies.

<column-break/>

The **Ladder** goes from bottom to top. It ascends from **Kingdom** <note>*Malchut*</note> to the **Crown** <note>*Keter*</note>. Abraham and Isaac are found there; they are called the "Arms of the World." But **Jacob**, who is the **Middle Line** <note>the central path of balance among the Sephirot</note>, ascended and took hold of the "Head." Thus all **Ten Sephirot** are held by him, more so than Abraham and Isaac who are the two "Arms." Thus Jacob ascends to the Crown, and this is the secret of "Then shall you delight yourself in the Lord, and I will cause you to ride upon the high places of the earth, and feed you with the heritage of Jacob your father" <note>Isaiah 58:14</note>. This "heritage" refers to the life of the **World to Come** which flows from **Understanding**. It is the "heritage of Jacob" and not the heritage of Abraham and Isaac. 

These are the three Sephirot: **Crown, Wisdom, and Understanding**. This is why it says "I will feed you with the heritage of Jacob your father," for this is the life of the World to Come from Understanding and above, and the "Other Side" <note>referring here to *Leah* or perhaps external forces</note> has no path in this place. This is the secret of the vocalization of the *Segol* <note>a Hebrew vowel mark of three dots, representing a triangle of balance</note>. The secret is that "God chose Jacob for Himself" <note>Psalm 135:4</note>; he is the secret of the *Segulah* <note>Treasured possession</note>.

Open your eyes and see wonders! This is the secret of "the Lord alone did lead him, and there was no strange god with him" <note>Deuteronomy 32:12</note>. This means the upper ministers, called "strange gods," have no ascent to the place of Jacob, which is from the "Arms" and above. This is why the verse immediately adds, "He made him ride on the high places of the earth." 

Understand these wonders! Jacob is found to unite all the Sephirot from bottom to top, and he influences from top to bottom. This is the secret of: "And behold, a **ladder** set up on the earth, and the top of it reached to heaven; and behold, the angels of God ascending and descending on it. And behold, the Lord stood above it and said, 'I am the Lord...'" <note>Genesis 28:12-13</note>. This describes the flow from top to bottom. "And Jacob rose up early in the morning, and took the stone that he had put for his pillows, and set it up for a pillar, and poured oil upon the top of it" <note>Genesis 28:18</note>. 

This is the secret of "the precious oil upon the head, running down upon the beard, even Aaron’s beard, that went down to the skirts of his garments; as the dew of Hermon, and as the dew that descended upon the mountains of Zion: for there the Lord commanded the blessing, even life for evermore" <note>Psalm 133:2-3</note>. You already know the secret of "descending upon the mountains of Zion." Jacob spoke after he had drawn down the flow of oil and goodness to this place... back to **Bet-El**. This is why he said, "I will surely give a tenth to You" <note>Genesis 28:22</note>. 

As for Jacob calling this place **Bet-El** <note>House of God</note> sometimes and **Bet Elohim** <note>House of the Almighty</note> other times in this portion, it follows the order of the rectifications that Abraham and Isaac performed for the **Kingdom**. When he mentions **Bet-El**, it is the secret of the rectification...

<meta>Continuity: Following the previous discussion on the nature of the Altar and the Divine names associated with the Patriarchs, the author now transitions into the mystical symbolism of the Altar's construction materials, specifically the distinction between natural "whole stones" and "hewn stones" as they relate to Mercy and Judgment.</meta>

And this is the secret of the life of the World to Come. Know that the **Altar of Burnt Offering** <term>Mizbeach Ha'olah</term> <note>The primary outdoor altar in the Temple where animal sacrifices were offered</note> is the "Courtyard," and through it, the wombs of Israel are shown mercy. The Name, blessed be He, commanded and said: "An altar of earth you shall make for Me, and you shall sacrifice upon it your burnt offerings and your **peace offerings**" <note>Exodus 20:21</note>. The term "your peace offerings" <note>Hebrew: *shelamecha*</note> is literal, for this Altar is that which **completes** <note>Hebrew: *hamashlemet*; a play on the word for peace offering, *shelamim*</note> the relationship between Israel and their Father in Heaven.

Furthermore, it is said: "With whole stones you shall build the altar of the Lord your God, and you shall offer burnt offerings upon it" <note>Deuteronomy 27:6</note>. And this is a great secret: "You shall not build them of hewn stone" <note>Exodus 20:22</note>. The reason is that this **Desire** <note>The Divine attribute represented by the Altar</note> seeks the benefit of Israel and does not scrutinize them for the multitude of their sins and transgressions. Therefore, He said, "Whole stones you shall build," for even though the stone is not shaped into a specific, intended form—being wide on one side, narrow on another, coarse here, and thin there—so too is Israel. Whatever they bring is acceptable to the Altar of Burnt Offering. This means that this Divine attribute does not deal strictly with them when they come to offer a sacrifice, even if the person is wicked or lawless; in practice, everything is received with favor. 

However, **hewn stones** <note>Hebrew: *avnei gazit*; stones cut and squared by tools</note> are made with great precision and known measurements. If there are parts of them that are not equal, one strikes them with a hammer until they are made level. Regarding such "hewn stones," He said they are not fit for the Altar of Burnt Offering. Therefore, He said: "An altar of earth you shall make for Me... you shall not build them of hewn stone." The reason for the term **hewn** <note>original: "גזית" (gazit)</note> is because all the accusing forces that prosecute Israel and scrutinize them for every iniquity and every sin are called "hewn." Therefore, they are not fit for the Altar of Atonement, as they prosecute Israel’s debts. This is what the Scripture means by "He has barred my ways with hewn stone" <note>Lamentations 3:9</note>, similar to the matter of "But your iniquities have separated..." <note>Isaiah 59:2</note>. 

However, it is possible that the building of the Temple itself was done with hewn stones because that attribute is called the **House of God** <note>Bet Elohim</note>, which is the "Lower Court of Law." Therefore, "the king commanded, and they quarried great stones, costly stones, hewn stones" <note>1 Kings 5:31/17</note>. The reason is that these stones sometimes represent the attribute of **Strict Judgment**. When the "channels" of the world of Mercy do not flow to it, it turns back and kills. The essence of its law is the secret of: "I, even I, will chastise you seven times for your sins" <note>Leviticus 26:18</note>.

<column-break/>

This is the secret called the "sword that executes the vengeance of the covenant" <note>Leviticus 26:25</note>. Therefore, its foundation is "hewn." Similarly, the place where the Great Sanhedrin <note>The supreme judicial council of ancient Israel</note> sat to judge was in the **Chamber of Hewn Stone** <note>Lishkat HaGazit</note>, for they do not show mercy in legal judgment. The nature of Law is like the nature of the "hewn" stone: everything must be investigated and scrutinized according to the letter of the law. 

Guard these principles well! For the "Place of Judgment" is the opposite of the Altar of Burnt Offering; in Judgment, there is no mercy, whereas the Altar is entirely atonement and mercy.

And know that the attribute of the **Altar of Incense** <term>Mizbeach HaKetoret</term> is the secret of **Understanding** <note>The Sephirah of Binah</note>, which is the secret that binds the entire **Chariot** <note>The Merkavah, or the structured arrangement of Divine forces</note> together, as we will yet explain with God’s help. This attribute is called **Beersheba** <note>Literally "Well of Seven"</note>, meaning the pool that is filled by the seven **Sephirot** <term>Sefirot</term> <note>The ten emanations or attributes of God in Kabbalah</note> that are above it, within the secret of **Bathsheba** <note>Literally "Daughter of Seven"</note>. This was already explained above. 

It is also called **the Garden of Eden**. I shall explain: "And the Lord God planted a garden in Eden, in the east <note>Hebrew: *mikedem*; can also mean 'from the ancient/preceding'</note>" <note>Genesis 2:8</note>. From where was it planted? From "the ancient" <note>Kedem</note>, meaning all the Sephirot emanated from the **Crown** <note>Keter, the highest Sephirah</note> which is called "Ancient." They are all planted within **Kingdom** <note>Malchut</note>. Behold, **Beauty** <note>Tiferet</note>, which is the **Tree of Life**, is in the place where it is planted within it—for that is the secret of Kingdom—and its sign is: "the work of His hands, that He may be glorified" <note>Isaiah 60:21; *le-hitpa'er*, a play on Tiferet</note>. 

This is a great secret from the mystery of His Unity. In this place, the matter is deep; it is the secret of **"cutting the shoots"** <note>Hebrew: *kitzutz ba-netiot*; a Kabbalistic term for heresy, specifically the act of mentally separating one of the Sephirot (usually Malchut) from the rest of the Divine Unity</note>. For anyone who separates a "shoot" <note>a Divine attribute</note> from the place of its connection is called one who "cuts the shoots." The reason is that Kingdom is the secret of this place where all the Sephirot are planted in order to "produce branches and bear fruit." 

I will hint further: "The Tree of Life was in the midst of the garden" <note>Genesis 2:9</note>—truly, for the "Life" is literally inside the Garden. This is "the shoot of My planting, the work of My hands, that I may be glorified," and this is the secret of connection and unity. But regarding the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**, it does not say that it is "in the garden" <note>The author suggests a different spatial or spiritual status for this tree</note>. Understand this very well, for the Tree of Knowledge and the Garden are different matters, but the Tree of Life is planted *inside* the Garden. Its secret is the letter *Vav* <note>ו</note>, which represents the central "Column." 

Thus, one who "cuts" any of these "shoots"—it is said of him: "He is a companion to a man who destroys" <note>Proverbs 18:9</note>. He is a companion to Jeroboam son of Nebat, who destroyed Israel's relationship with their Father in Heaven. "Go down, for your people have corrupted themselves" <note>Exodus 32:7</note>; "for all flesh had corrupted its way upon the earth" <note>Genesis 6:12</note>; "and the Lord saw the earth, and behold, it was corrupt." And similarly: "and when he went in to his brother's wife, he spilled it on the ground, to destroy it..." <note>Genesis 38:9</note>.

**Gates of Justice** <note>Sha'arei Tzedek</note>

<meta>Continuity: This page continues the exploration of the mystical structure of the divine realm, moving from the ascent of Jacob through the Sefirot (the ten divine attributes) toward a specific focus on the final Sefirah, Malchut (Kingdom), and how the Oneness of God is realized through it.</meta>

And [understanding] one in particular requires great effort. However, from what I have written to you in this gate, you will enter several "inner chambers." Open your eyes and see how many secrets, which were previously closed and sealed to you, are now before you. With the help of the Blessed Name, as we mention in this composition the other levels of the **Chariot** <term>Merkavah</term> <note>The mystical "Vehicle" or arrangement of divine forces seen in the visions of Ezekiel</note> and the arrangement of the order of the **Divine Emanations** <term>Sefirot</term>, we shall also mention the Names associated with the attribute of **Kingdom** <term>Malchut</term>. You already know that the true unifying principle of all the Sefirot is found when they all unite in the attribute of Kingdom. This is the secret of the verse: "And the Lord shall be King over all the earth" <note>Zechariah 14:9</note>. This is what is meant in the **Book of Formation** <term>Sefer Yetzirah</term> <note>One of the oldest and most influential books of Jewish mysticism</note> when it says: "Ten and not nine." You also know that there are nine **illuminating mirrors** <term>ispaklaryot</term> <note>Metaphor for the clear prophetic vision of the higher Sefirot</note> and one that does not illuminate on its own <note>referring to Malchut, which like the moon, reflects the light of the others</note>. There is no restoration without this one that "does not illuminate." This is the secret of the "flame bound to the coal" <note>A famous metaphor from Sefer Yetzirah meaning the divine light is inseparable from its physical manifestation</note>; it is a "Throne for the Throne," a great, deep, and sealed glory.

### **Know** 
That this Sefirah is the final light within the **Unique Name** <term>Tetragrammaton</term> <note>The four-letter Name of God, Y-H-V-H</note>. It represents the secret of the absolute Unity of the Name, for within it, all the Sefirot unite and into it all the divine channels flow. This is the secret of the final letter **Heh** (ה) of the Unique Name.

### **Know** 
And believe that all 613 **commandments** <term>taryag mitzvot</term> of the Torah are woven into the ten Sefirot, much like the patterns made by a master weaver or embroiderer, each according to its kind. There are commandments that take hold specifically in the attribute of Kingdom, such as the **blue thread** <term>tekhelet</term> of the ritual fringes <note>Tzitzit</note>, where there is one thread of blue and seven of white. This relates to the secret of "Bathsheba" <note>literally "Daughter of Seven"</note> and the phrase "In God is Seven." This is also the secret of the **Day of Atonement** <term>Yom Kippur</term> service: "One and one, one and two, one and three, one and four, one and five, one and six, one and seven" <note>The High Priest’s chant while sprinkling blood in the Temple</note>. This is the "Lyre," and this order descends and unites in the number Seven, which rises to represent the **Unification** <term>Yichud</term>—the complete calculation. The **Sabbath** <term>Shabbat</term> likewise takes hold in the Seven and unites in the Nine, for in the seventh [day/Sefirah], the three are united in this secret. This is the secret of the "Treasured People" <term>Segulah</term>, for "The Lord has chosen Jacob for Himself, Israel as His treasure." I am hinting to you here: "to set you high above all nations which He has made, for praise, and for fame, and for honor." Here is the principle of the three places.

<column-break/>

"He gives them their food in its time" <note>Psalm 145:15</note>—meaning specifically "in its time," and not at just any time. What is written after it? "You open Your hand and satisfy the desire of every living thing." This refers to the **Time of Favor** <term>Et Ratzon</term> <note>A specific cosmic window when divine mercy is most accessible</note>. This is what the scripture means: "The Lord shall open unto thee His good treasure, the heaven to give the rain of thy land in its season, and to bless all the work of thine hand." And it is written: "And he shall be like a tree planted by rivers of water, that brings forth its fruit in its season." This is the secret of "Remember" and "Observe" <note>The two versions of the Sabbath commandment</note>. Our Sages of blessed memory said: "The designated time for scholars is from Sabbath eve to Sabbath eve." Therefore, rain on a Sabbath eve is a sign of blessing for the world. It is also written: "Ye shall fear every man his mother and his father, and keep My Sabbaths." When Solomon, peace be upon him, delved into the depths, he explored the concept of "Time" and explained many things, saying: "A time to be born, and a time to die; a time to plant, and a time to pluck up," and all the other times he detailed in those verses. But he then came and explained one verse to rectify all types of times, saying: "At all times let your garments be white" <note>Ecclesiastes 9:8</note>. This means to draw down the **Superior Whiteness** <note>Divine Mercy/Chesed</note> through one's will; then all "times" will be filled with goodness and abundance. This is like the "Oil of Anointing" that flows from above through the celestial channels. He says at the end of that verse: "and let your head lack no oil." If it is like the "good oil upon the head, that ran down upon the beard, even Aaron's beard," it certainly draws down the world of Kindness and Mercy. Therefore, the High Priest would enter the Holy of Holies on the Day of Atonement in white garments. Because these garments appease and rectify all the "times," it is written: "Let him not come at all times into the holy place," and "At all times let your garments be white," and "With this shall Aaron come into the holy place." He shall put on the holy linen tunic, and he shall have the linen breeches upon his flesh—they are holy garments; therefore shall he wash his flesh in water, and so put them on. From here, the secret of "At all times let your garments be white" is made known. These are the great principles called the **Time of Favor**.

### **And After**
Having informed you of these great principles regarding the word **Kingdom** <term>Malchut</term>, know that it also has many names attributed to it according to its many levels, for these are its adornments and preparations. If we were to explain each one, [it would fill the] **Gates of Righteousness** <term>Sha'arei Tzedek</term> <note>Likely a reference to the title of the book or a specific section of it</note>.

<summary>This page explains the mystical importance of the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingdom) as the point of unity for all divine forces and the source of "The Time of Favor." It links the timing of ritual actions, the white garments of the High Priest, and the 613 commandments to the flow of divine abundance into the world.</summary>
