<meta>Continuity: Following the previous page’s discussion on the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingdom) as the unifying force of the divine realm, this page explores the various symbolic names given to this attribute—such as the "Cup," the "Hand-Tefillin," the "West," and "Time"—and how it acts as the interface between divine blessing and the physical world.</meta>

The general secret [of the verse] "You shall not destroy its trees by wielding an ax against them" <note>Deuteronomy 20:19</note> relates to the verse: "Corruptly have his children dealt with Him, they are their own blemish" <note>Deuteronomy 32:5; original: "שחת לו לא בניו מומם"</note> and "A seed of evildoers, children who deal corruptly" <note>Isaiah 1:4</note>. One who understands this matter will understand the secret of the verse: "Let the earth bring forth living souls according to their kind" <note>Genesis 1:24</note>. This attribute is also called:

# Cup

The reason for this name is that this attribute is the vessel that becomes filled with the divine flow and emanation from above when the **Ten Sefirot** <term>Emanations</term> <note>The ten creative powers or attributes through which God interacts with the universe</note> are united. This is the secret behind the law that the **Cup of Blessing** <note>The cup of wine used during grace after meals or sanctification rituals</note> requires "ten things." One who understands this will understand the mystery of these ten things. 

Know and believe that outside the partitions of the Divine Unity, there is another cup, which is the **Cup of Retribution**. Regarding this, the scripture says: "For in the hand of the Lord is a cup, and the wine foams, full of mixture, and He pours out from it" <note>Psalm 75:9</note>. Behold, the "Cup of Blessing" is internal, and it belongs to David <note>Symbolizing the Sefirah of Malchut</note>. This is what the scripture means: "You anoint my head with oil; my cup overflows" <note>Psalm 23:5</note> and "I will lift the cup of salvations and call upon the name of the Lord" <note>Psalm 116:13</note>. 

However, the "Cup of Retribution" is the "degenerate shoots of a foreign vine" <note>Jeremiah 2:21</note> which is planted on the "outside." When the people of Israel sin, they drink from that cup, as it is said: "You who have drunk from the hand of the Lord the dregs of the cup of trembling, and drained it" <note>Isaiah 51:17</note>. That wine is made from the "vine of Sodom," from the essence of the venom of the "poison of death" <note>Sammael/The Accuser</note> dwelling on the outside. This is the secret of: "Their wine is the venom of dragons..." <note>Deuteronomy 32:33</note>. Know and believe that those who do not merit the Cup of Blessing do violence to their own souls and lose many types of blessings. If we were to prolong this, we would explain many great matters regarding this subject. This attribute is also called:

# Hand-Tefillin

The secret lies in the verse: "It shall be a sign upon your hand and a reminder between your eyes" <note>Exodus 13:16</note>. Behold, the **Head-Tefillin** <term>Tefillin shel Rosh</term> represents the secret of the connection of **Beauty** <term>Tiferet</term> <note>The central Sefirah, often associated with the male aspect of the divine or the Written Torah</note> to the whole through **Understanding** <term>Binah</term>. The **Hand-Tefillin** <term>Tefillin shel Yad</term> represents the secret of the connection of **Kingdom** <term>Malchut</term> to Beauty through the **Foundation** <term>Yesod</term> <note>The Sefirah that acts as the channel or 'funnel' for divine energy</note>. With the help of the Exalted Name, when we discuss the word "Beauty," I will inform you of the great principles regarding the matter of the Tefillin, their construction, and their purposes. This attribute is also 

# Eye-Opener

->called<-
->and called<-
->The Tent for the Principles<-

<column-break/>

of the great [secrets] that you will yet grasp with the help of the Blessed Name. It is called the **Eye-Opener** <term>Petach Einayim</term> for the providence of the "Opening" and the "Tent" for the Torah. As it says: "I saw as the color of electrum [Hashmal]" <note>Ezekiel 1:27</note>, and "These seven are the eyes of the Lord" <note>Zechariah 4:10</note>, and "This is the Torah: when a man dies in a tent" <note>Numbers 19:14</note>. It is also called:

# West

The reason is that it is the secret place of movement and the growth of seeds. I will hint at this: "From the East I will bring your seed and from the West I will gather you" <note>Isaiah 43:5</note>. I will further add a hint regarding the Sun and the Moon. Our Sages of blessed memory have already said: "The Divine Presence [Shekhinah] is in the West." 

Know that since the day the Holy Temple was destroyed, the flavor of fruits was taken away, "there are no grapes on the vine and no figs on the fig tree, and the leaf has withered" <note>Jeremiah 8:13</note>. Everything is hinted at in the Act of Creation: "And there was evening and there was morning, one day"—literally one day. For this reason, the "Son of David" [the Messiah] will not come until all the souls in the "Body" <note>Guf; the celestial treasury of souls</note> are exhausted. "And he spent the night there because the sun had set" <note>Genesis 28:11</note>. This attribute is also called:

# Time

We must explain this. Know that we have already explained the reason it is called the "Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil." When you understand that path, you will be able to understand the secret of: "As for me, may my prayer to You, O Lord, be at a **favorable time**" <term>Et Ratzon</term> <note>Psalm 69:14</note>. For when "Knowledge" <term>Da'at</term> joins with "Will" <term>Ratzon</term>, then all the worlds are repaired, the Sefirot are in unity, the Assembly of Israel is in perfection, and the Moon is full. 

This is the secret of "As for me, may my prayer to You, O Lord, be at a favorable time." Know and believe that in that hour, the "Attribute of Judgment" does not rule, for all worlds are in a state of mercy, and there is no Accuser [Satan] and no evil occurrence. According to this path, the Torah said: "Tell Aaron your brother that he should not come at all times [any time] into the Holy place" <note>Leviticus 16:2</note>. Know that there are "times" that are not included among the good, when impurity unites within the Holy Palace. Regarding this, it is said: "Therefore the prudent man keeps silent at that time, for it is an evil time" <note>Amos 5:13</note>. Therefore, the early pious ones would "repair the channels" when they came to request from the Lord, until they reached the "Favorable Time." Know that when the "Attribute of Good" joins with "Kingdom," then all kingdoms of every kind receive sustenance, existence, food, and life from Kingdom. The secret is: "The eyes of all look hopefully to You, and You give them [their food in its time]" <note>Psalm 145:15</note>.

<meta>Continuity: This page continues the exploration of the Divine attributes, moving from the symbolic "Cup" of the previous page toward the transformation of Judgment into Mercy and the specific nature of the Second Sefirah, El Chai.</meta>

There is a great and hidden secret [here]. When [the Divine] is awakened, all [attributes] are transformed into mercy and all are subdued before Him. This is the secret of the prayer: "May Your mercy suppress Your anger from upon us," <note>original: "יכבשו רחמיך את כעסך"; a phrase from the liturgy and the Talmud, Berakhot 7a</note> for in that state, there is no need for the "rectification of prayers" about which many books have been written. This is the meaning of "May Your mercy roll over Your [stern] attributes." I will now hint to you how these attributes correspond to what is brought down from below: according to the "measure" of the cup, it is filled. If it is the measure of purity, they fill it with wonders from the upper holy mountains. But if it is the measure of impurity, they fill it from the "External Ones" <term>Chitzonim</term> <note>Forces of evil or spiritual shells that exist outside the realm of holiness</note> and from the filth. This is what the Sages meant when they said: "With the measure a person measures, it is measured out to him." <note>Mishnah Sotah 1:7</note> Therefore, when the name of the Most High God shines there, all those [harsh] attributes flee, become terrified, are scattered, roll away, and depart. Then, Judgment is transformed into Mercy. This is the secret of the phrase: "The Lord, the King, sits upon the Throne of Mercy." What it means is that at the time when the World of Mercy shines forth, then "Your mercy suppresses Your anger" and "Your mercy rolls over Your attributes." Know and believe that wherever the attribute of the God of Life shines, life comes with it. And now I shall begin: Abraham inherited the attribute of *Chesed* <term>Kindness</term> and his specific divine name was *El* <note>God</note>. And he...

<column-break/>

...in their souls. Guard this great principle for the sake of the entire Torah. After we have informed you of these matters, you will be able to know the power of Holiness—which is the secret of Purity, the divine flow, and the "holy increase"—and what is the power of Impurity—which is the secret of [negative] increase and distance. Impurity is the opposite of Purity: one is distance and the other is closeness. One who understands this can understand the secret of the "impurity of a virgin" <note>likely referring to the laws of Niddah</note> and the secret of "he has defiled the Sanctuary of the Lord." <note>Numbers 19:20</note> Let what we have hinted to you regarding the matter of **Kingdom** <term>Malchut</term> <note>The tenth and final Sefirah, representing the manifest presence of God in the world</note> suffice for you. May it be enough for you.

### The Second **Sefirah** is called
**Living God** <note>original: "אל חי" - El Chai</note>. Regarding this, David, peace be upon him, said: "My soul longs, even faints, for the Living God" <note>Psalm 84:3</note> and it is written: "My soul thirsts for God, for the Living God," and so on. <note>Psalm 42:3</note> The reason is that when the world is in a state of Divine Will, the **Crown** <term>Keter</term> <note>The highest, most transcendent Sefirah</note> reveals itself through the secret of the name *El*. Then the entire world exists in a state of Mercy. This is the secret of "God, the King, sitting on the Throne of Mercy." Now, if you should ask: "Is it not written that 'God is indignant every day'?" <note>Psalm 7:12</note>—in truth, this refers to the name *El* when He is indignant toward all types of "destroyers" and punishments at the time they are revealed. It does not say "the indignant God," but rather that He "is indignant" [at evil]. In this way, we understand the secret of the actual "Most High God" <note>El Elyon</note> and the secret of "Blessed be Abram by God Most High." <note>Genesis 14:19</note> This is what draws mercy and life from above. When He draws down mercy and life, He is called "Living God" <note>El Chai</note>. Therefore, [the Sages] established in the prayer: "Living God and Shield." He is a "Shield" against types of destroyers and punishments. Therefore, every place you find the name *El*, it refers to the side of the attribute of Mercy. This is the name *El* that is revealed at the hour when three hundred and seven forces of Judgment are awakened against the world, and it is called...

<column-break/>

...the power of the one who fulfills the commandment, or how corrupted is the one who commits a transgression. This is all hinted at in the matter of the *Niddah* <term>Menstruant</term> and the *Zavah* <term>Woman with an irregular flow</term>. For the *Niddah* has a small measure of impurity, and therefore she only has [a requirement of] seven days. But the *Zavah* flows outside of its proper place; therefore, "she shall count for herself seven [clean] days." <note>Leviticus 15:28</note> This is why [the verse says]: "They shall cast out of the camp every leper, and everyone who has a flow <note>Zav</note>, and everyone defiled by the dead." <note>Numbers 5:2</note> These are three levels of impurity taught here corresponding to the three camps [of Israel]. Woe to the people because of the affront to the Torah! If human beings only knew and understood the place where they are corrupted, and the extent to which they are corrupted, they would know how great the power of transgression is, and they would be careful...

<meta>Continuity: This page transitions from the discussion of Divine Mercy to the theurgic power of human action—the idea that performing commandments (Mitzvot) on Earth sustains the spiritual structures above. It specifically explores the hierarchy of the "Throne" and how holiness must be guarded from "External Forces" (Kelipot).</meta>

...as it is said: "It is a time to act for the Lord," <note>original: "עת לעשות לידוד"; Psalm 119:126. The author interprets this not just as acting *for* God, but as "making" or "affecting" the Divine realm through human action.</note> and so on. "A time to act" means quite literally, as it were, that anyone who performs a commandment below is considered as if they are sustaining the Torah which is above. This is the secret of what is said: "Or let him take hold of My strength, that he may make peace with Me; and he shall make peace with Me." <note>Isaiah 27:5</note> This is a great, wondrous, and hidden secret. This is also the secret of "There is none like the God of Jeshurun, who rides the heavens to your help." <note>Deuteronomy 33:26</note> It means "to your help" quite literally <note>The Hebrew "be-ezrecha" can be read as "with your help," implying God uses human merit to power the heavens.</note>. From this point forward, [He rides] "in His excellency on the skies." One who knows these secrets will understand the secret of "Because [ekev] Abraham obeyed My voice, and kept My charge, My commandments, My statutes, and My laws." <note>Genesis 26:5</note>

## And Know
And believe that you must investigate the form called "Throne upon Throne." There is an **External Throne** for an **Inner Throne**. The External Throne is called **Adam** <term>Man</term>. Outside of this External Throne, there are **Shells** <term>Kelipot</term> <note>External forces of impurity or "husks" that conceal the divine light</note> and various types of "coverings" that are not fit to enter within; their place is on the outside. If, God forbid, they were to enter within, the order would be corrupted and the Sanctuary would be defiled—of which it is commanded, "they shall not enter into your congregation." <note>Deuteronomy 23:4</note> There are many commandments that point toward the External Throne and the outer Shells, such as the matter of the **displaced nerve** <note>the sciatic nerve, Gid HaNasheh, which Jews are forbidden to eat</note> which points to the External Throne. So too regarding the "thread of the heart," the blood, and several other things. There are many commandments that point toward the Shells, such as the **uncircumcision of fruit** <note>Orlah; the prohibition of eating fruit from a tree for the first three years</note> and the **uncircumcision of the flesh** <term>Brit Milah</term>. Similarly, the laws of impure animals, birds, and fish—all these matters were commanded according to the order of the External Shells. These are called the **Thrones of Impurity** at a time when the spirit of impurity is on the outside and the spirit of purity is on the inside; then all things stand in their proper order. But if, God forbid, the order is reversed and the impure enter the Courtyard, then all the pure ones go outside, because both cannot enter one room together. The secret is that "the bed is too short for stretching, and the covering too narrow for wrapping." <note>Isaiah 28:20; a metaphor used in the Talmud to describe a space that cannot hold two opposing forces.</note> Our Sages, of blessed memory, went to great lengths to inform us of this verse and those similar to it.

## And It Is Proper
For you to open your eyes to the things we have told you regarding the matter of the commandments, for they are the secrets of the world, and from them you shall understand

<column-break/>

## The Unity
<term>Yichud</term>. And behold, I shall explain it: it refers only to that which rises to **Praise** <term>Tehillah</term>. For up until [the attributes of] the **Great** and the **Mighty**, all the princes of the nations rise within the secret of Abraham and Isaac; they are called "Arms" and they are called "High Places," as in "And you shall tread upon their high places." <note>Deuteronomy 33:29</note> But "I will cause you to ride on the high places of the earth and feed you with the heritage of Jacob your father"—not the heritage of Abraham and Isaac. <note>Isaiah 58:14. Jacob represents the Sefirah of Tiferet (Beauty/Harmony), which balances the extremes of Abraham (Kindness) and Isaac (Judgment).</note> Initially, the rivers flow until that place which is the secret of the **Kingdom** <term>Malchut</term>. I am hinting at what was said in the Prophets: "to bring up from there the Ark of God, which is called by the Name, the Name of the Lord of Hosts who dwells between the Cherubim." <note>2 Samuel 6:2</note> In Chronicles, it is written: "which is called by the Name," <note>1 Chronicles 13:6</note> and there he explained it all with a great explanation. The **Generation of the Division** <note>The builders of the Tower of Babel</note> knew these essential principles and they wanted to separate the "Chieftain" [God]. They said: "Let us make for ourselves a **Name**." <note>Genesis 11:4</note> There, they wanted to separate the **Kingdom** from the **Foundation** <term>Yesod</term>. Therefore, regarding the Generation of the Division, it is written: "Men of blood and deceit shall not live out half their days." <note>Psalm 55:24</note> Within the "Foundation," the name of the one called **Peleg** <note>Meaning "Division" or "Stream"</note> is hinted at, "for in his days the earth was divided." <note>Genesis 10:25</note> Literally in his days, "men of blood and deceit did not live out half their days." There remained "the stream [Peleg] of God, full of water." <note>Psalm 65:10</note> But they "shall not see the divisions [palgot], the rivers, the brooks of honey and butter." <note>Job 20:17</note> Nevertheless, it was there that the vessels were hidden by the exile, and "what they have begun to do will not be withheld from them." They remain there until the Messiah comes and returns things to their proper state. This is why our Sages, of blessed memory, said: "Anyone who goes up from Babylon to the Land of Israel transgresses a positive commandment, for it is said: 'To Babylon they shall be brought, and there they shall remain until the day that I visit them.'" <note>Jeremiah 27:22; Talmud, Berakhot 24b</note> **Gold**—this is **Glory** [Pe'er]. He then says **Beauty** [Tiferet], for *Tiferet* is the **Middle Line** that binds everything between "Praise" and "Name," similar to [the combination of] "The Lord God" [YHVH Elohim]. Within the secret of "My mouth shall speak the praise of the Lord, and let all flesh bless His holy name forever and ever." <note>Psalm 145:21</note> In this way, know that there are commandments specific to the attribute of **Kingdom** [Malchut], and there are commandments specific to **Foundation** [Yesod]—such as the laws of forbidden sexual relations, the Covenant of Circumcision, and many traditions. Likewise for **Victory** [Netzach] and **Splendor** [Hod], for **Beauty** [Tiferet], for **Greatness** [Gedulah/Chesed], for **Understanding** [Binah], for **Wisdom** [Chokhmah], and for the **Supreme Crown** [Keter Elyon]. In this manner, the commandments of the Torah are woven together. Happy is the one who knows them and fulfills them, and who knows the secret of this scripture.

It maps specific laws to the Sefirot (Divine emanations) and describes the spiritual danger of allowing "External Forces" (impurity) to enter the realm of holiness.</summary>

<meta>Continuity: This page continues the discussion of theurgic action—how the deeds of the Patriarchs (Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob) repaired the spiritual damage caused by the First Man (Adam). It transitions from the general concept of sustaining the Divine "Throne" to the specific mystical roles each Patriarch played in unifying the Sefirot (Divine emanations) and opening the "channels" of divine flow.</meta>

...of God. Jacob stood in the center <note>Jacob represents the "Middle Pillar" in the Sefirotic tree, balancing the extremes of Mercy and Judgment.</note> and prepared a place for a lodging. **"And he took the stone that he had put under his head, and set it up as a monument, and poured oil on top of it."** <note>Genesis 28:18</note> He caused the **ten Sefirot** <term>Sefirot: The ten creative powers or attributes through which God interacts with the universe</term> to flow into it and repaired the channels; through his hand, they were all unified. This is as he said: **"Of all that You give me, I will surely give a tenth to You,"** <note>Genesis 28:22. The "tenth" refers to the tenth Sefirah, Malchut (Kingdom), connecting it back to the source.</note> and he called them **Bethel** <note>original: "Beit El"; House of God</note> and the **House of Elohim** <note>original: "Beit Elohim"; House of God (Judgment)</note> after the names of Abraham and Isaac. Then the prophets came and called it the **House of YHVH** <note>original: "Beit Yadud"; House of the Lord (Mercy)</note> after the name of Jacob. For Jacob is the "Middle Line," and he inherits the name **YHVH** from every side. Regarding this, the prophet said: **"And it shall come to pass in the end of days, that the mountain of the Lord's house [House of YHVH] shall be established at the top of the mountains... and let us go up to the mountain of the Lord, to the House of the God of Jacob."** <note>Isaiah 2:2-3</note> Let **Binah** <term>Binah: The Sefirah of "Understanding," often personified as a Divine Mother who listens and discerns</term> hear this!

Therefore, you will find that the **Malchut** <term>Malchut: "Kingdom," the final Sefirah that represents the physical world and the Divine Presence (Shekhinah) residing within it</term> is called Bethel, the House of Elohim, and the House of YHVH, corresponding to Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob, each according to his attribute: Abraham is the attribute of **El** <note>The name of God associated with Chesed (Kindness/Mercy)</note>; Isaac is the attribute of **Elohim** <note>The name associated with Gevurah (Strength/Judgment)</note>; and Jacob is the attribute of **YHVH** <note>The name associated with Tiferet (Beauty/Balance)</note>. This is the secret of **"the God of Abraham, the Fear of Isaac, and the God of Jacob."** <note>Genesis 31:42</note> And this is the great secret that connects all the Sefirot into Malchut through Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. This is what the scripture means: **"A Psalm of Asaph: The God of gods, the Lord [El Elohim YHVH], has spoken and called the earth, from the rising of the sun to its setting..."** <note>Psalm 50:1. The author notes that these three names of God appear together here to represent the three Patriarchs.</note> Here, in this verse, they hinted at the secret of the Patriarchs' excellence: how they joined all the Sefirot and repaired all the channels that the First Man <note>Adam</note> had damaged.

The sign for this is "A Psalm of Asaph"—literally "for gathering" <note>The name Asaph (אסף) shares a root with the word "to gather" or "to collect."</note>. And the sign: "And a clean man shall gather [ve-asaph]..." <note>Numbers 19:9</note> and "Assemble [he-asfu] yourselves, that I may tell you..." <note>Genesis 49:1</note> as we have already explained. "The God of gods, the Lord [El Elohim YHVH], has spoken and called the earth"—then the three upper attributes were joined, corresponding to Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. They were the cause for the "calling of the earth" <note>Bringing the Divine light into the physical world</note> a "holy calling," for previously the earth had been "divorced" from Man and defiled by the First Man. But when it says "God, the Lord God, has spoken," then [the Divine] joined and illuminated the earth with His glory. The secret is found at the end of the verse: **"from the rising of the sun to its setting."** We have already informed you what "from the rising of the sun to its setting" means.

<column-break/>

This matter is the secret of the connection of the Sefirot and the perfect Union [Unification] through Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. Therefore, we say [in prayer]: **"Remember Abraham, Isaac, and Israel, Your servants..."** and so on; **"the covenant which He made with Abraham, and His oath to Isaac, and confirmed it to Jacob for a statute, to Israel for an everlasting covenant."** <note>Psalm 105:8-10</note> Therefore, it is called Bethel, the House of Elohim, and the House of YHVH through the "buildings" that their roots built. Thus, the Blessed Lord [YHVH] is unified through the Patriarchs. The channels that the First Man damaged and which were separated through him were unified and joined through Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob.

Afterward, the Psalm returns and says: **"Out of Zion, the perfection of beauty, God has shined."** <note>Psalm 50:2</note> This means that after Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob repaired the channels, in what place does the joining of the Sefirot occur? In **Malchut**. For the Union is the Sefirah called **Zion**. Therefore: "Out of Zion, the perfection of beauty, God has shined." Consequently, he is called the **"Mighty One of Jacob,"** <note>original: "Abir Yaakov"</note> for Jacob is the third of the Patriarchs. One who understands these fundamental secrets will understand the secret of: **"Only the Lord had a delight [hashak] in your fathers to love them,"** <note>Deuteronomy 10:15</note> a literal "delight" <note>The word "hashak" implies a passionate, binding desire or attachment.</note>. And the secret of: **"And He chose their seed after them."**

This is the secret of **"The Living God"** <note>original: "El Chai," associated with the Sefirah of Yesod</note> and **"Shield."** And this is the secret of the **Shield of David** <note>The Magen David</note>, as it is said: **"Your neck is like the Tower of David, built for an armory, whereon there hang a thousand shields..."** <note>Song of Songs 4:4</note> If so, observe whenever you find the name **El Shaddai** <term>El Shaddai: "God Almighty," a name traditionally associated with the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation) and the regulation of natural boundaries</term>, it is the secret of the connection of the Sefirot—**Yesod** within **Malchut**—and the matter of the Union. This is the Union that the Patriarchs repaired. Therefore the scripture says: **"And I appeared to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob, as El Shaddai [God Almighty], but by My name YHVH [The Lord] I was not known to them."** <note>Exodus 6:3</note>

**And Know** that at the time when Malchut is found with Malchut <note>Likely referring to the union of the lower and upper aspects of the Kingdom</note> in the union of *El Shaddai* with the name *YHVH*—which is the "Beauty" [Tiferet]—it is distinguished, etc., because the connection is the "tip of the lower [letter] *Vav*." <note>The letter Vav (ו) often represents the six masculine Sefirot; its "tip" connects it to the feminine Malchut.</note> The sign for this is "Your father Jacob." Therefore, He appeared as *El Shaddai*, but His name *YHVH* was with him. The sign for this is **"the hooks [vavei] of the pillars and their fillets."** <note>Exodus 27:10. A pun on the letter Vav, which means "hook."</note> And if you should say: "But it says 'I was not known to them'?" know that the name *YHVH* was visible to them through the "garment" of *El Shaddai*. This is the [letter] *Shin* <note>The first letter of Shaddai</note>, which is the **"mirror that does not shine."** <note>term: "Aspaklaria she-einah me'irah" — a technical Kabbalistic term for a dim or indirect prophecy, contrasted with the "shining mirror" of Moses' prophecy.</note> Therefore, they saw the King...

<summary>This page explains how the Patriarchs (Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob) acted as spiritual healers who repaired the "channels" of divine flow damaged by Adam. It details how different Names of God correspond to the Patriarchs' attributes and how they facilitated the unification of the Sefirot, specifically connecting the higher realms to the earthly realm (Malchut).</summary>
<keywords>Jacob, Patriarchs, Sefirot, Malchut, El Shaddai, Tikkun (Repair), Channels, Union, Zion, Names of God</keywords>

<meta>Continuity: This page transitions from the specific role of Jacob as the "Middle Pillar" to a broader explanation of the mystical names of God and how the Patriarchs—Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob—function as the "Chariot" for the Divine Presence. It uses verses from Isaiah and the Psalms to map Divine attributes (Sefirot) onto the physical and spiritual acts of the Patriarchs.</meta>

...regarding this verse: **"Of the increase of his government and peace there shall be no end, upon the throne of David and over his kingdom, to establish it and to support it with judgment and with righteousness."** <note>Isaiah 9:6 (9:7 in English bibles). The text uses this to explain the stability of the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingdom).</note> If so, open your eyes and see the wonders as we have explained regarding the matter of the **Living God** <note>original: "El Chai"</note>. You will see that in every place where the Torah mentions a "holy watch," it hints at a specific location and a specific attribute of Divine conduct. This is what is called in the Torah **God Almighty** <note>original: "El Shaddai"</note>.

**Behold**, I am opening for you the gates of the upper realms. Know that wherever you find the name **Shaddai** in the Torah, it is the secret of **Malchut** <term>Malchut: "Kingdom," the tenth and final Sefirah, representing the manifest world and the Divine Presence (Shekhinah)</term> alone. But wherever you find **Yah Shaddai**, it represents the union of **Yesod** <term>Yesod: "Foundation," the ninth Sefirah, which acts as the channel or "procreative" force for Divine energy</term> and Malchut. At that moment, the Kingdom is filled with life and blessing.

**Know** that Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob, the three of them together, joined Yesod to Malchut. This is because this union is the primary "repair" of the **Chariot** <term>Merkabah: The Divine Chariot, representing the structures that support and "carry" God's presence in the world</term> and the service of the **Shekhinah** <term>Shekhinah: The indwelling feminine aspect of the Divine</term> here below. Therefore, the Torah says: **"And I appeared to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob as God Almighty [El Shaddai]"** <note>Exodus 6:3</note> because one cannot enter to see the levels of prophecy except through the union of El Shaddai.

For this reason, the Sages said: "The Patriarchs are themselves the Chariot." They established the "house of rest" for the Foundation through its union with the Kingdom. The place of Abraham was called **Bethel** <note>original: "Beit El"; House of God</note>. **"And he moved from there to the mountain east of Bethel..."** <note>Genesis 12:8</note> and it is said, **"And he went on his journeys from the South even to Bethel."** <note>Genesis 13:3</note> This is because Abraham moved these qualities of Mercy from the **Keter** <term>Keter: "Crown," the highest and most primordial of the Sefirot</term>, which is the "Ancient One" of the world, through the name "El," which he joined with the Crown. From that side—the "South"—which is the **Kindness** <note>original: "Chesed"</note> of Abraham, he journeyed from the South to Bethel. For Abraham’s place was the South, as it is said, **"And Abraham journeyed, going on toward the South."** <note>Genesis 12:9</note> This is the meaning of **"The right hand of the Lord [YHVH]"** <note>Psalm 118:16. The "Right Hand" always symbolizes Mercy/Chesed.</note>. From there, the emanation goes to Simeon in the Kingdom.

Isaac, however, fenced in all the "breaches" on the Left side because of **Fear** <note>original: "Pachad"; Isaac is traditionally associated with the Sefirah of Gevurah/Judgment/Fear.</note>. He was called "The House of Elohim" in Isaac's name. The secret is: **"And he was afraid and said, 'How awesome is this place! This is none other than the House of God [Elohim], and this is the gate of heaven.'"** <note>Genesis 28:17</note> The word "And he was afraid" is the secret of the "Fear of Isaac." Thus, Isaac inherited the attribute of **Elohim** <note>The Divine name associated with Judgment and Law.</note>.

<column-break/>

...close to the World of Life, which is the world of **Binah** <term>Binah: "Understanding," the third Sefirah, often called the "Divine Mother" or the source of life</term>. The sign of this is **Living God** <note>original: "El Chai"</note>; like the attribute of "El," it shines forth and connects to the Kingdom. They are then called "merit" and "living witnesses," and then the whole world exists in mercy, sustainability, and life. This is the secret of what was established in the prayer: **"Who revives the dead with great mercy."** <note>A central line from the Gevurot blessing in the Amidah prayer.</note> For if Abraham had not preceded Isaac, there would be no resurrection of the dead. Therefore, **"God tested Abraham"** <note>Genesis 22:1</note> and it is written, **"And he took in his hand the fire and the knife,"** <note>Genesis 22:6</note> hinting at the ten Sefirot in this verse. 

If you should ask: "But is it not written: **'And his name shall be called Wonderful, Counselor, Mighty God, Everlasting Father, Prince of Peace'**?" <note>Isaiah 9:5 (9:6 in English bibles)</note>. Truly, **Wonderful** <note>original: "Pele"</note> is the secret of the three upper Sefirot, which is the place of wonders. This is what the verse means: **"I will praise You, for I am fearfully and wonderfully made; wonderful are Your works,"** <note>Psalm 139:14</note> and it says, **"To Him who alone does great wonders,"** <note>Psalm 136:4</note> "alone" literally, for **"The Lord [YHVH] alone shall be exalted."** <note>Isaiah 2:11</note>

**Behold**, I will now explain the Psalm called the **Great Hallel** <note>Psalm 136, characterized by the repeating refrain "His mercy endures forever."</note>: "Give thanks." It explains: **"Give thanks to the Lord [YHVH] for He is good," "Give thanks to the God of gods," "Give thanks to the Lord of lords."** In these three verses lies the secret of the **Crown [Keter]**, the **Beauty [Tiferet]**, and the **Kingdom [Malchut]**. The entire Psalm is tripled to explain how the Psalm returns to the first and the last. 

The meaning of **"To Him who alone does great wonders"** refers back to **"Give thanks to the Lord [YHVH] for He is good,"** which is the secret of "alone" in truth. The explanation following it, **"To Him that by wisdom made the heavens,"** is the secret of **"Give thanks to the God of gods,"** for this is the sustaining of the Work of Creation. The explanation following that, **"To Him that smote Egypt in their firstborn,"** is the secret of **"Give thanks to the Lord of lords,"** which is the secret of miracles and the changing of nature. 

For this reason, it says in this place: **"And his name shall be called Wonderful"**—this is the secret of **"Counselor"** <note>original: "Yoetz"</note>, with whom did He consult? He prepared him in truth. Afterward, close to it is **"God"** <note>original: "El"</note>, which is the attribute of **Greatness** <note>original: "Gedulah," another name for Chesed/Kindness.</note>. Afterward, close to it is **"Mighty"** <note>original: "Gibbor"</note>, which is the attribute of **Strength** <note>original: "Gevurah," associated with Isaac.</note>. Afterward, **"Everlasting Father"** <note>original: "Avi Ad"</note> which rises up and down, which is **Beauty [Tiferet]**, "forever and ever" <note>original: "Adei Ad"</note> to every side, may He be blessed. **"Prince of Peace"** <note>original: "Sar Shalom"</note> is the **Foundation [Yesod]**. This is what the one who understands the **Kingdom [Malchut]** has understood; therefore, he said close by...

It emphasizes how the Patriarchs acted as a "Chariot" to unify these forces, particularly connecting the qualities of Mercy (Abraham) and Judgment (Isaac) to sustain the world.</summary>

<meta>Continuity: This page continues the discussion of the Sefirot (Divine attributes), moving from the general union of Yesod (Foundation) and Malchut (Kingdom) to how this union was disrupted by historical sins—from Adam to the Tower of Babel—and how the Patriarchs, specifically Abraham, restored the correct order by separating purity from impurity.</meta>

And this is the secret of the verse: **"And by your sins you have defiled My land and My inheritance you have made an abomination, for the holy place of the Lord... he has defiled."** <note>A composite reference likely drawing from Jeremiah 2:7 and Leviticus 20:3.</note> Consequently, the First Man <note>Adam</note> desired to make use of the Sanctuary through impurity—which is to say, through evil. The people of the Generation of the Dispersion <note>original: "Dor Ha-Plagah," referring to the builders of the Tower of Babel</note> desired the same. Therefore, they were exiled. It is written: **"Which the sons of man built"** <note>Genesis 11:5</note>; these were the sons of the "First Man" <note>original: "Adam Kadmaa"</note>, and their sin was similar to the sin of their father. They sought to create a separation between the **"Stream of God full of water"** <note>Psalm 65:10; original: "Peleg Elohim"</note> and the **"Well of Living Waters"** <note>Song of Songs 4:15</note> which are filled from that very stream. They wanted to fill the well with "haughty waters" <note>original: "mayim hazadonim," meaning prideful or turbulent waters</note>—burning, radiant waters—and they wanted to use it for their own ends.

This is what the verse means by: **"To make cakes for the Queen of Heaven"** <note>Jeremiah 7:18. The Hebrew "kavanim" refers to sacrificial cakes used in idolatry.</note> and **"Since we stopped burning incense to the Queen of Heaven... we have lacked everything."** <note>Jeremiah 44:18</note> This was the primary sin involving the "Asherah tree" <note>A cult pole used in forbidden Canaanite worship</note> during the time of the Kings of Israel. Even in the time of King Manasseh, they separated the flow of Divine abundance from the "Stream" (*Peleg*) and sought to use it through impurity. This is what the scripture means: **"He shall not look upon the 'divisions' of the rivers, the streams of honey and butter."** <note>Job 20:17</note> The explanation is: he who looks at the "divisions" <note>original: "pelagot," a pun on the name Peleg and the word for division</note> cannot see the streams of honey and butter.

Abraham our father (peace be upon him) held in his hands all the powers of both impurity and purity. The purity was internal, and the impurity was external. He handed the powers of purity to Isaac, as it is said: **"And the Lord blessed Abraham with everything"** <note>Genesis 24:1</note> and **"Abraham gave all that he had to Isaac."** <note>Genesis 25:5</note> But the external powers of impurity he handed over to the "sons of the concubines." This is the meaning of: **"And to the sons of the concubines that Abraham had, Abraham gave gifts... and sent them away from Isaac his son... eastward, to the land of the East."** <note>Genesis 25:6</note> From this, understand that Abraham our father (peace be upon him) realized the "Chariots of Purity" were internal, for Isaac the son of Sarah, while the impurities were external, for the sons of the concubines. This is also what was said of King Solomon (peace be upon him): **"And Solomon's wisdom excelled the wisdom of all the children of the East."** <note>1 Kings 5:10</note> For the "children of the East" were the sons of the concubines to whom Abraham had given "gifts," which were the Names of Impurity. As our Sages of blessed memory said: in this way, people would perform idol worship during the time the Temple stood, by performing acts and making use of the Names of Impurity. The Generation of the Dispersion wanted to prepare the "Kingdoms" <note>original: "Malchuyot"</note> to be a throne for the forces of impurity, similar to the matter of the Tree of Knowledge of

<column-break/>

Good and Evil. They said: **"Let us make for ourselves a name"** <note>Genesis 11:4</note>—a literal "Name" <note>Referring to a Name of power used for magic or manipulation of Divine forces.</note>. This was identical to the sin of Adam, the First Man. Therefore, the Blessed Name said: **"Let us go down and there confound their language"** <note>Genesis 11:7</note> and **"Now nothing will be restrained from them..."** <note>Genesis 11:6</note> For this reason, Eber named his son **Peleg** <note>Meaning "Division"</note>, because in his days the earth was divided. They wanted to separate the Source of Living Water, which is called the **"Stream of God full of water"** <note>original: "Peleg Elohim"</note>. Because of this, they themselves were "divided" <note>dispersed</note>; "men of blood and treachery shall not live out half their days." Thus the matter of the "Dispersion" is explained to you.

Now, this attribute <note>The Sefirah of Yesod</note> is called **Good** (*Tov*) in the Torah, because it is the ninth of the radiating Sefirot. **This** is the secret of the "end" of the lower *Vav* <note>The sixth letter of the Hebrew alphabet, often representing the Sefirah of Tiferet</note> and the letter *Bet*, which is the secret of the **Kingdom** (*Malchut*) adjacent to it. This is the secret of "In the beginning (*Bereshit*) God created"—the "Great *Bet*" <note>Referring to the oversized first letter of the Bible</note>, the House of God (*Beit El*), the House of the Lord.

**Know** that all light recognized in the Work of Creation is sealed within this attribute. This is the light that flows from **Beauty** (*Tiferet*), as in the matter of: **"And God said, Let there be light."** It is also written: **"And God saw the light, that it was good (*tov*); and God divided..."** <note>Genesis 1:3-4</note>. He hid it for the righteous. He truly hid it within the **Kingdom** (*Malchut*), as it says: **"Light is sown for the righteous."** <note>Psalm 97:11</note> The "Righteous One" <note>term: "Tzadik" - the Sefirah of Yesod</note> and "Righteousness" <note>term: "Tzedek" - the Sefirah of Malchut</note> connect through this "sown light." It is also written: **"And from the wicked their light is withheld,"** <note>Job 38:15</note> for this light only shines within the **Kingdom** (*Malchut*) alone. All the "external forces" <note>forces of impurity</note> that are outside of the Kingdom cannot see and cannot benefit from this light, because it is hidden within the Kingdom. And the Kingdom was given to Israel alone; the nations of the world have no portion in it.

Therefore: **"And God saw the light, that it was good; and God divided between the light and the darkness."** The light is for Israel, and the darkness is for the nations of the world. As it says: **"For behold, darkness shall cover the earth, and gross darkness the peoples; but the Lord shall shine upon you, and His glory shall be seen upon you."** <note>Isaiah 60:2</note> This is the secret of the **Separation** <note>original: "Havdalah," the ritual marking the end of the Sabbath</note>: He who separates between holy and profane, between light and darkness, between Israel and the nations, between the seventh day and the six days of work. When you understand the secret of the entire order of the *Havdalah* prayer, you will see that "Holy," "Light," "Israel," and the "Seventh Day" are all one single concept. Then you will understand: "And God saw the light that it was good," and so on.

And behold, on the Second Day, which is the secret of Separation, the **Gates of Righteousness** <note>original: "Sha'arei Tzedek"</note>...

It defines the Sefirah of Yesod as "Good" and explains the "sown light" as a spiritual inheritance reserved for Israel through the Sefirah of Malchut.</summary>

And by the power of the Supernal Rock, He brought water out of the flint rock into a pool of water <note>original: "הוציא מים מצור החלמיש לאגם מים", referencing Psalm 114:8. In Kabbalah, "water" often represents the flow of Divine emanation.</note>, by the power of the transformer of the Rock into a pool of water, through the power of the Supernal Rock. And know that all the offspring of Israel came forth from the Rock, and so it says: "You neglected the Rock that begot you" <note>Deuteronomy 32:18</note> and so on. And it says: "There is no Rock like our God" <note>1 Samuel 2:2</note>—meaning there is no Artist <note>original: "אין ציר", a wordplay on "Tzur" (Rock) and "Tzayar" (Artist/Fashioner). The Talmud (Berakhot 10a) teaches that God is an artist who fashions form within form.</note> like our God.

### And here I
explain: "The Rock, His work is perfect" <note>Deuteronomy 32:4</note>. "Perfect" literally refers to the state of being unified with *Malchut* (Kingship). "I was perfect with Him" <note>Psalm 18:24</note>—this refers to the state where the soul is aligned with *Yesod* (Foundation), your God. "Walk before Me and be perfect" <note>Genesis 17:1</note>. Therefore, King David (peace be upon him) said: "And I shall be perfect with Him and keep myself from my iniquity." Therefore, "The Rock, His work is perfect." Now open your eyes and see it is written: "The way of a serpent upon a rock" <note>Proverbs 30:19</note>, because he [David] was "ruddy, with beautiful eyes and goodly to look upon" <note>1 Samuel 16:12</note>. Therefore, "these are the generations of Perez" <note>Genesis 38:29</note>, and Perez broke through, and his neighbor is the serpent <note>A complex pun on the name "Perez" (to break through) and the messianic line, which must "break through" the grip of the primordial serpent.</note>. And therefore, "these are the generations of Perez"—the "crooked serpent" <note>Isaiah 27:1</note>. And just as the judgment of the "Rock" regarding the matter of the serpent is crooked, the Scripture says: "for all His ways are justice" <note>Deuteronomy 32:4</note>. And know that this attribute is called:

### Rock
from the side of *Gevurah* (Strength/Judgment), yet it draws from *Gedulah* (Greatness/Mercy), within the secret of: "Look to the rock from which you were hewn, and to the hole of the pit from which you were digged. Look to Abraham your father and to Sarah who bore you" <note>Isaiah 51:1-2</note>. And it says: "the Rock that begot you, you neglected, and you forgot the God who labored for you." He who knows these secrets can understand the phrase "So shall your seed be" <note>Genesis 15:5</note>; for Abraham had no offspring serving him from the side of the arrangement of the stars and constellations <note>In Jewish thought, "Israel is above the constellations," meaning they are governed by direct Divine providence rather than astrology.</note>, but only by way of miracles and wonders. And this is the secret of "and upon the likeness of the throne was a likeness as the appearance of a man" <note>Ezekiel 1:26</note>. When David saw the likeness of the forms of the external thrones and the "husks" <note>term: <term>Klippot</term> (husks or shells) are the forces of impurity that conceal the Divine light.</note> that are called "man," and the inner one called "Adonai" <note>The Lord</note>, David said: "The Lord is for me, I will not fear; what can man do to me?" <note>Psalm 118:6</note>. And this attribute is called:

### The Mighty One
of Jacob, the Mighty One of Israel, because the attribute of *Yesod* (Foundation) is dependent upon Jacob, who is the "Middle Line" <note>Jacob represents *Tiferet* (Beauty/Harmony), the central pillar of the Sefirotic tree that balances Mercy and Judgment.</note>. And he has six names: three above and three below. Those above are:

<column-break/>

*Gedulah* (Greatness), *Gevurah* (Strength), and *Tiferet* (Beauty). Those below are *Netzach* (Victory), *Hod* (Splendor), and *Yesod* (Foundation). And this is why *Yesod* is called the "Mighty One of Jacob" and not the "Mighty One of Abraham" or the "Mighty One of Isaac." Therefore, contemplate a great principle. It is said: "Therefore says the Lord, the Lord of Hosts, the Mighty One of Israel: 'Ah, I will ease Me of My adversaries, and avenge Me of My enemies'" <note>Isaiah 1:24</note>. For David, who is the master of wars, is the "Throne" for the Lord of Hosts <note>original: "יהו"ד צבאות", a permutation of the Name.</note>, which is the secret of "Lord" (*Adon*). And Jacob ascended as far as *Binah* (Understanding), because he "rides upon the high places" <note>Deuteronomy 32:13</note> of Abraham and Isaac, as we have already explained. And when the Mighty One of Jacob draws from *Binah* above, then his blossom "shoots up like a flower" <note>Genesis 40:10</note>, and the "Eagle" <note>A symbol for the Sefirah of Tiferet or the ascending spirit.</note> is filled with feathers. And so it says: "Does the hawk fly by your understanding <note>original: "המבינתך", referencing the Sefirah of Binah.</note>, and spread his wings toward the south?" <note>Job 39:26</note>. Understand these supernal wonders: "he spreads his wings toward the south," for the "kindnesses of David" <note>Isaiah 55:3</note> are drawn through Jacob [to] Abraham, who is in the South <note>In Kabbalah, the South represents the right side, the attribute of *Chesed* (Mercy/Kindness), which is associated with Abraham.</note>. This is what the Scripture means by "Shield of Abraham" and "Shield of David," "Kindness to Abraham" and "the Kindnesses of David." Therefore, "he spreads his wings toward the south," toward the right side. And this is in his coming to embrace the Queen <note>A reference to the union of the masculine and feminine aspects of the Divine.</note>: "His left hand is under my head, and his right hand embraces me" <note>Song of Songs 2:6</note>. "God" <note>original: "אלוה"</note> is his secret; from [him] "He comes," and "His glory fills the earth." His glory indeed! And from the side of *Tzedek* (Justice) to *Tzedakah* (Righteousness) is the "Hand of God." Now open your eyes and understand the wonders of how the Torah is woven with the wisdom of the Name (may He be blessed). And this attribute is called:

### The Righteous One
<note>term: <term>Tzadik</term> (The Righteous One) is the primary title for the Sefirah of Yesod, acting as the funnel for all Divine energy.</note>, meaning that it is a "wonder" and a "balance of judgment." Through it, all the commandments and the worlds are sustained as a perfect unity. It sustains everything by way of the attribute of *Malchut* (Kingship). It stands in the center—above, below, and to the sides. Behold, this attribute is called *Tzadik* (Righteous One), and the attribute of *Malchut* is called *Tzedek* (Justice). When the flow of influence is channeled from the *Tzadik* into *Tzedek*, then that flow is called *Tzedakah* (Charity/Righteousness). This is similar to one who gives a penny to a poor person; that gift is called *Tzedakah*. Thus, it is found that the one who gives charity awakens the "Righteous One" to unite with the attribute of "Justice," bringing peace between them and joining the Sefirot into One, thereby bringing peace to the world. This is the secret of the verse: "And the work of righteousness (*Tzedakah*) shall be peace" <note>Isaiah 32:17</note>.

Gates of Justice <note>original: "שערי צדק", likely the title of the chapter or the work.</note>

It describes how human acts of charity (Tzedakah) trigger a Divine union between the masculine and feminine attributes of God, bringing peace to the world.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation), which was previously described using the imagery of the "Rock" (Tzur) and King David's "ruddy" appearance. The text now transitions into the role of Yesod as the "Life of the World," the conduit through which Divine vitality flows from the higher realm of Binah (Understanding) into the lower realm of Malchut (Kingdom).</meta>

He is Life; He is the life of his soul, for all souls depend upon Him along with all other existing things. He is the Power, and the phrase "He swears by the Life of the World" <note>original: "וישבע בחי העולם", referencing Daniel 12:7. In Kabbalah, "Life of the World" is a title for the Sefirah of Yesod.</note> means He is truly the life of the world. For when He draws forth life from *Binah* <term>Binah: the Sefirah of "Understanding," often viewed as the "Supernal Mother" or source of the divine flow</term> into *Malchut* <term>Malchut: "Kingship," the final Sefirah that represents the manifested world and the Divine Presence</term>, then all existing things are satisfied with life, blessing, and abundance from *Malchut*. 

This is like a pool filled with water from which one satisfies the need to water all the garden beds, and all types of seeds and plants according to their kind. This is the secret of "He swears by the Life of the World," yet He is not called "Life of the Worlds" (plural) <note>original: "בחי העולמים". The text makes a subtle distinction between the singular "World" and plural "Worlds" to highlight a specific stage of divine flow.</note>—rather, "Life of the World" (singular). Understand this well.

## And Know
and believe that one who denies an oath <gloss>denies</gloss> has denied this specific Divine attribute. Such a person takes their own life from the world. From this, understand the secret of one who violates an oath: they have no root, no permanence, and no standing on the side of the "World." For all seven days of Creation withdraw from them. By denying the oath, no permanence remains for them in the place of the "World." One who denies an oath is uprooted by the "Seventh" <note>referring to Malchut, the seventh of the lower Sefirot</note>; they have no place in the world to stand or lean upon, and they have no life, sustenance, or permanence in any matter. Therefore, "He swears by the Life of the World." Understand this well. 

From this place, we constantly draw life, as it is said: "For with You is the source of life" <note>Psalm 36:10</note>, and "They have forsaken Me, the source of living waters" <note>Jeremiah 2:13</note>. In this place, we ask on Rosh Hashanah <note>The Jewish New Year</note>: "Remember us for life," for this is the "Place of Remembrance." And we say: "Write us in the Book of Life"—meaning in *His* book, which is *Malchut*. As it is said: "Everyone who is written for life in Jerusalem" <note>Isaiah 4:3</note>. 

This is the Sefirah called "The Creator of the Beginning" <note>original: "יוצר בראשית" (Yotzer Bereshit)</note>, for this is the attribute in which all forms are fashioned within the attribute of *Malchut*. This is the secret of "There is no Rock like our God" <note>1 Samuel 2:2; the Hebrew word 'Tzur' (Rock) is read here as 'Tzayar' (Artist/Fashioner)</note>. I am hinting this to you in the verse: "I will put you in a cleft of the rock at Horeb" <note>Exodus 33:22</note>. The "cleft of the rock" is the secret of *Malchut*. Therefore: "You shall see My back" <note>Exodus 33:23</note>, but He did not place him [Moses] within the Rock itself. This is the secret of: "Behold, I will stand before you there on the rock at Horeb, and you shall strike the rock" <note>Exodus 17:6</note>—referring to the thing called "Rock."

<column-break/>

It was not said [of the second day of creation] "that it was good" <note>Genesis 1:6-8 lacks the phrase "God saw that it was good," unlike the other days.</note>, because on that day Gehenna <note>Purgatory or the realm of spiritual cleansing</note> was created. That is to say, all the forces of impurity of every kind were suspended in it, and permission was given to the Destroyer to cause damage. Therefore, "it was good" was not said of it. This is the "separation" of the second day. For when the upper and lower realms are unified, then everything is "good." One who understands this secret will understand why "It is not good for man to be alone" <note>Genesis 2:18</note> and "A slanderer separates a close friend" <note>Proverbs 16:28</note>. Conversely, it is written: "He who finds a wife finds good" <note>Proverbs 18:22</note>.

## And Know
that all the works of Creation are truly sealed with the attribute of "Good," which is its permanence, its power, and its unique virtue. From it comes the goodness, the abundance, the permanence, and the sustenance for *Malchut*, from which all creatures are sustained according to their kinds. But if, Heaven forbid, human beings should sin and the "Good" is withdrawn—meaning, "the righteous man is gathered in" <note>Isaiah 57:1</note>—then it is written: "Your sins have withheld the good from you" <note>Jeremiah 5:25</note>. 

Because David's attribute was in this place, it was said of him: "He was ruddy, with beautiful eyes and goodly to look upon" <note>1 Samuel 16:12</note>. The matter of being "ruddy" <note>original: "אדמוני" (admoni)</note> is because his attribute is comprised of both Judgment and Mercy. From the side of Judgment, the Prince of Edom <note>The guardian angel of Esau/Rome, representing harsh Judgment</note> laid his hand upon him and "struck the socket of Jacob's hip" <note>Genesis 32:26</note>. Therefore, "These are the generations of Peretz" <note>Ruth 4:18</note> contains the secret of "A hand upon the throne of God" <note>Exodus 17:16</note>. 

Esau is Edom, and it is said of David that "he was ruddy," while of Esau it is said: "the first one came out ruddy" <note>Genesis 25:25</note>. This continued until the time came for Zerah <note>meaning "shining" or "rising"</note> to emerge: "And the glory of the Lord shall rise (yizrach) upon you" <note>Isaiah 60:1</note>. This is the source and the foundation called "Life of the Worlds," because it draws life from *Binah* in the secret of the "Seventh" and extends it into *Malchut* in the secret of "Bathsheba" <note>Literally "Daughter of Seven," representing Malchut</note>. It is called "Life of the Worlds" from one world to the next. 

We have already explained above that He is called "The Living God" (*El Chai*). Because He is the secret of the Seventh and is the "Life of the Worlds," the secret of the oath is dependent upon Him. This is the secret of the phrases: "As the Lord lives" and "As your soul lives." However, we must awaken you to two signs held within this verse: the first is "Life of the Beloved" and the second is "Life of your soul."

## And Here I
explain "Life of the Beloved": that He has no life from any other source—meaning, aside from the essence of the Sefirot themselves, which are all a single unity.
