<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation). While the previous page discussed Yesod as the "Life of the World" and the source of vitality for all existence, this page elaborates on how the different ranks of the Jewish people connect to this Divine structure and how Yesod acts as the essential conduit (the "Tzadik") that sustains the world.</meta>

# And Know
Know that according to their spiritual levels, the people of Israel are categorized: some are **Righteous** <term>Tzadikim: individuals who embody the Sefirah of Yesod through moral and ritual purity</term>, some are **Elders**, some are **Pious**, and some are **Holy**. Each one corresponds to the specific supernal place to which they are attached—understand this well. Know that the attribute of the **Righteous One** (*Tzadik*) is that which flows goodness onto the "Land" <note>original: "הארץ הידועה", literally "the known land," a Kabbalistic reference to the Sefirah of Malchut (Kingship/Physical Reality)</note>, and likewise below. As it is written: *“Your people are all righteous; they shall inherit the land forever, the branch of My planting, the work of My hands, that I may be glorified”* <note>Isaiah 60:21</note>. Contemplate with full understanding the matter of the "branch of My planting," the "work of My hands," and what it means "to be glorified." Consider how great the beauty of a tree is when its branches are many, spreading its boughs and shoots to every side. This is the secret of the ornaments called *Pe'er* (Glory/Headdress), as in: *“As a bridegroom decks himself with a priestly headdress”* <note>original: "כחתן יכהן פאר", Isaiah 61:10</note> and *“You shall make holy garments for Aaron your brother, for honor and for glory”* <note>Exodus 28:2; the word "Tiferet" here refers to the Sefirah of Beauty</note>. Understand what we have said; these places are all great principles through which one may enter the many chambers of the Torah, which contain many treasures. And this attribute is called...

# Foundation
I must inform you of an essential general principle: Know that there is a **foundation** (*Yesod*) that is **below** a building, and the building stands upon it, as is the case with foundations in this lower, physical world. There is also a foundation that is in the **middle**, around which the building stands; this is the nature of the foundations of the heavenly structures. Because the heavens are circular, every sphere has its foundation in its center, and by means of its center, every sphere revolves and moves. Now, if you say that the foundations of the lower world are "toil-worn," it is because the buildings of the lower world are silent and resting, not moving; but the heavenly spheres are always revolving and moving. Therefore, the lower beings are called "dead" <note>In this context, "dead" refers to being static or lacking the constant, dynamic divine motion of the celestial bodies</note>, while the heavens and what is in them are called "living." Finally, there is a foundation that is **above**, with the buildings below it. This is the secret of the world of **Angels**; they are below, and they yearn and long to ascend and see the face of the Lord, His blessed Radiance. A sign for these three can be found in the elements: **Water** flows downward, **Wind/Air** surrounds, and **Fire** naturally ascends—representing the three worlds. After you know this:

# Know
that the Name bears all, and He is above,

<column-break/>

and His "burden" <gloss>the created world</gloss> is below Him. As it is said: *“For David said, High and Exalted.”* After you know this, know that the Sefirah called **Yesod** (Foundation) is that which gives life and blessing to **Malchut** (Kingship), and it is above her. The Sefirah of *Malchut* always yearns and longs to ascend and be united with the attribute of *Yesod*. Thus, the attribute of *Malchut* depends upon the attribute of the **Righteous One, the Foundation of the World** <note>original: "צדיק יסוד עולם", a common phrase based on Proverbs 10:25</note>, just as a building relies on its foundation. If, God forbid, the foundation is missing, the whole structure is destroyed. When the attribute of *Yesod* is withdrawn from the **Assembly of Israel** <term>Knesset Yisrael: the spiritual personification of the Jewish people, often identified with Malchut</term>, it results in the destruction of the world. One who understands this secret will understand what the Sages meant regarding the verse: *“Remember, O Lord, against the children of Edom the day of Jerusalem, who said: 'Raze it, raze it, even to its foundation'”* <note>Psalm 137:7</note>. Likewise, it says: *“The righteous one is gathered in because of the evil”* <note>Isaiah 57:1</note>. Contemplate well and you will see the meaning of "The Righteous One is the foundation of the world." For when the "Righteous One" (*Yesod*) is withdrawn from "Justice" (*Malchut*), then it is written: *“The crown of our head has fallen”* <note>Lamentations 5:16</note>, for the foundation is missing. Likewise, it says: *“She has fallen, she shall no more rise: the virgin of Israel”* <note>Amos 5:2</note>. Contemplate well the secret of: *“Fair in climate, the joy of the whole earth, the mountains of Zion, the sides of the north, the city of the great King”* <note>Psalm 48:2</note>. And concerning that day, He says: *“I will raise up the fallen Sukkah of David”* <note>Amos 9:11</note>, and the scripture says: *“Shake yourself from the dust; arise, and sit down, O Jerusalem”* <note>Isaiah 52:2</note>. One who understands this will understand what is meant by: *“How has the Lord covered with a cloud...”* and *“He cast down from heaven to earth the beauty [Tiferet] of Israel”* <note>Lamentations 2:1</note>. One who understands the construction of the foundation will understand the nature of the "Falling." However, when the **Unification** returns to its place, the building shall return to its former state. This is what is meant by: *“He who returns His Divine Presence (Shekhinah) to Zion.”* And it says: *“Out of Zion, the perfection of beauty, God has shined forth”* <note>Psalm 50:2</note> and *“Blessed be the Lord out of Zion...”* For truly, from Zion He shall shine forth to dwell in Jerusalem. The explanation is that **Zion and Jerusalem** are **Yesod and Malchut**. If there is no Zion (*Yesod*), there is no Divine Presence (*Shekhinah*) in Jerusalem (*Malchut*). Understand this very well. And this attribute is called...

# All
The reason is that all types of flow, emanation, blessing, and life are drawn down from the **Supernal Crown** (*Keter*) through all the channels, into all the chambers and all the supernal treasures. They are all drawn from every side, and they all unite in one: the attribute of the **Righteous One**.
<meta>catchword: יסוד (Yesod/Foundation)</meta>

<summary>This page explains the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation) as the vital link between the upper divine emanations and the lower world (Malchut). It uses the imagery of Zion and Jerusalem to illustrate the union of these two forces, warning that without the "Foundation" of the Righteous One, the spiritual structure of the world collapses into exile and destruction.</summary>
<keywords>Yesod, Malchut, Tzadik, Foundation, Zion, Jerusalem, Shekhinah, Sefirot, Divine Flow, Destruction of the Temple</keywords>

--- Page 59 ---
<meta>This page follows the discussion of the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation) as the channel of life. Here, the author transitions into the practical and mystical application of this concept through the commandment of Tzedakah (charity/righteousness), explaining how human actions on earth directly affect the union of the Divine Sefirot and the flow of blessing to the world.</meta>

# Understand a great secret I am hinting at, and know for yourselves
"Sow for yourselves according to righteousness and reap according to kindness" <note>original: "לצדק וקצרו לפי חסד", a variation of Hosea 10:12</note>. The "sowing" is literal, and this is "kindness" <term>Chesed: the Sefirah of Loving-kindness</term> in its literal sense. And here I am hinting: "Charity saves from death" <note>Proverbs 10:2</note>. When human beings sin, they cause a separation from the **Master** <note>original: "אלוף", Aluf, referring to God as the Master of the world</note>, and the "Land" <term>Malchut: the Sefirah of Kingship, representing physical reality</term> remains dry because there is no "water" in the heavens. Then, the "buckets of Kingship" descend toward death; the grave is her home. Anyone who encounters her in this state of harsh judgment dies without mercy or compassion—woe to that hour!

However, anyone who causes the **Righteous One** <term>Tzadik: referring here to the Sefirah of Yesod</term> to be joined to **Justice** <term>Tzedek: referring to the Sefirah of Malchut</term> causes rains of blessing to descend to the earth. Then, life is found. This is accomplished by one who gives charity (*Tzedakah*) to the poor, causing "Charity" to be enacted above. This is the literal secret of "Charity saves from death." When the fullness of charity—the union performed with Kingship—occurs, the one who encounters her judgment will not die.

Open your eyes and see wonders! Therefore it is said, "His throne shall be established in righteousness" and "she shall be redeemed through charity; in charity you shall be established." "Observe justice and perform charity, for My salvation is soon to come" <note>Isaiah 56:1</note>. One who understands these matters will realize that the commandment of **Charity** is the secret of the connection of the *Sefirot* <term>Sefirot: the ten divine emanations or attributes of God</term> and His unity, bringing peace between the "Righteous One" and "Justice."

This matter is literally visible to the eye; as you find, the three names are merciful to one another: *Tzadik* (Righteous One), *Tzedakah* (Charity), and *Tzedek* (Justice) join the "Tent" <note>original: "האהל", referring to the Divine Tabernacle or structure</note> to become one. "Charity exalts a nation." If so, contemplate the excellence of Charity. In the matter of the **Dove** <note>original: "יונה", possibly a metaphor for the soul or the Sefirah of Malchut</note> which connects between the Righteous One and Justice, she brings peace between the upper and lower worlds, bringing blessing, flow, and emanation to the world, causing the union of peace.

Because of this, a person must prepare themselves for every single commandment of the Torah, seeing how many worlds they sustain by performing them, and how many worlds they destroy by neglecting them—and certainly by committing a transgression. This is the secret of "Why was the land destroyed and laid waste like a wilderness?" and the Lord said: "Because you forsook My Torah."

One who understands this secret can understand the mystery of "Bring the full tithe into the storehouse, that there may be food in My house, and test Me now in this... if I will not open for you the windows of heaven and pour out for you a blessing until...
<meta>catchword: there is no limit</meta>

<column-break/>

...there is no limit" <note>Malachi 3:10</note>. Similarly, regarding what the Sages said about the sin of neglecting the **Challah** <term>Challah: the portion of dough set aside for the priest</term>, the sin of neglecting **Tithes**, the **Sabbatical Year** (*Shmita*), and other commandments: do not let it enter your mind that these are merely punishments meant to penalize those who neglect or transgress them.

Rather, it is not so. Instead, for anyone who neglects one of the commandments, the specific flow that would have been emanated through its performance—such as Charity—is withheld. This is like one who does not sow his field and therefore does not reap. Or it is like one who does not wear clothes and his body becomes cold. The "punishments" of the commandments are a truly **natural** thing. Just as it is the nature of fire to heat, and the nature of water to cool and moisten, and the nature of bread to satisfy the hungry, and the nature of water to quench the thirsty—so too it is in the nature of every commandment to produce the specific good mentioned regarding it, or the specific "punishments" mentioned regarding their neglect. This applies to Charity, and likewise to transgressions.

**And know** that there are transgressions that affect the **Inner Palace**, such as sexual immorality, desecrating the Sabbath, and others like them. And there are transgressions that affect the area outside the Palace, such as eating "creeping things" <term>Sheratzim: insects or small swarming creatures forbidden by the Torah</term>, or impure animals, beasts, birds, and fish. One who transgresses these damages the "ranks," breaks the "conduits," and brings an "idol into the Palace." The sign for this is: "But from the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil, you shall not eat of it."

**And know** that this is why the Righteous are called *Tzadikim*: because they maintain all inner matters in their proper place within, and outer matters on the outside, and nothing leaves its proper boundary. In this way, they are called Righteous. One who understands what we explained above will understand what is said: "The Righteous One is the foundation of the world" <note>Proverbs 10:25</note>. That is to say, the attribute of the *Tzadik* is the foundation of the upper and lower worlds, connecting the "Tent" to be one. Then the upper beings are connected and unified with one another. Then the "plantings of the Garden" are blessed, and all things are in their state of perfection. Through the Righteous ones below, everything is kept in its proper place, and every thing is found within its boundary and domain: the inner within, and the outer without.
<meta>catchword: And know</meta>

<summary>This page explains that Mitzvot (commandments), specifically Charity, are not just legal obligations but "natural" spiritual mechanisms that connect the Divine Sefirot and sustain the world's flow of blessing. Sins are described as natural consequences that disrupt these Divine conduits, separating the inner sanctuary from the outer world.</summary>
<keywords>Tzedakah, Charity, Tzadik, Yesod, Malchut, Sefirot, Spiritual Nature, Divine Union, Mitzvot, Transgression, Inner Palace</keywords>

--- Page 60 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirot, specifically focusing on how the Divine Attribute of Malchut (Kingship) receives flow from Yesod (Foundation). It transitions from the previous discussion of charity into the mystical meaning of circumcision, the symbolism of King David, and the concept of "statutes" as protective boundaries against impure forces.</meta>

And behold, Kingship <term>Malchut: the tenth Sefirah, representing the physical world and the Divine Presence</term>, when she receives the emanation from Foundation <term>Yesod: the ninth Sefirah, the channel of life-force</term>—which is the secret of all redemptions drawn from Understanding <term>Binah: the third Sefirah, the source of liberation</term>—Kingship is then called the **Redeeming Angel**. This means she acts as the emissary of the "Foundation of the World." This is the angel who walked with our forefather Jacob, referred to as "the God who shepherded me... the Angel who redeems me" <note>Genesis 48:15-16</note>. And this Divine attribute is called:

### Statute

Know that in every place you find the word **Statute** <note>Hebrew: *Chok* (masculine form)</note> in the Torah, it refers to the secret of the attribute of **Foundation**. In every place you find the word **Decree** <note>Hebrew: *Chukah* (feminine form)</note>, it refers to the secret of the attribute of **Kingship**. Understand and contemplate that "Statute" is the secret of the place of holiness and purity; it is the secret of inner Compassion. Everything adjacent to it on the outside is the secret of the "husks" <term>Kelipot: the outer shells or forces of evil that conceal holiness</term>, the "foreskin" <term>Arlah: a metaphor for spiritual blockage or impurity</term>, and the powers of impurity. That which is internal is holy, and that which is outside is profane <note>original: "חולין", Chullin</note>. The internal is called God, while the external is darkness. This is the place of separation <note>original: "הבדלה", Havdalah</note> that distinguishes between the holy and the profane, between light and darkness.

This is because this is the place where the "foreskin" <note>the external husks</note> takes hold from the outside. In this way, the attribute of Kingship is called the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**—it can be either this or that. This is the meaning of "as a rose among the thorns" <note>Song of Songs 2:2</note>. Therefore, whenever you find the language of "Statute" in the Torah, a person must distinguish between the partition of holiness and the partition of the profane. For "Statute" is a term for a boundary and a fence, as in "He set a statute for the sea, that the waters should not transgress His word" <note>Proverbs 8:29</note>. It says, "He gave a statute that shall not be passed." Behold, around this "Statute" are many types of thorns, briers, and obstacles. This is the "Place of David." Therefore, he required songs <note>the Psalms</note> to fight against the accusers. He said, "**Lord**, how many are my foes!" <note>Psalm 3:2</note> and said, "All nations surrounded me; in the name of the **Lord**, I will cut them off" <note>Psalm 118:10</note>. And he said, "They compassed me about, yes, they surrounded me; in the name of the **Lord**, I will cut them off." The "foreskin"—which is the spiritual blockage—is cut away through the Covenant of Circumcision in the name of the **Lord**, as it says "I will cut them off" <note>original: "אמילם", amilam, which shares a root with "Milah" or circumcision</note>.

### Know

That this is the place where David needed to fight his enemies. Therefore, he required a shield: "You have given me the shield of Your salvation" <note>Psalm 18:36</note>. This is the **Shield of David**. Therefore, he said, "I will pursue my enemies and overtake them." Know that David cut away all the forces of the "foreskin" and impurity surrounding the **Assembly of Israel** <term>Knesset Yisrael: a title for Malchut, representing the collective soul of Israel</term> and fought their battles.

<column-break/>

He found for himself a source of seed from the **Righteous One, the Foundation of the World**. This is the meaning of "And the **Lord** remembered Sarah as He had said" <note>Genesis 21:1</note>; and our tradition teaches that all barren women were remembered along with her. Behold, the first Man <note>Adam</note>, when he sinned, he sinned in this place with the "foreskin" regarding the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil. Then Abraham came and removed the "foreskin" through circumcision. At that moment, "Light was sown for the Righteous" <note>Psalm 97:11</note>, Sarah was remembered, and all barren women were remembered with her. Understand this! From here you can understand the verse "So shall your seed be" <note>Genesis 15:5</note>, for He is the creator of the heavens and those who stretch them out beneath, who spreads out the earth and its offspring, giving a soul to the people upon it <note>Isaiah 42:5</note>. This is the secret of Kingship, and this is the secret of "You shall surely cut" <note>original: "כרת תכרתי"</note>. And so on: "Thus says the God, the **Lord**... I have called you in righteousness and will hold your hand" <note>Isaiah 42:6</note>.

### Know

That the secret of Circumcision is the secret of Redemption. I will now detail how "on a night of guarding" <note>Passover</note> the children of Israel were circumcised.

### The Secret

Of Circumcision is the secret of Redemption. For the "foreskin" that was attached to the flesh was removed during the exile. This is the secret of "As for you also, by the blood of your covenant I have sent forth your prisoners out of the pit" <note>Zechariah 9:11</note>. For Foundation is the secret of Redemption because it draws from the **Jubilee** <term>Yovel: the 50th year, a symbol of ultimate liberty and the Sefirah of Binah</term>, of which it is written, "it shall have redemption" and "in the Jubilee it shall go out" and "in all the land of your possession you shall grant redemption for the land" <note>Leviticus 25</note>. The Jubilee is certainly the secret of all redemptions and the secret of all freedom.

### Know

That through the attributes of the Jubilee, Israel went out from the land of Egypt, as the **Gates of Understanding** <note>Binah</note> were opened for them. This is the secret of "and in fifties <note>usually translated as 'armed'</note> the children of Israel went up out of the land of Egypt" <note>Exodus 13:18</note>. You will find the Exodus from Egypt mentioned fifty times in the Torah, and no more. Therefore, you find that Redemption is dependent on the Covenant of Circumcision, which is Foundation. When Foundation joins with Kingship, Redemption is brought close to Kingship. This is why our sages said one must "join Redemption to Prayer" <note>connecting the blessing 'Redeemer of Israel' immediately to the Amidah prayer</note>, sealing with "Blessed are You, **Lord**, who redeems Israel," followed by "O Lord, open my lips." Understand this: anyone who is circumcised merits two redemptions—a redemption above and a redemption below—as he is saved from the "pit of destruction," which is the "foreskin." Therefore, "He redeems your life from the pit, and crowns you with kindness and mercy" <note>Psalm 103:4</note>. Understand this well. See how great the power of the Covenant of Circumcision is, for this is the gate through which one enters into all redemptions.

<summary>This page explains the mystical relationship between the Sefirot of Yesod (Foundation) and Malchut (Kingship), linking the act of circumcision to the historical and spiritual redemption of Israel. It portrays King David as a spiritual warrior who used Psalms to protect the holy "Statute" from the surrounding forces of impurity.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirotic system, specifically focusing on the ninth Sefirah, Yesod (Foundation), and its relationship with the tenth, Malchut (Kingship). It builds on the previous page's discussion of Divine flow and protective boundaries by explaining how "Foundation" serves as the cosmic channel for all blessings and is symbolized by the Sabbath, the Patriarchs, and the Covenant of Circumcision.</meta>

Foundation of the world—and from it, all goodness and 
all emanations flow into Kingship <term>Malchut: the tenth Sefirah, representing the manifest world and the Divine Presence</term>. For this reason, it is called in the language 
of **All** <note>Hebrew: *Kol*</note>—meaning the attribute upon which everything depends. And so 
the scripture says, *I esteem all Your precepts to be right* <note>Psalm 119:128</note>, and this is the secret of 
*I am the Lord, who makes all* <note>Isaiah 44:24</note>, meaning that from the attribute of "All," 
everything emerges. Indeed, this attribute is the ninth Sefirah, 
and to it come all the channels and all the emanations 
from the Infinite <term>Ein Sof: the Boundless or Infinite Godhead</term>. From it, everything is flowed and emanated 
into the attribute of Kingship, and she too is blessed by the attribute of 
"All." When she is blessed by it, she is 
also called by its name, *Kol* (All), for she is then called the Bride <note>Hebrew: *Kallah*, which shares linguistic roots with *Kol*</note>, 
meaning she includes everything from "All." This is 
the attribute that she [Kingship] requires and for which she watches, because it is 
the foundation of all and everything depends on it. Because Kingship 
is blessed by it, she is called by its name, "All," 
as we have written, for the attribute of Kingship is called in 
many places by the name of the Sefirah that fills her. 
We have already written great principles concerning this. In 
this manner, Kingship is also called "All," 
and so it says, *The profit of the land is in all* <note>Ecclesiastes 5:8; original: "בכ"ל", Bakol</note>. This attribute 
was given to the Patriarchs as an inheritance because the three of them 
received an emanation from the attribute above, as we have explained. 
Abraham prepared channels and rectifications for himself, and it was acquired 
by him; this is what is meant by *The Lord blessed Abraham in all* <note>Genesis 24:1; original: "בכל", Bakol</note>. 
It was called "the House of the Lord" after Abraham. It was given 
to Isaac as a gift, *and I ate of all* <note>Genesis 27:33; original: "מכ"ל", Mikol</note>, 
and it was also called the "House of God" after Isaac. And it was given to Jacob, 
as it is written, *For God has been gracious to me and I have everything* <note>Genesis 33:11; original: "כ'ל", Kol</note>. 
It was called "Bethel" <note>House of God</note> after Jacob, and through him it was found. 
Therefore, the three Patriarchs inherited the attribute called 
"All," and they merited everything and lacked nothing, above or below. 
They are truly called "Clusters" <note>Hebrew: *Eshkolot*, a pun on *Ish-she-kol-bo*, "a man in whom all is found"</note> in every place—
a man upon whom everything depends. This attribute is called 
**Zion**, because from Zion the foundation of the Torah is established 
(another version: because from Zion the foundation of the 
world is established, and from the Foundation the base of Kingship is established) and from the Foundation 
the base of the world is established. Behold, Zion and Jerusalem 
are Foundation and Kingship. As it is said, *Blessed be the Lord from Zion, 
who dwells in Jerusalem* <note>Psalm 135:21</note>, and it says, *He who restores His Presence 
to Zion*, like one who returns a lost item to its owners.

<column-break/>

You already know that the City of David is Zion. Behold, 
the fourth leg of the Chariot <term>Merkavah: the Divine Throne-Chariot</term> is David. And behold, 
this attribute is the secret of the attribute of the Sabbath, which is the seventh 
of the days of the week. Truly, on it He rested from all 
His work, because in it all things were finished in perfection 
and in fullness. On the Sabbath, the six days depend, and from its blessing 
and its goodness, the upper and lower worlds are blessed. 
Therefore, *God blessed the seventh day*. It 
has no partner since it is the seventh; therefore, the Assembly of Israel <term>Knesset Yisrael: the spiritual personification of the Jewish people, linked to Malchut</term> 
is its partner. Through them, the ten Sefirot are unified, 
and everything is blessed and held together. Therefore, regarding the Sabbath, it is said 
"Remember" and "Observe"—"Remember" for the day and "Observe" for the night. And the 
conjugal duty of the Torah scholar is from Sabbath eve to Sabbath eve. He who desecrates the Sabbath 
shall surely die, because he has denied the "World of Life." To 
this place, all court-ordered deaths are connected. Behold, this 
attribute is the literal secret of Circumcision. The secret of the circumcision 
and the uncovering <note>original: "מילה ופריעה", Milah and Periah</note> is "Remember" and "Observe." 
Therefore, it [circumcision] overrides the Sabbath. This is the secret of the Sabbatical years and Jubilees—
the Sabbatical and Jubilee years above. And if, Heaven 
forbid, the Sabbatical years were neglected, then the land would be liable for exile. 
Certainly, *all the days of its desolation it shall rest*, the rest 
it did not have during your Sabbaths—measure for measure, certainly. 
Stand and contemplate this Torah of the Lord, for this **Delight** 
is the foundation of the Covenant of Circumcision. 
The secret of circumcision and uncovering is as one: circumcision corresponds to Foundation <note>Yesod</note>, 
uncovering corresponds to Kingship <note>Malchut</note>. Therefore, one who circumcises but does not uncover 
the membrane is as if he did not circumcise. This is the gate through which one enters the secret 
of circumcision, for were it not for the Covenant of Circumcision, the 
Torah would not have been given to Israel. Therefore, you know what was said: through 
three things did Israel enter the covenant—circumcision, immersion, 
and the offering of a sacrifice. Behold, all who are circumcised are attached 
to Foundation and Kingship. Therefore, it is written of Abraham, 
*Walk before Me and be whole... and I will place My covenant 
between Me and you*—literally between Me and you. From that 
place, he merited offspring. Understand this well: were it 
not for the opening of the source, he would not have merited offspring. This is the secret 
of *He brought him outside* <note>Genesis 15:5</note>; once Abraham was circumcised 
and the source was revealed, Sarah bore him a son. This is the meaning of 
*He brought him outside and said: Look toward 
the heavens and count the stars*. And through these...

<summary>This page explains the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation) as the universal channel for Divine flow, identifying it with the "Covenant of Circumcision," the Sabbath, and the biblical "Zion." It describes how the Patriarchs inherited this attribute and how it unites with Malchut (Kingship) to bring blessing and life into the world.</summary>

--- Page 62 ---
<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirotic system, specifically focusing on the spiritual dynamics of "Marah" (bitterness) as a gateway to the miraculous, and the hidden reasons behind the commandments of forbidden mixtures and the role of the Levites. It transitions from the discussion of the ninth Sefirah, Foundation (Yesod), into its manifestations through statutes and the preservation of sacred boundaries.</meta>

Behold, all the wonders and miracles are dependent on Marah <term>Marah: A place in the desert where the water was bitter (Exodus 15); mystically, it represents the attribute of Judgment that contains hidden sweetness</term>. If one is worthy, from within it is sweet; if one is not worthy, from without it is Marah (bitter), and it goes out to the "Outside" <note>Hebrew: *Chutz*. In Kabbalah, "The Outside" often refers to the realm of the "Other Side" or demonic forces</note>. And so it says:

> *And the son of the Israelite woman went out* <note>Leviticus 24:10</note>

And this is the secret: in every place where it says "went out," it is because he removed himself from the collective and denied the Root (God). One who understands this will understand the secret of:

> *While as yet He had not made the earth, nor the fields (the outsides)* <note>Proverbs 8:26</note>

And "He brought him outside" <note>Genesis 15:5</note>, and:

> *The wife of the dead shall not be married outside* <note>Deuteronomy 25:5</note>

And "Laban ran to the man outside" <note>Genesis 24:29</note>—if not for the "Evil Eye," his heart's intention would have been different. Therefore, anyone who slaughters [sacrifices] outside [the Temple] is liable for spiritual excision (*Karet*). After the Temple was built, all these great principles were included in the word **Statute** <term>Chok: A divine decree that transcends human logic</term>. 

Now, set your heart and contemplate, and you will find the secret of the "Well of Living Waters" or the secrets of "broken cisterns that can hold no water" <note>Jeremiah 2:13</note>. This attribute is called by those who know the secrets **Grace** <term>Chen: A mystical beauty or favor associated with the divine flow</term>.

**Leviathan** 
And the secret is, *Now this time will my husband be joined (yillaveh) to me* <note>Genesis 29:34; the name Levi comes from the root for "joining" or "accompanying"</note>. Therefore, the tribe of Levi had no portion or inheritance with their brothers. David <note>Representing the Sefirah of Kingship/Malchut</note> is his inheritance, for he has no business with the "Outside," but only with the "Inside." One who understands this will understand that *Leviathan* <note>The Hebrew word used here is *Livyatan*, playing on *Liviya* meaning "wreath" or "garland"</note> are "a wreath of grace for your head and a necklace for your throat" <note>Proverbs 1:9</note>. And I am hinting: "as the space of each with wreaths (loyot) around" <note>1 Kings 7:36</note>. And I will add: "He who is gracious to the poor lends (malveh) to the Lord" <note>Proverbs 19:17</note>—all the "loans" in the world are dependent on this place. And it is written:

> *The wicked borrows (loveh) and does not repay, but the righteous is gracious and gives* <note>Psalm 37:21</note>

Therefore, great is the one who lends to the poor in his time of distress. For this reason, Levi had no portion or inheritance. Know that the Levites necessarily required a pure inheritance—this is a great secret among the secrets of the Faith. *Thus shall you do to them to cleanse them:*

> *Sprinkle the water of purification upon them, and let them pass a razor over all their flesh, and let them wash their clothes, and cleanse themselves.* <note>Numbers 8:7</note>

In this place lies the secret of Purity, because the Impurities surround from every side. Thus, shaving is required, according to the secret of "David’s Songs" <term>Zemirot: "Songs," but also related to the root "to prune" or "cut away" spiritual thorns</term>. We have already written the reason for this above: "the way of a snake upon a rock" <note>Proverbs 30:19</note>. This is the secret of what we wrote above: therefore, there is a need for shaving. Behold, we recognize the Greatness [Right side] for the righteous, and the Levites for the Left side; these are the Gates of Righteousness.

<column-break/>

He commanded to cut the foreskin, and Israel carried [the covenant] from within. This is the secret of:

> *And none of you shall go out of the door of his house until morning.* <note>Exodus 12:22</note>

One who understands this secret will understand the secret of Forbidden Mixtures (*Kilayim*) and *Sha’atnez* <term>Sha'atnez: The forbidden mixing of wool and linen in a single garment</term>. Why were they forbidden to Israel, and why was it permitted in the ritual fringes (*Tzitzit*)? It is written:

> *You shall not wear sha’atnez, wool and linen together.* <note>Deuteronomy 22:11</note>

Yet it is also written:

> *You shall make yourself twisted cords (Tzitzit).* <note>Deuteronomy 22:12</note>

He who understands this secret will understand the secret of the "Kingdoms" <note>Or "Queenship"</note> about which it is written concerning wool and linen: "she lays her hands to the distaff" <note>Proverbs 31:19</note>. Happy are you, O Israel, that you have merited this! Therefore, it is written *Kilayim* (mixtures), meaning that one brings two things into one boundary. Just as "hand" and "hand" make two hands, "foot" and "foot" make two feet, "day" and "day" make two days—however, there are things that cannot be separated, like "water" or "millstones" <note>In Hebrew, these words are always plural/dual: *Mayim* and *Rechayim*</note>. These things are like the "furnaces of the world" <term>Kivshono shel Olam: The deep, hidden mysteries of creation</term>.

**And know** that there is a great secret in white garments, and in the other priestly garments there is a great secret regarding the reason for the prohibition of mixtures. If you merit to enter, you will see wonders within "Marah." Now that we have explained this to you, know that it was at Marah that the Torah was given to Israel, and with it, statutes and ordinances were given to Israel. Happy is the people for whom it is so! Know that Marah is bitter from the outside and sweet from the inside. Therefore, it is written:

> *There He made for them a statute (Chok) and an ordinance (Mishpat)* <note>Exodus 15:25</note>

And "there He tested them." In this place is the secret of wonders and miracles; even the Egyptians ran toward them. Everything is contained in the word *Chok* (Statute), for it is attached to *Mishpat* (Ordinance). I shall now explain: *Mishpat* is the attribute of Beauty <term>Tiferet: The sixth Sefirah, representing balance and harmony</term>. *Chok* is the attribute of Foundation <term>Yesod: The ninth Sefirah, the channel of divine energy</term>, and it takes hold of Beauty, for the "Line" <note>The central column of the Sefirot</note> takes hold of Judgment, as it is said:

> *And what great nation is there that has statutes and ordinances so righteous?* <note>Deuteronomy 4:8</note>

And because Jacob strove for the birthright, he merited the *Chok* (Statute) "which He covenanted with our fathers, and His oath to Isaac, and He confirmed it to Jacob for a statute" <note>Psalm 105:9-10</note>. For the Statute is dependent on Jacob, and he is its "father"—for Israel, when they are righteous, are called the "Mighty One of Jacob." Therefore, we have written for you some of the wonders concerning the matter of the Statute. Behold, I am handing over to you a key to Marah, as it is said: "There He made for them a statute and an ordinance, and there..."

<summary>This page discusses the mystical significance of "Marah," explaining how divine statutes (Chok) serve as a boundary that protects the internal sweetness of the divine from the "Outside" forces. It links the tribe of Levi, the prohibition of mixed fabrics (Sha'atnez), and the Sefirot of Beauty (Tiferet) and Foundation (Yesod) to the maintenance of sacred order.</summary>

<keywords>Marah, Statute (Chok), Ordinance (Mishpat), Leviathan, Levites, Purity, Sha'atnez, Forbidden Mixtures (Kilayim), Yesod, Tiferet, Jacob, Miracles</keywords>

--- Page 63 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of "The Outside" (Chutz) and the concept of "Statutes" (Chukim), transitioning from the nature of divine boundaries to the specific laws governing purity, forbidden foods, and the cosmic distinction between the holy and the profane as represented by Jacob and Esau.</meta>

**This** is the great principle, for from it you shall enter into all matters of the "distant ones." **Know** that the pig possesses some signs of purity, for it has split hooves, but most of its nature is impure; therefore, it is entirely forbidden. There is a secret regarding the hare and the hyrax in the matter of chewing the cud while not having split hooves—these are great matters. 

Our sages asked: Why is its name called *chazir* <term>Chazir: The Hebrew word for pig</term>? Because in the future, the Holy One, Blessed be He, will "return" <note>Hebrew: *le-hachzir*. This is a famous play on words in Kabbalistic literature suggesting that the status of the pig may change in the messianic era, though it is usually interpreted symbolically to mean the "return" of its sparks of holiness</note> it to Israel to eat. One who knows these principles will understand how many books depend on the statutes and decrees. This is the meaning of "You shall not walk in the statutes of the nations whom I cast out before you" <note>Leviticus 20:23</note>. For the statute that faces inward is our statute, and it is pure; but the statute that faces outward is the statute of the nations, and it is impure. 

According to this path, you will find the secret of the two goats [of Yom Kippur]: one whose blood enters the innermost sanctum, and one that is sent outside to be cast to Azazel in the wilderness. Concerning this, it is said: "And this shall be a statute forever unto you... in the tenth day of the month" <note>Leviticus 16:29</note>. 

**The wise one** will understand the secret of the reason why Jacob and Esau were born as twins from one womb and then separated from each other. The secret of "And the goat shall bear upon him all their iniquities unto a land cut off" <note>Leviticus 16:22</note> contains wonders within the secret of what is written: "But Esau I hated, and I made his mountains a desolation" <note>Malachi 1:3</note>. Though both were born from one womb, both are the secret of the statute of Understanding <term>Binah: The Sefirah of "Understanding," which is the source of both Judgment and Mercy</term>—hear this! And the secret of "Was not Esau Jacob's brother? says the Lord: yet I loved Jacob, but Esau I hated." 

Both were born from one womb, and this is the secret of impurity and purity hanging upon the word "statute" (*Chok*). This is the secret of the foreskin and circumcision, and the secret of the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil. If you understand these principles, they will become clear to you in every place where you find the language of "statutes" or "decrees" in the Torah. He who understands this will understand the secret of "If My covenant be not with day and night, I have not appointed the statutes of heaven and earth" <note>Jeremiah 33:25</note>—deep secrets and hidden wonders are stored in these places. This is the meaning of "You shall keep My statutes." From here, you will enter the secret of Exile and the secret of Redemption, and the reason why Israel was redeemed from Egypt. 

**Know** that Egypt (*Mitzrayim*) is a "borderer" <note>Hebrew: *metzranit*, a play on the name *Mitzrayim*</note> to the Land of Israel, as we have already written. Therefore, it served as a prison-house for Israel, for they were dwelling on the side of the fence that faces the outside, which is the place of the Exiles. And the Holy One [commanded] in it—

<column-break/>

—and the clearing of the thorns from around the vineyard. Then came Solomon his son and commanded <note>The catchword "tzivah" (commanded) likely refers to this transition into Solomon's era</note> everything with tranquility, with neither adversary nor evil occurrence, and he established the Temple upon its foundation. 

Know that for this reason, it is said in the portion of Passover: "Draw out and take for yourselves..." and it concludes: "And you shall observe this thing for a statute to you and to your sons" <note>Exodus 12:21, 24</note>. This is the place where the wicked stumble, for they find no reason for the commandments that are called "statutes" or "decrees," and they do not know the great and good principles that depend upon them. Therefore, the Torah said: "You shall keep My statutes: you shall not let your cattle gender with a diverse kind (*kilayim*)..." and "You shall keep My statutes." 

These are the things that the Accuser <term>Satan: Here referring to the personified force of logic or doubt that "prosecutes" the validity of the law</term> challenges, and the nations of the world question them—such as the prohibition of mixed fabrics (*shaatnez*), the prohibition of meat and milk, the prohibition of interbreeding species, the eating of the pig, and other such things. All of these are included in the "statutes." Behold, the Red Heifer and the Scapegoat are both faithful witnesses that reveal the upper mysteries in the secret of "Separation" (*Havdalah*).

**Know** that surrounding the Kingship (*Malchut*) and the secret of the Throne are thorns and briers. And behold, surrounding the Foundation (*Yesod*) is the "Field" which is full of murderers and accusers. This field belongs to Esau, of whom it is said: "And Esau came from the field, and he was faint" <note>Genesis 25:29</note>, for he had spilled blood. This is the field in which forbidden sexual unions are revealed, as it says: "For he found her in the field... the betrothed damsel cried, and there was none to save her" <note>Deuteronomy 22:27</note>. This is the field in which Cain killed Abel: "And it came to pass, when they were in the field, that Cain rose up against Abel his brother, and slew him" <note>Genesis 4:8</note>. This is the field where the fallen slain lie: "And whosoever touches one that is slain with a sword in the open field, or a dead body, or a bone of a man, or a grave" <note>Numbers 19:16</note>. This is the place of impurities, because they [are outside].

**And yet** 
within the inner chambers, there is a "Good Field," and it is called the "Field of Holy Apples" <term>Sadeh Tapuchim: A mystical name for the Shekhinah (Divine Presence) in her receptive state</term>. This is the meaning of "as the smell of a field which the Lord has blessed" <note>Genesis 27:27</note>. This implies that there is another field which is cursed. Understand this well. 

Therefore, the Holy One, Blessed be He, commanded concerning the Red Heifer and called it a "statute"—"This is the statute of the Torah." This means that the commandment of the Red Heifer is a great secret that separates between the holy and the profane, and between impurity and purity. The side that faces inward is pure, and the side that faces outward is impurity. This is why the ashes of the Heifer "purify the impure and defile the pure" <note>A classic paradox of the Red Heifer: those who handle its ashes become ritually impure, even though the ashes are used to purify others from the impurity of death</note>. Keep [this].

<summary>This page explores the mystical nature of biblical "statutes" (Chukim), explaining them as laws that govern the boundary between the holy internal realm of Israel and the impure external realm of the nations. It uses the archetypes of Jacob and Esau, the "Field of Apples" versus the "Field of Murderers," and the paradox of the Red Heifer to illustrate how purity and impurity originate from the same divine source.</summary>

## Know
That prayer in the Land of Israel is like one who needs to speak with the King, and his house is very close to the King’s palace. There is no "evil occurrence" on the road and no "evil Accuser." However, prayer outside the Land is like one who needs to speak with the King but his house is very far from the King’s palace. There are many bandits, many thieves, and many wild beasts along the way. Now, see how much one must be careful to escape the dangers of the road! <meta>This page continues the exposition of the Divine Names and the Sefirot (emanations), focusing on the Sefirah of "Yesod" (Foundation)—referred to here as "El Chai" (The Living God)—and how the prayers of Israel ascend through these spiritual levels to reach the "Keter" (Crown).</meta>

The place of the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*; the highest Sefirah, representing the Divine Will</note> was established for the **Additional Prayer** <note>original: *Musaf*; the extra service on Sabbaths and festivals</note>. There, we say: "They shall give unto You a Crown, O Lord our God." This is said in the Additional Prayer and not in the **Morning Prayer** <note>original: *Yotzer*; the morning service</note>, because in the Morning Prayer, all the prayers have not yet been recited, and they have not yet reached the Crown. But in the Additional Prayer, the Morning Prayers have already been completed and have ascended to the head of the Crown. 

Now that I have awakened you to all these essential principles, we must now enter into the explanation of the remaining names, according to the order of the levels, and may the Blessed Name lead us and teach us the way of truth, so that we may be cleaving to Him; may He be my help forever, Amen.

---

And the text says: **"And the Lord blessed Abraham with all"** <note>Genesis 24:1; the word "All" (*bakol*) is seen by Kabbalists as a reference to the Sefirah of Yesod</note>. The reason is that all the channels and the flows—whether for Judgment or for Mercy—all flow through the attribute of **The Living God** <note>original: *El Chai*; a name associated with the Sefirah of Yesod</note>. And from where? From the attribute of **The Lord of Hosts** <note>original: *Yidud Tzva'ot*</note> and **God of Hosts** <note>original: *Elohim Tzva'ot*</note>, which are two attributes, one to the right and one to the left. 

From these two attributes, together with the third in the middle, the attribute of **"All"** <note>original: *Kol*</note> is comprised. Because King David (peace be upon him) was clothed in the attribute of *El Chai* along with the "tip of the letter Yod," which is the secret of the fourth leg of the **Divine Chariot** <term>Merkabah: The visionary "chariot" representing the structure of the spiritual worlds</term>. He was extended into the attribute of *El Chai*, and he included within himself Kindness, Judgment, and Mercy. Therefore, it is said of him: **"He was ruddy, with beautiful eyes and a goodly appearance"** <note>1 Samuel 16:12</note>. 

"He was ruddy"—this is the secret of the flow of Judgment. 
"With beautiful eyes"—this is the secret of the Kindness of Abraham. 
"And a goodly appearance"—this refers to David. 
**"Beauty"** <note>original: *Tiferet*</note>—this refers to Jacob, in whom all "good" is suspended. 

But regarding **Esau**, what is written? **"And the first came out ruddy, all over"** <note>Genesis 25:25</note>. He was without "beautiful eyes" or "goodly appearance," but rather like a "hairy mantle." Therefore, he was a shedder of blood, and it is written of him: **"And by your sword shall you live"** <note>Genesis 27:40</note>, for his inheritance was blood and the shedding of it.

<column-break/>

When the prayer cleaves to **The Living God**, it enters from all the chambers where the Glory resides. Then the prayer ascends to the place of **Understanding** <term>Binah: The Sefirah of divine intuition and the "womb" of the lower emanations</term>. From the place of Understanding, it cleaves to **Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah: The Sefirah of pure, undifferentiated thought</term>, which is called **Will** <note>original: *Ratzon*</note>. This is the meaning of "A time of favor" <note>original: *Et Ratzon*</note>. 

Therefore, we say at the beginning of the prayer: **"O Lord, open my lips"** <note>Psalm 51:17</note>, for this is the gateway to enter. And at the end of the prayer, we say: **"May the words of my mouth be for favor"** <note>Psalm 19:14</note>, because "Favor" (Will) is the end of the levels, and it is the letter *Yod* of the Unique Name <note>Y-H-V-H</note> which is called "Favor." From this Favor—which is the secret of the *Yod*—it ascends via the **tip of the Yod** and cleaves to the **Crown**, which is called the **Infinite** <term>Ein Sof: God as He is in Himself, beyond all definition or emanation</term>, because the prayers ascend until there.

### The Lord God of Hosts

**"Hear my prayer, O Lord God of Hosts; give ear, O God of Jacob, Selah"** <note>Psalm 84:9</note>. This is because the two names, **The Lord of Hosts** and **God of Hosts**, are unified one against the other. Both are prepared to pour forth through the channels above into the attribute of **"All"**—that is to say, the flows of the Sefirot of **The Living God**. 

Both of them act according to the righteous Judgment, like all the "Hosts" of the world. One acts from the side of **Kindness** <note>original: *Chesed*</note> and the second acts from the side of **Judgment** <note>original: *Din*</note>. Both of them draw Judgment and Mercy into the attribute of *El Chai*.

We must inform you that the attribute of **The Lord of Hosts** <note>original: *Yidud Tzva'ot*</note> is that which draws down the Supernal Kindness, the Kindness of Abraham, and Eternal Life into the attribute of *El Chai*. 
The attribute of **God of Hosts** <note>original: *Elohim Tzva'ot*</note> is that which draws the power of Might, Judgment, Fear, and Punishment into the attribute of *El Chai*. 

Therefore, the attribute of *El Chai* includes everything: Mercy and Judgment. This is the secret of the verse: **"I am the Lord, Who makes all"** <note>Isaiah 44:24</note>. And it is written: **"Therefore all the precepts of the Lord concerning all things, I consider right"** <note>Psalm 119:128</note>.

<summary>This page explains how prayer ascends through the Sefirot to reach the Divine Will (Ratzon) and the Crown (Keter). <meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirot—the divine emanations—focusing specifically on the attributes of "Netzach" (Victory) and "Hod" (Splendor/Majesty), and how they relate to the act of prayer, gratitude, and the protection of Israel.</meta>

...the truth. And concerning it, it is said: **"unto Me every knee shall bow"** <note>Isaiah 45:23</note>. Every blessing that comes into the world through these attributes comes because of this. Therefore, one who does not bow during the **Thanksgiving prayer** <note>original: *Modim*; the portion of the central *Amidah* prayer where the congregation bows in gratitude</note>, his spine becomes a snake after seventy years. 

The secret of the seventy years refers to the seventy **Guardian Princes** <note>original: *Sarim*; in Jewish thought, each of the seventy nations of the world has a celestial representative</note> who stand in a line in a circle, for the "snake" <note>symbolizing destructive forces or the "Other Side"</note> lurks outside, opposite the attributes of **Victory** <note>original: *Netzach*</note> and **Splendor** <note>original: *Hod*</note>. Anyone who is not careful with them deserves to be bitten by the snake. This is the secret behind the saying: "Anyone who transgresses the words of the Sages deserves the bite of the snake." 

As you already know, the "Wisdom" of these levels refers to **Victory and Splendor**. Therefore, follow this path: when the Name **Adonai** <note>The Divine Name associated with the Shekhinah or Divine Presence</note> clothes itself in the garment of **Splendor**, it wins all the wars of Israel, takes vengeance on our adversaries, and pays back the recompense to all the enemies of our souls. Therefore, we must give thanks, because He clothes Himself in the garments of **Splendor and Victory** for war. The secret of this is: **"Thou art clothed with glory and majesty"** <note>Psalm 104:1; original: *Hod ve-hadar lavashta*</note>. This is the meaning of "God of Thanksgivings, Lord of Wonders." Therefore, reflect upon every place where you find the language of "Thanksgiving" <note>original: *Hoda'ah*</note>, for it corresponds to this spiritual place.

## And Know
That corresponding to this attribute, Solomon built the **Holy Temple** and named the second pillar **Boaz**. For there were two pillars, **Jachin and Boaz**. They are "legs and pillars of marble set upon bases of fine gold" <note>Song of Songs 5:15</note>. One who understands the word *Bohu* <note>the "void" mentioned in Genesis 1:2</note> will understand the word *Boaz*. Know that this attribute receives strength <note>original: *Oz*</note> from the attribute of **Strength** <note>original: *Gevurah*</note> and a stronghold from **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*</note>. It gives "strength and power unto His people" <note>Psalm 68:36</note>. 

From this place, all types of power, emanation, and might are drawn from the side of **Justice and Awe**. With this power, the attribute of **Adonai** sits in "Majesty of the Kingdom" <note>original: *Hod Malkhut*</note>. Now, reflect: in every place where you find "Thanksgivings" or the *Modim* prayer, a bow is required, because it is close to this attribute. When the attribute of Adonai clothes itself in the attribute of **Splendor**, then the war is won. We are then obligated to give thanks before Him, as it is said in the *Aggadah* <note>traditional homiletic literature</note>: "Therefore we are obligated to thank, to praise, and to glorify." Regarding the blessing of *Modim* which was established in the prayer service, consider that it is entirely...

<meta>catchword: upon</meta>

<column-break/>

...they "did not come to the help of the Lord." Our Rabbis, of blessed memory, said: some say **Meroz** <note>a place or figure mentioned in the Song of Deborah, Judges 5:23</note> was a star. Following this path, know that the Name called **God of Hosts** <note>original: *Elohim Tzva'ot*</note> is what influences the Name **Adonai** with the power to fight the "wars of the Lord." 

The secret is: **"O God of hosts, return, we beseech Thee; look down from heaven, and behold, and visit this vine"** <note>Psalm 80:15</note>. "God of hosts, return now and see; return, O Lord, to the ten thousands of the thousands of Israel." Return to influence power into the Name of the Lord <note>original: *Yud-Dalet*, a shorthand for the Name</note> which is called "this vine." Through whom do You visit "this vine"? Through the appointed one, who is **The Living God** <note>original: *El Chai*; associated with the Sefirah of Yesod</note>. Following this path, it is said: "O God of hosts, restore us; cause Thy face to shine, and we shall be saved." And this attribute is called:

## Splendor
In the Torah, in the verse: **"Thine, O Lord, is the greatness, and the power, and the glory, and the victory, and the majesty"** <note>1 Chronicles 29:11; here "majesty" is *Hod*</note>. The reason it is called **Splendor** <note>original: *Hod*</note> is because it clothes itself in the attribute of **Strength** <note>original: *Gevurah*</note> to humble enemies, to win wars, and to save the lovers of the Most High. The secret is: **"my comeliness was turned in me into corruption, and I retained no strength"** <note>Daniel 10:8; here "comeliness" is *Hodi*</note>. 

All the types of thanksgivings that David said in the Book of Psalms are attached to this attribute. The thanksgivings for miracles and for wonders are fixed in this place, in the secret of: "Give thanks unto the Lord, for He is good, for His mercy endureth forever" and "His wonders to the children of men." This is also the secret of the "Four who must give thanks" <note>referring to the four categories of people who must offer a thanksgiving blessing after surviving danger, based on Psalm 107</note>. From this place, He rewards the undeserving with good, when the Name **Lord of Hosts** <note>original: *YHVH Tzva'ot*</note> joins with Him, which is the secret of the three acts of loving-kindness. 

This is the secret of "Give thanks to the Lord for He is good," and the secret of the *Modim* prayer established in the service, and the secret of the "Rabbis' Thanksgiving" <note>original: *Modim de-Rabbanan*; a shorter version of the prayer said by the congregation</note> which is fixed in this place. For the "Lord God of Hosts" is the secret of the attribute of the **Disciples of the Sages** <note>original: *Talmidei Chakhamim*</note>. Because of this, it is said: "Disciples of the Sages increase peace in the world." Its secret is known to the masters of the esoteric tradition <note>original: *Yodei Chen*</note>, for they are the ones who increase peace. The secret is: "For the seed of peace, the vine shall give her fruit, and the ground shall give her increase, and the heavens shall give their dew" <note>Zechariah 8:12</note>. You already know that the "dew of Hermon" descends upon the mountains of Zion, which are **Victory and Splendor**. Because they are the place of thanksgivings, the Sages said that one must bow during the *Modim* prayer, which is the secret of bowing for those entering into Knowledge.

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Divine Names and the Sefirot (emanations of God), specifically how the Name **Adonai**—representing the Shekhinah or Divine Presence—acts as the instrument of divine justice and warfare on behalf of Israel.</meta>

As it is said: **"The Lord will fight for you, and you shall hold your peace"** <note>original: *Adonai yilachem lakhem*; Exodus 14:14</note>. If the attribute of **Adonai** <note>The Divine Name associated with the Sefirah of *Malchut* (Kingship), the interface through which God interacts with the physical world</note> were not present in the war, why would the Torah say: **"The Lord is a man of war"**? Rather, there was a war in the heavens. 

Because the people of Israel were rebellious at the Sea, the war against the **External Ministers** <note>The celestial guardian angels of the enemy nations</note> was difficult. What did the attribute of **Adonai** do? It clothed itself in three types of garments: the garment of Kingship—which is **Beauty** <note>the Sefirah of *Tiferet*</note>, the "Lord of Jacob"; the garment of **Kindness** <note>the Sefirah of *Hesed*</note> from the side of **Abraham**, to save Israel; and the garment of War from the side of **Strength** <note>the Sefirah of *Gevurah*</note>, which is **Isaac**, to fight against the heavenly chariots of the Egyptians and the wicked **Samael** <note>In Kabbalistic tradition, the primary accuser or "Satan" figure, and the spiritual prince of Rome/Edom</note>.

He clothed Himself in the secret of the "Three Garments," corresponding to the secret of the three verses: **"And he moved," "And he came," "And he stretched out"** <note>original: *Vayissa, Vayavo, Vayet*; Exodus 14:19-21. Each of these three consecutive verses in the Hebrew Torah contains exactly 72 letters, which Kabbalists use to derive the 72-fold Name of God used in miracles</note>. These are the three garments we mentioned that waged the war of the Egyptians—or rather, the **Prince of Egypt** <note>The celestial angel representing Egypt</note>—and defeated them. 

This is the secret of the verse: **"And He took off the wheels of their chariots, so that they drove them heavily"** <note>Exodus 14:25</note>. The Egyptians said: **"Let us flee from the face of Israel, for the Lord fights for them against the Egyptians."** The secret of "He took off the wheels of their chariots" refers to the **Supreme Wheels** <note>original: *Ophanim*; a high rank of angels often depicted as celestial wheels</note> of Egypt, which were driving the earthly Egyptians below in their pursuit. The power of a wagon is in its wheels; when those "wheels" were broken, the powers of the Egyptians below were broken. 

Consider this metaphor: when the wheels of a wagon are broken, how can it carry the load upon it? Likewise, when the **Heavenly Wheels** of Egypt were broken, all the Egyptian multitudes below—who are the "load" carried by those wheels—remained stuck, "driving heavily." When the Egyptians found themselves in this heavy state, they knew that a "Wheel" had fallen—meaning the Prince of Egypt on high. Thus it is written: **"Let us flee from the face of Israel,"** for the reason was: **"Because the Lord fights for them in Egypt"**—meaning, in the heavenly Egypt. 

Through whose power did **Adonai** win all these wars? Through the power of **The Lord of Hosts** <note>original: *Elohim Tzevaot*; a Divine Name associated with the Sefirot of *Netzach* or *Hod* (Victory and Splendor)</note> in which He was clothed. This is the secret of the verse: **"The Lord shall punish the host of the high heaven on high, and the kings of the earth upon the earth"** <note>Isaiah 24:21</note>. It also says: **"My sword shall be bathed in heaven; behold, it shall come down upon Edom"** <note>Isaiah 34:5</note>. And it says: **"Curse ye Meroz, said the angel of the Lord... because they came not to the help of the Lord of Hosts"** <note>Judges 5:23</note>.

<column-break/>

The Prophets describe Him as the one who gives life and deals death, who makes poor and makes rich, who brings low and lifts up, who wounds and heals. All these things are the actions of **Adonai**, operating upon every single matter according to the **Flow** <note>original: *Shefa*; the divine energy or influence</note> that comes to Him from the higher qualities—whether for good or for evil, for war or for peace, for famine or for plenty. 

Now, we must inform you: at the hour when the Name **Adonai** goes out to war, to fight in the heavens or on the earth, He clothes Himself in the **Instruments of War** from the "Mighty Ones of Dread" <note>The intense aspects of the attribute of Judgment</note>. By whose hand? By the hand of **The Lord of Hosts** <note>original: *Elohim Tzevaot*</note>, who draws the power, the might, and the warfare to the Name **Adonai**. 

This is the secret of: **"O God, why hast Thou cast us off? ... and goest not forth with our armies?"** <note>Psalm 60:12</note>. Contemplate this verse and you will find the Name **The Lord of Hosts** in the context of war; for the matter of war works in this way:

### Know
That when the Blessed Name wants to uproot or humble a nation below, He does not humble it until He humbles its **Prince** <note>Guardian Angel</note> first in the heavens. For our Sages, of blessed memory, said: "No nation falls unless its Prince falls first," as it is said: **"In that day the Lord shall punish the host of the high heaven on high, and the kings of the earth upon the earth."**

### And Know
And believe, that when Pharaoh pursued Israel, the scripture says of him: **"And he took six hundred chosen chariots, and all the chariots of Egypt"** <note>Exodus 14:7</note>. This is because the war was both above and below, and the war above in the heavens was powerful. The secret of "six hundred chosen chariots" is that the **Prince of Egypt** joined with **Samael**, and they added six hundred "chosen chariots" <note>representing demonic forces</note> in addition to the physical chariots of Egypt. 

Otherwise, why would it say "six hundred chosen chariots" *and* "all the chariots of Egypt"? Were they not all the chariots of Egypt? Rather, it says "all the chariots of Egypt" to include those that came to fight against **Adonai**. This is why Moses our Teacher, peace be upon him, said to Israel: "Be aware that the war is not yours, but belongs to the Blessed Name."

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic mechanics of divine warfare, asserting that earthly conflicts are reflections of battles between celestial "Princes" and that God (as Adonai) defeats nations by first breaking their spiritual "wheels" or guardians.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirot (divine emanations), focusing on how the lower attributes of Netzach (Victory) and Hod (Splendor) serve as the "pillars" or "legs" of the divine structure, supporting the flow of the Torah and the administration of heavenly justice.</meta>

And **Beauty** <note>original: *Tiferet*; the central Sefirah</note> is the secret of the letter **Vav** <note>the sixth letter of the four-letter Name of God, YHVH</note> of the Name, and those pillars—**Victory** and **Splendor** <note>original: *Netzach* and *Hod*</note>—are beneath the *Vav*. For the Name YHVH <note>The Tetragrammaton</note> is the secret of the number **six**, and it sustains six attributes: **Greatness, Strength, Beauty, Victory, Splendor, and Foundation** <note>original: *Gedulah, Gevurah, Tiferet, Netzach, Hod, Yesod*</note>. We learn that the "legs" <note>original: *shokayin*</note> are *Netzach* and *Hod*, which are the pillars. And upon whom are they pillars? Upon the **Foundation** <note>original: *Yesod*</note> of **Adonai** <note>The Divine Name associated with the Shekhinah, the Divine Presence on earth</note>. **Know** that in the place of *Yesod*, *Hod*, and *Netzach*, the **Seraphim** <note>A high rank of angels, literally "the burning ones"</note> were created, the ones with six wings. 

Why are they called *Seraphim*? Because they **burn** <note>original: *shorfim*</note> anyone who does not properly respect the glory of their Creator when they gaze at the **Work of the Chariot** <note>original: *Ma'aseh Merkavah*; the most profound and dangerous level of Jewish mystical study involving the vision of God’s throne</note> or engage in the secrets of the **Glowing Amber** <note>original: *Chashmal*; an enigmatic celestial substance mentioned in the visions of the prophet Ezekiel</note>. They also burn all those who [mis]use the Holy Names. 

These are the places that flow from the six orders of the **Mishnah** <note>The foundational text of the Oral Torah</note> until they reach the attribute of *Adonai*, which is called the **Oral Torah**. Because these pillars are "pillars of **six**," <note>original: *ammudei shesh*; a pun on the Hebrew word *shesh*, which means both "six" and "marble"</note> they are the secrets of the two Tablets on which the **Written Torah** was inscribed. This Written Torah comes from **Beauty** and clings to the **Oral Torah** like the two sets of the Mishnah. Thus, you see that the two Tablets and the six orders of the Mishnah come as one, and both are bound to each other in the secret of "His legs," which are the two "pillars of marble/six." For the existence of the Oral Torah depends on the unification of the Name.

**And know** and see that in these heavenly halls, there is one angel appointed over the **legal rulings** that emerge from the Great Court <note>original: *Beit Din HaGadol*</note> concerning all the people of the world. Under his hand are two scribes. Before those rulings go out into the world, the appointed one has the power to tear them up or amend them according to the power of **repentance** <note>original: *teshuvah*</note> of the person against whom the judgment was decreed. There are many great and awesome matters in the treasuries of *Netzach* and *Hod* that we will yet awaken you to, with the help of God.

->The Lord of Hosts is with us; a stronghold<-
->for us is the God of Jacob.<-

<column-break/>

Because the prophets knew this great secret, they were able to nullify any decrees issued by the Court on High. This is the secret of: **"A righteous man rules in the fear of God"** <note>2 Samuel 23:3</note>. The Holy One, blessed be He, said: "Who rules over man? I do. And who rules over Me? The **righteous man**; for I issue a decree and he nullifies it." This is why it is written: **"And Moses besought the face of the Lord his God"** <note>Exodus 32:11</note>, for he raised his thoughts to the place where a decree can be nullified. 

This is also the source for the tradition: "How do we know that a sealed decree can be torn up?" This "Who" <note>original: *Mi*</note> certainly refers to the place called "Who shall annul it?" <note>Isaiah 14:27</note>. This is known as the **Place of the Annulment of Vows**. For in this place, the annulment of vows depends. This is the secret of: **"With whom [Mi] did He take counsel, and who instructed Him?"** <note>Isaiah 40:14</note>. Certainly, the Holy One, blessed be He, took counsel with **"Who"** <note>meaning the Sefirah of *Binah* (Understanding)</note> to save and create the world. This is the **Place of Repentance**.

Therefore, even if a decree has been signed above, Israel can nullify it through the power of Repentance, which is the secret of **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*</note>, which is the secret of the word **"Who"** <note>original: *Mi*</note>, which is the secret of the Annulment of Vows. Regarding "His outstretched hand," who can turn it back? **"Who"** <note>*Mi*</note> certainly can turn back His hand! The reason is that His hand receives its flow and taste from the attribute called **"Who"**. This is the secret of: **"Lift up your eyes on high and see: Who [*Mi*] created these?"** <note>Isaiah 40:26</note>. And see the secret of what is said: **"Turn from Your fierce wrath and repent"** <note>Exodus 32:12</note>; everything depends on the Place of Repentance. "And his return [*teshuvato*] was to Ramah, for there was his home" <note>1 Samuel 7:17</note>, and "In the Year of Jubilee, you shall return [*tashuvu*] every man to his possession" <note>Leviticus 25:13</note>.

**And know** and contemplate the awesome wonders stated in the *Book of Formation* <note>original: *Sefer Yetzirah*; the earliest extant book on Jewish esotericism</note>: that God put **Understanding** in the brain, **Judgment** in the heart, and **Counsel** in the kidneys. Thus, observe that every decree made in the Court on High begins with counsel in **Understanding** and concludes the counsel in **Victory** and **Splendor**.
