<meta>This page continues the discussion of the lower Sefirot (divine attributes), specifically Victory (Netzach) and Splendor (Hod). The catchword from the previous page, "and the bone," leads into a discussion of the "Bone of the Heavens" and the mystical "Luz" bone in the human body.</meta>

# Know
that the two names beneath them <note>The higher attributes mentioned on the previous page</note>, which are **Victory** <note>original: *Netzach*</note> and **Splendor** <note>original: *Hod*</note>—which are the names **The Lord of Hosts** and **God of Hosts** <note>original: *YHVH Tzvaot* and *Elohim Tzvaot*</note>—are called **Skies** <note>original: *Shachakim*; often associated with the layer of heaven where manna is prepared</note>. 
The **Heavens** <note>original: *Shamayim*</note> are above, and the **Skies** are beneath them. 
Regarding these two names, it is said: "O inhabitant of the **Heavens**" and "You shall hear in the **Heavens**." Following 
this path, contemplate the secret of the verse: "Who rides the **Heavens** to your help, and the **Skies** in His majesty" <note>Deuteronomy 33:26</note>. Consider also the secret of "Let the **Heavens** drip down from above" <note>Isaiah 45:8</note>, followed by "and let the **Skies** pour down justice." And if 
you understand the secret of what the Sages said—"What is **Heavens**? It is **Fire** and **Water**" <note>original: *Shamayim* is a portmanteau of *Esh* (fire) and *Mayim* (water)</note>—the name of God is revealed within it. And know and believe 
that from the place called **Heavens**, the divine flow comes 
to the place called **Skies**. This is the secret of "Let the **Heavens** drip 
from above," followed by "and let the **Skies** pour down justice." 
So too, "Who rides the **Heavens** to your help, and the **Skies** in His majesty." 
The verse says, "He commanded the **Skies**" <note>Psalm 78:23</note>; the meaning of "He commanded the 
**Skies**" is from the place above them, and from them "He opened the doors of **Heavens**." And know that **Victory** 
and **Splendor** are called the **Bone of the Heavens for Purity** <note>original: *Etzem ha-Shamayim*; Exodus 24:10</note>. 
It is said of them, "as the bone of the heavens for purity," because 
their essence and power come from the place called 
**Heavens**. They exist in a secret known to those with understanding: 
that they are the actual "bone" or essence of the Heavens. They receive 
all the "essences" according to their kind. "On **this very** <note>original: *etzem*; literally "the bone of"</note> day" refers to receiving power. And the secret is: "All my **bones** 
shall say: O Lord, who is like You?" <note>Psalm 35:10</note>.

# And Know
that there is one bone formed 
in the human body called the **Luz** <note>A legendary indestructible bone at the top of the spine or base of the skull from which the body is rebuilt at the Resurrection</note>, 
which is the "essence" of man. It is his core 
and his root; from it, the person was formed through the "essence of the drop" <note>The seed of conception</note>. 
When a person dies, this bone does not diminish. It does not dissolve, 
even if they put it in fire—it is not burned. 
In millstones, it is not ground; by a hammer, it is not shattered. 
It is the bone that exists forever. It 
receives delight and pleasures after the death of a 
righteous person; concerning it, it is said: "He shall strengthen your bones" <note>Isaiah 58:11</note>. 
It is also the bone that receives punishment in the wicked, 
concerning which it is said: "Their iniquities shall be upon their bones" <note>Ezekiel 32:27</note>.

<column-break/>

regarding **Victory** and **Splendor**, "And I will lead," and the secret of "He poured out the good." 
And it is fitting for you to give thanks <note>A wordplay on *Hod* (Splendor), which also means "thanks"</note>, and if you remember this still, 
you will see great and wonderful things here:

# And Know

Both are called throughout the Torah **Skies** <note>original: *Shachakim*</note>, and these 
are the two Names called *Shachakim* in which 
they grind manna for the righteous. Let it be known to you with certainty 
and clarity: through the secret of the *Shachakim*, the **Manna** 
and the **Dew** are drawn down. The sign of this is: "And when the dew fell upon the camp 
at night, the manna fell upon it" <note>Numbers 11:9</note>. You already know that the "dew 
of Hermon descends upon the mountains of Zion." For whom? For the righteous 
among them, for **The Righteous One** <note>original: *Tzadik*; the attribute of *Yesod*</note> and **Justice** <note>original: *Tzedek*; the attribute of *Malkhut*</note>. Through every *Tzadik* and *Tzedek*, 
they receive the power of sustenance and life for all the righteous people 
named after them. And when Israel are righteous 
and assist the **Higher Chariot** <note>The divine structure of the Sefirot</note>, the Name 
rides in the Heavens with the help of Israel. Do not wonder 
that I say "with the help of Israel," for 
our Sages of blessed memory said that the upper worlds need 
the lower worlds for "assistance." Thus the verse says: 
"Who rides the Heavens **to your help**." And it says: "Or let him take hold of My strength, that he may make peace with Me" <note>Isaiah 27:5</note>, 
and it says: "And now, I pray, let the power of my Lord be great." 
Conversely, if—God forbid—one is negligent, it says: "You neglected the Rock who begot you" <note>Deuteronomy 32:18</note>, 
and "You have wearied Me with your words," and "He has drawn back His right hand." Let us return to our subject: I say 
that God rides the Heavens with the help of Israel. 
What does He do? He prepares the **Skies** to pour out 
blessing and life upon Israel. This is the secret of "And in His majesty, 
the **Skies**." After He rides the Heavens, what is the result? 
When He is exalted above the Heavens, He prepares the Skies, 
and this is "Which the **Skies** drop down and distill upon man 
abundantly" <note>Job 36:28</note>, and it says: "He commanded the **Skies** from above and the doors 
of **Heavens** He opened." And the great secret is: "Let the 
**Skies** drip from above, and let the **Skies** pour down justice." 
This is actual **Justice** <note>original: *Tzedek*</note>, for they grind manna for the righteous. And *Tzedek* 
is called **The Righteous One** <note>original: *Tzadik*</note>. And what is written afterward? "Let the 
earth open, and let them bring forth salvation." Behold, I am enlightening your eyes.

<summary>This page explores the mystical relationship between the attributes of Netzach (Victory) and Hod (Splendor), identifying them as the "Skies" where divine sustenance (manna) is prepared. It also introduces the concept of the "Luz" bone as the eternal, indestructible essence of the human body that bridges this world and the afterlife.</summary>
<keywords>Netzach, Hod, Shachakim, Shamayim, Luz bone, Manna, Divine Chariot, Resurrection, Tzedek, Tzadik</keywords>

--- Page 72 ---
<meta>This page continues the exploration of the divine attributes (Sefirot), specifically Victory (Netzach) and Splendor (Hod). The previous page discussed the "Skies" (Shachakim) and how the divine flow descends through them. This page shifts to the practical application of these attributes in prayer for children and the mystical source of prophecy.</meta>

Regarding the request for children, one directs their intention toward **Victory** <note>original: *Netzach*</note> and **Splendor** <note>original: *Hod*</note>. One intends to draw the power down to the name **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*; the Sefirah of Kingdom/Malchut</note>, from which all the hosts of heaven and earth are drawn according to their kinds. From them, the abundance comes to all species to make them sprout and give birth, each according to its nature. The "book of the generations of man" <note>a reference to Genesis 5:1</note> is suspended in this place. All of this occurs through the union of **Victory** and **Splendor** with **Foundation** <note>original: *Yesod*; the attribute associated with procreation</note>. Toward this attribute, all who came to ask the Name for children would direct their hearts. 

When Hannah prayed, she did not limit her request to the specific "chamber" <note>level of the divine hierarchy</note> she required. What is written? "She made a vow and said: 'O Lord of Hosts, if You will indeed look on the affliction of Your maidservant'" <note>1 Samuel 1:11</note>. By saying **Lord of Hosts** <note>original: *YHVH Tzvaot*</note>, she mentioned the source of all those who are born. To what height did Hannah’s prayer intend to reach? To the place of the **Supreme Fortune** <note>original: *Mazal*; literally "flowing," referring to the divine influence descending from the highest source</note> known in the secret of the Thirteen Attributes of Mercy established in the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*; the highest Sefirah</note>. From this "fortune," all things depend. Even a Torah scroll in the Temple draws from and receives from that place. This is what our Rabbis of blessed memory meant when they said: "Everything depends on fortune <note>original: *Mazal*</note>, even the Torah scroll in the Temple." 

Hannah, in her prayerful intent, ascended to the place of the Fortune, higher and higher. This is the meaning of "And she was in bitterness of soul, and prayed **upon** the Lord" <note>original: *al YHVH*</note>. It does not say "to the Lord," but "upon the Lord." You already know there are three Names: **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*</note> below, **YHVH** in the middle, and **I Will Be** <note>original: *Ehyeh*</note> above. Within the secret of **I Will Be**, the Fortune is suspended. This is the secret of "prayed upon the Lord"—literally above the name YHVH. This is why our Sages of blessed memory said: "Children, life, and sustenance do not depend on merit, but rather on fortune" <note>Talmud Moed Katan 28a</note>. The "place of merit" refers to the Court of the Seventy <note>the Sanhedrin/Divine Court</note> which is called **God** <note>original: *Elohim*</note>, the attribute of Judgment and Fear, as will be explained in its proper place with God's help. Rather, these things depend on "Fortune"—within the mighty Sefirah of the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*</note>. One who wishes to give birth to children through a miracle must ascend to this place. So too, one who wishes to attain life or sustenance beyond what is strictly decreed for them. 

How do we know these three things—life, children, and sustenance—are above this name? Regarding life, it is written: "In Your laws grant me life" <note>referring to Hezekiah</note>, where fifteen years were added to his days. Regarding children, it is written:
**Gates of Righteousness** <note>original: *Sha'arei Tzedek*; likely the title of the work or a reference to the Sefirah of Malchut</note>...

<column-break/>

And the root of this "bone" <note>likely referring to the Luz bone or the essence of the human frame</note> emerges from the "essence of the heavens" <note>referring to Exodus 24:10</note>. From there, it receives the strength, power, permanence, and stability of the entire human body. 

**Know** that the place of decision in man is within the secret of the bones. Because "jealousy" <note>original: *Kin’ah*</note> stems from the essence of the heavens, wherever you find "jealousy" in the Torah regarding the One Above—such as "For the Lord your God is a **jealous** God" <note>Deuteronomy 4:24</note>, or "A God **jealous** and avenging," or "The Lord will be **jealous** for His land"—these are jealousies without an "Al" <note>a specific grammatical/theological distinction</note>. Regarding this, it is said: "Envy <note>or jealousy</note> is the rottenness of the bones" <note>Proverbs 14:30</note>. The secret is: "The **jealousy** of the Lord of Hosts will perform this" <note>Isaiah 9:6</note>. Because jealousy is suspended in this place, it is said regarding the meal offering of the suspected adulteress (*Sotah*): "It is a meal offering of **jealousies**, a meal offering of remembrance, bringing iniquity to remembrance" <note>Numbers 5:15</note>. Those who know the fundamental principle know that **Jealousy** and **Remembrance** are forever joined. 

**Victory** and **Splendor** <note>original: *Netzach* and *Hod*</note> are the place from which the prophets draw their nourishment. From this place, the prophets emerged; therefore, you find the prophets saying in their prophecies: "Thus says the **Lord of Hosts**." You will find them mentioning three lower Sefirot in their prophecy: *Adonai*, *Elohim Tzvaot*, and *YHVH Tzvaot*. All of them are included in the secret of "Thus says the Lord of Hosts," as I have already informed you. 

Following this path, you will know that because these are the secret of the "visions" <note>original: *mar'ot*</note> through which the prophets look, at the time of prophecy, the bodies of the prophets would tremble and be seized by fear. Their whole body would shake and all their bones would be terrified until they were transformed from their original state, and their souls became refined. Only then would they see what they saw. Regarding this, it is said: "In a vision I make Myself known to him." Sometimes it would be a dream through the "swords of the partitions" <note>mystical barriers</note>, where parables and riddles would multiply. 

However, the "Chariot" <note>referring to Moses</note> prophesied without the mediation of **Victory** and **Splendor**, for he would enter into the innermost sanctum. This is what the verse means: "Mouth to mouth I speak with him" <note>Numbers 12:8</note>. Therefore, the Torah does not mention the name "Lord of Hosts" <note>original: *YHVH Tzvaot*</note> in relation to Moses, because his place was in the innermost depths. All the hosts above and below would go out and come in at his command.

**And every** person who comes to pray before the Lord, may He be blessed...

<summary>This page explains that the Sefirot of Netzach and Hod (Victory and Splendor) are the sources for procreation and prophecy. It uses the prayer of Hannah to illustrate how one can transcend standard divine decrees by appealing to the highest source, the "Supreme Fortune" in Keter.</summary>
<keywords>Netzach, Hod, Lord of Hosts, Hannah's prayer, Mazal, prophecy, Moses, jealousy, bones, Keter, Sefirot</keywords>

--- Page 73 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the divine attributes, moving from the general concept of the "Skies" to the specific Fourth Name of God, associated with the attribute of Victory (Netzach). It connects this attribute to the permanence of divine decrees and the architecture of the Temple.</meta>

### The Fourth Name of the Names
According to the order of the levels from bottom to top, this name is called **Lord of Hosts** <note>original: *YHVH Tzvaot*</note>. Although we have already explained most of the meaning of this name in the previous gate, just as the name **God** <note>original: *Ad-im*, a scribal variation of *Elohim*</note> is used, so too is **Lord of Hosts**. For this name draws from the side of **Mercy** <note>original: *Rachamim*</note> and **Lovingkindness** <note>original: *Chesed*</note>, and it brings down all blessings through the name **Living God** <note>original: *El Chai*; associated with the attribute of Foundation</note>. The specific name for this mercy is **Lord of Hosts**, which is also called **Victory** <note>original: *Netzach*</note>. This is the "Victory" that maintains His lovingkindness for Israel, as it says: *"Has His lovingkindness ceased forever (lanetzach)?"* <note>Psalm 77:9</note>. This is the source that decrees good decrees upon Israel by clinging to the **Supreme Crown** <note>original: *Keter Elyon*; the highest divine level</note>, and it never retracts them. This is the secret of: *"And also the Strength [Victory] of Israel will not lie nor repent"* <note>1 Samuel 15:29</note>. Because Israel always needs to strengthen themselves and cling to the attribute of **Victory**, which is the secret of the Right Hand that stands to overcome the Left. It is called **Victory** <note>original: *Netzach*</note> because it wins <note>original: *menatzeach*</note> the wars for Israel. Therefore, David, King of Israel, always had to say in the Book of Psalms: *"To the Chief Musician [Conductor], a Psalm of David"* <note>original: *La-Menatzeach*; the word for Conductor shares the same root as Victory/Netzach</note>. Wherever he mentions *"To the Chief Musician,"* which corresponds to the attribute of **Victory**, I must awaken you to a great and deep matter.

### Know
That three lamps shine from the whiteness of the **Eye of Mercy**. They are called **Lovingkindness** <note>original: *Chesed*</note>, **Beauty** <note>original: *Tiferet*</note>, and **Victory** <note>original: *Netzach*</note>. While they are united, the entire world is in joy, gladness, and perfection, and there is no adversary <note>original: *Satan*</note> and no evil occurrence.

### And Sometimes
This attribute is called **Victory** if it stands in its place below. In such a case, it is possible for a judgment to be decreed for good, but that judgment may turn into a punishment, all according to the actions of man. However, if the attribute of **Victory** ascends through the conduits to the heights and clings to the **Forehead of Will** <note>original: *Metzach ha-Ratzon*</note> at the head of the **Crown**, a good decree is made for Israel even if they are not worthy of that good. It does not turn back from it. The reason is that it has already ascended to the place that is not called "Man" <note>meaning it is beyond human influence or change</note>, which is the **Crown**. This is the secret of: *"Also the Victory of Israel will not lie... for He is not a man, that He should repent."* But if the attribute of **Victory** stands below

<column-break/>

in **Beauty** <note>original: *Tiferet*</note>, it is possible for Him to "repent" <note>to change the decree</note>. For that is the known place upon which there is a likeness as the appearance of a **man** <note>original: *Adam*</note> above it. Where it stands, so it is sustained.

### Know
That there are permanent courts of law called **Victories** <note>original: *Netzachim*</note>. And there is a **Victory of Victories** <note>original: *Netzach Netzachim*</note>. These are the ones who conquer all those who stand on the Left side and all the accusers. Therefore, the Scripture says regarding the side of the accusers in every generation: *"It shall lie waste forever and ever (lenetzach netzachim); none shall pass through it"* <note>Isaiah 34:10</note>. This means any city whose courts belong to the **Victory of Victories** in their places and levels—the side of the accusers has no existence there, only destruction and wandering.

### Know
That corresponding to the attribute of **Victory**, Solomon—peace be upon him—made the right-hand pillar called **Jachin** <note>original: *Yachin*</note>, which is **Victory**. One who knows the secret of **Jachin** and **Boaz** <note>the two pillars of Solomon’s Temple</note> knows how the soul and the spirit fly upward by means of the **Living God** <note>original: *El Chai*</note> and **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*</note> through the influence of these two pillars and the secret of their extension and their forms. By this pillar, the city of **Zion** and **Jerusalem** shall be built and established. The sign for this is: *"You, O God, provided in Your goodness"* <note>Psalm 68:11</note>. If you understand these three words—*Tachin be-tovat ani* ("You established in the goodness of the poor")—you will know how the **Lord of Hosts** prepares and sends the goodness through the attribute of **Living God** to pour out abundance and blessing into the attribute of **Lord** <note>original: *Adonai* / Kingdom</note> when it is empty, resembling a poor man who has nothing. The sign for this is: *"And let the dry land appear."*

### Know
And contemplate that the intent of all the conduits and all the unformed masses <note>original: *golmim*</note> received their forms and structures in this place, through the clinging of the Emanation to the Name of the **Lord**. All the limbs within man received their forms and structures in this place, and the secret is: *"He made you and established you"* <note>Deuteronomy 32:6</note>.

<summary>This page explains the divine attribute of Victory (Netzach) and its corresponding Name, "Lord of Hosts," describing how divine decrees become irrevocable when they reach the highest levels of the Godhead. It also links these mystical concepts to the architectural pillars of Solomon's Temple, Jachin and Boaz, and the formation of the human body.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the technical breakdown of the Divine Names and their relationship to the Sefirot (Divine Emanations). Having discussed the attribute of Victory (Netzach) on the previous page, the author now integrates it with the surrounding attributes of Splendor (Hod), Foundation (Yesod), and the lower realm of Sovereignty (Malchut), explaining how these form the mystical structure of the Tetragrammaton.</meta>

For God <note>original: *El*</note> is in the attribute of Victory <note>original: *N'*, an abbreviation for *Netzach*</note>, and the aspect of God <note>original: *Aldim*, a scribal variation of *Elohim*</note> is in the attribute of Splendor <note>original: *Hod*</note>. These four attributes are clothed in four "garments" for the attributes of Judgment <note>original: *Elohim*</note>. For the name "Hosts" <note>original: *Tzvaot*</note> includes three names, which are Victory, Splendor, and Foundation <note>original: *Yesod*</note>, as well as Judgment <note>original: *Din*</note>. When the scripture says "Lord, Lord, God of Hosts" <note>original: *YHVH, YHVH, Elohim Tzvaot*</note>, five Sefirot <note>divine emanations</note> are implied. Each and every one of these attaches to what is appropriate for it, and the four unite in the attribute of Foundation. Therefore, it is hinted at in the verse: *"O Lord God of Hosts, restore us; cause Your face to shine, and we shall be saved"* <note>Psalm 80:20</note>. Similarly, it is written: *"The Lord is near to all who call upon Him, to all who call upon Him in truth"* <note>Psalm 145:18</note>. The meaning of "Truth" in this context is that one must know how to pray by understanding which "place" <note>level of divinity</note> they stand in during that specific verse or name they are mentioning, and to which Sefirah they are directing their intention within the Blessed Name. Now, we must enter into the secret of these matters according to the intention of this book, with the help of the Supreme One who is always with us.

### The Principal Principle
### and the Root
You must know that these four letters, which are **Y-H-V-H** <note>the Tetragrammaton, the four-letter name of God</note>, include within them the ten Sefirot, and within them lies the complete, true secret. When a person knows how to direct their intention to the ten Sefirot within the four letters, this is the "unification" <note>original: *yichud*; a meditative focus</note> of the Will. The matter of unifying the ten Sefirot with the ten Names of the Blessed Lord is as follows:

### The Final
### He [ה]
The final letter **He** of the Name refers to the attributes of *Adonai* and *Shaddai*, which are the secret of the Divine Presence <note>original: *Shekhinah*</note>, the Kingdom <note>original: *Malchut*</note>, and the "Assembly of Israel," along with all the other designations known for this level in the Torah.

### The Vav
### [ו]
The letter **Vav** of the Name is the secret of six names known to the six Sefirot <note>the 'emotional' attributes from Chesed to Yesod</note> included in the letter *Vav*. These are: *El Chai* (Living God), *Elohim Tzvaot* (God of Hosts), *YHVH Tzvaot* (Lord of Hosts), and *Elohei Elohim* (God of gods). The secret of these Sefirot is that *El Chai* is known as the attribute of Foundation <note>original: *Yesod*</note>. It is also called the "Left Side" <note>The author here follows a specific kabbalistic mapping</note> along with other matters we have alluded to. *YHVH Tzvaot* is known as the attribute of Victory <note>original: *Netzach*</note> and is called the "Right Side." *YHVH* is the secret of Beauty <note>original: *Tiferet*</note>, which is the secret of the "Middle Line." *Elohim* is the attribute of Might <note>original: *Gevurah*</note> and Dread <note>original: *Pachad*</note> in the upper four, along with other designations.
<meta>catchword: designations</meta>

<column-break/>

For there are prophets who receive their vision through complete Mercy <note>original: *Rachamim*</note>, others through complete Judgment <note>original: *Din*</note>, and some through a mixture of Judgment and Mercy. It is written that Wisdom, Kindness, and Mercy are found in the World of Formation <note>original: *Yetzirah*</note> through the conduct of the Blessed Name with the world. Sometimes He conducts the world with complete Mercy, sometimes with complete Judgment, and sometimes with both. You will sometimes find the Name of the Blessed Lord joined with the word "Hosts" <note>original: *Tzvaot*</note>. You will only find this combination in the books of the Prophets and the Writings <note>the latter two parts of the Hebrew Bible</note>, such as when a prophet says, "Thus says the Lord of Hosts," or in the Writings in places like: *"The Lord of Hosts is with us; the God of Jacob is our refuge. Selah"* <note>Psalm 46:8</note>. **Know** that when this Name "clothes" itself in the Name called *Tzvaot*, it executes justice and judgment for both the good and the bad. Just as judgment can issue in three "tongues" <note>modes</note>—*El, Elohim, YHVH*—so too does He finalize the judgment by clothing Himself in *YHVH Tzvaot*. The secret is: *"But the Lord of Hosts is exalted in judgment"* <note>Isaiah 5:16</note>. I am hinting at a great matter in this verse: when the Lord executes judgment through the prophets, He "puts on the garments of vengeance" and clothes Himself in the Name *YHVH Tzvaot*, as it says: *"The zeal of the Lord of Hosts will perform this"* <note>Isaiah 9:6</note>. Because the prophets come to rebuke Israel and warn them, they must mention the Name *YHVH Tzvaot* in the context of testimony and warning for good or ill. When judgment was finalized against Israel because they provoked Him, the Patriarchs raised the "Lord of Hosts" above to perform righteousness <note>original: *Tzedakah*</note> with Israel, so that, Heaven forbid, no remnant or survivor would be left. This is the secret of: *"The Lord of Hosts is exalted in judgment, and the Holy God is sanctified in righteousness."* Consider how "the Lord is exalted in judgment"—how far does it reach? To the "Holy God," which is the attribute of Kindness <note>original: *Chesed*</note>. What does the Holy One, blessed be He, do for Israel? He performs righteousness <note>charity/mercy</note> so as not to bring total destruction upon them. This is the meaning of: *"Righteousness belongs to You, O Lord, but to us, shame of face"* <note>Daniel 9:7</note>.

### And sometimes
You will find these three names in the verse: *"God of Hosts, hear my prayer"* <note>Psalm 80:5</note>. The intention here is that the attribute of Mercy, the attribute of Judgment, and Dread each clothe themselves in their proper place.

->(Foundation and the four attributes)<-

It highlights the role of the name "Lord of Hosts" (YHVH Tzvaot) in prophecy and its function in balancing strict judgment with divine mercy to preserve the Jewish people.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the technical breakdown of the Divine Names and their relationship to the Sefirot (Divine Emanations). Having discussed the attribute of Victory (Netzach) on the previous page, the author now integrates it with the surrounding attributes of Splendor (Hod), Foundation (Yesod), and the lower realm of Sovereignty (Malchut), explaining how these form the mystical structure of the Tetragrammaton.</meta>

Indeed, the scripture says, *"The heavens for height, and the earth for depth"* <note>Proverbs 25:3</note>. The explanation of the verse is as follows: any matter that is hidden from the "hosts of the earth" is called "deep" in our language, because it is far below in the depths. Do you not see what the scripture says regarding the skin-affliction? *"The appearance is deeper than the skin"* <note>Leviticus 13:3</note>. And our Sages—of blessed memory—said: *"Like the appearance of sunlight, which is deeper than shadow"* <note>A Talmudic metaphor for visual perception</note>. It seems to me that what I have awakened in you regarding this matter is sufficient, and I must now lead you into other matters appropriate to the intention of the Name.

**Know** that these two attributes <note>Netzach and Hod</note> are the place of "counsel" in the Heavenly Court <note>original: *Beit Din shel Ma'alah*</note>, within the secret of: *"For the Lord of Hosts has purposed [counseled], and who shall annul it? And His hand is stretched out, and who shall turn it back?"* <note>Isaiah 14:27</note>. When the Blessed Name decrees in the Heavenly Court, which is called the Great Sanhedrin <note>The supreme legal assembly, here transposed to the celestial realm</note>, He consults with these two attributes, which are Victory (*Netzach*) and Splendor (*Hod*). This is the secret of what is written: *"This is the counsel that is purposed [counseled] upon the whole earth"* <note>Isaiah 14:26</note>. And even though the beginning of counsel is from the place of Understanding (*Binah*), the conclusion of counsel is in Victory and Splendor. This is the great secret of: *"With whom did He take counsel, and who instructed Him... and taught Him in the path of judgment"* <note>Isaiah 40:14</note>. And it says: *"Counsel is mine, and sound wisdom; I am Understanding [Binah], I have might [Gevurah]"* <note>Proverbs 8:14</note>. This continues until the verse: *"And his name shall be called: Wonderful, Counselor, Mighty God, Everlasting Father, Prince of Peace"* <note>Isaiah 9:5</note>. 
*   **Wonderful** (*Pele*) refers to Wisdom (*Chokhmah*).
*   **Counselor** (*Yoetz*) refers to Understanding (*Binah*).
*   **God** (*El*) refers to Greatness/Kindness (*Gedulah/Chesed*).
*   **Mighty** (*Gibbor*) refers to Power (*Gevurah*).
*   **Everlasting Father** (*Avi-ad*) refers to Beauty (*Tiferet*).
*   **Prince of Peace** (*Sar Shalom*) refers to Foundation (*Yesod*), which includes within it Victory (*Netzach*) and Splendor (*Hod*).

**Know** that the Great Sanhedrin of above is called the "Great Court," which is the Court of Power/Might (*Gevurah*). When they come to decree judgment upon any creature, they deliberate and take counsel with Understanding (*Binah*), which is above, and with Victory and Splendor, which are below. The secret of *"Counsel is mine"* refers to Victory and Splendor; *"I am Understanding"* refers to the attribute of *Binah*. *"To me belongs Understanding and to me belongs Might"*—this is the Great Court from the side of *Gevurah*. This is the great secret of: *"For the Lord of Hosts has purposed, and who shall annul it?"* The "who" <note>original: *Mi*</note> that annuls is found in the secret of *"With whom [Mi] did He take counsel, and who instructed Him,"* because...

<column-break/>

...regarding the matter of Hannah's prayer: *"And Hannah prayed upon the Lord"* <note>1 Samuel 1:10</note>. It does not say she prayed "to" the Lord. From where do we learn about sustenance? As it is said: *"Cast your burden upon the Lord, and He shall sustain you"* <note>Psalm 55:23</note>. It says "upon" the Lord and not "to" the Lord. The reason is that anyone who wishes to achieve these things <note>like children or sustenance</note> cannot achieve them in this world according to strict Law (*Din*) in the place called "Merit," which is the Great Court that stands opposite the Middle Column, which is the Tetragrammaton <note>YHVH</note>.

**According** to these paths, know and understand that even though we say that one who desires to understand his wish should direct his heart toward one of the ten Names known to correspond to the ten Sefirot, our intention is not to say that his focus should reach that Name and stop there. Rather, he should extend his intention until the **Supreme Source**, that place from which the thing he desires was "born," until the flow of blessing reaches that place he is focusing on. Then, the one who prays is found to be uniting the Sefirot and bringing them close to one another. 
Afterward, contemplate what the Blessed Name said to Abraham: *"So [Koh] shall your offspring be"* <note>Genesis 15:5</note>. This means you must ascend from the attribute of "So" (*Koh*), which is the lower attribute <note>Malchut/Sovereignty</note>, until the attribute of the "Long-Suffering/Infinite" (*Arich*), which is the highest, if you desire to obtain offspring. This is the secret that King David, peace be upon him, spoke of: *"A Song of Ascents: Out of the depths I have called You, O Lord"* <note>Psalm 130:1</note>. He called from that place called "Deep, Deep," which is the Supreme Depth, the Infinite (*Ein Sof*). Do not think that "depth" only means "below." Anything that is very far from being grasped is called "depth." Similarly: *"That which is far off, and exceeding deep, who can find it?"* <note>Ecclesiastes 7:24</note>. The secret of *"A Song of Ascents: Out of the depths..."* is this: how can one say "A Song of Ascents" (language of rising) together with "Depths" (language of descending)? This is what our Sages—of blessed memory—said: *"And Joshua lodged that night in the midst of the valley [depth]"* <note>Joshua 8:9</note>, which teaches that he lodged in the "depth of the Law" (*Halakha*). And in the *Book of Formation* <note>original: *Sefer Yetzirah*</note> it mentions: **The depth of height and the depth of bottom; the depth of East and the depth of West; the depth of North and the depth of South.** It equates "depth" to all directions and...

<summary>This page explores the kabbalistic concept of "Depth" as a metaphor for the hidden, lofty reaches of the divine rather than a physical downward direction. It interprets biblical verses and names to map out how the Sefirot (Divine Emanations) act as a Heavenly Court of counsel and how prayer must ascend through these levels to reach the Infinite Source.</summary>

--- Page 76 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Divine Names as they relate to the structure of the Sefirot (Divine Emanations). The previous page explored the attributes of Victory (Netzach) and Splendor (Hod) and their role as the "counselors" of the Heavenly Court. This page transitions to the most central Name, the Tetragrammaton (YHVH), explaining why it only appears in the Creation narrative once the world reached a state of completion.</meta>

and all created beings were in a state of completion and perfect repair, only then did He come and mention **the Lord God** <note>original: *YHVH Elohim*</note>. These are two Names that become united. **God** <note>original: *Elohim*</note> is the secret of the Name **My Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*—the Name associated with the lowest Sefirah, Sovereignty</note>. Even though **God** is the secret of Strength and Justice <note>term: *Gevurah* and *Din*; the restrictive or "harsh" aspect of the divine</note>, and even though He initially began to create the world with the Attribute of Justice—as it is written: *"In the beginning, **God** [Elohim] created"*—He saw that the world could not endure. Therefore, He arose and associated the Attribute of Mercy <note>term: *Midat HaRachamim*; the expansive or "kind" aspect of the divine</note> with it. This is the meaning of what is written: *"In the day that the **Lord God** [YHVH Elohim] made earth and heaven"* <note>Genesis 2:4</note>. This matter is true, correct, and established.

However, from the essence of the Attribute of Strength and the various "Outer Forces," influence flowed into the Name **My Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*</note>, and it was "clothed" in the Name called **God** <note>original: *Elohim*</note>, and thus He created the world. This is because the Name **God** is sometimes "diminished" and sometimes "full." It was necessary to mention it during the creation of the world while the world was not yet "filled" to its capacity and was still "lacking." This relates to the verse: *"Who makes a man mute [illaym], or deaf, or seeing, or blind? Is it not I, the Lord?"* <note>Exodus 4:11</note>. The secret of *"Who makes a man mute? Is it not I, the Lord?"* is found within the Name **God** <note>original: *Elohim*</note>. For if you remove from the letters of **God** <note>א-ל-ה-י-ם</note> the two letters of the Name **Lord** <note>the letters *He* (ה) and *Yod* (י) which form a short version of the Tetragrammaton</note>, then the remaining letters form the word **Mute** <note>original: *Illaym* (אלם)</note>. This is the secret of "The Lord is Mute" <note>This wordplay suggests that without the infusion of the Lord's Mercy/Life, the Name of Justice remains 'silent' or 'mute'</note>.

Therefore, the world was created with the Name **God**. But when all things were perfected and all creations were finished, and the world was in its fullness, it was necessary to mention the Name **Lord God** <note>original: *YHVH Elohim*</note>. This is what our Sages of blessed memory meant when they said: *"He mentioned a full Name over a full world."* Therefore, after the act of creation, the text begins to specify **Lord God** regarding the world's fullness. You should understand that in every place in the Torah where you find **Lord God**, it is a "Full Name." All matters found in a passage that mentions a Full Name come with all the conditions of the Attribute of Justice and the Attribute of Mercy combined. You will find this in the creation of the First Man <note>Adam</note>, in his judgment, and in his expulsion. Indeed, in every place in the Torah where you find **Lord God**, you must contemplate that the Attribute of Mercy is clothed within the Attribute of Justice. Each of these attributes acts in perfection, whether for judgment or for mercy. Contemplate this principle very deeply and guard it always.

# The Name
->The Fifth of His Names<-
->The Holy<-

**Know and believe** that the Name

**YHVH**, may He be blessed, according to the order of the "Degrees" <note>The Sefirot</note>, is the one called **The Lord**. He is the one who stands over all the Sefirot; the upper and lower realms are grasped by Him. Through Him, everything is held together from bottom to top, and influence flows from top to bottom. This is the Name toward which all eyes look. All His other Holy Names are drawn from it and return to it. He is the one who unifies all the Sefirot through the letters <note>The four letters of the Tetragrammaton: Y-H-V-H</note>. By Him, all created beings exist. All the "Chariots" <note>Celestial hierarchies or Merkavot</note> in the world, both upper and lower, are drawn from Him. All the spheres of the world, its foundations, and its structures hang upon Him. There is nothing in all the worlds that is not perfected by Him, by the Name of the Lord, may He be blessed. Before we enter [this topic], we must inform you of a principle in which you will find rest regarding many matters of the Name:

**Know** that you will find this honored and awesome Name, **YHVH**, used in several ways and for several matters in the Torah. Sometimes you will find it standing by itself throughout most of the Torah, as in: *"And the Lord spoke," "And the Lord said," "And the Lord did."* Sometimes you will find another of the Holy Names joined to it, as it says after the act of creation: *"In the day that the Lord God made earth and heaven."* You will not find this in the entire [initial] account of creation, where only **God** <note>Elohim</note> appears alone. But when it began to speak of the perfection of creation in the section *"These are the generations of the heavens and the earth,"* it began to mention the Name **Lord God**. We must inform you of the reason:

**Know** that since the Name is perfect and all things are held together by it—namely the Sefirot, as I have informed you—it was not necessary to mention it during the [initial] act of creation. This is because all things were not yet finished in their completion of work, and the world was not yet at its fullness; rather, all things were being made and coming into being. But when all things

Designations: **God** <note>original: *El*</note> is the secret of the attribute of **Kindness** <term>Chesed; the divine attribute of expansive love and benevolence</term> and **Greatness** <note>original: *Gedulah*</note>, and the "Good Wellspring" from the side of the Supernal Source, along with several other designations. ## The Letter Yod
of the Name is the secret of the Name called **Yah** <note>original: *Y-H*</note>, which is the secret of **Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah; the initial flash of divine inspiration</term>, which is called the "beginning of <unclear>wisdom</unclear>." It is the secret of **Awe** and the <unclear>R' Tzechan</unclear> <note>original: *ha-yirah ve-ha-r' tzechan*</note> and several other designations.

## And the Secret
of the "Tip of the **Yod**" <note>the small serif at the top of the letter Yod</note> is the secret of the **Crown** <term>Keter; the highest Sefirah, representing the Divine Will</term> within the Name called **I Will Be** <note>original: *Ehyeh*</note>, which is the secret of that which is called the **Infinite** <term>Ein Sof; God's essence prior to any manifestation</term>. It is called **Nothing** <note>original: *Ayin*</note>, as in: *"From where [Ayin] shall my help come?"* <note>Psalm 121:1</note>. Within that tip, the **Crown** becomes unified with the rest of the **Sefirot** <term>the ten emanations or attributes of the Divine</term>. Thus, this Name, which is **Yod**, includes all ten **Sefirot** within its four letters, encompassing all the Holy Names known in the Torah. Everything is suspended from this Name, everything is unified within it, and there is nothing in the world that does not emerge from it. Therefore, when a person recites the *Shema* <note>the central Jewish prayer declaring God's unity</note> and reaches the mention of "**The Lord is One**," it is proper to focus on His four letters and unify within them the ten Names with great intention, in His unique unity [according to] His Will. Then, he will be beloved and desirable before the Lord.

## And we
must inform you that an inner tradition has already been received regarding the matter of the *Shema* prayer. When a person recites the *Shema*, they should focus on the four letters of the Name in this manner: When saying ***Hear, O Israel***, and reaching the mention of **The Lord**, one focuses on this Name's first letter, which is the letter **Yod**. [When saying] ***Our God***, one focuses on the Name's second letter, which is the letter **He**. [When saying the second] ***The Lord***, one focuses on the Name's third letter, which is the letter **Vav**. [When saying] ***One***, one focuses on the Name's fourth letter, which is the final letter **He** of the Name. Thus you have learned that a person can have two different intentions while reciting the *Shema* for the sake of the unity of the Name, may He be blessed. One intention is to include the ten **Sefirot** and ten Holy

Names within the four letters of the Name, as I have informed you, and to focus on the four letters of the Name while mentioning **The Lord our God, the Lord is One**—each and every letter with the intention of its [corresponding] Name.

## And after
we have informed you of this, we must inform you how this Name is the foundation and essence of all calculations in the world. You already know that calculations, according to the view of the philosophers <note>original: *chachmei ha-mechakker*; literally "sages of inquiry"</note>, consist only of nine [digits]. Some believe that the "one" <note>the unit</note> is not a number at all, but we need not speak of this now except in general terms. For you already know that the system of the Lord, may He be blessed, is comprised from above to below of three Names, which are: **I Will Be** [Ehyeh], **Y-H-V**, and **My Lord** [Adonai]. The letters of the Great Name are none other than <unclear>ten</unclear>—the **Yod**. The arrangements of the **Chariot** <term>Merkavah; the mystical structure of the divine realm</term> do not deviate from three paths. You already know that the first three **Sefirot** are unified together: they are **Crown, Wisdom, and Understanding** [Keter, Chokhmah, Binah]. The three middle **Sefirot** are unified together: they are **Greatness, Strength, and Beauty** [Gedulah, Gevurah, Tiferet]. And the three final **Sefirot** are unified as one: they are **Victory, Splendor, and Foundation** [Netzach, Hod, Yesod]. Thus, there are nine **Sefirot** in nine systems. All these nine **Sefirot** flow their blessings upon **Foundation** [Yesod], which is the ninth Sefirah <note>This refers to the flow into the final gate</note>, and onto the tenth and final Sefirah, which is **My Lord** <note>original: *Adonai*; associated with the Sefirah of Malchut/Sovereignty</note>.

## And now
open your eyes and see that just as the system of the nine **Sefirot** depends on the Unique Name, so too are all the calculations in the world: they are only nine. For when you begin to count and calculate from one to ten, you will find that the [first] nine are individual units. But when you reach ten [**Yod**], the **Yod** is similar to the [**Aleph**] <note>the number 1</note> <unclear>Y-H Y-H Y-H</unclear>. Just as you count A, B, G <note>Aleph (1), Bet (2), Gimel (3)</note>, so you count Y, K, L <note>Yod (10), Khaf (20), Lamed (30)</note>. Thus, the **Yod** [10] is like the **Aleph** [1], the **Khaf** [20] is like the **Bet** [2], and the **Lamed** [30] is like the **Gimel** [3]. The same rule applies when you reach ninety: you will find that ninety-nine are tens. But when one counts...

It is found that those which proceed by tens <note>referring to a decimal-based counting system</note> leave only nine remaining. How so? A tenth of a hundred is ten; a tenth of ten is one. Thus, the entire calculation results in nothing but nine. Consequently, the Holy Names are uniquely associated with the **nine Sefirot** <term>Sefirot; the ten creative attributes or emanations through which God interacts with the universe</term>. These nine Sefirot are included within the Names **YHVH** and **Adonai**. Furthermore, you will find that the letters of the Alphabet consist of twenty-seven letters, which are three times nine, according to the method we call **AIQ BEKeR** <note>original: אי"ק בכ"ר; a Kabbalistic system of gematria where letters are grouped by their units, tens, and hundreds: 1-10-100 (Aleph-Yod-Qof), 2-20-200 (Bet-Kaf-Resh), and so on</note>:
**A-I-Q**, **B-K-R**, **G-L-S**, **D-T-N**, **H-N-Y**, **V-S-M**, **Z-A-N**, **Ch-P-Tz**, **T-Tz-Tz**. 
Do you not see that there are only nine triple-levels? The Aleph corresponds to the Yod, and the Yod corresponds to the Qof, within the secret of **AIQ**.

## And Now
we must inform you that the calculations in the world, which are nine, and the levels, which are ten, all depend on the Seal of the Great Name, may He be blessed, through the **permutation** <term>Tzeruf; the mystical practice of rearranging Hebrew letters to reveal hidden truths</term> of the letters. This follows the secret of the ten letters **A-B G-D H-V Z-Ch T-Y** in the permutation of **A-T B-Ch G-Z D-V** <note>These are specific systems of letter substitution</note>. Do you not see that the Name **Y-H** <note>Yah</note> fills the world and what is outside of it? And by way of the second permutation regarding the conduct of the levels: **A-Y B-T G-Ch D-Z H-V**. Do you not see that the **Yod** is the seal of all the world's levels and forms, according to the order of the Prophets? It follows that **Y-H-Y-H** <note>a variation of the Tetragrammaton</note> is the seal of all levels and divine conduct.

## And Know
that the **Four-Letter Name** <note>The Tetragrammaton, YHVH</note> is the secret of the Name of God, may He be blessed. It testifies and teaches that in the true essence of God, may He be blessed, there is no change, no diminish, and no lack. Rather, His truth and His greatness remain equal, both before He created the world and after He created the world. Even should He desire to destroy the world, He remains in His existence, equal and enduring eternally without any change in the world.

## And I Must
awaken you to a great matter. This is stated in the **Book of Formation** <term>Sefer Yetzirah; one of the earliest and most influential Kabbalistic texts, describing the creation of the world through the Hebrew letters</term>: that God, may He be blessed, created all creatures according to the nature of the letters Bet, Gimel, Dalet, etc. Through the permutation of the letters and their delivery, He created all the various species. You must understand what the term "permutation" <note>Tzeruf</note> means.

## Know
that if you see pieces of

<column-break/>

white silver, even though it appears white, you do not know for a certainty, without a doubt, if it contains an admixture of something else. When is the matter known to you with certainty? Only when you place it inside a **crucible** <note>original: *kur*; a melting pot for refining metal</note> over the fire for **refining** <note>original: *mצרף* - a pun on the word *Tzeruf* (permutation)</note>. Then you will know for sure if it contains any mixture, as it is said: *“And I will refine them as silver is refined, and I will test them as gold is tested”* <note>Zechariah 13:9</note>. Thus, the "refiner" <note>metzaref</note> refers to anything that tests the truth of the silver to see if it has a mixture. In exactly this way, when God, may He be blessed, joins these letters to one another to create words—as when you say the letters **Kaf-Tav-Resh** and make from them the word **Keter** <term>Keter; "Crown," the highest Sefirah</term>—you must permute these letters and test if they contain a mixture that is not in the word **Keter**. You will find through the permutation that it also contains the word **Karet** <term>Karet; "Excision" or being cut off spiritually</term>, which is the opposite of **Keter**. Who revealed this permutation? Just as the refiner from the outside tests the silver, so too the permutation from the outside tests the words. This is the secret of *“The word of the Lord is refined”* <note>Psalm 18:31</note> and *“The words of the Lord are pure words, silver refined in a crucible on the earth”* <note>Psalm 12:6</note>, and indeed, *“The word of the Lord is refined.”*

## And After

Now, see how much difference there is between the word **El** <note>God/Power</note> and the word **Lo** <note>No/Nought</note>. This is the secret mentioned by the author of *Sefer Yetzirah*: the letters **A-M-Sh** <note>Aleph, Mem, Shin; the three "Mother Letters" representing Air, Water, and Fire</note>. A male is formed through **A-M-Sh**, but a female is formed through **A-Sh-M** <note>guilt/asham</note>. Do you not see that **A-M-Sh** and **A-Sh-M** are the same letters? What caused one to beget male and the other female? You must say it is the reversal of the letters, even though the letters are identical. It is the same with this: **Pleasure** <note>original: ענ"ג; Oneg</note> when reversed and read back-to-front becomes **Plague** <note>original: נג"ע; Nega</note>. The *Sefer Yetzirah* states regarding the secret of all opposites that they were created in a single utterance: *“There is no good higher than Pleasure [Oneg]...”*

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Divine Names and the Sefirot. While the previous page focused on the numerical structure of the Hebrew alphabet (AIQ BEKeR), this page moves into the practical and mystical significance of how different Divine Names are paired in Scripture, specifically focusing on the relationship between the Tetragrammaton (YHVH) and Adonai.</meta>

The Name **Adonai** appears in two aspects: where the Tetragrammaton <note>original: יי' (YHVH)</note> precedes Adonai, and where Adonai precedes the Tetragrammaton.

When it says, *"YHVH Adonai is my strength"* <note>Habakkuk 3:19</note> or *"to YHVH God belong the escapes from death"* <note>Psalm 68:21</note>, or *"For my eyes are toward You, YHVH Adonai"* <note>Psalm 141:8</note>, you should know that when these two Names are mentioned as one, the Name's vocalization changes. It is read as **Elohim** <term>Elohim; the Divine Name associated with Justice and the Sefirah of Binah</term>. Even though it is written as YHVH, it is pronounced with the vowels of Elohim. This is a hint to the Attribute of **Binah** <term>Binah; "Understanding," the third Sefirah</term>, which unites with the Attribute of **Malchut** <term>Malchut; "Sovereignty," the tenth Sefirah</term>, represented by the Name **Adonai**. This is according to the will of the Blessed One.

**And know** and believe that when the order is **YHVH Adonai**, the divine flow <note>original: שפע (shefa)</note> descends from the Higher Sefirot until it reaches the Name **Adonai**, and then the entire world is blessed.

However, when **Adonai** is mentioned first and followed by **YHVH**, as in the verses: *"Adonai YHVH, what will You give me?"* <note>Genesis 15:2</note>, *"Adonai YHVH, You have begun..."* <note>Deuteronomy 3:24</note>, or *"Adonai YHVH, do not destroy Your people and Your inheritance"* <note>Deuteronomy 9:26</note>, know and believe that this sequence intends to **elevate** the Sefirot. It represents the unification from below to above, as the lower world seeks to be unified and cling to the Supreme Light.

Sometimes you will find the Name **YHVH** unified with **El** <term>El; a name of God meaning "mighty" or "powerful," associated with the Sefirah of Chesed (Mercy)</term>, as in: *"El YHVH, and He gave us light"* <note>Psalm 118:27</note>. The intention here is that when **El** is mentioned with **YHVH**, the entire world is under the influence of **Mercy** and **Loving-kindness** <note>original: חסד (Chesed)</note>, and all the lights are in their full perfection. This is because He is the Supreme "God of Kindness," the flow of "Kindness to Abraham" <note>Micah 7:20</note>. This is the secret of the Name *"YHVH, the Everlasting God"* <note>Genesis 21:33</note>. 

The Name **YHVH** is also the Name of Mercy. When the two Names **YHVH YHVH** are unified, all the Sefirot are in joy and perfection, and all created beings receive kindness and mercy from above to below. Thus, in the **Thirteen Attributes of Mercy**, these two Names are mentioned alone: *"YHVH, YHVH, El, merciful and gracious..."* <note>Exodus 34:6</note>, because within them, all kindness and mercy are included.

Sometimes you find the Name **YHVH** unified with **El** and **Elohim** together, as in: *"El Elohim YHVH, El Elohim YHVH, He knows"* <note>Joshua 22:22</note>, and as the Psalm says: *"A Psalm of Asaph: El Elohim YHVH has spoken and called the earth from the rising of the sun to its setting"* <note>Psalm 50:1</note>. Know that the intention here is for the Name to be "clothed" in every "garment," 

<column-break/>

whether for Judgment or for Mercy. How so? Consider the time when the children of Reuben, the children of Gad, and the half-tribe of Manasseh built an altar on the other side of the Jordan. All Israel intended to go up against them in war. But before they fought, they sent Phinehas and ten princes of Israel to speak with them regarding the matter of the altar.

What was the answer given by the children of Reuben, Gad, and the half-tribe of Manasseh? *"El Elohim YHVH, El Elohim YHVH, He knows, and Israel shall know; if it be in rebellion or in treachery against YHVH, do not save us this day! To build ourselves an altar to turn away from following YHVH, or if to offer on it burnt offerings or meal offerings... but out of concern for this matter, saying: In time to come your children might say to our children... 'See the replica of the altar of YHVH which our ancestors made, not for burnt offerings nor for sacrifice; it is a witness between us and you.' Far be it from us to rebel against YHVH..."* <note>Summarizing Joshua 22:22-29</note>.

Now, why did the children of Gad, Reuben, and the half-tribe of Manasseh need to mention these three Attributes as one? They mentioned:
1. The Name of Kindness (**Chesed**), which is **El**.
2. The Name of Judgment, Strength, and Punishment (**Din** and **Gevurah**), which is **Elohim**.
3. And the Name that is clothed in both of them, which is **YHVH**.

They were saying: "If we made this altar to YHVH as a memorial and not for sacrifice, may the Blessed Name flow upon us His blessings, goodness, and kindness through the attribute of **El**, who is the Seer who knows the intention with which we did this. But if we made the altar as a return to rebellion against the Tabernacle of YHVH... then may **Elohim**, who is the Witness, the Judge, and the Litigant, collect from us and punish us for this great transgression."

Therefore, they were required to mention **El Elohim YHVH**, which are the secret of the "God of Abraham," "God of Isaac," and "God of Jacob"—representing **Greatness** (Chesed), **Strength** (Gevurah), and **Beauty** (Tiferet) <term>Tiferet; the sixth Sefirah, representing the balance between Mercy and Judgment</term>.

So too, regarding the **Creation of the World**, it was necessary to mention in this Psalm: *"El Elohim YHVH has spoken and called the earth,"* because the Name "clothed" itself during the creation of the world in the garment of Great Mercy, and from there it clothed itself in the garment of Absolute Judgment, and from there in the Middle Path of Mercy and Judgment. All of this is within the secret of the creation of all creatures, for...

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the relationship between God and Israel, specifically how the Divine Names ensure that even in times of exile or "not being a people," the connection remains unbreakable. It transitions into a foundational Kabbalistic metaphor: the Divine Names as "garments" that the Infinite King wears to interact with the world.</meta>

And this is the meaning of *Aknios* <note>original: אקניאס; likely a mystical name or a derivation from a Greek term meaning "unshared" or "singular," used here to describe God's unique relationship with a people even when they seem abandoned</note> without a pen from God, as if to say: "They have turned away from God." And they said to Him: "Since the Blessed Name is *Aknios* without a nation <note>original: אקניאס בלא עם; a play on words suggesting that God could exist without a nation, yet the text argues the opposite</note>, they are not destroyed, and they cannot cast off the yoke of His Sovereignty, for the seal of His Name remains established within them." 

This is the secret of the verse: *"As I live, says the Lord YHVH, surely with a mighty hand and with wrath poured out will I be king over you"* <note>Ezekiel 20:33</note>. And it says: *"In the place where it was said to them, 'You are not My people,' it shall be said to them, 'You are the children of the Living God'"* <note>Hosea 2:1</note>. In the very thing through which they acted corruptly, the matter returns to its repair. You already know that **El Chai** <term>El Chai; "The Living God," the Divine Name associated with the Sefirah of Yesod (Foundation)</term> is the attribute of the "Sign of the Holy Covenant" <term>Sign of the Holy Covenant; Brit Milah or circumcision, which Kabbalists link to the Sefirah of Yesod</term>. Because the Children of Israel accepted the sign of the covenant of circumcision, it is said of them: *"Children of the Living God."* 

If you understand the depth of this, you will understand that even if Israel were to commit every provocation, they cannot be destroyed; rather, sufferings come upon them. This is the secret of the verse: *"When all these things come upon you, the blessing and the curse..."* and *"You shall take it to your heart..."* and *"YHVH your God will return your captivity and have compassion on you"* <note>Deuteronomy 30:1-3</note>. And it says: *"When you are in distress and all these things find you... He will not fail you nor destroy you"* <note>Deuteronomy 4:30-31</note>. The attribute of strict Justice turns back into the attribute of "Merciful God." Everything returns to Mercy. If you have eyes to see, look at how the denial of Israel is actually a clinging to the Blessed Name.

# And After
we have informed you of all these principles, we must return to inform you how the Great Name, **YHVH** <term>YHVH; The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter essential Name of God</term>, may He be blessed, is the leader of the entire world. Everything is within His power. We will explain how all the Names in the Torah are united in Him, and how the other titles in the Torah are like **Garments** <term>Garments; Malbushim, a metaphor for how the Infinite light is filtered through different attributes</term> which the King wears. 

These garments are not the actual essence of the King, but they are tools that the King puts on. Sometimes He wears royal clothes when the King is at ease, in quiet and security throughout His land, and His servants are all at peace. As the sign says: *"He made a feast for all his princes and his servants"* <note>Esther 1:3</note>

<column-break/>

*"...showing the riches of his glorious kingdom"* <note>Esther 1:4</note>. But sometimes the King is in a great war, and many enemies are plundering and looting His land, coming to take spoil and destroy His kingdom. Then the King wears different clothes: coats of mail, helmets, and other types of weapons—sword, spear, shields, and bows. Then the King and His servants are in haste and agitation, and His entire land is in turmoil until the wrath passes. 

And sometimes the King sits in His house, and none of His army or horsemen are with Him, only the members of His household: His wife and His children and those close to Him. The King removes most of His garments and dwells with them in quiet and joy. They do many things together that He cannot do in front of the common people. Because the members of His household are as dear to Him as His own limbs, He does not hesitate to remove some of His garments and tools in their presence. This is the secret of the verse: *"Your Teacher shall not be hidden anymore"* <note>Isaiah 30:20; implying a direct revelation without the "garment" of concealment</note>. 

And sometimes the King is secluded even from his household, and no one remains with Him except the Queen alone. Then the King strips off all His garments entirely and clings to the Queen, and they become one essence. As the sign says: *"I have taken off my robe; how shall I put it on again?"* <note>Song of Songs 5:3</note>. 

After this introduction:

# Know
that the Blessed Name is glorified and adorned with the Holy Names, and all of them unite in the truth of this Blessed Name. Each one indicates a specific matter in the way we have described. 

For instance, when the Blessed Name wants to have mercy on His world and show them grace, He wears the garment of Kingship, Judgment, and Mercy. The banner that carries the Name **YHVH** is the banner engraved with the form of **El** <term>El; A Divine Name often associated with the Sefirah of Chesed (Kindness)</term>. The sign for this is: *"Every man by his own banner, with the ensigns..."* <note>Numbers 2:2</note>. When the Blessed Name is happy with His creatures, He has mercy on His world and rejoices with them. This is the secret of: *"May the glory of YHVH endure forever; let YHVH rejoice in His works"* <note>Psalm 104:31</note>. Then He wears and is glorified by the **Gates of Justice** <note>original: שערי צדק (Sha'arei Tzedek); also the title of a related work by Gikatilla</note>...

But the letters of **YHVH** <note>The Tetragrammaton, the four-letter Name of God</note>, blessed be He—you will find in them twelve permutations <note>Hebrew: *Tzerufim*; the different ways the four letters can be arranged</note>, and you will not find in them a reversal <note>Hebrew: *Hafuch*; a rearrangement that changes the meaning into an opposite</note>. Rather, all permutations of the Blessed Name all point to a single matter: and that is the secret of the states of being <term>Havayot; modes of existence that flow from God</term> regarding the Name, blessed be He, and His existence. This is within the secret of: *"For I am the Lord [YHVH], I have not changed"* <note>Malachi 3:6</note>. That is to say, in all the opposites in the world you will find change, except in His existence, for there is no change in Him in the world. That is to say: just as there is no change in Me, so the children of Jacob have no end. And this is what is said: *"But you who cleave to the Lord..."* <note>Deuteronomy 4:4, implying those who attach themselves to the Unchanging survive eternally</note>. And following this path, understand that just as the Blessed Name has twelve permutations, so according to their number came the twelve tribes of the children of Jacob. Now see how much difference there is between "God" [El] and "Not" [Lo]. And if in two letters there is such a reversal, how much more so with three letters, for as letters increase, the reversals increase. <meta>This page continues the discussion on the nature of the Divine Names. While the previous page focused on the internal structure and permutations of the Tetragrammaton (YHVH) to show God's unchanging nature, this page shifts to how that singular essence branches out into various attributes or "garments" like Mercy and Judgment, specifically through the imagery of Ezekiel’s Chariot and the "thirty appellations" of God.</meta>

...and all the host of heaven stand over Him from His right and from His left, and by the word of all of them, they are upheld by the Name of the Name, blessed be He. This is the secret of: "from His right" for merit, and "from His left" for liability; the attribute of *El* <note>original: אל; often associated with God's kindness</note> for mercy and merit, and the attribute of *Elohim* <note>original: אלהים; often associated with divine justice</note> for judgment and liability.

# And After
we have informed you of this, we must enter and explain the secret of the Appellations <term>Kinuyim; descriptive titles of God like "Great," "Mighty," or "Merciful" that act as "garments" for His essence</term> which are alluded to at the beginning of Ezekiel's Chariot <note>The mystical vision of the divine throne-chariot in Ezekiel chapter 1</note>: *"And it was in the thirtieth year"* <note>Ezekiel 1:1</note>. These are the secret of the thirty Appellations that support the Chariot. And when the Name, blessed be He, judges all His creatures for good or for evil, to grant reward or punishment, or for death or for life, as the world deserves—even though His judgments, blessed be His name, are all built upon mercy, nevertheless, at the conclusion of judgment He does not waive anything. And even though He is long-suffering and waits, ultimately He concludes the judgment. This is what the Sages of blessed memory meant when they said: *"Whoever says the Merciful One is a waiver [of justice], his life shall be waived"* <note>original Aramaic: מאן דאמר רחמנא וותרן הוא מוותרין לו חייו; from the Talmud, Bava Kamma 50a</note>; rather, He is long-suffering but eventually collects His due. Therefore, you must know that each of these three names—which are *El*, *Elohim*, and **YHVH**—carry the Appellations like one who puts on clothing; thus, everything is found to be Appellations, and all of them depend on the Name, blessed be He. All these Appellations are called "wings," and the sign for this is: *"Spread your wings over your handmaid"* <note>Ruth 3:9</note>. And when the Holy One, blessed be He, judges all His creatures according to the judgment that is proper before Him, it is through those thirty Appellations. These are the "variations" <note>A wordplay on *Kinuyim* (Appellations) and *Shinuyim* (Changes/Variations)</note> by which creatures differ from one another, whether for good or for evil. How so? This righteous person is different from that wicked person; just as this one differs from that one, so their judgments differ one from another. And according to the variations among human beings, so are the variations in the judgments proper to them. And according to the variations in the proper judgments, so are the variations found in the thirty Appellations that cause the judgment. For example: "Merciful" acts upon His judgment and concludes it for good; "Man of War" <note>Exodus 15:3</note> concludes His judgment for punishment; "Truth" concludes His judgment for both mercy and punishment, according to the state of

the Names, such as *El*, *Elohim*, *Shaddai*, *Tzevaot* <note>Commonly translated as "Almighty" and "Lord of Hosts"</note> and the like—in which attribute He stands or in which garment He is clothed. When one calls upon one of the names, such as *El*, *Elohim*, *Eloah*, or *Shaddai* and their like, you must know who are the camps and hosts of *El*, or who are the camps and hosts of *Elohim*. In this way, you will understand that all the Names and the Appellations are all dependent on the Name of the Name, blessed be He.

<meta>This page continues the exposition of how the Divine Names function as "garments" for the Infinite. Having established that the thirty Appellations support the Chariot, the text now focuses on the two primary "flags" or standards of divine conduct: the Attribute of Mercy (associated with the name El) and the Attribute of Judgment (associated with the name Elohim). It explains how these names intermingle during judgment to ensure the world’s survival.</meta>

...in His garments, which are called *El* <note>original: אל; the name of God representing pure kindness</note>, and the secret of *El* is: "A King sitting upon a throne of mercy." And this is the secret of the verse: *"For the Lord your God is a merciful El"* <note>Deuteronomy 4:31</note>, and the sign of this is: *"For the Lord is a great El, and a great King above all gods"* <note>Psalm 95:3</note>. The meaning of the "Cherub" <note>An angelic figure, here used to represent a specific divine manifestation</note> is the Attribute of Mercy, a "great King above all gods." "Gods" <note>original: אלהים (*Elohim*)</note> refers to the Attribute of Judgment. From here you can understand the secret of the verse: *"And God [Elohim] tested Abraham"* <note>Genesis 22:1</note>; for God placed the "flag" <term>Degel; a standard or banner representing a specific divine power or camp in the celestial hierarchy</term> in the hand of Abraham, whose own attribute is *El* <note>Mercy</note>. This is the secret of why we say: *"The God [Elohei] of Abraham, the God [Elohei] of Isaac, and the God [Elohei] of Jacob"* and not *"and the God of Isaac"* <note>The text notes the repetition of the title "God of" for each patriarch individually</note>, because the flag of Isaac—which is *Elohim* [Judgment]—is held in the hand of Abraham <note>Meaning Judgment is tempered or "sweetened" by Mercy</note>.

### The Attribute of El:

### And when the Name, blessed be He, is angry with His world
on account of their evil deeds, He then brings forth the "vessels of His indignation" and clothes Himself in garments of vengeance. He repays every person according to the ways of the world, with the specific judgment they deserve. The secret of this is: *"Let God [Elohim] arise, let His enemies be scattered"* <note>Psalm 68:2</note>, and the sign is: *"For judgment belongs to God [Elohim]"* <note>Deuteronomy 1:17</note>. At that time, the form of the name *Elohim* <note>Judgment</note> is engraved upon the flag of the Tetragrammaton [YHVH]. This is hinted at in the secret of the verses: *"Rise up, O Lord [YHVH], and let Your enemies be scattered"* <note>Numbers 10:35</note> and *"Let God [Elohim] arise, let His enemies be scattered."* If so, why does it say *Lord [YHVH]* in one place and *God [Elohim]* in the other? I have already informed you that all the Names are suspended from the Name of the Name, blessed be He, and He clothes Himself in them. When it says, *"Rise up, O Lord [YHVH],"* the Name *YHVH* has taken up the "vessels of His indignation," and *Elohim* is engraved upon His flag—this is the secret of *"Let God [Elohim] arise."* And when the Name, blessed be He, brings the people of the world into judgment, He wears neither garments of pure mercy nor garments of pure judgment. Rather, because He does not act with pure judgment nor with pure mercy, but with the Attribute of Judgment mingled with the Attribute of Mercy. The secret of this is: *"Slow to anger [Arkh-Appayim] and abundant in kindness [Rav-Hesed]"* <note>Exodus 34:6</note>. Since the verse says "abundant in kindness," it testifies that there is a mixture of judgment within it; for if it were entirely kindness, why would the scripture say "abundant"? What need have we for "much" or "little"? Rather, the majority is the judgment of kindness, and the minority is judgment. And when the Name, blessed be He, judges the world, He is sometimes called "The Lord [YHVH] in judgment,"

<column-break/>

and sometimes He is called "God [Elohim] in judgment." It all depends on the attribute the Name intends to use and according to what the world deserves. Thus the scripture says: *"For Your hand, Your hand has judged me"* <note>Likely a variation of Psalm 38:3</note>, and it says: *"Correct me, O Lord [YHVH], but with justice"* <note>Jeremiah 10:24</note>, and it says: *"The Lord [YHVH] will enter into judgment"* <note>Isaiah 3:14</note>. Here, judgment is attributed to the Lord [YHVH]. Yet at other times, judgment is attributed to God [Elohim], as it says: *"When God [Elohim] arose to judgment"* <note>Psalm 76:10</note>, and it says: *"For God [Elohim] is the judge"* <note>Psalm 75:8</note>. This secret depends on the depth of the case: the Name, blessed be He, is called by the name that matches the final verdict of the trial. If the judgment is mostly mercy, it is *YHVH*; if it leans toward strict justice, it is called *Elohim*. Following this path, contemplate every place in the Torah where you find the Crowned Name <term>The Tetragrammaton, YHVH; the highest and most essential name of God</term> called alongside one of the other names; then you will understand that the Name *YHVH* is "clothed" in known "gardens" <note>Possibly a metaphor for the emanations/Sefirot</note> as the hour requires.

### And Know
that the other Appellations <term>Kinuyim; descriptive titles</term>—those which are not the Holy Names and which are permitted to be erased <note>According to Jewish law, the seven primary Names of God cannot be erased, but descriptive titles can be</note>—such as *Merciful*, *Gracious*, *Devout*, *Holy*, and the like, are the "vessels" for the Holy Names that may not be erased. Through them, the Holy Names perform their actions. For example: the Name appointed over the "Flag of Kindness" is *El*. Its Appellations—which take the form of its soldiers—are *Merciful*, *Gracious*, *Devout*, *Slow to Anger*, *Abundant in Kindness*, *Holy*, *Forgiving*, and *Pardoning*. The Name appointed over the "Flag of War and Judgment" is *Elohim*. Its Appellations—which take the form of its soldiers—are *Mighty*, *Judge*, *Magistrate*, *Strong*, *Great in Power*, *Man of War*, *Hero*, *Visiting Iniquity*, and *Paying Recompense*. The Name appointed over the judgment that includes both Mercy and Judgment—which is *YHVH Elohim*—is the Name called *YHVH*, blessed be He. He possesses both types of Appellations, which are His soldiers; He is the one who *"forgives iniquity and passes over transgression,"* *"Truth,"* *"Exalted,"* *"Preserver of Kindness,"* *"Dweller of Eternity,"* and *"Holy."* We have now informed you of the general rule, and I shall continue to enlighten your eyes regarding the Name, blessed be He.

at the time that He, blessed be He,
is called by one of
the Names...
