<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirot (divine attributes), shifting focus from the attribute of Judgment (Gevurah), associated with Isaac on the previous page, to the attribute of Kindness (Hesed) and Greatness (Gedulah), which are personified by the patriarch Abraham.</meta>

And it is written: "The kindness of God" <note>original: *Hesed El*</note>. And it is written: "Kindness of the Most High." And it is written: "Eternal kindness." And it is written: "Kindness to Abraham" <note>Micah 7:20</note>. Abraham revealed the path to attaining kindness to the prophets. And it is written: "All the paths of the Lord are kindness and truth" <note>Psalms 25:10</note>. And it is written: "You will give truth to Jacob and kindness to Abraham." But Judgment <term>Din — divine justice and restriction</term> belongs to Isaac, to guard the breach so that the "destroyers" <note>harmful spiritual forces</note> do not enter. And kindness is included in all the paths of the Name <note>God</note> through seventy-two bridges <note>A reference to the 72-letter Name of God, often associated with the attribute of Kindness</note> until they reach the attribute of Truth. And it is written: "My kindness shall not depart from you." And it is written: "The faithful kindnesses of David." From Abraham, these were drawn down to David. Therefore, it is said: "The Shield of David" and "The Shield of Abraham." His throne shall be established in kindness, a fearsome throne. Therefore, you will find that in the "Great Hallel" <note>Psalm 136, a liturgical song of praise</note> it is engraved: "For His kindness endures forever" <note>original: *Ki Le-olam Hasdo*</note> twenty-six times. All of this, Abraham drew down below <note>The number 26 corresponds to the numerical value of the four-letter Name of God, YHWH</note>. Therefore, he merited an abundance of kindness; he tips the scale toward kindness. And this attribute is called:

# Greatness
<note>original: *Gedulah* — another name for the Sefirah of Hesed</note>

->**Because of "Long-suffering"**<-
->**and "Great in Kindness."**<-

And behold, I am hinting at that High Mountain called "The Infinite" <term>Ein Sof — God's essence prior to self-manifestation</term>, for "The Lord is a Great God." Therefore, "To You, O Lord, is the Greatness and the Might" belongs to You certainly. The Infinite is everything, and all else is small. Therefore: "Strength is in Your hand, and Might is in Your right hand." And "God tested Abraham." "And he took the fire and the knife in his hand." "Who does great things beyond searching," and so on. Therefore, it is called Greatness <note>*Gedulah*</note>. "Great is the Lord," and so on. How many wonders are hinted at in the "Crown of the Sefirot" <note>*Keter*</note>! Woe to the creatures for the shame of the Torah! They do not know and they do not understand; they walk in darkness. For the Torah is open before them, yet in their folly, they close off their own arguments. And this is the attribute of Abraham. And he is close to the border of "Understanding" <term>Bina — the third Sefirah, the "Mother" of the lower attributes</term>. Therefore, he looked through astrology <note>original: *astrologia/iztagnonit*</note> and could not find a son born of a legal wife, but only from foreign women who were near his border. This was until he drew down [influence] from "Understanding" <note>*Bina*</note>, which is close to him.

<column-break/>

Peace began to draw the hidden powers from the "Highest Secret" downward. For in the days of Adam the first man, the Righteous One was suppressed; and the people of the "Generation of the Division" <note>The builders of the Tower of Babel</note> and the "Generation of the Flood" exchanged [the truth for lies]. They darkened the rivers and blocked all the springs. All the Sefirot withdrew higher and higher, for there was no one seeking and no one asking. Therefore, the Name, may He be blessed, was not known in the world because the channels of the Divine Presence <term>Shekhinah — the indwelling presence of God</term> were blocked by evil deeds. This continued until our father Abraham, peace be upon him, came and repaired the channels to draw "living waters" from "Understanding" <note>*Bina*</note>, which is the secret of the "Upper Rivers." Therefore, it is written: "And he moved from there to the mountain east of Bethel" <note>Genesis 12:8</note>. From the "Ancient of the World" <note>God</note>, he moved the Upper Waters from "the Mountain"—which is "Understanding" <note>*Bina*</note>—until he drew them to "Bethel" <note>literally "House of God," referring to the lower Sefirot</note> through a single channel called "Kindness" <note>*Hesed*</note>. And this is the "Right Side." Therefore, all the journeys of our father Abraham, peace be upon him, were toward the Right. And this is the secret of: "And Abram journeyed, going and journeying toward the South" <note>Genesis 12:9; in Hebrew geography, the South is the "Right"</note>. If not for the "Right" of our father Abraham, the attribute of the Right, the South would be dry and parched. But through the "Right" of Abraham, the Right was filled with water. "I will open rivers on the high hills." Therefore, it is said of him: "The word of the Lord to my master: Sit at my right hand." And through Abraham, the Right was exalted. Therefore: "The right hand of the Lord is exalted," and "His right hand embraces me." How great was his level, for he drew the Upper Waters from "Understanding" <note>*Bina*</note> downward and prepared channels for them to flow to Bethel. And this is the secret of: "God comes from Teman" <note>Habakkuk 3:3; *Teman* means South/Right</note>. This includes the secret of drawing influence from "Understanding" to the "Right." "On the way to Timnah" and "They shall behold the image <note>*temunat*</note> of the Lord." And this attribute is called:

# Kindness

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirot—the divine attributes or emanations through which God interacts with the world. Having previously discussed the attribute of Judgment (Gevurah), the text now shifts to the seventh Sefirah, commonly known as Hesed (Kindness) or Gedulah (Greatness), which is represented by the Divine Name "El."</meta>

# The Name El

The seventh of the <term>Sefirot — divine attributes or emanations</term> is called—as you already know—the Name **El**. This is the attribute of Mercy. I will further clarify for you that when this Name alludes to the <term>Keter — "Crown," the highest and most hidden Sefirah</term>, which is the realm of absolute compassion, it is called **El Elyon** <note>original: "God Most High"</note>. This is the secret of the phrase: "God, the King who sits upon the throne of mercy." 

However, when speaking of the Name as it manifests in the world—where justice and mercy are mingled together—it is called **El Olam** <note>original: "God of the World" or "Eternal God"</note>. This is the attribute of the "Kindness of Abraham," which is also called "Eternal Kindness." There is also a "Higher Kindness" associated with **El Elyon**. 

Furthermore, when this attribute connects to the <term>Yesod — "Foundation," the Sefirah of connection and procreation</term>, similar to the concept of the "faithful kindnesses of David," it is called **El Shaddai** <note>original: "God Almighty"</note>. At that point, the spiritual union is found between the Foundation and the <term>Malchut — "Kingship," the final Sefirah representing the physical world</term>. And when it draws kindness and mercy from the <term>Binah — "Understanding," the source of divine intuition</term>, it is called **El Chai** <note>original: "Living God"</note>. We have already explained this above.

## Truly,
We must awaken your attention at this point to the verse: "And his name shall be called Wonderful, Counselor, Mighty God, Everlasting Father, Prince of Peace" <note>Isaiah 9:5</note>. This verse lists the order of the Sefirot. When it says that the Great Lord <note>original abbreviation: "יהו"ד" — referring to the four-letter Name of God, YHWH</note> is "God," it refers to the secret of the two "measures" and the building between them, as we mentioned regarding the weaving of the Names. For the phrase "The Lord your God is a merciful God" <note>original: "El Rachum YHWH Elohecha"</note> represents the secret of the union between Kindness and Mercy. 

We have already explained above that **El** represents the secret of the union of the initial Will with Thought for the creation of the world through the combinations of letters. The beginning of all letter combinations through which the world was created is called **El**. It is the secret of the "central point" upon which all the "spheres" or "wheels" turn—whether in the intellectual world, the lower world, or in all other structures. Indeed, the wheel of the Lord surrounds this; the wheel of the celestial structures turns upon it. This secret is entirely hidden within the mystery of *Elohim* <note>the Name of God associated with justice</note> and the mystery of *Eloah*. We have already explained the allusion found in the conclusion of the Book of Psalms: "Hallelujah! Praise God in His sanctuary; praise Him in the firmament of His power" <note>Psalm 150:1</note>. Here is an allusion to the combination of the letters and the secret of the structure of the celestial wheel and its movement.

<column-break/>

Therefore, our father Abraham—peace be upon him—informed all who enter the world of the secret of the truth of the Lord, blessed be He, through the Name **El**. This is the secret of the verse: "And he called there upon the name of the Lord, the Eternal God" <note>original: *El Olam*; Genesis 21:33</note>. For **El** is the secret of the world's creation and the secret of how it is conducted through the attributes of Judgment and Mercy. 

The Blessed Name cannot be known directly through the Tetragrammaton <note>the four-letter Name, YHWH</note>, for that Name is deep and hidden; therefore, it is not even pronounced as it is written. Rather, He is known through His actions, as it says: "Lift up your eyes on high and see: who created these?" <note>Isaiah 40:26</note>. From the created things, we perceive the Blessed Name. This is the secret of "And he called there upon the name of the Lord, the Eternal God." It is "the God of the world" (*El Olam*) specifically because our father Abraham (peace be upon him) held fast to the attribute of **El**, which is the secret of the world of Mercy and the secret of the world's creation. Thus, this attribute is called by his name. 

Wherever the Name **El** appears on its own, it refers to the attribute of Abraham. As it is written: "I said: The world shall be built by kindness" <note>Psalm 89:3</note>. The building of the world is Kindness, in the secret of *El Olam*. And it is written: "The kindness of God <note>original: *Hesed El*</note> is all the day." Therefore, it is "Kindness to Abraham." One who knows these essential principles knows the secret of the verse: "Who is a God like You, pardoning iniquity and passing over transgression" <note>Micah 7:18</note>. This Sefirah is also called:

## Living Waters
<note>original: *Mayim Chayim*</note>
Because this secret emerges from the *Binah* (Understanding), which is called:

## Rehoboth of the River
<note>original: *Rehovot HaNahar* — literally "wide places of the river," referencing Genesis 36:37</note>
And in this attribute, there are seventy-two "bridges" through which the Upper Waters flow in the order of the three verses: *Vayissa, Vayavo, Vayet* <note>Exodus 14:19-21. Each verse contains 72 letters, which Kabbalists use to derive the 72-letter Name of God</note>. By the power of these waters, the [Red Sea] waters were split, and Israel passed through the midst of the sea on dry land. Those waters represent the Kindness shown to Israel. One who knows the order of the Divine Chariots will understand deep secrets regarding this matter. This Sefirah is also called:

## Right Hand
<note>original: *Yamin*</note>
And from it, Israel merited to receive the Torah from His right hand, for our father Abraham—peace be upon him—

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirah of Hesed (Kindness) and its connection to the higher divine realms, specifically how the Names of God act as channels for divine flow between the upper and lower worlds.</meta>

Our ancestors knew the secret of this verse: that the "place of healing" is also the "place of repentance" <note>This refers to the Sefirah of **Binah** (Understanding), which is considered the source of cosmic return and healing.</note>. It was from there that Abraham drew his spiritual sustenance when he clung to the attribute of **Kindness** (*Hesed*). What we have explained thus far is sufficient.

Because of this, you will find it written in several places as **YHVH Adonai** <note>The Tetragrammaton followed by the Name associated with Lordship/Sovereignty.</note>. This is the secret of the "divine springs" that flow down until they reach the **Kingship** (*Malchut*) <note>The final Sefirah, representing our physical reality.</note>. This is also the secret behind the days of **Passover**, the eight days of the **Festival** <note>Sukkot</note>, and the secret of **Shemini Atzeret** <note>The "Eighth Day of Assembly" following Sukkot.</note>—one part of which belongs to Him and one to us.

When the prophets ascend from below to above, they recite in the Torah: "**Lord YHVH**," as in the verse: "*Lord God, what will You give me?*" (Genesis 15:2) and similar passages. This occurs when the abundance flows from above to below, and from below to above. The secret here is "they go from strength to strength" (Psalms 84:7). Therefore, you will find that this Sefirah is called:

<margin>
The upper one—and this is the secret of the names **Adonai YHVH** that we have written.
</margin>

# Righteousness

The sign for this is the verse: "*Righteousness, righteousness shall you pursue*" (Deuteronomy 16:20). The first "**Righteousness**" (*Tzedek*) is the secret of the **Kingship** (*Malchut*). The second "**Righteousness**" is the secret of **Understanding** (*Binah*). For therein lies the secret of the perfect Union across all the worlds. This is what is meant by the continuation of the verse: "*...that you may live and inherit the land.*"

<column-break/>

To lick up [all faults]—for "*love covers all transgressions*" (Proverbs 10:12). Here, I am hinting at the qualities of **Truth**, **Kindness**, and **Fear**. Know that **Love** is bound up with **Generosity** (*Nedavah*), as it is said: "*I will heal their backsliding, I will love them freely*" (Hosea 14:5). One who knows the secret of...

<margin>
The eighth in the counting; it is written with the letters **Y-H-V-H** but read as **Elohim**.
</margin>

# The Name

"*YHVH Adonai is my strength*" (Habakkuk 3:19). This is a secret because this is the place where judgments begin and are prioritized. I shall now explain this to you. 

Know that the **Supreme Crown** (*Keter Elyon*) is absolute Mercy, containing no mixture of Judgment whatsoever. However, as **Wisdom** (*Hokhmah*) and **Understanding** (*Binah*) emanate from it, you will find both Judgment and Mercy mingled together. Yet, their power does not manifest as actual Judgment because they are still bound to the **Crown**. 

Rather, Mercy and Judgment are drawn down through **Greatness** (*Gedulah/Hesed*) and **Power** (*Gevurah*), much like the arms that extend from the source within the brain to perform actions. Since the primary location for Mercy and Judgment is through the **Throne** and **Understanding**—which are called "**Eden**" and called the "**Source**"—they remain attached to the **Crown**. They only act through the "arms" of **Greatness** and **Power**. 

Therefore, because **Understanding** (*Binah*) is the source of Judgment but only acts through the hand of **Power** (*Gevurah*), the Name is written as **YHVH** <note>The name of Mercy</note> because it is bound to the world of Mercy, but it is pronounced as **Elohim** <note>The name of Judgment</note> because of the judgments that flow from it.

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the divine emanations (Sefirot), specifically moving from the concept of Righteousness discussed on the previous page to the attribute of Kindness (*Hesed*) embodied by the patriarch Abraham. Abraham is the source of **Generosity**.
Therefore, everyone who comes to ask...

# And it was

From the **Right** <note>The Right Side of the Sefirotic tree represents **Kindness** (*Hesed*).</note>, there is nothing preventing or hindering. For the Right is open to give gifts and to receive those who return in repentance. Were it not for the "dim hand" <note>original: *yad kehah*; a reference to the restrictive force of the Left Side</note> that hinders, and the **North** <note>The North is associated with **Power** (*Gevurah*) and judgment</note> which hides its abundance, every person would achieve their request, but then they might return to sin. The one who understands the nature of **Generosity** <note>original: *nedavah*</note> holds the key to the verse: *“And there was the hiding of His power”* (Habakkuk 3:4) and *“How great is Your goodness which You have hidden for those who fear You”* (Psalms 31:20). 

Behold, the secret of **Generosity** is the secret of the pure emanation of divine flow, without any mixture of judgment—flowing from the **Supreme Crown** <note>The highest Sefirah, **Keter**</note> to the Right, and from the Right until it reaches **Power** <note>The Sefirah of **Gevurah**</note>. However, the place of danger is the place of **Power**, for that is where the various "accusers" <note>Spiritual forces of judgment and prosecution</note> stand. Were it not for the generosity of Abraham, his children would have no strength to endure. Regarding this, it is said: *“I will heal their backsliding, I will love them freely”* <note>Hosea 14:5; "freely" is the same root as "generosity" (*nedavah*)</note>, which is the secret of overcoming those who hinder and prevent. Therefore, *“You, O God, did pour down a generous rain”* (Psalms 68:10). The one who understands these secrets will understand the mystery of why *“It is better to take refuge in the Lord than to trust in man; it is better to take refuge in the Lord than to trust in princes”* (Psalms 118:8-9). These "princes" <note>original: *nedivim*, "generous ones"</note> are the celestial ministers who stand on the side of Abraham; they are called "generous," yet even their "eye is narrow" <note>A Hebrew idiom for being stingy or limited</note>.

# And Know

That the attribute of **Love** is the attribute of Abraham. Through this quality, Abraham is beloved within Jacob <note>This suggests the integration of Kindness into the balance of Truth represented by Jacob</note>. This is the secret behind the blessing: *“Who chooses His people Israel in love.”* This is also the secret of the "Generous Ones" and the divine emanation: *“Better is a dinner of herbs where love is...”* (Proverbs 15:17). Why is it compared to *“the ox licking up the grass of the field”* (Numbers 22:4)? Because *“Hatred stirs up strife, but love covers all transgressions”* (Proverbs 10:12). 

This is the key to every instance where we pray: *“Remember Abraham, Isaac, and Israel Your servants,”* or *“I will remember My covenant with Jacob, and also My covenant with Isaac, and also My covenant with Abraham will I remember; and I will remember the land”* (Leviticus 26:42). Understand this secret: **“Love covers all transgressions.”** For this reason, the sages placed the recitation of the **Shema** <note>The central Jewish declaration of faith</note> adjacent to the word "Love," and they placed "Truth" at the very end of the Shema—as it is written: *“You will give Truth to Jacob and Kindness to Abraham”* (Micah 7:20). And if the ox comes to lick...

<column-break/>

...nearby. Therefore: *“So shall your seed be”* (Genesis 15:5). Whoever knows the secrets we have explained will understand from this that there is a "place to find silver" and it is as accessible as finding water. For Abraham drew this flow from the **Supernal Whiteness** <note>A symbol for pure, unadulterated Kindness/Hesed</note>. 

However, the "place for gold" <note>associated with Gevurah/Fire</note> is where damage can occur, a sign that fire emerged from the water. Therefore, Abraham’s attribute is called **Eternal Kindness** (*Hesed Olam*) rather than "Supreme Kindness," for Ishmael and Esau represent the "dross" of that silver and gold. Therefore, the wealth of Esau is **Edom** <note>meaning "Red"</note>, which is the dross of gold. This is the attribute called:

# Morning

The reason for this name is that Abraham is the "morning" of the world. *“In the morning You shall hear my voice”* (Psalms 5:4)—the verse does not say *ba-boker* ("in the morning") but simply **Morning** <note>treating it as a proper name for a divine state</note>. It is also written: *“And Abraham rose early in the morning”* (Genesis 22:3) and *“There was evening and there was morning”* (Genesis 1:5). Regarding the light, it says *“the morning shone”* rather than "the morning gave light"—it was the **Light** itself. Like a *“shepherd seeking out his flock”* (Ezekiel 34:12), after the world had become "evening," Abraham arrived and it became "morning," allowing one *“to behold the beauty of the Lord and to visit in His temple”* (Psalms 27:4). 

This attribute is the secret of the **Donation** (*Nedavah*). What is the difference between a **Vow** (*Neder*) and a **Donation**? A donation is when one says "This [specific object] shall be [consecrated]," while a vow is when one says "It is incumbent upon me [to bring an offering]."

# Know

That **Understanding** <note>The Sefirah of **Binah**</note> is the source of the **Vow**, as we will explain later. That spiritual level cannot be reached except through the "Vow." We shall explain this with the help of the Blessed Name. 

Behold, Abraham is the chief of the **Generous Ones**, and he is the secret of the **Contribution** <note>original: *Terumah*, the offering for the Tabernacle</note>. Through him, Israel merited the **Divine Presence** (*Shekhinah*), as it is said: *“From every man whose heart moves him [generosity] you shall take My contribution”* (Exodus 25:2). I am now explaining the verse: *“The princes of the people are gathered, the people of the God of Abraham”* (Psalms 47:10)—it is all explained. Furthermore: *“How beautiful are your steps in sandals, O prince’s daughter!”* (Song of Songs 7:2). For the "daughter of the generous" <note>referring to the community of Israel or the Shekhinah</note> requires "sandals" <note>spiritual protection</note> because of the "thorns" of the lower crowns. Happy is the one who merits this verse and the "covering of the restrainers" who guard Israel: *“For I will cast out nations before you and enlarge your borders; neither shall any man covet your land when you go up to appear before the Lord your God three times a year”* (Exodus 34:24).

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the divine emanations, moving from the "Generosity" of the Right Side (Abraham/Kindness) to the mysteries of "Understanding" (Binah). It specifically addresses the mystical nature of wine, the prohibition of libation wine, the concept of Repentance (Teshuvah), and how these relate to the structure of the Sefirot.</meta>

...The nations drink the wine of their libations; their secret is shattered and estranged. But Israel alone draws wine from **Understanding** <note>The Sefirah of *Binah*, the third divine emanation, often associated with the "Higher Mother" and the source of the soul.</note>. This is the wine that "gladdens God and men," and this is the secret alluded to in the Talmud: that wine is the cause for revealing secrets and hidden things. Therefore it is said: **"When wine enters, the secret comes out"** <note>original: *nichnas yayin yatza sod*. In Hebrew numerology (Gematria), both the word for wine (*yayin*) and the word for secret (*sod*) equal 70.</note>. It also speaks of the "wine of the seventh year," the reason being that wine is higher than the seventy "Princes of the Nations" <note>In Kabbalah, the 70 nations of the world are governed by 70 angelic princes; wine/secrets transcend this earthly level.</note>, and it is the place of mysteries. Thus: *"The secret of the Lord is for those who fear Him, and His covenant is to make them know"* (Psalms 25:14).

For this reason, He forbade the wine made by a Gentile as "libation wine" <note>original: *yayin nesech*; wine used in idolatrous ritual.</note>. Within the place of **Understanding**, a different power is drawn toward **Power** <note>The Sefirah of *Gevurah*, associated with judgment and the patriarch Isaac.</note>, which is the wine fit to be poured as a libation upon the Altar. This is to say, one must blend the two attributes: the power of Abraham must be unified with the power of Isaac, as it is said: *"And the two of them went together"* (Genesis 22:8). For Abraham represents **Water** <note>Kindness/Hesed</note> and Isaac’s mystery is **Wine** <note>Judgment/Gevurah</note>. The wine requires a libation on the Altar. But concerning the "wine preserved in its grapes" <note>A Talmudic metaphor for the spiritual reward in the World to Come</note>, it is said: *"No eye has seen it, O God, beside You"* (Isaiah 64:3). This is the secret of the prohibition of libation wine for those who possess knowledge.

Behold, the **Nazirite** <note>A person who takes a vow of holiness and abstinence</note> is forbidden from wine because wine is the secret of the beginning of the **World of Judgment**. The Nazirite draws from the **Crown** <note>The highest Sefirah, *Keter*</note> alone, which is the **World of Mercy**. Thus it says: *"All the days of his Nazirite vow, he is holy to the Lord"* (Numbers 6:8), and it is written: *"All the days of his vow... no razor shall pass over his head."* His vow is certainly a "vow of Nazirite-ship," and therefore he is forbidden from wine. And after <unclear>you have known this</unclear>—

**Know** that this Sefirah <note>Binah/Understanding</note> is the secret of the perfection of the emanations and the **Supreme Unity**. Regarding it, it is said: *"The Lord shall be one and His name one"* (Zechariah 14:9). When a person sins and is liable for *Karet* <note>Spiritual excision or "cutting off" of the soul</note> or other punishments, and is distanced from **Understanding**, he falls into the hand of the "Princes" who are beneath **Understanding**, and his soul is liable for exile. For all exiles occur in the place of Abraham and Isaac <note>The lower attributes where judgment can take hold</note>. But when a person takes hold of **Understanding**, they do not fear damage or evil occurrences. Regarding such a person, it is said: *"He who dwells in the secret place of the Most High"* (Psalms 91:1)—meaning the actual "Most High"—and he does not fear mishaps or damages because no adversary, accuser, or tempter has permission to ascend to that place. Therefore, *"He shall abide in the shadow of the Almighty"*—

<column-break/>

—below in the **Kingdom** <note>The Sefirah of *Malchut*</note>, because he holds that **Understanding** in his hand. Then he is not handed over to the power of "Fear" <note>original: *pachad*; a name for the attribute of strict Judgment</note>. This is what is meant by: *"And Cain went out from before the Lord"* (Genesis 4:16)—he literally "went out" and *"dwelt in the land of Nod, east of Eden."* This means he was moved away from the "front" of Eden. Happy are those who know the secrets of the Torah! For when they removed him from this place, he became afraid and said: *"Whoever finds me will kill me"* (Genesis 4:14), for there are many oppressors and accusers surrounding the "seventy houses."

Therefore, **Understanding** is called "Repentance" <note>original: *Teshuvah*</note> in every place. This means that when Israel sins and is separated from this place—which is the place of **Freedom**—and transgresses in their deeds, but then regrets all their crimes and returns to the place of **Understanding**, they are accepted just as they were at the beginning. This is the secret of: *"Before the mountains were brought forth... You turn man back to contrition and say: Return, O children of men"* (Psalms 90:2-3). This is the place of **Repentance** from which the souls of Israel emerge, and it is the "return of the soul" because that is its home. It is also written: *"In this Jubilee year, you shall return, every man to his possession"* (Leviticus 25:13).

**Know** and believe that this is the place of Israel’s repentance, as it is said: *"And he shall return and be healed"* (Isaiah 6:10). The one who understands this secret will understand what is said: *"And you shall return unto the Lord your God"* and *"The Lord your God will return your captivity and have compassion upon you, and will return and gather you from all the peoples"* (Deuteronomy 30:2-3). Did I not write above that exile comes from the "Princes" who are adjacent to Abraham and Isaac? *"And He will return and gather you"* happens when Israel returns to the "Broad Places of the River" <note>A kabbalistic term for the expansive flow of *Binah*</note>, to the place where the hands of the nations and their Princes have no dominion. This is the secret of: *"To set you high above all the nations... for praise, for a name, and for glory"* (Deuteronomy 26:19). Therefore: *"And you shall return unto the Lord... and the Lord will return your captivity."*

**Know** that **Egypt** <note>original: *Mitzrayim*</note> was second to the Land of Israel, as you already know. Therefore, in Egypt, there was **Pi-Hahiroth** <note>The place where the Israelites camped before the parting of the sea; literally "The Mouth of Freedom."</note>. The one who knows this secret knows why they were hidden in the matter of Pi-Hahiroth. This is the secret of: *"I will also surely bring you up"* (Genesis 46:4), and the secret of what is said [in the Haggadah]: *"If the Holy One, Blessed be He, had not brought our fathers out of Egypt..."*

**The Gates of Justice** <note>original: *Sha'arei Tzedek*</note>...

<summary>This page explains the mystical connection between the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding), the secret of wine, and the process of Teshuvah (Repentance). It describes Binah as a realm of spiritual freedom and the source of the soul, where one finds protection from the forces of judgment and exile.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion on the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding), linking it to the concepts of the Jubilee year, the 50th gate of wisdom, and the mystical origins of the Torah and wine. It explores why the neglect of the Sabbatical years leads to exile and how the spiritual "place" of the Jubilee is the source of ultimate freedom.</meta>

For from the place of freedom the Tablets were drawn. And this is what the scripture says: *“The writing was the writing of God, engraved (charut) on the tablets”* (Exodus 32:16). Do not read it as *charut* [engraved], but as **freedom** <note>original: *cherut*. This is a famous Rabbinic wordplay from Pirkei Avot 6:2, suggesting that true freedom is only found through the divine law.</note> certainly. And behold, in the **Jubilee** <note>The 50th year in the biblical cycle, which represents the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding) in Kabbalistic thought.</note> Israel went out from Egypt. Thus it says: *“And the children of Israel went up armed from the land of Egypt”* (Exodus 13:18) <note>The word for "armed" (*chamushim*) shares a root with "fifty" (*chamishim*), hinting at the fifty gates of Understanding.</note>. From all the things we have explained, one should contemplate in every place the secret of the exiles, the secret of the redemption, and the secret of the plagues, and similar matters. Even a young child should be able to contemplate from here on.

## And I
must awaken you to what
our Rabbis, of blessed memory, said: **Exile**
comes to the world because of idolatry, forbidden sexual relations,
bloodshed, and the neglect of the Sabbatical year of the land.
It is written: *“Then shall the land enjoy its sabbaths, as long as it lies desolate”* (Leviticus 26:34). The reason is that it is written: *“And you shall count for yourself seven sabbaths of years”* (Leviticus 25:8). Behold, redemption
is dependent on the **Seventh** for those who know the hidden mysteries.

## And now
we must awaken you to
wonderful secrets concerning the matter of
**libation wine**, for not everyone reaches the knowledge of the principles of the Torah and its secrets.

## Know
that the **Upper Wine** which *“gladdens God and men”* (Judges 9:13) is drawn from **Understanding** <note>The Sefirah of Binah.</note>, which Abraham merited through the Love that is adjacent to the Name. Thus it says: *“He brought me to the banquet house [literally: house of wine], and his banner over me was love”* (Song of Songs 2:4).

## And know
that the wine a person drinks rises
to the place that was the beginning of
his birth. Behold, wine is a cause for the revealing of
secrets. And behold, the World—which is the spring of
**Understanding**—of which it is said *“No eye has seen it, O God, beside You”* (Isaiah 64:3)—is
the secret of the **Preserved Wine** <note>original: *yayin ha-meshumar*. In Jewish tradition, this is the wine kept in its grapes since the six days of creation, reserved for the righteous in the World to Come.</note> kept in its grapes since the six days of creation certainly. No Gentile has ever touched it, for there is no way for the Princes of the Nations to ascend there, as it is above all the "high places." It is the place of the secret: **“When wine enters, the secret comes out”** <note>original: *nichnas yayin yatza sod*. In Hebrew numerology (Gematria), both the word for "wine" (*yayin*) and "secret" (*sod*) equal 70.</note>. Therefore, it is not for the seventy nations to ascend to it or have a portion in it. The touch of a Gentile idolater turns it into **libation wine** <note>original: *yayin nesech*. Wine used for pagan sacrifice, which is strictly forbidden in Jewish law.</note>, because the nations worship idols.

<column-break/>

it is assured for him that he is a child of the **World to Come**. One who knows the secret of these hints will understand the secret of: *“You have enlarged my steps under me”* (Psalms 18:37). The text should have said *tachtayni* [under me], but it says *tachtay* <note>The author is playing on the word *tachtay* (under me) as being *tachat nun* (under the letter Nun).</note> because it is the way of the language to say *tachat nun* [under the Nun], which is the secret of the **Fifty Gates of Understanding**. This place is called:

# Redemption
For from this place, light is drawn
to the **Eighth** <note>Likely referring to the Sefirah of Binah which is the 8th counting from the bottom up, or the transcendence beyond the 7-day cycle.</note>. One must link **Redemption** to **Prayer**. If you understand the secret of the Jubilee and the fifty gates of Understanding, you will understand this matter with perfect comprehension, and I shall explain it to you.

## Know
that all the seventy Princes we wrote of
above surround Abraham,
Isaac, and Jacob. They surround Jacob up to the **Shoulder**.
That place [below the shoulder] is the place of Exile. But
from the place of the **Shoulder** and above is the place of **Freedom**
for Jacob and his children. One who knows this secret will understand that *“the Lord has redeemed Jacob and ransomed him from a hand stronger than his”* (Jeremiah 31:10). This is what the scripture means: *“And they shall come and sing in the height of Zion”* (Jeremiah 31:11). **The height of Zion** is certainly the secret of Understanding, which is the "height of Zion." *“And your soul shall be like a watered garden”*—literally like a watered garden. *“He brought me out to abundance.”* One who understands these principles will understand the secret of **Redemption** in truth. One who knows these secrets will understand what is said: *“it shall have redemption; and in the jubilee it shall go out”* (Leviticus 25:31, 33).

## And know
that this Sefirah is called:

# Jubilee
And thus it says:
*“A jubilee shall that fiftieth year be unto you”* (Leviticus 25:11)—fifty certainly. This is the secret of the **Fifty Gates of Understanding**. And it is written: *“In this year of jubilee you shall return every man unto his possession”* (Leviticus 25:13).

## And know
that the **Jubilee** is the secret of **Freedom**;
it is like "rivers and streams, broad and wide."
This is what the scripture says: *“And he shall spread out his roots by the river [yoval] and shall not see when heat comes”* (Jeremiah 17:8). The reason is that heat and thorns do not ascend to that place. Therefore, we count forty-nine days until the Giving of the Torah, and on the **Fiftieth day** the Torah was given, because the Torah was drawn from the **Jubilee**. If not for the incident of the [Golden] Calf and the breaking of the Tablets, there would be no exile at all.

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the higher Sefirot, shifting focus from the Jubilee and the Sabbatical years to the mystical nature of the World to Come and the "Sons of Ascent." It bridges the gap between the structure of the Divine and the fate of the righteous soul after death.</meta>

...holiness, and Understanding reaches until the Crown <note>original: *Keter*, the highest Sefirah</note>, the secret of the Ascent and the action regarding the Holy of Holies. And as for what was said, "I saw the **Sons of Ascent** <note>original: *Bnei Aliyah*, referring to the spiritually elite who can ascend to the highest divine realms</note> and they are few," I shall explain it to you:

### Know
that those who merit the life of the **World to Come** <note>original: *Olam Ha-Ba*</note> do not know their status explicitly until the Day of Judgment. On that day, they will be distinguished by their names, as it is written: *"And many of those who sleep in the dust of the earth shall awake"* (Daniel 12:2). However, for the completely righteous, an announcement is made concerning them even before the Day of Judgment. It is said, "So-and-so is destined for the life of the World to Come," as was the case with Rabbi Hanina ben Teradion, Rabbi Akiva, and other righteous figures about whom announcements were made before the Day of Judgment. Concerning these, it is said: "I saw the Sons of Ascent and they are few." We have already explained great wonders and compositions regarding this. As for the matter of "those who enter with a son" and "those who enter without a son" <note>This is an esoteric reference to the merit of one's offspring or perhaps a deeper mystical union</note>, it is truly called an **Ascent** because it is the highest of all ascents. He who merits to know this Ascent will understand the secret of every place mentioned in the Torah, such as "And she prayed *by the hand* of the King" <note>original: *al yado*</note>. And behold, I shall explain:

### Know
that the Great **N** <note>The Hebrew letter *Nun*, which equals 50, symbolizing the Fifty Gates of Understanding</note> which stands between the Middle Line and the Attribute of God called "to dwell," is where all judgments are decided. There, they take counsel in the Heavenly Court of the Hosts <note>original: *Tzeva'ot*</note>. There the counsel is finalized, as it says: *"In the face of counsel He shall remove it."* And it is written: *"For there the Lord of Hosts shall scatter."* This is like what is said in the *Book of Formation* <note>original: *Sefer Yetzirah*, an ancient foundational text of Jewish mysticism</note>—that counsel is finalized in the kidneys. Thus, here the counsel is finalized in the Great Sanhedrin <note>The supreme religious assembly, here used to describe the divine court</note> along with the Hosts. **Then** all is given over to the Attribute of Justice <note>original: *Tzedek*</note> to complete the judgment. If a person's path is upright, but through their sins they did not merit to rise above the **Arms** <note>original: *Zeru’ot*, referring to the Sefirot of Chesed and Gevurah</note>, they remain like all the signs of the Zodiac and are sustained according to the stars. This is because the stars and the constellations are located from the "Arms" and below, for the "Arms" are called "Heaven." But if a man merits through his uprightness—even if the constellations indicate poverty, lack, childlessness, or a short life—that righteous person rises above the place of the stars. This is the "Ascent," and there he attains all his needs, overriding the celestial arrangement of the constellations. This "Ascent" is the secret of the source of all constellations; they are the "Great Constellation" from which all others draw. Concerning this, we learn: *"Life, children, and sustenance do not depend on merit, but rather on Mazal [constellation/destiny]"* <note>A famous Talmudic aphorism from Moed Katan 28a, here reinterpreted kabbalistically to mean the highest source of destiny above the physical stars</note>. And this is the matter of rolling over onto...

<column-break/>

...the Sanhedrin of **Harmony** <note>original: *Tiferet*, the central Sefirah</note>, and this is the secret of *"His hand is stretched out, and who shall turn it back?"* (Isaiah 14:27) certainly. And this Sefirah is called **World**.

# World
is here, and this is the place that even the prophets did not grasp clearly. No clear perception was achieved from the Sefirah of Harmony and upward. Though Moses achieved up to the 49th Gate of Understanding, know that the **Fiftieth Gate**, through which one enters the treasures of Understanding and its chambers, was not even grasped by Moses, peace be upon him. This is what is written: *"I will proclaim the name of the Lord before you... and I will be gracious to whom I will be gracious"* (Exodus 33:19). This is the meaning of: *"Eye has not seen, O God, besides You"* (Isaiah 64:3).

### And behold,
many later scholars have made a great error regarding the matter of the World to Come. They did not know and did not understand, thinking that it is entered immediately at the moment of passing. But anyone who delves into the entire Talmud will see the refutation of their error. For behold, after Rabbi Akiva passed away, it was said to him: "Happy are you, Rabbi Akiva, for you are pure and your soul left your body with the word 'One' [*Echad*], and you are *destined* for the life of the World to Come." The reason is that after a person passes, they enter the realm of **Sovereignty** <note>original: *Malchut*</note>, receive the face of the Divine Presence <note>original: *Shekhinah*</note>, and come before the Sefirah of Harmony <note>original: *Tiferet*</note> to hear Torah from the mouth of the Holy One, blessed be He. There, all souls are stationed until the Resurrection of the Dead and the Day of Judgment. Thus our Sages, of blessed memory, said in the tractate Sotah: *"'When you walk, it shall lead you'—this refers to this world; 'When you lie down, it shall watch over you'—this refers to the day of death; 'And when you awake, it shall talk with you'—this refers to the World to Come."* Everything is revealed.

### And behold,
know and believe that just as the Land was divided among the assembly of Israel, so too, when all souls are gathered after the Day of Judgment, an inheritance in the World to Come will be divided among them, each according to what is fitting for them. Regarding this place, great explanations have already been set forth in the *Book of the Commandments*, where they entered several inner chambers. And this Sefirah is called:

# Ascent
referring to the Ascent that was above the Holy of Holies. Concerning this, it was said in the Talmud, "And an upper chamber was built." In truth, this "Ascent" <note>original: *Aliyah*</note> is above Harmony. Behold, the secret of Sovereignty is the secret of the Temple, and Harmony is the Holy...

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the "Sons of Ascent" and the higher realms, specifically moving into the power of Repentance (Teshuvah) and its connection to the Sefirah of Understanding (Binah). It explains how spiritual return allows a soul to transcend lower judgments and reach the "Throne of Glory."</meta>

...for we and our children were enslaved to Pharaoh in Egypt. Therefore, the children of Israel went up from the land of Egypt **armed** <note>original: *chamushim*. The author hints at the word's relation to "fifty" (*chamishim*), representing the Fifty Gates of Understanding</note>. And it is written: *"I bore you on eagles' wings and brought you to Me"* (Exodus 19:4). Happy are those who know the secrets of this hint in its literal sense. And it is written: *"Return, O Israel, unto the Lord your God"* (Hosea 14:2). Great is **Repentance** <note>original: *Teshuvah*, literally "return"</note>, for it reaches until the **Throne of Glory** <note>original: *Kise HaKavod*, the highest level of divine manifestation</note>. For there is "a throne for a throne." And so He says: *"If you return, O Israel, says the Lord, unto Me shall you return"* (Jeremiah 4:1). And He says: *"If you remove your abominations from before Me, you shall not wander."*

**Know** and believe that the "Right Hand" is **Greatness** <note>original: *Gedulah*, often synonymous with the Sefirah of *Chesed* or Loving-kindness</note>, and it is the secret of Abraham, extended to receive those who return. Understand this very well, and you will see that via the path of the Right one ascends to Repentance. "In the place where penitents stand, the perfectly righteous do not stand" <note>A famous Talmudic dictum from Berakhot 34b</note>. There dwell the **strangers** <note>original: *gerim*, can also mean converts/proselytes</note>, each man according to the blessing He blessed them. Open your eyes and see how great is the power of Repentance and how far it reaches. And this **Sefirah** <note>original: *Sefirah*, one of the ten divine attributes or emanations</note> is called:

**Who** <note>original: *Mi*</note>
And with the letter *He* <note>The final letter of the Divine Name, often associated with the feminine presence or manifestation</note>, they say: *"Lift up your eyes on high and see: Who (Mi) created these (Eleh)?"* (Isaiah 40:26). And so He says: *"In the beginning, God created"* <note>In Hebrew: *Bereshit Bara Elohim*. Mystical commentary often links the word "Elohim" (God) to the combination of "Mi" (Who) and "Eleh" (These)</note>. And it all hints at: *"With whom did He consult, that He should give Him understanding and teach Him?"* (Isaiah 40:14). And behold, I am hinting at **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*, the third Sefirah</note> above, which is that which annuls harsh and evil decrees, whether for an individual or for the community. The reason is because it is the place of Repentance. This is what our Sages of blessed memory said: "The decree of a community is torn up, but the decree of an individual is not torn up" <note>Rosh Hashanah 18a</note>. And what is its "tearing"? Understanding tears up all the promissory notes <note>original: *shetarei hovot*, metaphor for sins or debts to heaven</note> in the world. The reason is because of Repentance, for Understanding is above all the angelic ministers, accusers, and masters of prosecution. And behold, all who return to Her shall not fear the dread of evil. And the secret is: *"For the Lord of Hosts has purposed, and who shall annul it? And His hand is stretched out, and who shall turn it back?"* (Isaiah 14:27). "Who shall annul" and "who shall turn it back" are certain.

**The reason** it is called by the term **"Who"** (*Mi*) is because it is the place of secrets and mysteries; *"no eye has seen it, O God, besides You"* (Isaiah 64:3). Therefore,

<column-break/>

it was given to the question of "Who." That is to say, regarding one who does not change <note>original: *meshineh*, possibly "repeats" or "strays"</note> and asks "Who," for even to Moses it was given as the question of "Who." And the secret: *"Who can express the mighty acts of the Lord, or make heard all His praise?"* (Psalm 106:2). This is the secret of the **Mighty Acts** <note>original: *Gevurot*, plural</note> which annuls all decrees. "Make heard all His praise" certainly refers to *"Let every soul praise the Lord"* (Psalm 150:6). Three words point one above the other. He who understands these hints will see the secrets of the Torah and hidden things laid bare before him. The secret of Repentance annuls the decree.

A great secret was hinted at in the **Talmud**. And behold, I shall explain: *"Anyone who sits in fast on the Sabbath, they tear up for him his decree of seventy years"* <note>Shabbat 11a</note>. The reason is because from the **Sabbath** a man ascends to Repentance, which is the "seventh unto the Lord." Thus, it is found that he is saved from the decree that was entangled in the three "houses" surrounding Greatness and Might. And if he has ascended in Repentance, which is of the nature of Understanding, he will not fear any foe or accuser, for he is above them all. Consequently, they tear up for him his decree of seventy years. The comprehensive secret is all that is said: *"For the Lord of Hosts has purposed, and who shall annul it? and His hand is stretched out, and who shall turn it back?"* How many chambers and how many hidden treasuries are concealed in this verse! The "Lord of Hosts" is literal, for there is the secret of the Hosts. Some are within and some are without, but Understanding is above them all.

**And Understanding** is called in the Torah "Mighty Acts" in the plural, as it is said: *"Who can express the mighty acts (Gevurot) of the Lord?"* For *"who is a God in heaven or on earth who can do according to Your works and according to Your mighty acts (Gevurotecha)?"* (Deuteronomy 3:24). But the attribute of the **Dread of Isaac** <note>original: *Pachad Yitzchak*, a name for the Sefirah of Gevurah</note> is called "Might" (*Gevurah*) in the singular. Therefore, the "Mighty Acts" [the plural, Binah] annul power and [singular] Might, and by the letter *He*: *"and His hand is stretched out, and who shall turn it back?"* And it is written: *"He who rules over men must be just, ruling in the fear of God"* (2 Samuel 23:3). The Holy One, blessed be He, said: "I rule over all. And who rules over Me? The **Righteous person** <note>original: *Tzaddik*</note>, for I issue a decree and he annuls it." How many secrets are hidden in this place! And this is the secret of why they tear up for him his decree of seventy years. For when a man reaches the level of the Righteous, he ascends up to Understanding and tears up every decree that the **Sanhedrin** <note>The heavenly court or council</note> decrees against him.

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding), here associated with the concepts of Praise (Tehillah), Vows (Neder), and the ultimate source of spiritual Life (Chayyim). The previous page established Binah as the destination of Repentance; this page explores how the soul connects to that high level through speech and prayer.</meta>

...she did not find a place to produce children in the **Great Sanhedrin** <note>original: *Sanhedri Gedolah*. In this mystical context, it refers to a lower celestial court or level of judgment that lacked the power to grant her a child.</note>. She ascended to the place of **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*</note>, which is the place of ascent, the place of the "Stranger" <note>original: *Nekhar*, perhaps hinting at the soul's status as a traveler in this world</note>, the place from which children are drawn down. And she prayed to **the Lord** <note>original: *Y-H-V-H*, the Tetragrammaton</note>, as it is written: *"And Hannah prayed to the Lord... and she vowed a vow to the Lord and said: O Lord of Hosts, if You will indeed look on the affliction of Your maidservant"* (1 Samuel 1:11). Therefore, *"It is better that you should not vow than that you should vow and not pay"* (Ecclesiastes 5:4). And it is written: *"That which is gone out of your lips you shall observe"* (Deuteronomy 23:24). This refers to the Vow, which corresponds to the "Lips" in the higher realms. He who knows the **Secret of the Sefirot** <note>The ten divine emanations or spheres</note> knows that the form of the tongue in the mouth is the secret of Understanding (*Binah*) above. Consider that the lips are red; if, God forbid, a person sins in this place <note>the realm of speech and vows</note>, they are scrutinized very strictly. Therefore, "it is better that you should not vow." *"Your lips are like a thread of scarlet... dropping sweet wine"* (Song of Songs 4:3, 11). And this **Atonement** <note>original: *Kapporah*</note> is called:

### **Praise**
<note>original: *Tehillah*</note>

This is the place of the Great and Complete Praise. In this place of praise, there is no "skipping" <note>referring to the liturgical practice of "Half-Hallel," where certain paragraphs are omitted on specific holidays</note>, but rather a truly complete and perfect praise.

### **And Behold**
<note>original: *Ve-Hineh*</note>

I will inform you that whenever you find "Praise" (*Hallel*) in the Torah, the Prophets, or the Writings, it is a hint to the sphere of **Understanding** (*Binah*). And I am hinting: *"And to make you high above all nations... for praise, for a name, and for glory"* (Deuteronomy 26:19). "To make you high" certainly refers to this level. Therefore, regarding Praise: *"Let every soul praise the Lord [Yah]"* (Psalm 150:6). These three words—"Soul" (*Neshamah*), "Praise" (*Tehalel*), and "Yah"—indicate levels stacked one above the other. The higher soul (*Neshamah*) praises the Name *Yah* <note>a shorter divine name associated with the higher spheres</note>, while the lower soul (*Nefesh*) is for prayer. Therefore, the name used there is **the Lord** <note>Y-H-V-H</note>. And regarding what you find: *"Praise the Lord [Y-H-V-H], O my soul [Nafshi]"* (Psalm 146:1)—this is the secret of the "incomplete" Praise, such as the Praise recited on the New Moon or the intermediate days of Passover. You will never find the phrase "Praise *Yah*, O my soul" <note>original: *Halleli Nafshi et Yah*</note>. Rather, the specific Name of God used makes the distinction clear.

### **And Know**
<note>original: *U-Da*</note>

That wherever you find "Complete Praise," it refers to **Understanding** (*Binah*) and **Praise** (*Tehillah*). But "Incomplete Praise" is the secret of **Kingship** <note>original: *Malkhut*, the lowest of the ten Sefirot</note>. *"These are the generations of Perez"* (Genesis 38:29). Therefore, anyone who recites "A Praise of David" (*Tehillah L'David*) <note>Psalm 145, the core of the *Ashrei* prayer</note> every day is assured that he is a "son of the World to Come." The order of the levels is taught here. Therefore, you find psalms that begin with *Hallelujah* and end with...

<column-break/>

...they know nothing. And it is said: "The righteous in their death are called living, and the wicked in their lives are called dead" <note>A famous Talmudic teaching from Berakhot 18b</note>. I am now hinting at wondrous secrets: *"The dead do not praise Yah"* (Psalm 115:17). The reason is that the thing called "Dead" is the opposite of the **World of the Living**, which is called **Yah**. But *"We will bless Yah from this time forth and forever"* (Psalm 115:18).

### **I Am Here**
<note>original: *Hineni*</note>

Adding further explanation. [King Hezekiah] said: *"I said, I shall not see Yah, even Yah, in the land of the living; I shall behold man no more with the inhabitants of the world"* (Isaiah 38:11). The Hebrew for "world" used here (*Heled*) actually implies "ceasing" (*Hadel*). Everything is clear to those who have eyes to see. He who knows these secrets knows the secret of a man's life or death. He will know and understand the secret of our request on **the New Year** <note>Rosh Hashanah</note>: *"Remember us for life, O King who desires life, and write us in the Book of Life."* The mouths of the mistaken err in this matter, thinking it means "write us for *good* lives." Their understanding is not good, for they do not understand the words. They do not know that there is a level called **Life** in a literal sense. It is the source of all goodness and all types of flow and emanation. They think "Life" is not a spiritual essence itself, but merely the life of a person while the soul is in the body. Heaven forbid! Therefore, they do not understand the secret of reward and punishment, since this matter is hidden from them.

And this Sefirah is called:

### **Vow**
<note>original: *Neder*</note>

This is the place of Vows. They are not attained through a "freewill offering" <note>original: *Nedavah*</note>, where one says "This is [sanctified]" <note>original: *Harei Zeh*</note>, because [this Sefirah] is prepared but not present in this physical time to be pointed to as "This." Therefore, in all Vows, one says: "It is *upon me*" <note>original: *Harei Alai*</note>. It is written: *"Your vows are upon me"* (Psalm 56:13). And behold, every "Oath" (*Shevuah*) is by **the Lord** <note>Y-H-V-H</note>, for in Him is the secret of the **Seventh** <note>original: *Shevi'it*, a play on the word for oath, *Shevuah*</note>, and every Vow (*Neder*) is to the Lord.

### **And Know**
<note>original: *U-Da*</note>

That from the place of the **Vow**, children are drawn down when they do not come from the side of the **astrological arrangement** <note>original: *Ma'arekhet HaKokhavim*</note>. Children are also drawn to the constellations and stars—this happens previously. Therefore, when children come through the astrological arrangement and a man sins regarding a vow, that divine quality is taken away from the sinner. For the sin of vows, [children] literally die. And behold, when Hannah saw...

It explains the mystical difference between various names of God (Yah vs. YHVH) in the context of prayer and the profound spiritual consequences of vows.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirah of Understanding (Binah) as the source of life and the destination of the soul's ascent. It moves from the mechanics of prayer and vows to the distinction between the "inner" and "outer" spiritual realms, eventually identifying the Sefirot of Beauty (Tiferet) and Foundation (Yesod) as the "Tree of Life."</meta>

He cast [his prayer] through this, and thus: *"Behold, I will add to your days"* (Isaiah 38:5). And the place of Merit is the **Great Sanhedrin** <note>original: *Sanhedri Gedolah*. In this context, it refers to the celestial court of judgment. The text suggests that to change a decree, one must ascend beyond this court.</note>. Behold, every judgment decreed in the Great Sanhedrin, if one is found worthy, must rise to the **Ascent** <note>original: *Aliyah*, a term for the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding)</note>. This tears up every decree in the world. The reason is that once one clings to the quality of the "Ascent," no damage, no evil occurrence, and no lack can cling to that place, for it is the place that is perfect with every kind of perfection. There, joy and gladness are eternal. This is what is meant whenever they call it the **Inner Chambers** <note>original: *Battei Gavai*</note>. Regarding what is said in the Talmud <note>Tractate Hagigah 5b</note>, *"And on that day the Lord God of Hosts called for weeping and for mourning,"* the text asks: Is it not written, *"Splendor and majesty are before Him; strength and joy are in His place"* (1 Chronicles 16:27)? This is not a contradiction: one refers to the **Inner Chambers** and the other to the **Outer Chambers** <note>original: *Battei Barai*</note>.

**Know** that the Inner Chambers are called the aspect of **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*</note> above, which is the line of the Ascent. The Outer Chambers consist of **Beauty** <note>original: *Tiferet*</note> and **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malkhut*</note>. This is because surrounding Beauty are seventy princes <note>The celestial representatives of the seventy nations of the world</note> who distress Israel when they sin. Surrounding the Kingdom are all the thrones of foreign kingdoms. She <note>the Sefirah of Kingdom/the Shekhinah</note> is like *"a rose among the thorns"* (Song of Songs 2:2). Those who sin in those places experience exile, illness, troubles, lacks, and various types of [mocking] laughter and [foreign] songs. However, from Understanding and above, there is nothing but joy and gladness; there is no Accuser <note>original: *Satan*</note> and no evil occurrence, for everything is in its fullness. That which you find written, *"No evil descends from the Lord,"* refers to the place of Ascent, which is the secret of Understanding. That which you find written, *"For evil has come down from the Lord,"* refers to the crowns that surround Beauty down to the "Arms" <note>The lower Sefirot</note>. Therefore, it is written regarding the gate of Jerusalem: *"He has cast down from heaven to earth the beauty of Israel"* (Lamentations 2:1). This means He separated the Beauty—whom did they cast down? The **Beauty of Israel** <note>original: *Tiferet Yisrael*</note>, meaning the Foundation and the Kingdom were separated. Understand this well. 

This Sefirah is called
**Life** <note>original: *Chayyim*</note>, for from it life is drawn to every living thing. When these are drawn into the **Foundation** <note>original: *Yesod*</note>, it is called the **Living God** <note>original: *El Chai*</note>. And when the Kingdom is filled from the Ascent, it is called the **Living God** <note>original: *Elohim Chayyim*</note>.

<column-break/>

And from the Sefirah called **Life**, the **Written Torah** is drawn forth and given to Israel. Therefore, it is called the **Torah of Life** <note>original: *Torat Chayyim*</note>, as it is said: *"For they are life to those who find them"* (Proverbs 4:22), and it is written, *"And live by them"* (Leviticus 18:5). Those who cling to the Torah cling to actual Life, as it is said: *"But you who held fast to the Lord your God are all alive today"* (Deuteronomy 4:4). The reason is that it is called the **Tree of Life**. This is the secret of **Beauty** <note>original: *Tiferet*</note>, which is the Tree of Life. Therefore, the Torah is called by its name, the Tree of Life, as it is said: *"It is a tree of life to those who grasp it"* (Proverbs 3:18). For the Written Torah is the secret of Beauty, which is called the Tree of Life, and it is all one thing. 

We have already explained great matters regarding the Sefirah of the **Living God** <note>original: *El Chai*</note> and the ninth Sefirah <note>Yesod</note>... [some] think that there is "good life" or "evil life" in all the verses of the Torah.

**Know** that in every place called **Life**, there is no evil—not a little and not a lot. And in every place called **Evil**, there is no [life]—not a little and not a lot. For the thing called Life is the root and essence of all goodness and delights, and all types of future reward for the righteous and the pious. The place called **Death** is the opposite of Life. Therefore, the Torah said: *"See, I have set before you today life and good, death and evil"* (Deuteronomy 30:15). With Life comes Good, and with Death comes Evil. For actual Life is the essence of the thing so named; it is the secret of the higher Sefirot, the place of all kinds of **Emanation** <note>original: *Atzilut*</note>, the flow of blessing, goodness, salvation, health, and life. Actual Death is an essence unto itself, containing all the external forces of **Impurity** <note>original: *Tum'ah*</note> called the "Strange Woman," the "Prostitute," the "Foreigner," and "Foreign Trouble." Actual Death is an essence. 

One who knows this secret will understand the secret of the decree, the binding, and the "death" mentioned in relation to the righteous, and the secret of death mentioned in relation to the wicked, and the secret of the **Impurity of the Dead** <note>original: *Tum'at Met*</note>. Great secrets depend on the matter of the impurity of the dead, as we have hinted here. Therefore, the sages hinted in Tractate Berakhot that *"The living know they will die,"* but the dead [know nothing]...

<summary>This page distinguishes between the "Inner Chambers" (Binah), which are purely joyful and divine, and the "Outer Chambers" (Tiferet and Malkhut), which are susceptible to external "thorns" or foreign forces. It identifies the Torah and the Sefirah of Beauty as the Tree of Life, the source of all vitality and goodness.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding), specifically how it functions as the source of forgiveness and the "World of Mercy." The previous page established that ascending to this "Inner Chamber" can nullify even the harshest decrees from the lower courts of judgment.</meta>

> “...all wickedness shall vanish like smoke when You remove the dominion of arrogance from the earth.” <note>original: *ve-khol ha-rish'ah ke-ashan tikhleh...* This is a quote from the *Amidah* prayer of the High Holidays (Rosh Hashanah and Yom Kippur), looking forward to a time of universal divine justice.</note> Then He will remove the reproach of His people from all the earth. This attribute is called in the Torah by the simple term:

->### **Kippurim (Atonements)**<-

And from this place, the iniquities of Israel are atoned for at all times, because it is the "Place of Repentance" <term>Repentance: In Kabbalah, the Sefirah of Understanding (Binah) is synonymous with *Teshuvah* (Repentance/Return), as the soul "returns" to its divine source here.</term>, and there is no atonement without repentance. Behold, wherever you find written in the Torah an "addition" of atonement—such as on the Sabbath, the New Moon, and the Festivals which have an **Additional Offering** <note>original: *Korban Musaf*</note>—it is the secret of the revelation of this attribute upon the **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malkhut*</note>. Based on the concept of divine flow and emanation, because light is added on that day, we add an offering and an "additional" service. This signifies an addition of atonement. As it says in the prayer for the New Moons: *“For Your people You gave it, a time of atonement for all their generations.”* The end of the matter is this: wherever you find the language of "atonement" in the Torah, it refers to the secret of Repentance, Healing, and Forgiveness. All of this is because this level is "Above the Arm" <note>The "Arm" refers to the Sefirah of *Gevurah* (Severity/Judgment). By being "above" it, Binah acts as a higher court of mercy that can override strict justice.</note> and is the second level, the Place of the World of Mercy. When the **Gates of Understanding** open, then the "Gates of Light" increase within the Kingdom during those times. The secret is written:

> “The gate of the inner court that faces east shall be shut the six working days; but on the Sabbath day it shall be opened, and on the day of the New Moon it shall be opened.” <note>Ezekiel 46:1</note>

Great things have been revealed here! Therefore, this is the time of atonement. And behold, I shall explain: **Know** that when the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*, the highest Sefirah</note> is revealed, there is no place for the "Face of Wrath" to stand, not even a little. The sign for this is:

> “May the Lord shine His face upon you and be gracious to you.” <note>Numbers 6:25</note>

All is hinted at here: for in the presence of the "Supernal Radiance of the Face," there is no room for the Face of Wrath.

> “And I will be gracious to whom I will be gracious.” <note>Exodus 33:19</note>

Therefore, all types of atonement depend on the "Radiance of the Supernal Face"—that is, the revelation of the Face. At that hour, it is called a **Time of Favor** <note>original: *Et Ratzon*</note> in all the worlds. **And know** that when the Kingdom stands in **Judgment** to give an accounting for all that Israel did during the past year, she stands in shame and embarrassment because of Israel’s deeds. Therefore, she "covers herself" for the day of our festival <note>A wordplay on Psalm 81:4, *Ba-keseh le-yom hagenu*. The word *keseh* (full moon/festival) is linked to *kisui* (covering/hiding).</note>, just as 

<column-break/>

one wraps themselves and is ashamed to look. But the Name, may He be blessed, opens the Gates of Light from the **New Year** until **Yom Kippur** <note>original: *m'R"H ad Y"K* (from Rosh Hashanah to Yom Kippur)</note>. These are the Gates of Understanding. And when those gates are opened, then it is written:

> “Seek the Lord while He may be found; call upon Him while He is near. Let the wicked forsake his way, and the unrighteous man his thoughts; let him return to the Lord, that He may have compassion on him, and to our God, for He will abundantly pardon.” <note>Isaiah 55:6-7</note>

He who understands this path will understand the secret of the **Ten Days of Repentance**—why they are called the Days of Repentance in the literal sense. Because the Gates of Understanding, the Gates of Freedom, the Gates of Redemption, and the Gates of Atonement are open from the New Year until the Day of Atonement, for both the community and the individual. Therefore:

> “Seek the Lord while He may be found; call upon Him while He is near.”

He is certainly "near," for if the Gates of Understanding were not open, the path would be very far indeed. And so it says:

> “From afar the Lord appeared to me, and [with] an everlasting love I have loved you.” <note>Jeremiah 31:3</note>

Incline your ear and hear: **Know** that on the day Isaac was bound upon the altar, our father Abraham (peace be upon him) requested that these gates be opened for all Israel to serve as an atonement for all their sins. The Holy One, blessed be He, agreed to give him this gift for his children: to open before them the Gates of Repentance. Abraham called the name of that place:

> “The Lord will see/be seen (*Adonai Yireh*), as it is said this day: On the mount of the Lord it shall be seen.” <note>Genesis 22:14</note>

And that day is an atonement for the seed of Abraham, who is called the **Attribute of Love** <note>original: *Middat Ahavah*, synonymous with the Sefirah of *Chesed* (Loving-kindness)</note>. Because the "Distant Sefirot"—which are Crown, Wisdom, and Understanding—are revealed on the day Isaac was bound. From the New Year until the Day of Atonement, the gates are opened by the merit of Abraham's love. This is:

> “From afar the Lord appeared to me... with an everlasting love I have loved you; therefore I have drawn you with loving-kindness.”

It is certainly "loving-kindness" (*Chesed*). Therefore, this is one day in which two kinds of atonement are held: the atonement of the **Kingdom** above and the atonement of the **Children** below. The sign for this is:

> “And so he shall do for the Tent of Meeting, which dwells with them in the midst of their uncleanness.” <note>Leviticus 16:16</note>

The Day of Atonement (*Yom Ha-Kippurim*) is certainly for both above and below. And it is written:

> “And he shall make atonement for the holy sanctuary, and he shall make atonement for the Tent of Meeting and for the altar; and he shall make atonement for the priests and for all the people of the assembly.” <note>Leviticus 16:33</note>

It is certainly on the Day of Atonement. It is not called the "Day of Atonement" (singular) but the "Day of Atonements" (plural/Kippurim). And he who understands the **Gates of Righteousness**...

It emphasizes that atonement is a dual process affecting both the heavenly realms (the Sefirah of Kingdom) and the earthly community.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding), focusing on how this high spiritual realm acts as the source of ultimate redemption, the "Great Shofar," and the transition from the labor of prayer to the joy of praise.</meta>

And we conclude with "Hallelujah" <note>original: *Hallelujah* (Praise the Lord)</note>. How many wonders are hidden in this place of "Hallelu" (Praise)? For when the soul departs from the World of Prayer and returns to the World of Praise—Hallelujah. There is a "Hallelujah" at the beginning and a "Hallelujah" at the end. One who knows these secrets will understand the secret of the **Verses of Song** <term>Verses of Song: *Pesukei d’Zimra*, a section of the morning prayer service consisting of Psalms intended to prepare the heart through praise.</term> which begin with Hallelujah and end with Hallelujah. Contemplate this: everything is for prayer, but not everything is for praise. Know that anyone who recites the "Full Hallel" <note>The *Hallel* consists of Psalms 113–118. In Jewish law, reciting it every single day as an obligation is discouraged because it treats a special celebration as a mundane routine.</note> every day is considered a blasphemer and a reviler. One must not use **holy vessels** for mundane purposes on a weekday. This attribute is called:

### The Great Shofar

Within it are suspended all types of joys, all types of freedom, and all types of redemptions. At the hour when this attribute is awakened to redeem Israel, then the gathering of the exiles from the four corners of the earth will occur. This is because this is the attribute called:

### Socheret

It encompasses everything, and nothing encompasses it. This is because the **Kingdom** <term>Kingdom: *Malkhut*, the lowest Sefirah, representing the physical world and the community of Israel.</term> is surrounded by many other external forces, and therefore it is in exile among them. This is the state of Jerusalem, of which it is said: "in the midst of the nations," which gives life to the lands surrounding it. As it is said: "Jerusalem has become as an impurity among them" <note>Lamentations 1:17</note>. Therefore, her children were scattered to the four corners of the earth. But when **Understanding** <term>Understanding: *Binah*, the third Sefirah, often called "The Mother" and the source of liberation.</term> is awakened, which is called:

### Rechovot

It is called "Socheret" <note>original: *Socheret* (a precious stone or pavement mentioned in Esther 1:6). In Kabbalah, it refers to the expansive light of Binah.</note>, a "merchant’s bypass," which is the secret of the **Great Shofar**. Then the gathering of the exiles will be complete. This is what the scripture means: "And it shall come to pass in that day, that a great shofar shall be blown; and they shall come that were lost in the land of Assyria, and they that were dispersed in the land of Egypt" <note>Isaiah 27:13</note>. Why was the land "lost"? Because the "Righteous One" was lost from the land. With the blowing of the Great Shofar, the "lost item" will return to its owners, and the **Divine Presence** <note>original: *Shekhinah*</note> will return to Zion. However, know that the giving of the Torah at Sinai was with the "Shofar of Isaac." Behold, Jacob is the secret of the Shofar, and Isaac is the one who blows it. Therefore, the Shofar of Isaac is like the New Year (Rosh Hashanah); it is the secret of the blowing of the Shofar of Isaac. It is written: "How shall Jacob stand? For he is small" <note>Amos 7:5</note>. Therefore, it says: "And it shall come to pass in that day..."

<column-break/>

"...and a Great Shofar shall be blown." Then, with the blowing of that specific Shofar, the gathering of exiles will be absolute. For that Shofar is "Great," and it draws all the exiles of Israel upward, entirely above the "Arms" <note>The "Arms" refer to the Sefirot of *Hesed* (Mercy) and *Gevurah* (Judgment). Being "above the arms" means reaching the level of *Binah*, where judgment is nullified.</note>. The enemy will no longer prevail, nor will they continue to defile her. For Jerusalem will be attached and unified with Understanding (*Binah*). Therefore, the ruined city shall walk and expand and go upward and upward. It is written: "For I will cast out nations before thee, and enlarge thy borders" <note>Exodus 34:24</note>. From that point forward, there will be no place for the forces of impurity to ever enter Jerusalem. For at the blowing of the Shofar, the enemies and the accusers will be cut off and perish. No oppressor or crusher will remain. "And also the idols and the spirit of impurity I will cause to pass out of the land," and "the idols shall utterly pass away." Then the wings of Understanding will be separated and spread over the Kingdom in the secret of the Hand of the Lord. This is the secret of: "In that day the Lord shall be one and His name one" <note>Zechariah 14:9</note>. There will be no place for the "Serpent" to breach or enter. Then the memory of Amalek will be erased from the Throne, as it is said: "For a hand is upon the throne of God, the Lord will have war with Amalek" <note>Exodus 17:16</note>. Then "sinners shall be consumed out of the earth, and the wicked shall be no more. Bless the Lord, O my soul, Hallelujah" <note>Psalm 104:35</note>. Truly Hallelujah! For from there, the lineage will be perfect above and below. Then the remaining **Husks** <term>Husks: *Kelippot*, the shells of impurity that conceal divine light.</term> that are fit to remain will stand outside in their places; they will not shout nor destroy in all My holy mountain. What is the reason? "For the earth shall be full of knowledge"—truly full. No empty space will remain in all the known earth that is not full of knowledge. There will no longer be a "Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil," but rather it will be entirely full of good, "as the waters cover the sea." Regarding that hour, it is written: "For then will I turn to the peoples a pure language, that they may all call upon the name of the Lord, to serve Him with one consent" <note>original: *shekhem echad* (with one shoulder/consent)</note>—literally with one shoulder, certainly. Regarding this, the sages established in the prayer: "And they shall all become a single band to do Your will with a perfect heart." And then...

<meta>catchword: ...the wickedness (ha-rish'ah)</meta>

### The Name
