<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Sefirah of Binah (Understanding), exploring how the proper alignment of internal and external spiritual forces maintains the world's stability. It specifically addresses how the reversal of this order—caused by human sin—leads to the exile of the Divine Presence (Shekhinah), and how the rituals of Yom Kippur work to restore this cosmic balance.</meta>

in its place—meaning the internal powers are on the inside and the external powers are on the outside. This is what the Scripture means when it says, **"And God saw all that He had made, and behold, it was very good"** <note>Genesis 1:31</note>. But when the orders that He initially arranged are overturned, and the "external" elements enter the "inside" while the "internal" elements go "outside," then the rows are spoiled, the **Divine Presence** <term>Divine Presence: *Shekhinah*, the aspect of God that dwells within the world.</term> departs, and Exile is found. Therefore, one must contemplate the order of the internal and external paths; then he will understand that every act of God is perfect, for **"nothing can be added to it and nothing taken from it"** <note>Ecclesiastes 3:14</note>. And it is written: **"God has acted so that they should fear before Him"** <note>Ecclesiastes 3:14</note>. It is also written: **"Only this I have found, that God made man upright, but they have sought out many schemes"** <note>Ecclesiastes 7:29</note>. This means that the First Man <note>Adam</note> spoiled the qualities and the orders, bringing the "impure" inside and pushing the "pure" outside. Then the bowl was overturned, and all the structures and orders were ruined. This is the secret of **"placing an image in the Sanctuary"** <note>A reference to King Manasseh placing an idol in the Temple (2 Kings 21), a metaphor for spiritual corruption</note>, for "she saw nations enter her sanctuary, whom You commanded should not enter Your congregation" <note>Lamentations 1:10</note>. And it is written: **"the uncircumcised and the impure shall no longer enter you"** <note>Isaiah 52:1</note>, and it says: **"the lawless one shall no longer pass through you; he is utterly cut off"** <note>Nahum 2:1</note>. But if all things stood in their proper order, the initial repair would have been "good in its place"; it is only when its place is changed that its "taste" and "appearance" are altered.

### And Know 
that when the **Crown** <term>Crown: *Keter*, the highest and most hidden of the Sefirot.</term> is revealed, a flow of abundance and benefit comes to all things, both internal and external. This is the secret of the "two goats" for the two realms—the internal and the external <note>Refers to the two goats of Yom Kippur, one for God and one for Azazel</note>. One who understands this will understand the "secret of the bribe" given to **Samael** <term>Samael: *Sam"a*, the primary accuser or "adversary" in Kabbalistic thought.</term>, as well as the secret of the "fruit tree" of the Festival <note>Sukkot</note> and the other types of abundance that used to come to the nations. This is the great secret that encompasses everything: **"And in you shall all the families of the earth be blessed"** <note>Genesis 12:3</note>, and the secret of **"He who gives rain upon the face of the earth and sends water upon the face of the outdoors"** <note>Job 5:10</note>. It seems to us that what we have hinted at regarding the word "Yom Kippurim" <note>Day of Atonement</note> is sufficient.

### And Know 
that in the "path of the vineyards," there are several extensions to the external forces through the abundance that comes to them when **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*</note> is revealed through the **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malkhut*</note>.

### And Know 
that this Sefirah is the secret of the letter **He** of the Name <note>The Tetragrammaton Y-H-V-H</note>, which is the **first He**.

<column-break/>

If one wishes, he may understand how great the power of **Repentance** <term>Repentance: *Teshuvah*, literally "return," viewed here as returning the world to its original divine state.</term> is, and how many worlds a person who returns in repentance repairs. He will also understand how many levels are spoiled and how many fences are breached by one who sins below. Thus He says to you: **"Go down, for your people have acted corruptly"** <note>Exodus 32:7</note>. Truly, they are "corrupting children" <note>Isaiah 1:4</note>; they truly destroy buildings, spoil the rows, uproot plantings, destroy worlds, and break the spiritual channels above through the transgressions they commit—certainly, without a doubt. Therefore, "Yom Kippurim" exists both above and below. And thus it says: **"And for your transgressions was your mother sent away"** <note>Isaiah 50:1</note>.

### And Know 
that when the secrets of **Understanding** <note>Binah</note> are revealed—which are the secrets of atonement—it is written: **"For on this day He shall atone for you, to cleanse you"** <note>Leviticus 16:30</note>. It is written **"He shall atone for you"** <note>original: *yekhaper aleikhem*</note> and not "I shall atone for you" <note>original: *akhaper etkhem*</note>. All of this is hinted at by the fact that the three Upper Sefirot are tied to the word **"He"** <note>original: *Hu*</note>, the middle ones to the word **"You"** <note>original: *Atah*</note>, and the lower ones to the word **"I"** <note>original: *Ani*</note>. How many gates are open in this place for one who has eyes to see!

### And Know 
that the services performed on **Yom Kippur** in white garments are the specific "services of the day," while the services performed in golden garments are the services of other days—the regular daily offerings and the additional offerings. This is all hinted at in the Tractate of Yom Kippur <note>Tractate Yoma in the Talmud</note>. **"Though a host should encamp against me, my heart shall not fear; if war should rise against me, in *this* I trust"** <note>Psalm 27:3</note>. **"With *this* shall Aaron come into the Holy Place"** <note>Leviticus 16:3</note>. Because on this day the "World of Mercy" is revealed, therefore "in *this* I trust." And the secret: **"I have blotted out your transgressions like a thick cloud, and your sins like a mist; return to Me, for I have redeemed you"** <note>Isaiah 44:22</note>. Everything is explained. The reason the "Youth with his ornaments" <note>A reference to the angel Metatron or the revealed light of the divine order</note> is revealed on this day is why the Satan, the Accuser, has no permission to prosecute. Behold, the "two goats of Yom Kippur" and the other matters of the service of this day teach us the secret of the revelation of Justice and Mercy, and the need in the world for the external forces. The Name gives a "reward" to every force according to what it is; for if all the forces in the world stood at their watch in the "Repair of Creation" as the Name ordered them, everything would be good, and there would be no "damager" in the world.

It highlights the Sefirah of Binah as the source of atonement and the reason the Accuser (Satan) is silenced on this day.</summary>

That "a woman of valor" prays to the Name <note>God</note> is found in the verse: **"He who finds a wife finds good and obtains favor from the Lord"** <note>Proverbs 18:22; the word for favor is *Ratzon*, also meaning Divine Will</note>. This is the place that no created being in the world has grasped—to know the gifts of the Will and the Thought. Therefore, in this place, the matters of "the wicked who prospers" and "the righteous who suffers" are united. <meta>This page continues the exploration of the highest Sephirot, moving from the inscrutable Divine Will (Ratzon) into the realm of Divine Thought (Machshavah). It explains how the names of God and the structures of the Sephirot govern both historical events, like the Exodus, and the internal spiritual path of the soul.</meta>

It preserves the place of the males and the place of the females in this verse. Therefore, in every place that you find the name **Yah** <note>original: *Yah* (Y-H), a shortened name of God often associated with the union of Wisdom and Understanding</note> in the Torah, it is a place of "wide spaces" where there is no Satan and no evil occurrence. Therefore it is written: **"For a hand is upon the throne of Yah"** <note>Exodus 17:16</note>. In truth, the majesty is upon the throne of *Yah*; it is all explained. It is also written: **"Yah is my strength and song, and He has become my salvation"** <note>Exodus 15:2</note>. This was said regarding the war with Pharaoh. But regarding the future to come, what is written? **"Yah is my strength and song, and He shall be to me for salvation"** and it is written: **"I will praise You, for You were angry with me; Your anger is turned away, and You comfort me"** <note>Isaiah 12:1</note>. This is the secret of the verse: **"If You, Yah, should keep watch over iniquities, O Lord, who could stand?"** <note>Psalms 130:3</note>. Everything is hinted at here. And this Sephirah is called:

## Thought

Everywhere, this is the secret of the "Extension of Thought" from the **Crown** <term>Crown: *Keter*, the highest Sephirah, representing the primordial Divine Will.</term>. This is the place of the abstraction of the Will and the Emanation. This is the secret of the **"Tip of the Yod"** <note>The small stroke at the top of the Hebrew letter *Yod*, representing the very beginning of manifestation from the void</note>, which is the beginning of the abstraction of the letter *Yod*. The *Yod* is a point of **Thought** <term>Thought: *Machshavah*, the Sephirah of *Chokhmah* (Wisdom) where the first spark of an idea emerges.</term>. This is the place called "Deep." Happy is he who reaches this place! This is the secret of the "Inner Face." Concerning this, Solomon said: **"Deep, deep, who can find it?"** <note>Ecclesiastes 7:24</note>. He said "Deep" twice to refer to **Understanding** <term>Understanding: *Binah*, the Sephirah that processes the spark of Wisdom into logic.</term>, and deeper than Understanding is the depth of **Wisdom** <term>Wisdom: *Chokhmah*</term>, which is called "Thought." These two attributes are held within the Crown. This secret is very deep: "Your thoughts are very deep," understand these wonders! This is the Thought that is mentioned everywhere.

## Depth

This is the secret of the **Book of Creation** <term>Book of Creation: *Sefer Yetzirah*, an ancient mystical text describing how God created the universe using the Hebrew letters and the Sephirot.</term>, because Thought is called "Merchandise" <note>original: *Sechorah*</note>. Wisdom and Understanding are merchants, as it says: **"For her merchandise is better than the merchandise of silver"** <note>Proverbs 3:14</note>. How many wonders are hinted at here! For even though the "merchandise of silver" is the place of **Kindness** <term>Kindness: *Chesed*, associated with the color silver and the patriarch Abraham.</term>, many enemies and accusers surround it. But that which is "for those who dwell [before the Lord]" and is internal, its "merchandise shall be for food to satisfy and for stately clothing" <note>Isaiah 23:18</note>. Everything is hinted at. Therefore, Abraham sought the **Cave of Machpelah** <note>The burial place of the Patriarchs in Hebron; the name *Machpelah* means "double" or "folded."</note>. He bought it from the sons of Ephron and the sons of Heth for **"four hundred shekels of silver, current for the merchant"** <note>Genesis 23:16</note>. This is because "her merchandise is better than the merchandise of silver." Therefore, Thought is what subdues all the denials of the accusers from the Crown. This is the secret from the *Book of Creation*: "The depth of the beginning and the depth of the end, the depth of the east and the depth of the west..."

<column-break/>

And regarding Pharaoh: in the place of the Egyptians <note>original: *Mitzrayim*, which also means "narrow straits" or "limitations"</note> there are all kinds of punishments. The verse says **"And Pharaoh brought near"** <note>Exodus 14:10</note>, though it should have said "And Pharaoh drew near." Rather, it means that Pharaoh [by chasing them] "brought Israel near" to their Father in Heaven. What is written immediately after? **"And the children of Israel lifted up their eyes, and behold, Egypt was marching after them, and they were very afraid, and the children of Israel cried out to the Lord."** This is the meaning of: **"Lord, in trouble they visited You; they poured out a silent prayer when Your chastening was upon them"** <note>Isaiah 26:16</note>. It is also written: **"And these are the nations that the Lord left to test Israel with"** and **"Because you did not serve the Lord your God... therefore you shall serve your enemies"** <note>Deuteronomy 28:47-48</note>.

## And Know

That when Israel distances themselves even a little bit from the King’s Palace, they immediately encounter types of wild beasts: bears, lions, snakes, and scorpions. Similarly, if the "fence-breakers" of Israel break the boundary, then **"He who breaks a fence, a snake will bite him"** <note>Ecclesiastes 10:8</note>. This is the secret of: "Do not turn to the right or to the left; remove your foot from evil." Do not turn to the side of the "Upper Yes" which comes from Abraham, which is the Right, for the accuser Ishmael stands around it. And do not turn to the Left, to the side of Isaac, which is the place of **Fear** <term>Fear: *Pachad*, the Sephirah of *Gevurah* (Severity/Judgment).</term> and the ministers of punishment. Instead, **cling to Beauty** <term>Beauty: *Tiferet*, the "Middle Line" of the Sephirot which balances Kindness and Severity.</term>, which is the "Middle Line," and you shall not fear any evil. For then you are standing in the Inner Palace, in the King’s Palace, in the Palace of the Abode—a place of rest where there is no Satan and no evil occurrence. This is the secret of: **"But you who cling to the Lord your God are all alive today"** <note>Deuteronomy 4:4</note>. Guard this verse. It is also written: **"There is a way that seems straight before a man, but its end is the way of death"** <note>Proverbs 14:12</note>. This is the secret of "Do not turn to the right or to the left." Everywhere you find the words "Straight" or "Uprightness," these refer to the two attributes called:

## Arms

Likewise, "Uprightness" and "Straight" refer to the unification of the Sephirot, each one in its proper place. This is the secret of: **"The upright shall behold His face."** Truly, **"The ways of the Lord are straight; the righteous walk in them, but the rebellious stumble in them"** <note>Hosea 14:10</note>. All is prepared for those who know the Truth.

## And Know

And believe that when all the Sephirot are united in their proper arrangement, they are also called: **Uprightness**. However, "there is a way that seems straight before a man, but its end is the way of death."

<meta>This page continues the transition from the abstract Divine Thought mentioned on the previous page into the specific Sephirah of Wisdom (Chokmah). It discusses the distinction between holy wisdom and "external" or mundane wisdom, and how the structure of the Sephirot protects the holiness of the Divine realm from external "transgressors."</meta>

Wisdom reaches until the Will, which is the secret of **First Wisdom** <note>original: *Chokmah Rishonah*</note>, and it is far from him.

## And Know
That there is an external wisdom called the **"Wisdom of the People of the East"** <note>original: *Chokmat Bnei Kedem*</note>. This is the wisdom that Abraham gave to the sons of the concubines, as it is written: "And Abraham gave all that he had to Isaac. But to the sons of the concubines whom Abraham had, Abraham gave gifts; and while he was still living he sent them eastward, away from Isaac his son, to the country of the east" <note>Genesis 25:5-6</note>. Therefore, "Solomon’s wisdom excelled the wisdom of all the people of the east" <note>1 Kings 5:10</note>. And regarding what it says, "he sent them away from Isaac his son," it is like that which is said: "that they put out of the camp every leper and everyone who has a discharge" <note>Numbers 5:2</note>. However, regarding the Wisdom of Thoughts, what is written? "It is deep, deep; who can find it?" <note>Ecclesiastes 7:24</note>.

## And Know
That the scripture which says, "How manifold are Your works, O Lord! In wisdom You have made them all" <note>Psalm 104:24</note>, hints at the **Wisdom of Thought** and the **Final Wisdom** <note>Final Wisdom usually refers to the Sephirah of Malchut (Kingdom), the bottom of the divine chain</note>, and it is all one matter. And as for why the *Sefer Yetzirah* <term>Sefer Yetzirah: The "Book of Formation," one of the earliest and most fundamental books of Jewish mysticism.</term> begins with "In thirty-two paths of Wisdom, Yah, the Lord of Hosts, engraved..." it is because it is written: "And in the heart of all who are wise-hearted I have put wisdom" <note>Exodus 31:6</note>. Wisdom is in the heart. The secret is that the letters of the Name do not clothe themselves in the letter *Aleph* or *Tet*, but in these two letters <note>likely referring to the Yod and Heh of the name Yah</note>. Regarding the Higher Wisdom, it is written: "The Lord by wisdom founded the earth; by understanding He established the heavens" <note>Proverbs 3:19</note>. And it is written: "In the day that the Lord God made earth and heaven" <note>Genesis 2:4</note>, and it is written: "In the beginning God created the heaven and the earth" <note>Genesis 1:1</note>. It is also written: "Who is wise, and will understand these? Prudent, and he shall know them? For the ways of the Lord are right..." <note>Hosea 14:10</note>. In this verse lies the secret of the **Sephirot** <term>Sephirot: The ten creative forces or attributes through which the Infinite reveals Himself.</term> and the secret of all the "External Forces" that surround them from the outside. Behold, in this verse, he states the order of the **Emanation** <note>original: *Atzilut*</note> of the Sephirot and the drawing down from the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*</note> which is called the **Infinite** <note>original: *Ein Sof*</note>. For Wisdom is emanated from the "Nothingness" <note>original: *Ayin*</note>, which is a "Mother" <note>Source</note> that is not perceived. As it says: "But Wisdom, where <note>original: *Me-ayin*, literally 'from where' or 'from nothing'</note> shall it be found?" <note>Job 28:12</note>. This is the "Tip of the Yod," because the "Nothingness" has no specific letter. Therefore it is called *Ayin* (Nothingness)—I am above, and "I" <note>original: *Ani*, an anagram of *Ayin*</note> am below, a receptacle for the flow of the Sephirot. Therefore, "Wisdom is found from nothing" to bring a person close to the unification of the Emanation: Wisdom into **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*</note> and Understanding into **Knowledge** <note>original: *Da'at*</note>. This is "Who is wise and will understand these"—bringing Understanding close to Wisdom; "Prudent and he shall know them"—bringing Knowledge close to Understanding. For "Righteous" <note>original: *Yesharim*</note> is the secret of **Might** <note>Gevurah</note> and **Greatness** <note>Gedulah</note>.

<column-break/>

In Knowledge, this is from here and this is from here, for "Righteous" are certainly one against the other. "The ways of the Lord"—these are **Victory** <note>Netzach</note> and **Splendor** <note>Hod</note>, his legs, the pillars of the Lord. "The ways of the Lord" certainly are "in them," your ways. "And the righteous shall walk in them"—referring to the **Righteous One** <note>the Sephirah of Yesod</note> and **Justice** <note>the Sephirah of Malchut</note>. Therefore, "Righteous" <note>plural</note> is written, and it also lacks one letter *Yod* <note>a scribal or mystical note on the spelling</note> for the Righteous One and Justice. "But transgressors shall stumble in them"—this is the place of the transgressors and the stumbling blocks; "the wicked walk on every side." The stumbling blocks are with the wicked. "That they put out of the camp every leper, and everyone who has a discharge, and whoever is defiled by the dead." "He shall dwell alone; outside the camp shall his dwelling be." "And he shall carry it outside." "And he shall go out outside the camp." "He shall not come into the midst of the camp." "But transgressors shall stumble in them," for there is a place that is a stumbling block for the wicked. It is written: "The way of the wicked is as darkness; they know not at what they stumble." "And you shall not put a stumbling block before the blind." Therefore: "This is Jerusalem; I have set her in the midst of the nations and the countries that are round about her" <note>Ezekiel 5:5</note>. "And the son of an Israelite woman went out." "And Dinah the daughter of Leah went out." Everything is hinted at and explained. Therefore: "Whoever is wise, let him observe these things, and they shall understand the loving-kindness of the Lord" <note>Psalm 107:43</note>.

## And the one who understands
This verse will understand the secret of what the Lord said to the first man: **"to work it and to keep it"** <note>Genesis 2:15</note>. "To work it" from within, and "to keep it" from without. To make "fences" and "hedges" for the guarding of the Garden, so that the forces of impurity—called "transgressors"—do not enter inside. "But the destruction of the transgressors and the sinners shall be together, and they who forsake the Lord shall be consumed" <note>Isaiah 1:28</note>. Therefore, the Torah said to make hedges for the Torah and for the Palaces. "Therefore shall you keep My charge, that you do not any of these abominable customs, which were done before you, and that you do not defile yourselves therein: I am the Lord your God" <note>Leviticus 18:30</note>. "Customs" <note>original: *Chukot*, also meaning 'laws' or 'engravings'</note> refers to the place of the hedge and the fence; it is called a **Statute** <note>original: *Chok*</note>, "He gave a statute that shall not be crossed." Therefore, "keep My charge"—if I have placed a statute and a hedge, how much more so must you place hedges and fences because of the **Evil Inclination** <term>Evil Inclination: *Yetzer Hara*, the innate human drive toward selfishness or sin.</term> that is within you. Therefore, "make a guard for My guard," and this is what the Sages, of blessed memory, said: "Make a fence for the Torah." Nothing is made except by way of Wisdom and Understanding. "Whoever is wise, let him observe these things, and they shall understand the loving-kindness of the Lord."

<summary>This page explains the mystical hierarchy of the Sephirot, specifically focusing on how Wisdom (Chokmah) emanates from the Divine Nothingness (Ayin). It contrasts holy wisdom with "external" wisdom and emphasizes the importance of spiritual "fences" to protect the sacred from impurity.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the Sephirot (Divine emanations), moving from the concept of the "External Wisdom" discussed on the previous page into the nature of the first two primary emanations: the "Beginning" (associated with the Crown and Wisdom) and "Understanding." It uses biblical figures like Asher, Joseph, David, and Leah to illustrate how these divine structures manifest in the world of human relationships and destiny.</meta>

And he said: "Blessed is Asher above sons; let him be acceptable to his brothers, and let him dip his foot in oil" <note>Deuteronomy 33:24</note>. "To dip his foot in oil" is certainly all explained, and the reason is that he draws from the **Will** <note>original: *Ratzon*; in Kabbalah, this refers to the highest Divine Will emanating from the Keter/Crown</note>. And this is "he dips in oil <margin>Explanation: [...] like oil [...] <br/> [...] <br/> [...]</margin> his foot," etc. For one who clings to the Will, he "dips his foot in oil." And this is the Sephirah called:

# **Beginning**

<note>original: *Reshit*</note>

It is the "all," and it is the first drawn from the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*, the highest Sephirah</note>. And in this "Beginning," all created beings were created, and all types of emanations were emanated. This is also the secret of "In the beginning God created" <note>Genesis 1:1</note>. The secret of the "Bet" <note>The first letter of the Hebrew Bible</note> in *Bereshit* is large, which the Jerusalem Targum translates as "In Wisdom"—meaning "In the Beginning," in the **Will**, the secret of Thought, with which He makes everything. From the secret of this "Beginning," all things called "Beginning" were emanated, each in its place according to the types of emanation. Therefore, regarding the creations of the worlds, such is the secret of each of them. It is written: "The Lord possessed me at the beginning of His way, before His works of old" <note>Proverbs 8:22</note>. It is written: "Israel is holiness to the Lord, the firstfruits of His increase" <note>Jeremiah 2:3</note>. It is written: "Of the first of your dough you shall lift up a cake as a heave offering" <note>Numbers 15:20</note>. And "He provided the first part for himself, for there the portion of the lawgiver was hidden" <note>Deuteronomy 33:21</note>. It is written: "The beginning of wisdom is the fear of the Lord" <note>Psalm 111:10</note>. Behold, the secret of "In the Beginning" is the secret of the second Sephirah drawn from the Crown, in the secret of the letter *Yod*. And this is the Sephirah called:

# **Wisdom**

<note>original: *Chokmah*</note>

It is written: "The Lord possessed me at the beginning of His way, before His works of old." "Before" <note>original: *Kedem*</note> is the tip of the letter *Yod*. "Then" <note>original: *Az*</note> is the secret of **Understanding** <note>original: *Binah*, the third Sephirah</note>, and this is its "building." "Then Moses sang," "Then Israel sang," "I was anointed from then, from the beginnings of the earth," "Your throne is established from then." And also regarding the Wisdom of which Solomon said: "All this I have tested with wisdom; I said, 'I will be wise,' but it was far from me" <note>Ecclesiastes 7:23</note>. We have already explained "far" in "That which was far off and exceeding deep, who can find it?" and "She brings her bread from afar." This is because it is written: "And the Lord gave wisdom to Solomon" <note>1 Kings 5:26</note>; however, this was the **Final Wisdom**, which is called **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malchut*, the tenth and final Sephirah</note>. From within the "Kingdom," Solomon desired to enter the Wisdom called "Beginning," the Thought that clings to the Crown. Thus he said: "All this I have tested with the wisdom called Kingdom, and I said I will be wise [in the higher wisdom]..."

<column-break/>

...in mercy, and also the "Great One" you shall hear in the tradition (Kabbalah) "mouth to mouth," with God's help. Therefore it is written: "He who finds a wife finds good and obtains favor (*Ratzon*) from the Lord" <note>Proverbs 18:22</note>. One who knows this secret will understand the secret of: "And the Lord God built the side (rib) which He had taken from the man into a woman, and brought her to the man" <note>Genesis 2:22</note>. And it is written: "And the man said: 'This time it is bone of my bones and flesh of my flesh; this shall be called Woman (*Ishah*), for from Man (*Ish*) was this taken.'" Our Sages of blessed memory said in Tractate Niddah regarding "And the Lord God built the side": this teaches that the Holy One, blessed be He, gave an "extra understanding" (*Binah*) to women more than to men. It is written: "And Leah went out to meet him... and said, 'You must come to me, for I have surely hired you'" <note>Genesis 30:16</note>. And it is written: "And of the children of Issachar, men who had understanding of the times" <note>1 Chronicles 12:33</note>—they inherited all of this from their mother, for "if you call out to Understanding" <note>Proverbs 2:3; playing on the word for 'Mother', *Im*</note>. Therefore, for the sin of [unfulfilled] vows, a man's wife dies, Heaven forbid. One who understands this reason will understand what they said: "Bathsheba was destined for David since the six days of Creation, but he ate her as unripe fruit" <note>meaning he took her before the proper time</note>. Sometimes there is a virgin woman who is not a man's destined mate. Therefore they said: "An unlearned man (*Am Ha'aretz*) who marries the daughter of a priest—their match will not go well; either she will bury him, or he will bury her." It is written: "They have dealt treacherously against the Lord, for they have begotten strange children; now shall the new moon devour them with their portions" <note>Hosea 5:7</note>. Therefore it is written: "If you call out to Understanding," and therefore they said: "He who finds a wife finds good and obtains favor (*Ratzon*) from the Lord." A "finding" only refers to something that a man had lost. Therefore David said: "For I am ready for a side (*tzela*) <note>usually translated as 'fall', but here linked to the 'rib/side' of Adam</note> and my pain is continually before me." But because Joseph the Righteous merited a proper destined mate, it says of him: "And the favor (*Ratzon*) of Him who dwelt in the bush" <note>Deuteronomy 33:16</note>. Therefore he inherited the birthright—"I have made him my firstborn, higher than the kings of the earth"—and he was appointed sustainer of the world, as it is written: "And Joseph sustained his father and his brothers." Therefore, the coffin of Joseph went through the wilderness alongside the Ark of the Covenant. **And know** that at the start of the prayer it is written: "O Lord (*Adonai*), open my lips, and my mouth shall declare Your praise," and at the end it is written: "Let the words of my mouth... be acceptable (*Ratzon*)." Consider what was said regarding Jacob's words: "From Asher his bread shall be fat, and he shall yield royal dainties," but he did not explain further. Moses our Teacher, however, explained all the keys.

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the Divine emanations (Sefirot), transitioning from the definition of "Beginning" to the complex relationship between Fear and Love. It then explores the Sephirah of "Understanding" or "Eden," describing how Divine flow descends from the highest hidden source into the structured world. It connects to the previous page's discussion of the "Beginning" and the "Will" (Keter).</meta>

...and his lack will be found, for "an unseemly thing" <note>original: *ervat davar*</note> has cast us out of His palace. And so he remains all his days in fear and anxiety lest the King find in him a blemish or lack or "unseemly thing" and cast him out, as the verse says: *lest He see an unseemly thing in you and turn away from you* <note>Deuteronomy 23:15</note>. Thus, he is fearful and anxious all his days because of his great love and desire to cling to the King and be among His servants. And this is the Fear that is **above** Love. Regarding this, it was said of Abraham when he sought to bind Isaac: *For now I know that you fear God* <note>Genesis 22:12</note>. This is the **Internal Fear**, for the motivator for this level is such that his body, his wife, his children, and his possessions are not worth as much to him as the surrender to the Blessed Connection—like one who buys something worth a hundred in silver and gold for a single penny. Therefore, "Now I know that you fear God," and this is the Internal Fear for which Abraham our father, peace be upon him, surrendered his body, his wife, his children, and his possessions, and of him it was said: *Abraham my beloved*. And here I hint: Fear is below, Love is above it, and Fear is above that. And behold, all is hinted at in the matter of the levels of the **Sefirot** <term>Sefirot: the ten divine attributes or emanations through which God interacts with the world</term>.

## And Now
Observe what the scripture says:
->The beginning of wisdom is the fear of the Lord<-
<note>Psalm 111:10</note>
And it is written: *Happy is the man who fears the Lord, who greatly delights in His commandments* <note>Psalm 112:1</note>. This is the Fear called "Above Love." And so: *Happy is everyone who fears the Lord, who walks in His ways* <note>Psalm 128:1</note>. However, *so that His fear may be upon your faces, that you do not sin* <note>Exodus 20:17</note>—there is an internal and external aspect to these things. Contemplate great principles; great chambers hang by a hair. Happy is he to whom the Blessed Lord grants eyes to see and understand. And now, contemplate the secret of **Internal Fear** and the secret of **External Fear**. Internal Fear is the secret of the levels of the Blessed King, the fulfillment of positive commandments. External Fear is the secret of guarding against the "External Husks" <note>original: *Kelipot*; the forces of concealment or evil</note> called the "uncircumcision" <note>original: *orlah*</note> and the impurity of
->negative commandments<-

## And Know
That when the secret of this Fear clings to the **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malchut*, the final Sephirah</note>

<column-break/>

it is written: *A woman who fears the Lord, she shall be praised* <note>Proverbs 31:30</note>. She shall surely be praised; all is hinted at, and all is through the worthy children. And this Sephirah is called:

## Eden
That is, the source that flows from the **Crown** <note>original: *Keter*, the highest, most hidden Sephirah</note>. For *Eden* is a term for "source" or "origin." And so we say regarding Sarah our mother, after the source of her blood had ceased and she was no longer fit to give birth, and when it was renewed as a source of blood: *After I have grown old, shall I have pleasure?* <note>original: *ednah*, which the author links to *Eden*; Genesis 18:12</note>—meaning the source of life and the source of milk, for milk is drawn to a woman from the blood, according to those who know the secrets of creation. And according to thought and will, this is the secret of the beginning of emanation and the flow into creation called *Eden*, meaning the source drawn from the **Crown**. It is called a "source" <note>original: *makor*</note> <note>The manuscript has a typo, *pakor*, likely meaning *makor*</note> just as a person sees water coming out of a source, yet no one sees the origin from which the source begins, for it is deep, deep. And so we say: *And a river went out of Eden* <note>Genesis 2:10</note>; it goes out from the source. In the Arabic language, they call a source *ma'adan* <note>meaning a mine or a source of minerals/precious things</note>. And so, the beginning of Thought, which is the secret of the first **Wisdom** <note>original: *Chokhmah*</note>, is drawn from the **Crown** which is called "Mother." Therefore, *It is deep, deep; who can find it?* <note>Ecclesiastes 7:24</note>. And it is written: *Counsel in the heart of man is like deep water; but a man of understanding will draw it out* <note>Proverbs 20:5</note>.

## Therefore
The secret of the **Chariot** <note>original: *Merkavah*; the structure of the Divine realm</note> and the unification
->of the Perfect One I hint at<-
"And a river went out of Eden to water the garden." "And a river went out of Eden" is the Central Column <note>The middle path of the Sefirotic tree that balances extremes</note> drawn from the **Crown** through **Wisdom** and **Understanding** to water the Garden—which is **Kingdom** <note>original: *Malchut*</note>. Thus far is the Unification. But *from there it was parted, and became four heads*—from here onwards is the secret of the **World of Separation**, the secret of the four camps of the **Divine Presence** <note>original: *Shekhinah*</note>, and the Palaces and the other appointed ministers were all separated from one another. But "the river going out of Eden to water the garden"—this is the secret of the Lord, **Beauty** <note>original: *Tiferet*</note>, and **Kingdom**, the secret of Unification. And this is the secret of the three Sephirot of the Sabbath: *And God blessed the seventh day and sanctified it, for on it...*
<note>Sha'arei Tzedek 13a 13 1 Sabbath</note>

<summary>This page explores the mystical hierarchy of Fear and Love, defining a "Higher Fear" that stems from an intense desire to remain in the Divine presence. It also details the Sephirah of "Eden" (Understanding) as the hidden source of Divine flow that unites the Godhead before branching into the lower, separated worlds.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the exploration of Abraham’s spiritual character, specifically his role as the architect of "safeguards" for the Divine law. It links the previous discussion of "Internal Fear" to the Kabbalistic structure of the Sefirot, explaining how Abraham’s actions in the physical world (building altars and journeying) mirrored the protection of Divine light from external, unholy forces.</meta>

The safeguards that Abraham made at the beginning are a revelation of the matter itself. Prepare yourself, for a great secret is revealed to the eyes: this is the mystery of the altars that Abraham built and the mystery of his journeys and his deeds. He created types of "watches" around the Torah in truth. Therefore, the Torah says: *Because Abraham obeyed my voice, kept my charge, my commandments, my statutes, and my laws* <note>Genesis 26:5</note>. This implies two Torahs—the Written and the Oral—and both began from the "Place of Abraham." This refers to the emanation of **Tiferet** <term>Tiferet: the Sefirah of "Beauty" or "Harmony," often associated with the patriarch Jacob but here linked to the flow beginning from Abraham's attributes</term>, which is the mystery of **Gedulah** <note>Greatness, another name for Hesed/Kindness</note> and **Gevurah** <note>Strength or Judgment</note> down to the "neck." From the "arms" and below, a great deal of guarding is necessary, for this is the realm of the "External Ministers" <note>unholy or secondary spiritual forces</note> down to **Malchut** <term>Malchut: "Kingship," the lowest Sefirah that manifests Divine will in the physical world</term>. Therefore, both the "Shield of Abraham" and the "Shield of David" <note>referring to the Sefirot of Hesed and Malchut</note> require protection.

## And Now
Contemplate every place in the Torah where you find the concept of "guarding" <note>Hebrew: *shmirah*</note> and the matter of Abraham, as it is said: *and he kept my charge*. There are many deep matters and hints in what we mentioned above. Once you know this, contemplate the many matters hinted at in the verse: 
->The beginning of wisdom is the fear of the Lord<- 
<note>Psalm 111:10</note> 
We have already hinted to you that this **Sefirah** is called both "Wisdom" and "Fear." That is to say, it is the place of Fear, for one who achieves even a slight knowledge of the heights of this emanation realizes how much they must fear before God—due to the extreme fragility and lowliness of man compared to the exaltedness of the Blessed Name.

Fear exists in two ways: **External Fear** and **Internal Fear**. External Fear is lower than Love, while Internal Fear is higher than Love. Therefore, this specific Fear <note>Internal Fear</note> is above Love. How so? External Fear is the basic mystery of fearing to transgress the King’s command simply to avoid punishment, death, or being seized by the King’s decree and judged with harsh judgments. This is merely External Fear. 

But Internal Fear occurs when a person perceives the exaltedness of the Blessed Creator and the abundance of delights, wealth, and honor found in His palaces, prepared for those servants He desires. When a person reaches this level of knowledge, he will be struck with awe and trembling, saying: "Perhaps I am not worthy to stand in the King's palace?" For relative to His great loftiness, my own degradation and lack are vast.

<column-break/>

One must surround "Understanding" <note>the Sefirah of Binah</note> toward the place of "Kindness" <note>the Sefirah of Hesed</note>, because Kindness is the place where the "Ministers," the "Adversaries," and the "Oppressors" <note>spiritual forces of judgment or distraction</note> attempt to take hold. Therefore, in the place of **Gedulah** <note>Greatness/Kindness</note> and **Gevurah** <note>Strength</note>, guarding is required. This is the mystery of the "Guarding of the Sanctuary" <note>Mishkan</note> that God commanded the Priests and Levites. 

Behold, everything contains an internal and external hint. Regarding the constant guarding of the Sanctuary, the scripture says: *And you and your sons with you before the Tent of the Testimony* <note>Numbers 18:2</note>. This means the Priests and Levites guard the Sanctuary constantly. Happy are those who know the hidden things! Because around the Sanctuary, there are many "camps of impurity." Behold: 
->Solomon’s bed; sixty mighty men are around it, all of them handling the sword and expert in war<- 
<note>Song of Songs 3:7-8</note>
Therefore, "Who is wise and will observe these things?" It is also written: *And He placed him in the Garden of Eden to work it and to keep/guard it* <note>Genesis 2:15</note>. Therefore, regarding the Sabbath: "Remember" <note>Zakhor</note> corresponds to the day and "Keep" <note>Shamor</note> corresponds to the night. 

*You make darkness, and it is night, in which all the beasts of the forest creep forth. The young lions roar for their prey... the sun rises, they gather together* <note>Psalm 104:20-22</note>. 

Therefore, "He placed him in the Garden of Eden..." <insert>to work it and to guard it</insert>. But man did not do so; instead, he brought the "external forces" into the Palace, and because of this, all the spiritual orders were damaged. *But transgressors shall stumble in them* <note>Hosea 14:10</note>—specifically "in them" <note>meaning within the holy things themselves</note>, for *the wicked walk on every side* <note>Psalm 12:9</note>. 

Therefore, *a wise son makes a glad father, but a foolish son is the grief of his mother* <note>Proverbs 10:1</note>. It is also written: *My son, be wise, and make my heart glad, that I may answer him that reproaches me* <note>Proverbs 27:11</note>. Here, the order of the levels of the mystery of the "First Wisdom" and the "Last Wisdom" has been explained to you. Some Kabbalists say: "Wisdom is at the beginning and Wisdom is at the end." I am hinting to you: one who merits the "Last Wisdom" merits the "First Wisdom." As it says: *He gives wisdom to the wise* <note>Daniel 2:21</note>, and *In the heart of all that are wise-hearted I have put wisdom* <note>Exodus 31:6</note>. It is also written: *And it shall come to pass, if you shall diligently listen...* and *But if your heart turns away and you will not listen...* Everything is now explained.

## And Now
Contemplate: *And you shall keep my charge* <note>Leviticus 18:30</note> and *Because Abraham obeyed my voice and kept my charge*. Our Sages of blessed memory said: "Even the 'Mixing of Cooked Dishes' <term>Eruv Tavshilin: a Rabbinic law allowing one to prepare food for the Sabbath on a holiday that falls on a Friday</term> Abraham our father, peace be upon him, fulfilled." He made so many safeguards around the "Assembly of Israel." For everything that the Sages saw and enacted as a "fence around the Torah," they only did so in correspondence to [the higher spiritual structures]...

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic significance of "guarding" the Divine realm, using Abraham's life and the laws of the Sanctuary as examples. It distinguishes between the fear of punishment and a higher, internal awe of God's majesty, framing the Rabbinic "fences" around the law as essential protections against spiritual impurity.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of Abraham’s spiritual lineage and the structure of divine protection. It transitions from the physical journeys of Abraham to the mystical mechanics of the Exodus from Egypt, linking the historical event to the Sefirotic structure—specifically the role of Binah (Understanding) and the significance of the Tefillin (phylacteries).</meta>

Pharaoh refused to let us go, and the Lord killed every firstborn in the land of Egypt <note>original: "הקשה פרעה לשלחנו ויהרוג יד"ה ' יי ' כל בכור בארץ מצרים", referencing Exodus 13:15</note>. This "firstborn" refers to the head of all the governments among the celestial ministers of the nations; Egypt is their firstborn. Therefore, the Lord killed every firstborn in the land of Egypt. For Abraham our father (peace be upon him) went down to Egypt, and Egypt is the head of all the governments of the celestial ministers. Egypt is "second to the First" <note>This likely refers to Egypt's status in the hierarchy of worldly powers relative to the divine order</note>. Therefore, the mystery of Egypt is that it is the "firstborn" among the nations. This is the meaning of "To Him who smote Egypt in their firstborn" <note>Psalm 136:10</note>. All of this occurs through the power of the **Supernal Firstborn** <term>Supernal Firstborn: A reference to the Sefirah of Chokhmah (Wisdom), the first active manifestation of divine thought</term>, as it says: *Sanctify to me every firstborn* <note>Exodus 13:2</note>. This is the mystery of the power that stands over the firstborn of Egypt.

Therefore, "against all the gods of Egypt I will execute judgments; I am the Lord" <note>Exodus 12:12</note>. And who acted against the gods of Egypt? **Binah** <term>Binah: "Understanding," the third Sefirah, associated with liberation and the "Fifty Gates" of wisdom</term>, which is higher than them. She is the "Sukkah" <note>protection/covering</note> for them. Therefore, the Exodus from Egypt is mentioned in the Torah fifty times, corresponding to the fifty gates of **Binah**. It was through **Binah** that they left Egypt; her fifty gates were opened for Israel, and only then did they depart. If the fifty gates of **Binah** had not been opened, Israel would have had no power to leave Egypt.

As it is written: "If the Holy One, blessed be He, had not brought our ancestors out of Egypt, then we and our children and our children's children would still be enslaved to Pharaoh in Egypt." This is because the celestial minister of Egypt is a "firstborn" <note>a primary power in the lower realms</note>, and none can overcome him except **Binah**, who is above him with her fifty gates. For all freedom and all redemption depend upon **Binah**, since she is higher than all the celestial ministers. She is called "Rechov" <note>literally "Broad Place" or "Street"</note> because the ministers and the various exiles exist only from the "Arms" <note>the Sefirot of Chesed and Gevurah</note> downward. Therefore, the exile in Egypt was the first of all exiles.

Abraham went down to Egypt, and behold, **Binah**—which possesses fifty gates—was opened, and thus redemption and freedom were found. This is the great secret of: *And you shall count seven sabbaths of years... seven times seven years... it shall be a Jubilee for you* <note>Leviticus 25:8-10</note>. The **Jubilee** <term>Jubilee (Yovel): The 50th year in the biblical cycle, mystically linked to the 50 Gates of Binah and the ultimate return to one's source</term> is the fiftieth year, and it is written: *In this year of Jubilee each of you shall return to his possession.* Then the servants go out to freedom, because in this spiritual "Place" there is no exile, no celestial minister, and no adversary. Therefore, "he shall have redemption" and "in the Jubilee he shall go out."

Therefore, the second portion of the **Tefillin** <term>Tefillin: Ritual leather boxes containing Torah scrolls, worn during prayer as a physical connection to divine intellect</term>, which is *And it shall be when He brings you* <note>Exodus 13:11</note>, contains the secret of **Binah**, the secret of the **Jubilee**, and the secret of freedom and redemption. This is why it is written at the end of the passage: *And it shall be for a sign on your hand and for frontlets between your eyes, for with a strong hand the Lord brought us out of Egypt.* This "Hand" <note>Yad</note> is literal—a great and explicit secret, for in this place...

->Sha'arei Tzedek 13b 13 II [entry]<-

<column-break/>

...Fear and Understanding are the two portions of the **Tefillin of the Head**. And behold, I shall explain this with the help of the One who "teaches man knowledge." It is written: *The Tree of Life was in the midst of the garden* <note>Genesis 2:9</note>. "Life" literally refers to **Binah**, and their source is **Chokhmah** <term>Chokhmah: "Wisdom," the second Sefirah, the raw flash of intuition</term>, and their "Tree" is **Da'at** <term>Da'at: "Knowledge," the bridge between the upper intellect (Wisdom/Understanding) and the emotions</term>. This is **Tiferet** <term>Tiferet: "Beauty" or "Glory," the central Sefirah that balances the system</term>. This is the secret of why the Holy One, blessed be He, "wears Tefillin" <note>A famous Talmudic metaphor (Berakhot 6a) suggesting that God also binds Himself to His people through these spiritual symbols</note>.

Now you see how great the commandment of Tefillin is! Consider their power below, and how grave a matter it is to neglect them. It is a great matter, as it was said: "Whoever wishes to accept upon himself the yoke of the Kingdom of Heaven in its fullness should relieve himself, wash his hands, put on Tefillin, and recite the Shema." See the great wonders! To the eyes, there are four portions in the Tefillin of the head, corresponding to four sections:

### The Portions
**First: Sanctify to me every firstborn** <note>Exodus 13:1-10</note>
**Second: And it shall be when He brings you** <note>Exodus 13:11-16</note>

**Third: Hear O Israel** <note>Deuteronomy 6:4-9</note>
**Fourth: And it shall come to pass if you surely listen** <note>Deuteronomy 11:13-21</note>

I shall explain this to you. You already know that the secret of Unity (*Yichud*) exists in two places: the foundation and the union of **Malchut** <note>Kingship/The Presence</note> within **Tiferet** through the secret of the union of **Tiferet** within **Keter** <term>Keter: "Crown," the highest and most transcendent Sefirah</term> via **Chokhmah** and **Binah**. It is written: *On that day the Lord shall be one and His name one* <note>Zechariah 14:9</note>. "One" in the Supernal Unity, and "One" in the Unity of **Malchut**. The secret of the Tefillin of the head and its four portions is the secret of the union of **Tiferet** in **Keter** via **Chokhmah** and **Binah**. How so?

## First Portion: Sanctify to Me Every Firstborn
This is literally **Chokhmah**. It is the mystery of the "Firstborn," the beginning of all. As it says, "In the beginning (*Reishit*) God created," and it is written, "for he is the first (*Reishit*) of his strength." And since the law of the firstborn applies here, **Tiferet** takes hold of **Keter** through **Chokhmah**. This is the portion of *Sanctify to me every firstborn*.

## Second Portion: And It Shall Be When He Brings You
This is the mystery of the Exodus from Egypt in all its aspects. As it is said: "And it shall be when your son asks you tomorrow, saying... you shall say to him: With a strong hand..." The secret of the "Strong Hand" is **Binah**, from which the "Hand" <note>the lower Sefirot</note> draws its power. "He brought us out with His hand from Egypt, from the house of bondage." See the wonders! "From the house of bondage"—from the "Arms" <note>Chesed and Gevurah</note> downward is the house of bondage. Egypt is the head of those governments, and because of it, there shall be...

<meta>The text cuts off here, with the catchword "יהי" (shall be) indicating the start of the next page.</meta>

<summary>This page explains the Kabbalistic significance of the Exodus and the Tefillin, mapping the first two portions of the head-tefillin to the Sefirot of Chokhmah and Binah. It highlights how the Exodus was achieved through the "Fifty Gates of Understanding" (Binah), which overrides the celestial powers of Egypt.</summary>
<keywords>Exodus, Egypt, Binah, Chokhmah, Tefillin, Firstborn, Jubilee, Fifty Gates, Sefirot, Redemption</keywords>

It specifically focuses on the transition from the unknowable "Ayin" (Nothingness) to the manifest "Yesh" (Being), using the patriarch Jacob’s vision of the ladder as a primary proof-text.</meta>

...rested from all His work, for on it He surely rested <note>original: "שבת מכל מלאכתו כי בו שבת", referencing Genesis 2:3</note>. The secret of **Rest** lies in **Malchut** <term>Malchut: "Kingship," the tenth and final Sefirah, which receives the divine flow and acts as the "Sabbath" or resting place of the higher energies</term>. Everything done in the world is done through its hand; therefore, it is surely "Eden."

The scripture cries out and says: *And the Lord God planted a garden in Eden from the east* <note>Genesis 2:8</note>. This is the secret of **Eden** which emerges from the **Keter** <term>Keter: "Crown," the highest Sefirah, representing the Divine Will beyond human comprehension</term>, the Will from which there is no limit and no end <note>The Infinite, or Ein Sof</note>. And the "Garden" is **Binah** <term>Binah: "Understanding," the third Sefirah, often depicted as a womb or a garden watered by the river of Wisdom</term>; it is the secret of the river that flows out from **Chokhmah** <term>Chokhmah: "Wisdom," the second Sefirah, the primal point of revelation</term> to water the Assembly of Israel. As it is written: *They are abundantly satisfied with the fatness of Your house, and You give them drink from the river of Your pleasures* <note>Psalm 36:8; the Hebrew word for "Your pleasures," "Eden'echa," shares a root with Eden</note>. This literally means the "River of your Edens." And it is written: *The river shall rise over its banks on this side and that, every tree for food whose fruit shall not fail and whose leaf shall not wither* <note>cf. Ezekiel 47:12</note>. 

Therefore, the garden requires guarding: *And He placed him in the Garden of Eden to work it and to guard it* <note>Genesis 2:15</note>. This is the secret of guarding the Sanctuary below. He who knows the secret of Eden knows the secret of **Atzilut** <term>Atzilut: "Emanation," the highest of the four worlds in Kabbalah, where the Divine Light is most pure</term> and the flow through the levels and the channels until they reach **Malchut**. This Sefirah is 

->called<-

**Yesh** <term>Yesh: "There is" or "Existence," representing manifest divine substance</term>. For the Sefirah above it is called **Ayin** <term>Ayin: "Nothingness," referring to the Keter, which is so sublime it cannot be grasped or described</term>, meaning it cannot be attained; therefore, it is hinted at by the "thorn" <note>the small stroke on top</note> of the letter **Yod**. But "Thought"—which is the secret of the expansion of the Desire called **Yesh**—is the secret of the letter **Yod** of the Unique Name <note>The Tetragrammaton, Y-H-V-H</note>.

**Know** that when Jacob our father (peace be upon him) reached the secret of the Ladder at the known levels, he stood between the Assembly of Israel <note>Malchut</note> and **Tiferet** <term>Tiferet: "Beauty" or "Harmony," the central Sefirah that balances the tree</term>. *And behold, the Lord stood over him* <note>Genesis 28:13</note>; it means He arrived to emanate upon him ten types of flow from **Chokhmah** and **Binah**. This is because he [Jacob] is the secret of the **Middle Line** <term>Middle Line: The central column of the Sefirot, associated with Jacob, which mediates between Mercy and Judgment</term>. When he ascended above the "Shoulders" and took hold of these two Sefirot, he took hold of **Keter**. Then he began to say: *Surely the Lord is in this place, and I did not know it* <note>Genesis 28:16</note>. For until now, he had only ascended to the "Shoulders," but now he had taken hold of the Sefirah called **Yesh** <note>The word "Surely" in Hebrew is "Achen," but the text focuses on "Yesh" (There is) found in the verse: "Achen Yesh YHVH..."</note>. Therefore, *He was afraid and said, "How awesome (Nora) is this place!"* <note>Genesis 28:17</note>. He literally "feared" the **Place of Awe**, and it is called "Awesome" (Nora) after His name. The sign for this is: *Awesome in praises, doing wonders* <note>Exodus 15:11</note>, for the place of wonders is the Supernal Sefirah. As it is written:

<column-break/>

*I will praise You, for I am awesomely and wonderfully made; wonderful are Your works* <note>Psalm 139:14</note>. *To Him who alone does great wonders* <note>Psalm 136:4</note>. And in the Assembly of Israel, it hints at a great matter: *He has made a memorial for His wonders; the Lord is gracious and full of compassion* <note>Psalm 111:4</note>.

**Know** that when the Lord performed miracles and wonders in the wilderness, they [the Israelites] questioned and investigated at the place of "Testing and Strife" <note>Massa and Meribah</note>. It is said of them: *Because of the strife of the children of Israel, and because they tested the Lord, saying: "Is (ha-Yesh) the Lord among us or not (Ayin)?"* <note>Exodus 17:7</note>. They wanted to act here within these three Sefirot, in the place where there is no distinction or separation at all. For **Ayin** is the secret of **Keter**, while **Yesh** is the secret of **Chokhmah** and **Binah**. 

And now, know and believe that the "Great Good" hidden in the utmost heights is the secret of these three Sefirot, which are the secret of the World to Come. The good hidden for the righteous is called "Ascents," as we explained regarding the level of **Binah**. For wherever we mention **Binah**, understand that it connects **Keter** and **Chokhmah**. And behold, the World to Come, of which it is said: *How great is Your goodness which You have hidden for those who fear You* <note>Psalm 31:19</note>—literally for those who "fear" You—is called **Yesh**. As it is said: *To grant substance (Yesh) to those who love me, and I will fill their treasuries* <note>Proverbs 8:21</note>. "Those who love me" literally, for in the place of Love one ascends to **Chokhmah** and **Binah**, which is the secret of **Yesh**. This inheritance is hidden for those who love You and fear You, and everything is clarified.

**Know** that in the place called **Yesh** lies the secret of the beginning of the **Chariot** <term>Chariot: "Merkavah," the divine structure or "vehicle" of God's manifestation</term>, in the secret of refinement and mercy. Therefore, *I will fill their treasuries* (Otzrot-ehem Amale) can be read as *I will fill the treasuries of Yah* <note>a wordplay on the name of God, Yah (Yod-He)</note>. And it is written: *The fear of the Lord is a treasure* <note>Isaiah 33:6</note> and *By knowledge (Da'at) the rooms are filled* <note>Proverbs 24:4</note>. Rather, the Sefirot are the secret of the **Tefillin of the Head** <term>Tefillin: Phylacteries. The "Tefillin of the Head" correspond to the higher Sefirot (Chokhmah, Binah, Da'at), representing divine intellect</term> which the Holy One, Blessed be He—who is **Tiferet**—places [on His head] when He reaches **Da'at** <term>Da'at: "Knowledge," the point where Chokhmah and Binah unite</term>. 

When **Keter** takes hold through the secret of **Chokhmah** and **Binah**, via the secret of the **Middle Line** that ascends to the very top—as when Jacob our father (peace be upon him) ascended and took hold of these two Sefirot—the verse says of him: *I will cause you to ride on the high places of the earth and feed you with the heritage of Jacob your father* <note>Isaiah 58:14</note>. What is the secret of the heritage? It is the Tefillin and the marking of the head, and the secret of: *Then all the peoples of the earth shall see that the name of the Lord is called upon you, and they shall be afraid of you* <note>Deuteronomy 28:10</note>. For the **Awe**...

It links Jacob’s ladder and the Tefillin of the Head to the "Middle Line," representing the conduit of divine flow from the highest crowns to the physical world.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the Kabbalistic analysis of the Sefirot, shifting focus from the origins of "Eden" to the practical application of these divine mysteries in prayer and ritual. It specifically examines the "Shema" prayer and the "Kiddushah" (Sanctification), explaining how the human voice and physical ritual (Tefillin) serve to unify the divine emanations from the lowest realm back to the Infinite source.</meta>

One must prolong the word **One** <note>original: "אחד" (Echad), the final word of the first verse of the Shema</note> until he binds 

## Now
the **Kingship** <term>Malchut: The tenth Sefirah, representing the manifest world and the Divine Presence among us</term> to the **Foundation** <term>Yesod: The ninth Sefirah, acting as the channel or "funnel" for divine energy</term>, which is the "tip of the [letter] Vav," which is **Beauty** <term>Tiferet: The sixth Sefirah, the "heart" of the system, often represented by the letter Vav</term>. From Beauty, he ascends to **Understanding** <term>Binah: The third Sefirah, the source of divine logic and structure</term>, which is the secret of the Divine Name <note>YH</note>; and from Understanding to **Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah: The second Sefirah, the point of pure revelation</term>; and from Wisdom to the **Crown** <term>Keter: The highest Sefirah, the Divine Will</term>, which is the tip of the [letter] Yod, which is the **Infinite** <note>Ein Sof</note>. 

Thus, the one who recites this one verse of the *Recitation of the Shema* <note>the central declaration of Jewish faith</note> unifies the **Sefirot** <term>Sefirot: The ten divine attributes or emanations through which God interacts with the world</term> from top to bottom and from bottom to top. This is also the matter of the *Sanctification* <note>the "Kadosh, Kadosh, Kadosh" prayer</note>: wherever you find the word **Holy** <note>Kadosh</note>, it is the secret of the Pure Palaces and the Unity. 

"Pure" means to distance oneself from the "external forces" <note>the Sitra Achra or "Other Side," representing spiritual impurity or evil</note>. Thus, "Holiness" is found to be an additional **Emanation** <term>Atzilut: The highest of the four worlds in Kabbalah, the realm of pure divinity</term> from the Crown, which is the place of Holiness. Because of this, it is said of the Nazirite: *He shall be holy, he shall let the locks of the hair of his head grow long* <note>Numbers 6:5</note>, and it is said of the bald spot: *He is bald, he is pure* <note>Leviticus 13:40</note>. The "Holy" is the secret of the letter *Lamed*, and "Purity" is the secret of the letter *Mem*. This one is the secret of Emanation, and that one is the secret of Cessation. 

Therefore, "Holiness" is assigned to the Priests, and "Purity" to the Levites, as it is said: *Anoint Aaron and his sons and sanctify them* <note>Exodus 30:30</note>, and *Thus shall you do to them to purify them... let them pass a razor over all their flesh* <note>Numbers 8:7</note>. Like the light of the heavens for purity, as in "purifying the altar," the word "pure" shall be said. And after we have hinted at this:

## Know
that "Holiness" is the secret hint of the drawing down of the **Emanation** from the **Crown**. The secret of "Holy, Holy, Holy" is the secret of drawing an "alphabet of holiness" from the three upper Sefirot to the middle ones, and from the middle ones to the final ones. From the final ones, every holiness is united in **Kingship**. 

Behold, I shall explain: "Holy, Holy, Holy" are the secrets of **Greatness** <term>Gedulah: Another name for Chesed (Loving-kindness), the fourth Sefirah</term>, **Strength** <term>Gevurah: The fifth Sefirah, representing judgment and restraint</term>, and **Beauty** <term>Tiferet</term>. They receive the emanation of the flow of Holiness from the **Crown**, **Wisdom**, and **Understanding**. From them, it flows to **Victory**, **Glory**, and **Foundation** <note>Netzach, Hod, and Yesod</note>—this is what is written afterward: *The Lord of Hosts* <note>YHVH Tzevaot</note>. From them, it flows to **Kingship**, regarding which it is written: *The whole earth is full of His glory*. 

Here you have the secret of the Unity and the Sanctification—how they are united within the **Ten Sefirot**. Regarding the "Holiness" that Isaiah spoke of when he said: *I saw the Lord sitting upon a high and lofty throne* <note>Isaiah 6:1</note>—let it not enter your mind that it is written as anything other than *Adonai* <note>Lord</note>. *My eyes have seen the King, the Lord of Hosts* <note>Isaiah 6:5</note> upon the throne.

<meta>catchword: And this is</meta>

<column-break/>

...of the head first, for this is the proper order. For if **Kingship** is not prepared, the **Crown** will not shine upon **Understanding** to provide the flow of emanation into **Beauty**. Likewise, there is a secret: in the middle, it is a transgression, and a "melodic note" separates the Chieftain <note>a reference to spiritual disruption or sin separating the soul from God</note>. The essential thing is not to converse or speak from the moment one recites the first blessing, so that they may be unified in a complete unity as one.

## However
the [letter] *Shin* with three heads <note>the letter ש appearing on the Tefillin</note> is a hint toward the unification of the upper Sefirot within **Beauty**, in the secret of *Y-H-V* <note>the first three letters of the Divine Name</note>, in the secret of the **Phylactery of the Head**. The [letter] *Shin* with four heads <note>the unique form of the letter ש on the other side of the Tefillin</note> is the secret of the **Phylactery of the Hand**, relating to the connection of the final letter *Heh* <note>the fourth letter of the Divine Name</note>, which is the secret of **Kingship** in connection with *Y-H-V*. Thus, the entire Name becomes one, in the secret of "The Name is One." 

Now, contemplate how **Kingship** is bound to the Head and is called a "Crown" <note>Atarah</note>, uniting with the **Crown** <note>Keter</note> in the secret of the three-headed *Shin* and the secret of the four-headed *Shin*. Therefore, the four-headed *Shin* is a "Law given to Moses at Sinai." After we have explained this, you will understand the power of the commandment of the Phylacteries of the Head and the Hand. Regarding the Tefillin, it is written: *O Lord, by these things men live* <note>Isaiah 38:16</note>. This is the secret of "The Holy One, Blessed be He, wears Tefillin," and it is all hinted at in the Talmud.

## And I am
explaining how the Unity is achieved through speech. It is written: *The Lord our God, the Lord is One* <note>the Shema</note>. This is the secret of the unity of the Four-Letter Name <note>the Tetragrammaton, YHVH</note>. As one says: 
1. The first "Lord" [YHVH] hints at the first letter of the Name, which is the *Yod*, encompassing the secret of the **Supreme Crown** and **Wisdom** (the secret of the tip of the *Yod* and the *Yod* itself). 
2. "Our God" [*Eloheinu*] is the secret of **Understanding**, which is the secret of the first *Heh* of the Name <note>OCR says Vav, but contextually refers to the first Heh</note>. 
3. The [second] "Lord" [YHVH] is the secret of **Beauty**, which is the secret of the letter *Vav* of the Name, the third letter. 
4. "One" [*Echad*] is the secret of the unification of the letter *Yod* and the final *Heh* of the Name—the secret of **Kingship**. 

When one prolongs the word "One," he must prolong it until his thought ascends to the place where he began at the start. How? From the beginning, he started from the tip of the *Yod*, expanding from there to Understanding and Beauty, until reaching Kingship in the secret of **One**.

It details how the letters of the Divine Name and the specific shapes of the letter Shin represent the flow of energy from the Infinite (Ein Sof) down to the manifest world (Malchut).</summary>

<meta>This page continues the Kabbalistic exploration of how physical rituals mirror the divine structure. Having established how the "Shema" prayer unifies the Sefirot from the Infinite down to the earthly realm, the text now focuses on the Tefillin (phylacteries), explaining how the four scriptural passages contained within them correspond to the upper Sefirot and how the act of binding them on the head and arm achieves a total unification of the Divine Name.</meta>

Let the "strong one" come to the **Hand** <note>original: "Yad," meaning the hand or the Sefirah of Gevurah</note>, for this represents **Strengths** and that represents **Strength** <note>original: "Gevurot" and "Gevurah"</note>. Therefore, it is written: "By a strong hand the Lord [brought us out]," because the Exodus from Egypt occurred only through the "strong hand." Consequently, this is the 

second section of the **Tefillin** <term>Tefillin: Black leather boxes containing parchment scrolls with Torah verses, worn during weekday morning prayers</term>. You thus find that the first two sections—"Sanctify to Me every firstborn" <note>Exodus 13:1</note> and "And it shall be when He brings you" <note>Exodus 13:11</note>—represent the secret of **Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah: The second Sefirah, the flash of intuition</term> and **Understanding** <term>Binah: The third Sefirah, the processing of that intuition</term>.

### The Third Section: "Hear, O Israel"
"And you shall love"—this is the attribute of **Love**, which is the attribute of Abraham, the **Sefirah** <term>Sefirah: One of ten divine attributes in Kabbalah</term> called **Greatness** <term>Gedulah: Also known as Chesed or Lovingkindness</term>. It is written: "And you shall write them upon the doorposts of your house and upon your gates." These "gates" are the **Fifty Gates of Understanding**, for one must unify **Greatness** and **Strength** with them, so that they may enter the Gates of Understanding. Therefore, the verse says, "and upon your gates."

### The Fourth Section: "And it shall be if you surely listen"
This is the secret of **Strength** <term>Gevurah: The Sefirah of judgment and restraint</term>, for this entire section speaks of punishments, such as "Take heed to yourselves" and so on, and "the anger of the Lord will be kindled." Thus, it is explained to you that the four sections in the Tefillin are the secret of the unification of the **Crown** <term>Keter: The highest Sefirah</term>, written through Wisdom and Understanding in the first two sections, and through **Beauty** <term>Tiferet: The central Sefirah, often called the "King"</term>, Greatness, and Strength in the last two sections. You find that in the Tefillin of the head, Beauty is unified with Wisdom, Understanding, Greatness, and Strength—all becoming one single matter.

### And behold, Beauty, which is the "Middle Pillar"
unifies these four sections, which are the Divine Name <note>YHWH</note>, in the secret of the **Crown**, which is the "tip of the [letter] Yod" <note>the small stroke at the top of the letter Yod</note> that clings to Wisdom. Thus, the letter **Vav** <note>representing the number six and the Sefirah of Beauty</note> of the Unique Name is unified above, and all six Sefirot become one Vav, one world, and one matter. 

Therefore, "the Holy One, Blessed be He, wears Tefillin." This refers to the actual "Holy One, Blessed be He," who is the secret of Beauty. Now the secret of the Tefillin of the head, their reason, and their benefit has been explained to you. The secret of God wearing Tefillin is that the head Tefillin represent the "Upper Unification." However, the Tefillin of the hand represent the secret of the unification of **Kingship** <term>Malchut: The final Sefirah, representing the physical world and the Divine Presence</term> with Beauty. Therefore, it says: "And it shall be for a sign upon your hand" <note>Exodus 13:16</note>.

<column-break/>

with the Divine Name. For we have explained to you that "Strength," which is the secret of the **Hand**, completes the sections within the Tefillin. They are the secret of the six Sefirot. The four sections represent four Sefirot unified in **Beauty**, which is the secret of the letter **Vav** in the **Supreme Crown** by means of these sections. Thus, it becomes unified in the three letters **Y-H-W** <note>the first three letters of the Tetragrammaton</note> when the Sefirot are drawn down to the **Hand**, which is the secret of Strength and the secret of the fourth section. 

Behold, the drawing down of all of them is held by the Hand, which is in the secret of **Y-H-W**. One must then join the final **H** <note>the last letter of the Divine Name, representing Malchut</note> to the Hand. Therefore, "it shall be for a sign upon your hand" <note>spelled "Yad-Khaf-He" in the Torah</note>—they are joined forever and ever. You find that **Kingship**, which is the secret of the final **H** of the Name, is joined to the Hand, which is the end of the four sections held in Beauty. They are bound together by the "knot" of the hand, which is shaped like the letter **Yod**. 

This knot is the secret of the "Small Yod" called **Foundation** <term>Yesod: The ninth Sefirah, associated with the procreative bond</term>. For the "Large Yod" is the **Supreme Wisdom**, while the "Small Yod" is Foundation. Therefore, the Covenant of Circumcision <note>Brit Milah</note> is the secret of revealing the letter **Yod**. Thus, Kingship is bound to Beauty by means of Strength (called the Hand), which is the end of the sections. Therefore, Kingship is brought close to the Hand at the end of the verse "And it shall be for a sign upon your hand"—it is a literal "sign," and the end of the "Sign of the Holy Covenant" is **Foundation**, which is the secret of the Tefillin knot of the hand in the shape of a Yod.

For this reason, you place the Tefillin of the hand on the **left** [arm], which "grasps" Kingship with it. Thus, the Unique Name becomes unified through two types of unification in the matter of the Tefillin. Because of this, the Tefillin of the head are called "Severe Holiness" and the Tefillin of the hand are called "Light Holiness," each thing in its proper place. I shall now explain further.

### Know that when the Tefillin are in a person's possession,
it is proper to put on both of them in order to unify Kingship with Beauty, and Beauty with the Crown. But if a person only has one of the Tefillin, he should put it on, even though it does not achieve the unification completely; he unifies it within the Sefirot adjacent to that specific Tefillin he is wearing. Understand further: whenever one puts them on, he puts the hand Tefillin on first, for such is the order of the world and the unification. But when he removes them, he removes the [head Tefillin first].
