...of the Head first, for this is the correct order. [If] the **Kingship** <term>Malchut: The tenth Sefirah, representing the manifest world and the Divine Presence</term> is not rectified, the **Crown** <term>Keter: The highest Sefirah, the source of Divine Will</term> will not shine upon **Understanding** <term>Binah: The Sefirah of divine logic and processing</term> to bestow emanation into **Beauty** <term>Tiferet: The central Sefirah, balancing Mercy and Judgment</term>. Similarly, the secret of [an interruption] in between is a transgression, for "a grumbler separates a close friend" <note>Proverbs 16:28; in Kabbalah, this refers to speech that causes a rift between the divine attributes</note>. The essential rule is not to chat or speak from the moment one recites the first blessing until the end, so that they may unite in a complete **unification** <term>Yichud: A meditative focus intended to unify aspects of the Divine</term> as one.

**Indeed**, the three-headed letter *Shin* <note>The letter 'ש' embossed on the leather of the head-Tefillin</note> is a hint to the unification of the upper **Sefirot** in **Beauty**, through the secret of the Divine Name <note>original: "Y-H-V-H"</note> and the secret of the **Tefillin of the Head**. The four-headed letter *Shin* <note>A special version of the letter 'ש' with four branches found on the other side of the head-Tefillin</note> is the secret of the **Tefillin of the Hand** in the knot of the final letter *Heh* <note>The last letter of the Tetragrammaton, associated with Malchut</note>, which is the secret of **Kingship** in connection with the Divine Name. Thus, the entire Name becomes one, in the secret of "The Lord is One." Now, contemplate how **Kingship** is tied to the Head and is called a **Crown** <term>Atarah: A diadem; a name for Malchut when it ascends to the highest level</term>, unifying with the **Crown** <note>Keter</note> through the secret of the three-headed *Shin* and the four-headed *Shin*. Therefore, the four-headed *Shin* is a **Law given to Moses at Sinai** <note>An oral tradition with the status of biblical law</note>. Once we have explained this, you will understand how great is the power of the commandment of **Tefillin** of the head and of the hand. Regarding **Tefillin**, it is written: "The Lord is upon them, they shall live" <note>Isaiah 38:16; the word 'Aleihem' (upon them) is read homiletically as referring to the Tefillin worn on the body</note>. This is the secret of why the **Holy One, Blessed be He, dons Tefillin** <note>A famous Talmudic passage in Berakhot 6a</note>, and it is all hinted at in the **Talmud**.

**And behold**, I shall explain how the **unification** is achieved through speech. It is written: "The Lord our God, the Lord is One" <note>The Shema: Deuteronomy 6:4</note>. This is the secret of the unification of the four-letter Name. As it is said: The first "Lord" refers to the first letter of the Name, which is the *Yod*, including the secret of the **Supreme Crown** and **Wisdom** <term>Chokhmah: Divine intuition</term>, represented by the "tip of the *Yod*" and the *Yod* itself. "Our God" is the secret of **Understanding**, which is the secret of the first *Heh* of the Name. And the second "Lord" is the secret of **Beauty**, which is the secret of the letter *Vav*. "One" <note>original: "Echad"</note> is the secret of the unification of the letter *Dalet* <note>The final letter of the word 'Echad'</note> and the final *Heh* of the Name, which is the secret of **Kingship**. When one prolongs the word "One," he must extend his thought until it ascends back to the place where it began. Just as it started from the tip of the *Yod*, it expands here to **Understanding** and to **Beauty**, until it reaches **Kingship** in the secret of "One."

<column-break/>

<margin>One must prolong the word "One" until he binds the **Kingship**...</margin>
**Now**, **Kingship** is in **Foundation** <term>Yesod: The Sefirah of connection and transmission</term>, which is the "tip of the *Vav*" (representing **Beauty**), and from **Beauty** to **Understanding** (which is the secret of the Lord), and from **Understanding** to **Wisdom**, and from **Wisdom** to the **Crown**, which is the "tip of the *Yod*," which is the **Infinite** <term>Ein Sof: The Divine as It exists beyond all human comprehension</term>. Thus, the one who recites the first verse of the **Shema** <note>The central declaration of Jewish faith</note> unifies the **Sefirot** from top to bottom and from bottom to top. This is also the matter of **Holiness** <note>Kedushah</note> wherever you find it. "Holy" is the secret of the Chambers of Purity and Unification. "Pure" means to distance oneself from the "External Forces" <term>Sitra Achra: Literally 'the other side'; the forces of evil or impurity</term>. Thus, **Holiness** is an additional emanation from the **Crown**, which is the place of Holiness. Because this is so, it is said of the **Nazirite**: "He shall be holy, he shall let the locks of the hair of his head grow long" <note>Numbers 6:5</note>, and it is said of **Korah**: "Korah is pure" <note>A play on words; Korah's name relates to baldness/purity</note>. **Holiness** is the secret of the [letter] *Lamed*, and **Purity** is the secret of [the letters] *Mem* and *Samekh*—this is the secret of emanation and the secret of the separation. Therefore, **Holiness** is for the **Priests** <note>Kohanim</note> and **Purity** is for the **Levites**, as it is said: "You shall sanctify Aaron and his sons," and regarding the Levites: "Thus you shall do to them to cleanse them... let them pass a razor over all their flesh." As clear as the appearance of the heavens for **purity**; when he purifies the altar, it shall be called "pure." After we have hinted at this:

**Know** that **Holiness** is the secret hint of drawing down emanation from the **Crown**. The secret of "Holy, Holy, Holy" <note>The angelic sanctification from Isaiah 6:3</note> corresponds to the drawing down of the light of holiness from the three upper **Sefirot** to the middle ones, and from the middle ones to the final ones. From the final ones, all holiness is unified in **Kingship**. I shall now explain: "Holy, Holy, Holy" are the secrets of **Greatness** <note>Chesed/Mercy</note>, **Might** <note>Gevurah</note>, and **Beauty**. They receive the emanation of the flow of holiness from the **Crown**, **Wisdom**, and **Understanding**. From them, it flows to **Victory**, **Glory**, and **Foundation** <note>Netzach, Hod, and Yesod</note>—as it is written: "After the Lord of Hosts." And from them, it flows to **Kingship**, as it is written: "The whole earth is full of His glory." Behold, you have the secret of **Unification** and **Holiness**, how they are unified within the **Ten Sefirot**. As for the vision of **Holiness** described by **Isaiah**: "I saw the Lord sitting on a high and exalted throne," let it not enter your mind that he saw the Essence; rather, it is written "the Lord" <note>original: "Adonai," the name associated with Malchut</note>... "I saw the Lord of Hosts" upon the Throne.

# Section
## Three: "Hear, O Israel"
"And you shall love" <note>Deuteronomy 6:4-9</note>—this is the attribute of **Love**, which is the attribute of Abraham, the **Sefirah** <term>Sefirah: One of the ten divine channels or attributes through which the Infinite interacts with the world</term> called **Greatness** <term>Gedulah: Another name for Chesed (Loving-kindness), the fourth Sefirah</term>. These "gates" are the **Fifty Gates of Understanding**, for one must unify with them **Greatness** and **Strength** so that they may enter through them into the **Gates of Understanding**. Therefore, it says: "And on your gates."

# Section
## Four: "And it shall come to pass, if you surely listen"
This is the secret of **Strength** <term>Gevurah: The fifth Sefirah, representing restraint and judgment</term>, for this entire section <note>Deuteronomy 11:13-21</note> speaks of **punishments**, such as: "Take care for yourselves," and "lest your heart be seduced," and "the anger of the Lord will burn against you," etc. Thus, it is explained to you that the **four sections** in the Tefillin are the secret of the **unification of the Crown** <term>Keter: The highest Sefirah, the Divine Will</term>, which is written through **Wisdom** and **Understanding** in the first two sections, and through **Beauty** <term>Tiferet: The sixth Sefirah, the heart of the system that balances Mercy and Judgment</term>, **Greatness**, and **Strength** in the last two sections. You find yourself learning that in the **Tefillin of the Head**, **Beauty** is unified with **Wisdom**, **Understanding**, **Greatness**, and **Strength**, and everything becomes one single matter.

And behold, **Beauty**, which is the **Middle Line** <note>The central column of the Sefirotic tree that balances the right and left sides</note>, is unified in these four sections, which are the letters **Yod** and **Dalet** <note>Spelling 'Yad' (Hand), but also referencing the numerical structure of the Sefirot</note> in the secret of the **Crown**, which is the "tip of the [letter] Yod" <note>The topmost point of the first letter of the Divine Name, representing the highest level of emanation</note> that is attached to **Wisdom**. Thus, the six are found: the **Unique One** unifies above and becomes all **six Sefirot**, and [they are] one world, one matter. Therefore, **The Holy One, Blessed be He, wears Tefillin** <note>A famous Talmudic teaching from Berakhot 6a</note>—referring to the Holy One, Blessed be He, Himself, who is the secret of **Beauty**. Thus, the secret of the **Tefillin of the Head** is explained to you, along with their reason and benefit, and the secret of why the Holy One, Blessed be He, wears Tefillin. For the **Tefillin of the Head** are the secret of the **Supreme Truth Unification**. The **Tefillin of the Hand** is the secret of the unification of **Kingship** <term>Malchut: The tenth Sefirah, representing the Divine Presence in the world</term> with **Beauty**. Therefore, it says: "And it shall be for a sign upon your hand" <note>original: "yadchah" (ידכה), often interpreted as 'yad keh' (weak hand) or linked to the Sefirah of Gevurah</note>.

<column-break/>

...with the letters **Yod** and **Dalet** <note>י-ד, spelling 'Hand'</note>. For we have explained to you that in **Strength**, which is the secret of the **Hand**, the sections of the Tefillin are completed. They are the secret of the six **Sefirot**. The four [sections] are from the [seven] Sefirot, and the secret of the four **Sefirot** are unified in **Beauty**, which is the secret [of the Name], and in the **Supreme Crown** through the sections. And behold, the return of all of them is held in the **Hand**, which is in the secret of **Y-H-V**. This is the secret of the **Small Yod**, which is called **Foundation** <term>Yesod: The ninth Sefirah, the channel of procreation and connection</term>. For the **Large Yod** is **Supreme Wisdom**, while the **Small Yod** is **Foundation**. Therefore, **Kingship** is brought close to the **Hand** at the end: "And it shall be for a sign upon your hand"—a literal **sign** <note>The Hebrew word for 'sign' (ot) also means 'letter'</note>, the end of the sign of the **Holy Covenant**, which is **Foundation**. This is the secret of the knot of the **Tefillin of the Hand** in the shape of a **Yod**. For this reason, you find the **Tefillin of the Hand** on the **left** [arm], which holds **Kingship** with it. Thus, the **Unique Name** is unified in two kinds of unifications regarding the matter of **Tefillin**. Therefore, the **Tefillin of the Head** are called **"Strict Holiness"** and the **Tefillin of the Hand** are called **"Light Holiness,"** <note>A legal distinction in Jewish law regarding the sanctity of the two types of Tefillin</note> everything in its proper place. And behold, I shall explain further:

## Know
That when the **Tefillin** are in a person's possession, it is proper to don both of them in order to unify **Kingship** with **Beauty**, and to unify **Beauty** with the **Crown**. <meta>This page concludes the mystical discussion of the Exodus from Egypt and the Sefirot, specifically focusing on the Divine Presence (Shekhinah) and the highest emanations, Keter and Binah. It marks the end of the manuscript "Sha'arei Tzedek" (The Gates of Justice).</meta>

...the Divine Presence <term>Shekhinah: The feminine, indwelling aspect of God that accompanies Israel in the physical world</term>, and Israel went out with the armor <note>original: "השריון" (hashiryon). This may refer to divine protection or be a scribal variant related to the "remnant" of the people.</note> alongside her.

And this is the meaning of the verse: "And I shall act for the sake of My Name" <note>Ezekiel 20:9</note>—this refers to the **Shekhinah**. We have already explained that she is called **Name** (**Shem**). This is the secret of the Lord regarding the matter of "and let us make for ourselves a name." <note>Genesis 11:4</note> Therefore, the Blessed Name said to Moses: "**I Shall Be What I Shall Be**" <note>original: "Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh" (Exodus 3:14)</note>. I am the Supreme Name that cannot be grasped or known by any other.

However, He said to him: "Say to them again, 'I Shall Be has sent me to you.'" And the secret of what is written, "And I will show favor to whom I will show favor," <note>Exodus 33:19</note> applies even if they are not worthy. All of this is because of the oath He swore to Abraham at the **Covenant Between the Pieces**, as it is said: "Which He covenanted with Abraham, and His oath to Isaac, and He established it for Jacob as a law." <note>Psalms 105:9-10</note> For He said to him: "I will go down with you to Egypt, and I will also surely bring you up." <note>Genesis 46:4</note>

What is the meaning of "also surely bring you up"? It means: "When I come to take the **Shekhinah** out of Egypt, I will take your children out with her." This is the reason that the **Shekhinah** goes into exile with Israel in every place. This is the secret of the verse: "I am the Lord; in its time I will hasten it." <note>Isaiah 60:22</note> Regarding this, the sages said: "If they merit, I will hasten it; if they do not merit, then in its time," for He must eventually bring out the **Shekhinah**. Then Israel will be drawn out along with the **Shekhinah**, for they are attached to her—even in their state of impurity, as it is written: "Who dwells with them in the midst of their impurity." <note>Leviticus 16:16</note> This is the true meaning of the Exodus from Egypt: "And I shall act for the sake of My Name." And this is what the scripture means: "Everything that turns white is pure." <note>Leviticus 13:13</note>

**Already** we have explained to you that the **Shekhinah** is the secret of **Redemption** and the **Freedom** that brought 

<column-break/>

Israel out of Egypt. The reason is that **Understanding** <term>Binah: The third Sefirah, associated with the 'Divine Mother' and the source of liberation</term> is bound to the **Crown** <term>Keter: The first and highest Sefirah, representing the Infinite Will</term>, which is the world of Mercy. The **Crown** itself brought them out of Egypt through the channel of **Understanding**.

And this **Cover** <note>original: "Kapporet." Usually the lid of the Ark, here used as a metaphor for a protective divine layer.</note> is called **He** (**Hu**). The reason is that among all existing things, there is none who can reach the **Supreme Crown**, as it is not attainable by any being other than Himself. Therefore, He is called **I Shall Be** <note>Ehyeh</note> regarding His true essence; He is grasped by Himself, but not by any other.

Behold, even regarding **Understanding**, which is the third **Sefirah**, Moses our Teacher (peace be upon him) did not reach the knowledge of its true nature, nor did anyone else. For this reason, it is called **Nothingness** <term>Ayin: A term for the Divine that transcends human comprehension</term>. There is no one who can grasp its truth, its way of guiding the world, its desire for its creations, or why a certain thing is one way and not another.

Let this small amount I have hinted to you regarding the **Holy Names** be enough for you. If you are worthy, you will yet hear more from me with the help of the Blessed Name. May the Name, for the sake of His mercy and kindness, open our hearts to His Torah and merit us to the time of which it is written: "For the earth shall be full of the knowledge of the Lord, as the waters cover the sea." <note>Isaiah 11:9</note> Thus it is finished.

The book **Sha'arei Tzedek** (The Gates of Justice) is completed. Praise and thanks to God who formed all. We offer our supplications to every reader: if they find within it an error, an addition, or a linguistic omission, let them not blame us. Rather, it is proper to judge us favorably for all the toil we expended on it. We had many copies before us, and no two were alike; where one was missing something, another revealed it; when one stood, the other fell. We filled in the ruins and corrected it according to our limited intellect and what was bestowed upon us from Heaven to ensure the flow and continuity of the language. Therefore, it is proper to praise us for our good intentions. Blessed be the Lord, God of Israel, Hallelujah!

The entire work was completed here in **Riva di Trento** <note>Riva del Garda, Italy, a major center for Hebrew printing in the 16th century.</note> in the year **321** <note>1561 CE</note>, on the 15th of the month of Elul.

<meta>This page continues the mystical exploration of the Divine Names and their roles in the Exodus from Egypt. ...ministers who surround the **Tiferet** <term>Tiferet: The central Sefirah, representing beauty, harmony, and the ideal balance between mercy and justice</term>. And may the Blessed Name guide us in the truth:

---

They were steeped in idolatry in Egypt; therefore, they were not worthy of being redeemed, neither in a small measure nor a large one. This is as we will explain regarding the book of Ezekiel: "On the day I chose Israel, and lifted my hand to the seed of the house of Jacob, and made myself known to them in the land of Egypt... On that day I lifted my hand to them to bring them out of the land of Egypt to a land I had scouted for them, flowing with milk and honey. And I said to them, 'Cast away every man the detestable things of his eyes, and do not defile yourselves with the idols of Egypt.' But they rebelled against Me and would not listen to Me; they did not cast away the detestable things... then I thought to pour out My fury upon them, to spend My anger against them in the midst of the land of Egypt. But I acted for the sake of My Name, that it should not be profaned in the sight of the nations..." <note>Ezekiel 20:5-9</note> Thus you see that they were not worthy of being redeemed, even by the Blessed Name which is the Attribute of Mercy that includes Judgment. At that moment, the Holy One, Blessed be He, said to him: "From the perspective of the Attribute that is either slightly or greatly mixed with judgment, they do not deserve to be redeemed. But they shall be redeemed through the Attribute that is entirely pure Mercy, which is the secret of the **Crown** (**Keter**) <term>Keter: The 'Crown,' the highest and most hidden Sefirah, representing the Divine Will and absolute compassion</term>, which is the World of Mercy." This is the level of the "Long Face" <note>original: "אד"ר" (AD"R), likely an abbreviation for Arich Anpin, the Kabbalistic configuration of God as pure, infinite mercy</note> in which there is no judgment, neither a little nor a lot. And the secret of the verse "And I acted for the sake of My Name" refers to the literal Name. This is the **Shekhinah** <term>Shekhinah: The Divine Presence that dwells within the world and accompanies the people of Israel</term> who was with them in exile, who went down with our father Jacob, peace be upon him, as it is said: "I will go down with you to Egypt and I will also surely bring you up." <note>Genesis 46:4</note> For Jacob saw that his children were not worthy of being redeemed; therefore, the **Shekhinah** went down with them, and the Blessed Name came to take out

<column-break/>

And this is the place of Holiness. Therefore, **Seraphim** <term>Seraphim: A high order of angels described by Isaiah as having six wings</term> stand above Him, surrounding the Throne, and four Seraphim stand, each with six wings. For above the "Arms" <note>referring to the Sefirot of Hesed/Kindness and Gevurah/Strength</note> there is no angel, nor Seraph, nor minister; rather, every place you find mentioned in this context is above **Yesod** <term>Yesod: 'Foundation,' the Sefirah that channels divine energy into the world</term> and **Malchut** <term>Malchut: 'Kingship,' the lowest Sefirah, associated with the physical realm and the Shekhinah</term>. As it is written: "I saw the **Lord** <note>original: יו"ד - a shorthand for the Tetragrammaton</note> sitting on His throne, and all the host of heaven standing by Him on His right hand and on His left" <note>1 Kings 22:19</note>—"By Him" means above **Yesod** and **Malchut**. # The Name
->The tenth of the Sefirot is<-
->the Name called<-
# Ehyeh
->And it is the Name<-
->called "Nothing"<-
->the Highest over the Sefirot<-
which is the **Crown** (**Keter**), which is known only to itself and cannot be grasped by anything else. This is the language of the "Long Face" <note>Arich Anpin</note>, and behold, this Sefirah is the world of complete Mercy, having no mixture of judgment in the world at all. Rather, it is entirely pure Mercy, as we will explain with God's help.
**Know** that when the Blessed Name sent our teacher Moses, peace be upon him, to Egypt, Moses said before Him: "Master of the Universe, by which Attribute are You sending me? Is it by the Attribute of Judgment or the Attribute of Mercy?" And this is what we mean by the verse: "And they shall say to me, 'What is His name?' what shall I say to them?" <note>Exodus 3:13</note>. He replied: "I am sending you with the Attribute of Mercy," and I shall now explain.
**Know** that there is a Name called the Attribute of Absolute Judgment, which is the name **Elohim**. And there is a Name called Mercy, which is **YHVH**, may He be blessed. However, in **YHVH** there is a small mixture of judgment because the name **YHVH** is the secret of **Tiferet**, the Middle Column, which is comprised of both Mercy and Judgment, as you already know. And our teacher Moses, peace be upon him, said to the Master of the Universes: "If You send me to Egypt with the Attribute of Judgment, they will never leave there—not even with the Attribute of pure Judgment. Moreover, even with the name **YHVH**, which is a mix of mercy and judgment, Israel will not go out from there," because the people of Israel

<summary>This page explains that the Exodus was only possible through the highest Divine Name, Ehyeh (I Shall Be), which represents the Sefirah of Keter (the Crown) and absolute mercy. The author argues that because the Israelites were unworthy due to idolatry, standard levels of mercy mixed with judgment would not have sufficed to redeem them.</summary>
<keywords>Sefirot, Keter, Tiferet, Mercy vs Judgment, Ehyeh, Exodus, Moses, Ezekiel, Shekhinah, Seraphim, Divine Names, Kabbalah</keywords>

<meta>This page serves as a foundational diagram for the concepts discussed previously. Having transitioned from the specific discussion of the tenth Sefirah (Malchut) and the exodus from Egypt, the text now provides a systematic reference table and a visual map of the ten Divine Emanations (Sefirot), linking them to specific Divine Names as taught in the classical Kabbalistic text, "Sha'are Tzedek" (The Gates of Justice).</meta>

# From the Bottom Upward

## Table of Signposts for the Origins of the Sefirot
### According to "The Gates of Justice" <note>Sha'are Tzedek: A seminal 13th-century Kabbalistic work by Rabbi Joseph Gikatilla, which maps the relationship between the Sefirot and the Names of God.</note>

<margin>1</margin> The first Sefirah is **Malchut** <term>Malchut: "Kingdom"; the lowest of the ten emanations, representing the physical world and the Divine Presence (Shekhinah) among us.</term>, which is called **Adonai** <note>original: "אדנ'" - a common abbreviation for the Name signifying Lordship.</note>.
<margin>15</margin> The second Sefirah is **Yesod** <term>Yesod: "Foundation"; the channel that connects the upper emanations to the world below.</term>, called **El Chai** <note>original: "אל חי" - "The Living God."</note>.
<margin>22 etc.</margin> The third Sefirah is **Hod** <term>Hod: "Splendor" or "Majesty"; associated with the left leg or the quality of surrender.</term>, called **Elohim Tzevaot** <note>original: "אלקים צבאות" - "God of Hosts."</note>.
<margin>26</margin> The fourth Sefirah is **Netzach** <term>Netzach: "Victory" or "Eternity"; associated with the right leg or the quality of endurance.</term>, called **YHVH Tzevaot** <note>original: "יי' צבאות" - "The Lord of Hosts."</note>.
<margin>27</margin> The fifth Sefirah is **Tiferet** <term>Tiferet: "Beauty" or "Harmony"; the central balance between Mercy and Judgment.</term>, called **YHVH** <note>original: "יהוה" - The Tetragrammaton, the most sacred four-letter Name of God.</note>.
<margin>35</margin> The sixth Sefirah is **Gevurah** <term>Gevurah: "Might" or "Strength"; representing the attribute of strict Judgment and restraint.</term>, called **Elohim** <note>original: "אלקים" - "God"; a Name associated with judgment and the laws of nature.</note>.
<margin>38</margin> The seventh Sefirah is **Chesed** <term>Chesed: "Lovingkindness"; representing God's infinite benevolence and the attribute of Mercy.</term>, called **El** <note>original: "אל" - A Divine Name signifying power and kindness.</note>.
<margin>40</margin> The eighth Sefirah is **Binah** <term>Binah: "Understanding"; the "Divine Mother" where the spark of wisdom is processed and developed.</term>, called **YHVH Adonai** <note>original: "יהוה אדנ'" - A combination of the Tetragrammaton and the Name of Lordship.</note>.
<margin>66</margin> The ninth Sefirah is **Chochmah** <term>Chochmah: "Wisdom"; the initial flash of Divine inspiration or pure intuition.</term>, called **Yah** <note>original: "יה" - A shorter, poetic Name of God.</note>.
<margin>52</margin> The tenth Sefirah is **Keter** <term>Keter: "Crown"; the highest point of the Divine will, touching the Infinite.</term>, called **Ehyeh** <note>original: "אהיה" - "I Will Be," as revealed to Moses at the Burning Bush.</note>.

It illustrates the hierarchical structure of the "Tree of Life," from the highest source (Crown) to the lowest physical manifestation (Kingdom).</summary>

It features a mystical diagram that serves as a visual guide to Kabbalistic thought, though the ink has significantly faded over time.</meta>

-><unclear>The Secret of the Ten Sefirot</unclear> <note>original: "Sod Eser Sefirot." The <term>Sefirot</term> are the ten attributes or emanations in Kabbalah through which the Infinite reveals itself and continuously creates both the physical and metaphysical realms.</note><-

-><unclear>Crown</unclear> <note>original: "Keter." The highest Sefirah, representing the divine will or the "nothingness" from which all being emerges.</note><-

->Understanding &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Wisdom<-
<note>original: "Binah" (Understanding) and "Chokhmah" (Wisdom). These represent the feminine and masculine intellectual potencies of the divine mind.</note>

->Strength &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Loving-kindness<-
<note>original: "Gevurah" (Strength/Judgment) and "Chesed" (Loving-kindness). These represent the primary emotional attributes: the impulse to give and the necessity of restraint.</note>

->Beauty<-
<note>original: "Tiferet." The central point of the diagram, balancing the tension between Loving-kindness and Strength; it is often associated with the heart and the written Torah.</note>

->Splendor &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Victory<-
<note>original: "Hod" (Splendor) and "Netzach" (Victory/Endurance). These are the lower emotional attributes, often described as the "legs" or supports of the divine structure.</note>

->Foundation<-
<note>original: "Yesod." The channel that gathers all the energies from the upper Sefirot to transmit them to the final sphere.</note>

->Kingdom<-
<note>original: "Malkhut." The final Sefirah, representing the physical world and the indwelling divine presence, known as the Shekhinah.</note>

Ten Emanations <note>original: "Eser Sefirot"</note>, Crown <note>original: "Keter"</note>, Wisdom <note>original: "Chokhmah"</note>, Understanding <note>original: "Binah"</note>, Loving-kindness <note>original: "Chesed"</note>, Strength <note>original: "Gevurah"</note>, Beauty <note>original: "Tiferet"</note>, Victory <note>original: "Netzach"</note>, Splendor <note>original: "Hod"</note>, Foundation <note>original: "Yesod"</note>, Kingdom <note>original: "Malkhut"</note>, Tree <note>original: "Ilan"</note>, Kabbalah.
